### [1] [Real-Time Atomic-Resolution Electron Phase Imaging without Probe Calibration via Ptychography-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25684)
*H. Yue,C. -C. Chen,C. -N. Hsiao,J. Cheng,Y. Liu,X. Z. Liao,Steve F. Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种叠层成像监督的局部推理框架（ptychography-supervised local inference framework），将四维扫描透射电镜（4D-STEM）数据转化为采集过程中可实时运行的相位成像流程，以约0.27毫秒/探针位置的延迟实现约1,000倍加速，并保持原子级晶格对比度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度相位成像对解析缺陷、界面与弱散射原子至关重要，但电子叠层成像（electron ptychography）虽具备亚埃级相位灵敏度，其迭代重建计算昂贵且对实验标定敏感，属于离线技术，无法在数据采集过程中实时使用。

Method: 由单一实验 AuPd 数据集重建的物理约束参考相位图（physics-constrained reference phase maps）作为教师标签，训练一个紧凑模型，直接从衍射测量预测局部相位图块，无需显式探针输入或在线迭代优化；全视场图像通过确定性重叠拼接（deterministic overlap stitching）组装。

Result: 在线延迟约 0.27 ms/探针位置，吞吐量约 20,000 位置/秒，相较 GPU 加速的 ePIE 提速约 1,000 倍，同时保留原子级晶格对比度与倒空间保真度（reciprocal-space fidelity）。模型无需微调即可迁移至不同材料（WS2）、不同离焦条件（高熵合金纳米颗粒）与不同仪器（300 kV 下的 hBN）。

Conclusion: 该方案将叠层成像的冗余性摊销（amortize）为快速且可泛化的工作流程，使材料显微学中的实时原子尺度相位成像成为可能。

Abstract: Atomic-scale phase imaging is central to resolving defects, interfaces, and weakly scattering atoms that govern the behavior of nanoscale materials. Electron ptychography delivers sub-ångström phase sensitivity but remains an offline technique, because its iterative reconstruction is computationally expensive and sensitive to experimental calibration, preventing live use during data acquisition. Here, a ptychography-supervised local inference framework is presented that converts four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) into an acquisition-compatible phase-imaging workflow. Physics-constrained reference phase maps reconstructed from a single experimental AuPd dataset serve as teacher labels for a compact model that predicts local phase patches directly from diffraction measurements, without explicit probe input or online iterative optimization. Full-field images are assembled by deterministic overlap stitching. The workflow reaches an online latency of about 0.27 ms per probe position and a throughput of about 20,000 positions per second, an approximately 1,000-fold speed-up over GPU-accelerated ePIE, while preserving atomic-scale lattice contrast and reciprocal-space fidelity. Without fine-tuning, the same model transfers across materials (WS2), defocus conditions (high-entropy alloy nanoparticles), and instruments (hBN at 300 kV). The approach amortizes ptychographic redundancy into a fast, generalizable workflow that enables real-time atomic-scale phase imaging for materials microscopy.

</details>

### [2] [Hypergeometric Mixed-Type Multiple Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26110)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 在正实轴上为秩一 $q\times p$ 权矩阵构造了两类超几何混合型多重正交形式（Jacobi 型与 Laguerre 型），在近对角容许范围内给出显式表达、Mellin 公式与 Rodrigues 型公式，并导出了双正交序列的对偶递推、带状系数的 Gamma–Pochhammer 表达式及递推矩阵的双对角分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为正实轴上任意阶 $q\times p$ 秩一权矩阵建立超几何型混合多重正交形式的一般框架，并给出其显式构造与表示公式。

Method: 在正实轴上构造 Jacobi 型与 Laguerre 型两类混合型多重正交形式，限定于近对角容许范围；通过 Gamma 商 Mellin 公式、Meijer $G$ 表示、Rodrigues 型公式以及 Christoffel 变换与 Gauss–Borel 分解进行推导。

Result: 混合形式被显式获得：幂向量分量为单一终止广义超几何多项式，超几何向量分量为此类多项式之有限和；Jacobi 型情形归结为终止 Kampé de Fériet 多项式在 $(x,1)$ 处取值，Laguerre 型情形由有限三角连接系统控制。步线上双正交序列满足含 $p$ 条次对角线与 $q$ 条超对角线的对偶递推，各带状系数由有限 Gamma–Pochhammer 表达式显式给出，递推矩阵在 Christoffel 变换与 Gauss–Borel 分解下呈双对角分解。

Conclusion: 下因子具闭合 Pochhammer 公式，上因子可由 Christoffel tau-行列式或平移矩子式的交叉比表示；两条链在混合 Piñeiro 特殊化下显式闭合，且合流极限将 Jacobi 型系统与 Laguerre 型及 Hermite 型系统相联系。

Abstract: Two hypergeometric families of mixed-type multiple orthogonal forms are constructed on the positive real line for rank-one $q\times p$ matrices of weights, with arbitrary $p$ and $q$: Jacobi-like and Laguerre-like systems. In the admissible near-diagonal range, both mixed forms are obtained explicitly. Their power-vector components are single terminating generalized hypergeometric polynomials, whereas their hypergeometric-vector components are finite sums of such polynomials. In the Jacobi-like case these sums reduce to finite combinations of terminating Kampé de Fériet polynomials evaluated at $(x,1)$; in the mixed beta--Euler Laguerre-like case they are governed by a finite triangular connection system. Gamma-quotient Mellin formulas, Meijer $G$-representations, and Rodrigues-type formulas are obtained for the complete forms. On the step-line, the associated biorthogonal sequences satisfy dual recurrences with $p$ subdiagonals and $q$ superdiagonals, and every band coefficient is given explicitly by finite Gamma--Pochhammer expressions. Christoffel transformations and Gauss--Borel factorization yield bidiagonal factorizations of the recurrence matrices. The lower factors have closed Pochhammer formulas, while the upper factors are expressed through Christoffel tau-determinants or cross-ratios of shifted moment minors. Both chains close explicitly in the mixed Piñeiro specialization. Confluent limits connect the Jacobi-like systems with the Laguerre-like and Hermite-like systems.

</details>


### [3] [Voltage and current density in a rectangle with point in- and ejection via Green's functions and $q$-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26649)
*P. Van Mieghem*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文在二维欧几里得空间矩形域中计算一对异号点电荷（分别位于源点 s 与汇点 d）产生的电流密度，通过 q-分析（q-analysis）给出势与电流密度大小的精确且高效解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩形内电流密度虽属经典问题，但存在双重傅里叶级数（double Fourier series）收敛极慢等计算障碍，需要更高效的解析求解手段。

Method: 将电流密度表示为满足泊松方程（Poisson equation，即麦克斯韦第一定律）的势之梯度，并借助与高斯多项式（Gaussian polynomials）和雅可比 theta 函数（Jacobi's theta-functions）相关的 q-分析（q-analysis）进行求解。

Result: 给出了矩形内势与电流密度大小的精确解析公式，且计算效率很高。

Conclusion: q-分析与椭圆函数（elliptic function）基本构件可有效缓解经典求解中的收敛瓶颈，为矩形域电流密度问题提供精确、高效的解析表达。

Abstract: The current density in a rectangle in the $D=2$ dimensional Euclidean space, where opposite point charges are placed in a source $s$ and a destination $d$, is computed as the gradient of a potential that obeys the Poisson equation (first law of Maxwell). Although the computation of the current density in a 2D rectangle is a classical problem, several barriers (e.g. very slow convergence of double Fourier series) were eventually alleviated by a $q$-analysis, related to Gaussian polynomials and Jacobi's theta-functions, that form the basic building blocks for any elliptic function. We present an exact and computationally very efficient analytic formula of the potential and the magnitude of the current density in a rectangle.

</details>

### [4] [Scaling of qubit coherence in quantum dot based Majorana chains: Rabi and Ramsey oscillations of Majorana qubits formed by four and six quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25223)
*Haining Pan,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了由双 $N$ 位量子点人工 Kitaev 链（artificial Kitaev chain）构成的 Majorana 量子比特（Majorana-based qubit）的 Rabi 与 Ramsey 相干时间（coherence time），比较四量子点与六量子点比特，并考察无序（disorder）与链长对相干性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于近期实验实现（arXiv:2607.09511），需要将量子比特推广到任意链长，并厘清在存在无序的真实条件下，增加链长是否以及在何种条件下能提升 Rabi 与 Ramsey 相干时间。

Method: 将双量子点链比特推广至任意长度 $N$，在理想（pristine）极限与含真实无序条件下，对四量子点与六量子点比特的 Rabi 和 Ramsey 振荡进行数值模拟，分别采用三组参数：小超导能隙、大超导能隙以及实验参数。

Result: 理想极限下 Rabi 振荡与链长无关，而六量子点比特的 Ramsey 振荡有更大泄漏（leakage），源于第三个格点既抑制偏离甜点（sweet spot）的杂散能级劈裂，又使有意劈裂需要更大失谐（detuning）从而可能激发体态（bulk）。含无序时两种相干时间都并非随链长普遍改善：Rabi 相干由三条退相位（dephasing）通道竞争决定，仅在链间隧穿（interchain tunneling）涨落被抑制时额外量子点才有收益；Ramsey 相干由 $\\sigma_z$ 旋转期间能级劈裂的涨落决定，四量子点比特仅由甜点无序决定且与平均劈裂无关，而六量子点比特中产生劈裂的失谐会带来随劈裂增大的涨落，故仅在小的劈裂下额外量子点才有收益。

Conclusion: 更长的链更适合作为量子存储器（quantum memory），但不一定是更适合操控的量子比特；尽管更长的 Kitaev 链比特理论上应具备拓扑保护（topological protection），在当前 Rabi 与 Ramsey 测量方案中观测到该保护仍是实验挑战。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of the Rabi and Ramsey coherence time of Majorana-based qubits formed by double $N$-site quantum-dot artificial Kitaev chains, following the recent experimental realization arXiv:2607.09511. We generalize the qubit to two chains of arbitrary length and compare the Rabi and Ramsey oscillations of the four-dot and six-dot qubits, simulated with realistic disorder for three sets of parameters: small and large superconducting gaps, and the experimental parameters. We find that, in the pristine limit, the Rabi oscillation is independent of the chain length, whereas the Ramsey oscillation of the six-dot qubit suffers from larger leakage, because the third site suppresses the unwanted energy splitting away from the sweet spot but at the same time makes the deliberate splitting difficult to achieve, which requires a larger detuning that could excite the bulk. In reality with disorder, neither coherence time improves universally with the chain length. The Rabi coherence is set by the competition of three dephasing channels, and the additional dots pay off only if the fluctuation of the interchain tunneling is suppressed; the Ramsey coherence is set by the fluctuation of the energy splitting during the $σ_z$ rotation, which for the four-dot qubit is fixed by the sweet-spot disorder alone and does not depend on the mean splitting, whereas for the six-dot qubit the detuning that generates the splitting adds a fluctuation growing with the splitting itself, so the additional dots pay off only for small splittings. This implies that a longer chain is thus a better quantum memory but not necessarily a better qubit under manipulation. Although topological protection is expected in a longer Kitaev chain qubit, observing it in the current Rabi and Ramsey measurement protocols remains an experimental challenge.

</details>


### [5] [Finite-state automata for exact matrix product operators of tight-binding Hamiltonians: fractals, quasicrystals, trees and hyperbolic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25276)
*Marta Brzezińska,Jeanne Colbois,Loïc Herviou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种针对递归结构格点上单粒子哈密顿量的系统性矩阵乘积算符（matrix product operator, MPO）构造方法，将格点几何编码为有限状态自动机，从而获得精确解析的 MPO 表示，并结合核多项式方法（kernel polynomial method）实现指数大规模体系的谱性质计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受张量网络（tensor networks）在大格点紧束缚哈密顿量（tight-binding Hamiltonians）模拟中最新进展的启发，需要一种能统一处理多种格点几何、且避免显式构造完整哈密顿量的高效表示方法。

Method: 利用格点的递归结构将其几何编码为有限状态自动机（finite-state automaton），并借鉴阿贝尔对称张量网络（Abelian-symmetric tensor networks）的思想，构造精确且解析的 MPO 表示；张量数目随系统尺寸对数增长，键维度（bond dimension）由自动机状态数决定。

Result: 该框架在多种格点上展示了通用性，包括规则格点、分形（fractals）、Cayley 树、双曲格点（hyperbolic lattices）以及一维和二维 Fibonacci 准晶（quasicrystals）；结合核多项式方法可实现大规模谱性质计算而无需显式构造完整哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 该 MPO 构造为在指数级大规模体系下探索广泛类别格点的电子性质提供了一条统一路径。

Abstract: Inspired by the recent progress in the simulation of tight-binding Hamiltonians on large lattices using tensor networks, we introduce a systematic matrix product operator (MPO) construction for single-particle Hamiltonians on recursively structured lattices. Taking advantage of this recursive structure, we encode the lattice geometry in a finite-state automaton and, adapting ideas from Abelian-symmetric tensor networks, obtain an exact and analytical MPO representation of the Hamiltonian, where the number of tensors grows logarithmically with the system size and the bond dimension is set by the number of automaton states. Several examples demonstrate the generality of the framework: regular lattices, fractals, Cayley trees, hyperbolic lattices, and one- and two-dimensional Fibonacci quasicrystals. Combined with the kernel polynomial method, these MPO representations enable large-scale calculations of spectral properties without explicitly constructing the full Hamiltonian. This provides a unified route to the exploration of electronic properties across a broad class of lattices at exponentially large system sizes.

</details>


### [6] [Influence of Electrostatic Environment on the Proximity Spin-Orbit Coupling in Graphene on Transition-Metal Dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25305)
*Bert Jorissen,Bart Partoens,Aires Ferreira,Lucian Covaci*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作采用紧束缚（tight-binding）方法研究石墨烯/过渡金属硫族化物（TMD）异质结中近邻诱导的自旋轨道耦合（SOC）。作者提取了描述石墨烯低能物理的有效四带模型参数，并发现自旋-谷（spin-valley）与Rashba自旋轨道耦合能及Rashba相位强烈依赖扭转角和静电调控，同时能带对齐与电荷转移的差异可解释文献中自旋轨道参数的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯置于过渡金属硫族化物上时会产生近邻诱导自旋轨道耦合，但其有效参数受多种因素影响且文献报道值分散。本文旨在系统考察扭转角、材料组分、能带对齐（band alignment）与电荷转移偶极（charge-transfer dipole）对石墨烯层低能自旋轨道参数的影响。

Method: 采用紧束缚（tight-binding）方法，提取描述石墨烯层低能物理的有效四带（4-band）模型的紧束缚参数，并将其表示为扭转角、材料组成（MoS₂、MoSe₂、WS₂、WSe₂）、能带对齐与电荷转移偶极的函数；重点纳入近邻硫族层上由电荷重分布产生的电荷转移偶极，并考察能带对齐变化的影响。

Result: 自旋-谷（spin-valley）与Rashba自旋轨道耦合能，以及决定动量空间自旋织构（spin texture）取向的Rashba相位，均表现出对扭转角和静电调控的强烈依赖。能带对齐与电荷重分布的变化可贡献于文献中自旋轨道参数的分散分布。

Conclusion: 在石墨烯/TMD异质结中，近邻诱导自旋轨道耦合参数并非单值，而是由扭转角、组分、能带对齐及电荷转移偶极共同调控。纳入电荷转移偶极并考虑能带对齐变化，有助于解释文献中自旋轨道参数报道值的分散性。

Abstract: We investigate the proximity-induced spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in graphene on transition-metal dichalcogenide (TMD) heterostructures using a tight-binding approach. The tight-binding parameters of an effective 4-band model describing the low-energy physics of the graphene layer are extracted as a function of twist angle, material composition (MoS${}_2$, MoSe${}_2$, WS${}_2$, WSe${}_2$), band alignment, and charge-transfer dipole. We investigate the effect of a charge-transfer dipole induced on the chalcogen layer closest to graphene, which is typically neglected in tight-binding models but included in ab initio calculations through charge redistribution. In addition, we explore the influence of variations in the band alignment. We find that the spin-valley and Rashba spin-orbit coupling energies as well as the so-called Rashba phase, governing the orientation of momentum-space spin textures, show a strong dependence on the twist angle and electrostatic tuning. Our results suggest that variations in band alignment and charge redistribution contribute to the spread of spin-orbit parameters reported in the literature.

</details>


### [7] [Observation of the magnetic spin Hall effect in a ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25406)
*Nicholas Davey-García,Jone Mencos,Luciano Bravo,Inge Groen,Luis E. Hueso,Andreas Berger,Fèlix Casanova*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作利用非局域横向自旋阀（non-local lateral spin-valve）几何结构，在垂直磁化的钴基多层膜中电学识别出磁自旋霍尔效应（magnetic spin Hall effect, MSHE）及其逆效应，并测得磁自旋霍尔角约为 (3.8 ± 0.6)%，量级可与 Pt 等重金属相比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋霍尔效应（spin Hall effect）中自旋流方向、自旋极化与驱动电场相互垂直，对称性受限；磁有序可解除该限制并引入时间反演对称性奇（time-reversal-symmetry-odd）的自旋电导张量分量，从而实现自旋极化与磁有序的耦合调控。MSHE 已在反铁磁体中被观测，但在常规铁磁体中的实验确认仍属空白。

Method: 采用非局域横向自旋阀（non-local lateral spin-valve）几何结构，在垂直磁化的钴基多层膜（Co-based multilayer）中进行电学测量，通过翻转多层膜磁化方向比较 MSHE 信号与常规自旋霍尔/逆自旋霍尔信号的行为差异。

Result: 磁化翻转使 MSHE 与磁性逆自旋霍尔（magnetic inverse spin Hall）信号反向，证实其时间反演对称性奇特征及磁化可控性；相同器件中常规自旋霍尔与逆自旋霍尔信号在磁化翻转下保持不变，符合时间反演对称性偶（even）特征。测得磁自旋霍尔角 θ_MSH = (3.8 ± 0.6)%，量级与 Pt 等自旋电子学常用重金属的自旋霍尔角相当。

Conclusion: 结果将 MSHE 确立为常规铁磁体中一种可观的、磁可切换的自旋-电荷互转换（spin-charge interconversion）机制。

Abstract: The conventional spin Hall effect generates spin currents whose flow direction, spin polarization, and driving electric field are mutually perpendicular. Magnetic order lifts this symmetry restriction and enables additional time-reversal-symmetry-odd components of the spin-conductivity tensor, giving rise to the magnetic spin Hall effect (MSHE). These components also couple the generated spin polarization to the magnetic order, providing a degree of control absent in the conventional spin Hall effect. Although the MSHE has been observed in antiferromagnets, its experimental identification in conventional ferromagnets has remained elusive. Here, using a non-local lateral spin-valve geometry, we electrically identify the MSHE and its reciprocal effect in a perpendicularly magnetized Co-based multilayer. Reversal of the multilayer magnetization reverses the MSHE and magnetic inverse spin Hall signals, revealing their time-reversal-symmetry-odd character and magnetization control. By contrast, the conventional spin Hall and inverse spin Hall signals measured in the same devices remain unchanged under magnetization reversal, consistent with their time-reversal-symmetry-even character. The central object is the correlation matrix, which takes the form of Toeplitz or block-Toeplitz matrix. Together with the extensivity of the entropy function, it explains the robust $\log_2 L_A$ scaling of entanglement at criticality. Abstract: Cryogenic electronics forms a rapidly emerging research domain for high-performance and power-efficient computing applications. To fully leverage these opportunities, magnetic control schemes require alternative options to current-based writing, which is the main bottleneck regarding power consumption and downscaling. In this regard, voltage-based gating of the magnetic state could drastically enhance operational efficiency and integration density. In this work, we investigate cryogenic Voltage Control of Magnetism (VCM) in epitaxial SrTiO$_3$/Fe thin film heterostructures on a CMOS compatible Si substrate. We demonstrate and quantify voltage-controlled modifications of the magnetic domain structure, consistent with electric field-controlled magnetic anisotropy at the Fe/SrTiO$_3$ interface.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weyl 与多重简并（multifold）半金属的贝里曲率通量源自单极型（monopole-like）源，而具有偶极型 BC 源且陈数（Chern number）为零的体系其磁电响应尚不明确，故需系统考察此类体系。

Abstract: We compute the linear magnetoelectric conductivity of a two-band vortex nodal-ring (VNR) semimetal and a three-band Hopf semimetal (HSM) within the semiclassical Boltzmann formalism in the relaxation-time approximation. Working consistently to cubic order in the magnetic field, we evaluate the Drude, BC, orbital-magnetic-moment (OMM), anomalous-Hall, and Lorentz-force contributions to the conductivity. In both systems, the dipolar BC and OMM generate odd powers of $|\boldsymbol B|$ in in-plane response channels that are symmetry-forbidden in systems with monopole-like BC sources. For the HSM, the internode-scattering contribution to the conductivity vanishes identically. By comparing the VNR with a $\mathcal{PT}$-broken gapped nodal ring and the HSM with a pseudospin-1 triple-point semimetal, we demonstrate that the dipolar nature of the BC gives rise to distinctive features in magnetoelectric response that are absent in semimetals with monopole-like BC flux.

</details>

Selective spin transport is achieved, leading to a high degree of spin polarization.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 仅给出简要指令后，智能体自主规划多步测量方案，安全操作低温（cryogenic）仪器，分析数据并输出最终报告。该流程通过 FermiLink 智能体框架（agent harness）实现，设计上强调仪器安全以及测量与分析的可靠性；实验对象为多个单层与双层 MoS2 器件，包含长达六天的自主测量活动。

Abstract: Artificial-intelligence (AI) agents are beginning to enter experimental laboratories, automating experiments and accelerating scientific discovery. Herein, we introduce an AI-driven workflow in which an AI agent performs multi-step, multi-day quantum transport measurements end-to-end. Specifically, given brief instructions, the agent starts by planning the multi-step measurements, then safely operates the cryogenic instruments, analyzes the data, and concludes with a final report. We demonstrate this AI workflow on multiple monolayer and bilayer MoS2 devices. Through autonomous measurement campaigns lasting up to six days, the agent determined the conduction-band spin-orbit coupling energy in monolayer MoS2, and mapped a layer- and valley-resolved phase diagram in bilayer MoS2. This experimental workflow is implemented through the FermiLink agent harness, which emphasizes instrumental safety and the reliability of the measurement and analysis. The framework is general and can be readily adapted to other types of experiments, representing a step toward self-driving laboratories.

</details>


### [14] [Benchmarking the generalized Kadanoff-Baym ansatz and second-order adiabatic expansion using time-dependent spintronic effects: Spin pumping, torque, and inertia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26694)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡格林函数（NEGF）计算时间依赖量子输运代价高昂，GKBA 与绝热展开（AE）是两类流行的简化策略，但二者在含经典自由度（如自旋电子学中的局域磁矩）系统中的相对精度尚缺乏系统比较。

Result: GKBA 无法描述进动局域磁矩对自旋流的泵浦、STT 矢量及由此诱导的磁矩动力学；二阶 AE 则与数值严格基准在自旋泵浦和 STT 两方面均高度吻合，并揭示自旋泵浦本质上具有非绝热（nonadiabatic）特性。但即便纳入磁矩时间二阶导数，AE 仍无法刻画作为磁惯性标志的快速章动振荡。

Conclusion: AE 为精确描述 STT 驱动的磁化动力学提供了路径，可与第一性原理哈密顿量结合，且计算成本仅为完整 NEGF 时间演化的一小部分；然而其在描述磁惯性的章动特征上仍存在局限。

Abstract: The generalized Kadanoff-Baym ansatz (GKBA) [P. Lipavský {\em et al.}, Phys. Rev. B {\bf 34}, 6933 (1986)] has emerged as a popular and numerically efficient algorithm for simplification of nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF)-based calculations of time-dependent quantum transport. For systems that can be split into classical and quantum degrees of freedom, another popular simplifying strategy is adiabatic expansion (AE) of NEGF [N. Bode {\em et al.}, Phys. Rev. Lett. {\bf 107}, 036804 (2011); S. Deghi {\em et al.}, Phys. Rev. B {\bf 110}, 115409 (2024)] in terms of the velocity of classical degrees of freedom, such as localized magnetic moments (LMMs) in spintronics or coordinates of nuclei in nanoelectronics. Here we compare GKBA and second-order AE with numerically exact benchmarks for two-terminal junctions whose central region hosting quantum electrons and classical LMMs is attached to two semi-infinite normal metal leads. Three simple models are employed to exhibit cornerstone time-dependent effects in spintronics---spin pumping and spin-transfer torque (STT), as well as magnetic inertia as a recently explored phenomenon. We find that GKBA fails to describe pumping of spin current by precessing LMMs, or STT vectors, and thereby induced LMM dynamics. Conversely, the second-order AE matches numerically exact benchmarks for both effects remarkably well, thereby also revealing the essentially {\em nonadiabatic} nature of spin pumping. Thus, AE opens a path toward an accurate description of STT-driven magnetization dynamics, including combination with first-principles Hamiltonians, while incurring a fraction of the cost of time evolution of full NEGF. However, despite including terms up to the second time derivatives of LMMs into AE, this approach fails to capture fast nutational oscillations on top of the precessional motion of LMMs as the hallmark of magnetic inertia.

</details>


### [15] [Nonlinear Spin Polarization Enables Néel Order Switching in Centrosymmetric Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26709)

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种非线性电学路径，用于在中心对称交错磁体（altermagnet）中实现奈尔序（Néel order）的确定性翻转，并结合反常霍尔响应实现写入-读取方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称交错磁体中，反演对称性（inversion symmetry）禁止线性电流诱导的自旋极化，使奈尔序的确定性电学翻转面临挑战。

Method: 基于自旋群对称性（spin group symmetry）分析非相对论极限下的非线性自旋极化，并构建 d 波交错磁体 FeSb2 的最小模型，结合 Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert 方程进行宏观自旋模拟。

Result: 非线性自旋极化被约束沿奈尔矢量方向，且在 10 个非平凡自旋-劳厄群（spin-Laue group）中有 8 个允许交错响应；有限自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）产生垂直于奈尔矢量的均匀分量，该分量单独引起振荡动力学，而与交错响应协同可实现确定性 180° 翻转。

Conclusion: 电场取向可反转交错自旋响应并选择翻转方向，相反奈尔态可由反常霍尔响应（anomalous Hall response）区分，从而建立中心对称交错磁体的非线性电学写入-读取方案。

Abstract: Deterministic electrical switching of the Néel order remains challenging in centrosymmetric altermagnets, where linear current-induced spin polarization is forbidden by inversion symmetry. Here, we establish a nonlinear route to Néel order control. We further show that changing the electric field orientation reverses the staggered spin response and selects the switching direction, while the opposite Néel states can be distinguished by their anomalous Hall response. Our results establish a nonlinear electrical write-read scheme for centrosymmetric altermagnets.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 计算中仅发现极小的自旋极化（spin polarisation）；两种对映体（enantiomers）的自旋极化因子（即最大与最小总电流的相对差）基本相同，但达到最大与最小电流所需的针尖极化方向在两种对映体间不同。此外，只有自洽地计入微扰论二阶项，才能获得与实验观测的定性一致。文中还讨论了不同 U/t 比下链上各原子自旋布居（spin populations）的分布。

Conclusion: 尽管电子-电子相互作用（electron-electron interactions）对于理解分子结中的自旋选择性很重要，但不能单独将其视为实验中观测到的高自旋极化因子的成因。

Abstract: The Non-Equilibrium Green's Function (NEGF) approach is applied to a chain of Hubbard atoms in a helical geometry sandwiched between two leads, one of which is magnetic, to assess what role the electronic correlation effects within the molecule may play in explaining the Chirality Induced Spin Selectivity (CISS) effect. The correlation effects are treated at the level of the self-consistent second Born approximation. Two chiralities, Laevorotatory (L) and Dextrorotatory (D), of the helix, are discussed. Further, we account for spin-orbit coupling (SOC) within the chains using a simplified model of Kane and Mele that exploits next-nearest neighbour interactions intrinsic to a curved geometry. The interaction with the leads is considered within the Wide Band Approximation. We consider the ratio, U/t, of the Hubbard constant U to the hopping t between atomic sites along the chain of up to two, but found very little spin polarisation in our calculations. Even though the spin polarisation factor defined as a relative difference between the largest and smallest total current is found for both enantiomers practically the same, the orientation of the tip's polarisation required to reach the largest and smallest currents for the two enantiomers differed. It is also found that the qualitative agreement with observations can only be achieved if we account, self-consistently, for the second order of the perturbation theory. The distribution of the spin populations on the chain atoms for different U/t ratios is also discussed. This study concludes that even though accounting for electron-electron interactions are important in understanding spin selectivity within molecular junctions, they cannot be made solely responsible for the high spin polarisation factors observed in experiments.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Parnassus is a fast detector-simulation and reconstruction framework that maps truth-level particles directly to reconstructed particles. In this work, we investigate cross-detector transfer learning by adapting a Parnassus model for the ALEPH detector to the SLD detector. ALEPH and SLD have similar detector responses, as they were both targeting hadronic $Z$-pole $e^+e^-$ events, while differing substantially in the underlying detector technology, reconstruction, and archived data representation. We initialize the SLD particle model with weights learned on ALEPH, fine-tune it on SLD, and compare it with the same architecture trained directly on SLD. The ALEPH-initialized model gives substantially improved particle- and jet-level agreement with the SLD reference, including a factor of 5.2 improvement in the charged-particle angular response and an improvement in the jet angular resolution from $1.73$ to $1.15$ times the SLD reference. The results show that detector-response information learned on ALEPH can be reused when modeling SLD and motivate reusable pretrained Parnassus models for legacy $e^+e^-$ detectors, which is especially critical for experiments without access to the original software pipeline. With this paper, we also release an AI-ready version of simulated SLD $e^+e^-$ events.

Radiation damage in SiPMs manifests through two main mechanisms: ionizing energy loss and non-ionizing energy loss (NIEL), which increases dark current and dark count rate. In this work, breakdown voltage, dark current, and quenching resistance were systematically measured as functions of accumulated dose and fluence. Gain and dark count rates were indirectly measured from the characterized electrical parameters. Both technologies exhibit remarkable stability of most of the functional parameters across all dose levels, while dark current shows predictable increases significantly more pronounced under proton irradiation due to displacement damage. Abstract: Liquid scintillator detectors instrumented with photosensors inside the scintillation volume preserve local optical information that is largely lost in conventional boundary-readout geometries. ### [21] [KPolaris: GPU-accelerated Polarized Radiative Transfer in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26298)

Main category: astro-ph.HE

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏振黑洞图像同时携带磁化等离子体（magnetized plasma）与辐射传播所经弯曲时空（curved spacetime）的信息，为提取这些信息需要专门的辐射转移计算工具。

Method: 基于 Kokkos 框架构建 GPU 加速的广义相对论偏振辐射转移代码，支持解析等离子体模型与 GRMHD 模拟数据，涵盖快光（fast-light）与慢光（slow-light）辐射转移，并提供多种诊断功能，如发射率加权等离子体图、特定区域发射贡献、选定光线历史以及斯托克斯参数（Stokes parameters）对等离子体参数的敏感性分析。

Result: 通过数值收敛测试（numerical convergence tests）及与独立辐射转移代码的对比，评估了计算的准确性；GPU 加速降低了图像生成的计算成本。

Conclusion: KPolaris 提升了偏振黑洞图像生成效率，有助于开展多频与时间依赖的偏振黑洞图像研究。

Abstract: Polarized black-hole images carry information about both the magnetized plasma and the curved spacetime through which the radiation propagates. We present KPolaris, an open-source GPU-accelerated general-relativistic polarized radiative-transfer code built on the Kokkos framework. It supports analytic plasma models and GRMHD simulation data, with both fast-light and slow-light radiative transfer. The code also provides diagnostics that connect image structure to the emitting plasma and to propagation effects along the rays. These include emissivity-weighted plasma maps, emission contributions from selected regions, selected-ray histories, and sensitivities of the Stokes parameters to plasma parameters. Numerical convergence tests and comparisons with an independent radiative-transfer code assess the accuracy of the calculations. GPU acceleration reduces the computational cost of image generation, facilitating multi-frequency and time-dependent studies of polarized black-hole images.

</details>


### [22] [Dense Dump Codec: Error-Controlled, Random-Access Compression of GRMHD Time Series for Slow-Light Radiative Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26317)
*Zelin Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Bin Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出稠密转储编解码器（Dense Dump Codec, DDC），用于压缩稠密广义相对论磁流体力学（GRMHD）时间序列，在保留精确锚点状态与可选时刻直接访问能力的同时，将存档体积较原始稠密数据缩小约10倍，并支持慢光（slow-light）偏振辐射转移计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞影像研究依赖慢光辐射转移（slow-light radiative transfer）刻画吸积等离子体的时间演化，该过程需要高时间密度的模拟输出；但完整保存输出代价高昂，稀疏采样又会引入时间插值误差。

Method: DDC 存储精确的锚点状态（anchor states），并对中间演化过程进行紧凑表示，同时允许对选定时刻与变量直接访问；并在偏振慢光辐射转移中集成了原生 DDC 读取器。

Result: 在八组涵盖 SANE 与 MAD、多种黑洞自旋、0.1M 输出的生产序列上，DDC 存档较原始稠密数据小约10倍，总量上甚至较原始 0.5M 输出小约50%；在 86、230、345 GHz 测试中，相较使用原始 0.5M 输入的慢光计算，DDC 可将总体斯托克斯（Stokes）图像误差降低 54–79%。

Conclusion: DDC 使高时间密度（high-cadence）慢光计算成为可能，而无需承担以原始形式保存每一稠密 GRMHD 状态的过高成本。

Abstract: Black-hole movies provide a unique venue to study the time evolution of accreting plasma. Modeling this evolution with slow-light radiative transfer requires closely spaced simulation outputs, because the plasma changes as light propagates through it. Saving these outputs in full is costly, whereas sparse sampling introduces time-interpolation errors. We present the Dense Dump Codec (DDC), a compression method for dense GRMHD time series. DDC stores exact anchor states and compactly represents the intermediate evolution, while allowing direct access to selected times and variables. Applied to eight production sequences spanning SANE and MAD simulations at several black-hole spins with 0.1M output, DDC archives are about 10 times smaller than the original dense data and even about 50% smaller than the original 0.5M output in aggregate. We further integrate a native DDC reader into polarized slow-light radiative transfer. Tests at 86, 230, and 345 GHz show that DDC may reduce aggregate Stokes-image errors by 54-79% relative to slow-light calculations using original 0.5M input. These results indicate that DDC enables high-cadence slow-light calculations without the prohibitive cost of saving every dense GRMHD state in its original form.

</details>

### [23] [Exact linearisation of the nonlinear shallow-water equations over a moving bottom: classification, wave generation and run-up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26253)
*Yong Sung Park*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 将 Carrier–Greenspan 速端变换（hodograph transformation）推广至运动底部，刻画保持其不变量的一族底部运动，并给出闭合解与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Carrier–Greenspan 变换仅适用于恒定坡度海滩上的非线性浅水方程（shallow-water equations）线性化，无法处理底部运动情形。本文旨在扩展该变换以涵盖运动底部，并确定哪些底部运动仍保持原有不变量。

Method: 识别出底部梯度须在空间上均匀、即 h(x,t)=ρ(t)x+ℬ(t) 的一族底部运动；对该族引入水平加速参考系以消除强迫项，使底部运动从速端方程中消失，再通过两次求积（quadrature）恢复物理变量。并以底部倾斜式造波机（bottom-tilting wave maker）作为该族的物理实现。

Result: 给出铰链响应、生成波的波峰-波谷陡度（steepness）及主导非线性修正的闭合形式；修正依赖于陡度的参考点，故前导隆起波与凹陷波结果不同。由于铰链位于海滩坡脚，这些闭合形式提供经典平滩解所需的入射波，可从板运动直接预测爬高（run-up）。陡度预测无需拟合常数，与参考计算一致。在水平床面上进行的 61 组试验（首次报道）将检验拓展至更深水域与更长板运动，实测爬高与预测趋势一致，但存在无粘理论（inviscid theory）未涵盖的偏移。

Conclusion: 该推广在不丢失 Carrier–Greenspan 不变量的前提下纳入一类运动底部；若梯度随时间变化，床面位移向离岸方向无界增长且远场无法保持静止，故计算域必须有限（枢轴位置可任意）。闭合解可从板运动预测海啸爬高，实验基本支持该理论，但存在无粘理论未能解释的偏差。

Abstract: The Carrier--Greenspan hodograph transformation linearises the nonlinear shallow-water equations on a beach of constant slope. We extend the transformation to a moving bottom and identify the family of bottom motions that preserves the Carrier--Greenspan invariants. The bottom gradient must be uniform in space, that is, $h(x,t)=ρ(t)x+\mathcal{B}(t)$, and constant $ρ$ returns the classical slope. For this family a horizontally accelerating frame removes the forcing. The bottom motion then disappears from the hodograph equation, and two quadratures recover the physical variables. If the gradient varies in time, the bed displacement grows without bound offshore and the far field cannot remain at rest. The domain must therefore be finite, although the pivot may be placed anywhere. A bottom-tilting wave maker realises this family. Closed forms follow for the hinge response, for the crest-to-trough steepness of the generated wave, and for the leading nonlinear correction. The correction depends on the point to which the steepness is referred, and so differs between a leading-elevation and a leading-depression wave. Because the hinge is at the beach toe, these closed forms provide the incident wave required by the classical plane-beach solution. The run-up can therefore be predicted from the plate motion alone. The steepness is predicted without a fitted constant and agrees with the reference computations. Sixty-one runs over a level bed, reported here for the first time, carry the test to deeper water and longer plate motions. The measured run-up follows the predicted run-up, with an offset that the inviscid theory does not account for.

</details>

*Jianhui Zhang,Ahmed Salem,Robert Tidswell,Haowei Wang,Vikramjeet Singh,Laurence B. Lovat,Manish K. Tiwari*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

Method: 采用生物模板化（bio-templated）且可规模化的策略，利用天然蚕丝纤维表面的有序纳米原纤，选择性生长连续的 MOF 纳米带，形成直径约 10 μm 的图案化纤维结构，从而提供低缺陷的界面通道以实现超快质子传输。

Conclusion: 通过将微米线传感器阵列集成于纺织品并结合深度学习，实现了动态流场中的高分辨率时空湿度映射，并解析出常规单点探测器无法捕捉的不对称呼吸模式；该结果在可穿戴健康监测、生物电子学与人机界面等领域具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Transforming textile fibres into sensors can facilitate continuous physiological monitoring for improving human healthcare. Coating fibres with electronic conductors can help detect physiological stimuli but their sensitivity scales with thickness and often lowers the fibre mechanical flexibility. Proton conductors are potential alternatives; however, they suffer from fragile physical interfaces, and sluggish kinetics. Here, we report a bio-templated, scalable strategy to create superprotonic interfaces using natural silk fibres. We exploit ordered nanofibrils on silk fibres to selectively grow continuous metal-organic framework (MOF) nanostrips. This patterned, ca. $10\,μ\mathrm{m}$-diameter fibre-structure provides low-defect interfacial pathways for ultrafast proton transport, yielding conductivities up to 40 $S\,cm^{-1}$ - two orders of magnitude above the state of the art - with millisecond response and high mechanical robustness. The individual micro-thread sensors facilitate respiratory monitoring with a mean bias of 0.01 breaths $min^{-1}$ in volunteer testing, unaffected by human movement and readily capture breathing signature in high-flow oxygen therapy devices. We integrate arrays of micro-thread sensors in textiles and exploit deep learning to demonstrate high-resolution spatiotemporal humidity mapping in dynamic flow-fields and resolve asymmetric respiratory patterns that elude conventional single-point detectors. Our results should have broad-ranging implications, e.g. in wearable health monitoring, bioelectronics, and human-machine interfaces.

</details>

### [25] [Multimodal Visual Sensing of Temperature and Pressure: From Spectroscopic Readout to Multiple Linear Regression-Enhanced RGB Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25075)
*Maja Szymczak,Yufan Meng,Guanjun Xiao,Miguel A. Hernández-Rodríguez,Iga Sawaryn,Bo Zou,Lukasz Marciniak*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单一发光主体通过组分调控转化为分别面向温度与压力传感的不同体系，可为多功能光学传感器提供高效路径；同时需解决传统 RGB 比值读出灵敏度有限、缺乏定量成像能力的问题。

Method: 构建浓度可调的 KGaGeO4:Bi3+,Eu3+ 平台，利用光谱上可区分的 Bi3+ 发光中心与 Eu3+ 发射对温度和压力的差异化响应，实现比值发光、色度坐标、光谱位移与可见颜色变化的多模态读出；并结合 RGB 成像与多元线性回归（MLR）将颜色变化转为定量温度与压力分布图。

Result: 通过同时利用多个颜色通道，MLR 将最大相对热灵敏度（relative thermal sensitivity）从传统 RGB 比值的约 1% K-1 提升至 8.8% K-1，并将压力灵敏度从 169% GPa-1 提升至接近 721% GPa-1。

Abstract: Transforming a single luminescent host into distinct temperature- and pressure-sensing systems through composition control provides a powerful route toward multifunctional optical sensors. Here, we introduce a concentration-tunable KGaGeO4:Bi3+,Eu3+ platform whose sensing function can be selectively directed toward thermometry or manometry by adjusting the dopant balance. Spectroscopically distinct Bi3+ centers and Eu3+ emission exhibit differentiated responses to temperature and pressure, enabling multimodal readout through ratiometric luminescence, chromaticity coordinates, spectral shifts, and visible color changes. Importantly, these color changes were translated into quantitative temperature and pressure maps using RGB imaging combined with multiple linear regression (MLR). By simultaneously exploiting multiple color channels, MLR increased the maximum relative thermal sensitivity from approximately 1% K-1 for conventional RGB ratios to 8.8% K-1 and the pressure sensitivity from 169% GPa-1 to nearly 721% GPa-1. This work presents the first application of MLR-assisted RGB analysis for quantitative luminescence pressure sensing. The proposed strategy integrates composition-controlled functionality, multimodal spectroscopic sensing, direct visual readout, and data-assisted imaging, demonstrating the synergy between material engineering and multivariate analysis for highly sensitive multifunctional optical sensing.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将 NaLu2Ga3Ge2O12:Cr3+, Ni2+ 作为 NIR 发光压力计，利用压力对 Cr3+ 与 Ni2+ 发射带的光谱位移及其相对发光强度的调制；并通过在压致响应发射带内选取窄光谱区间实现比率型（ratiometric）读出。

Conclusion: 极高的压力灵敏度、NIR 工作波段与显著抗热干扰特性的结合，使 NaLu2Ga3Ge2O12:Cr3+, Ni2+ 成为在温度同步变化条件下进行可靠压力测定的极具前景的发光压力传感器。

Abstract: The increasing demand for highly sensitive luminescent manometers capable of precise pressure determination, particularly those operating in the near-infrared (NIR) spectral range, motivates the continuous search for new pressure-responsive luminescent materials. In this work, we introduce NaLu2Ga3Ge2O12:Cr3+, Ni2+ as a highly sensitive NIR luminescent manometer in which pressure not only induces pronounced spectral shifts of the Cr3+ and Ni2+ emission bands but also strongly modulates their relative luminescence intensities. Remarkably, the 3T2(3F)->3A2(3F) emission band of Ni2+ exhibits a record-high absolute pressure sensitivity of SA = 34 nm GPa-1. Furthermore, by appropriately selecting narrow spectral ranges within the pressure-responsive emission bands, a ratiometric NIR luminescent manometer was developed, providing a maximum relative pressure sensitivity of SR = 158% GPa-1. Most importantly, the proposed ratiometric readout exhibits a thermal-invariance manometric factor (TIMF) of 19,900 K GPa-1, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the highest value reported to date for a luminescent manometer. The combination of exceptionally high-pressure sensitivity, NIR operation, and remarkable resistance to thermal interference establishes NaLu2Ga3Ge2O12:Cr3+, Ni2+ as a highly promising luminescent pressure sensor for reliable pressure determination under conditions involving simultaneous temperature variations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究铪锆氧纳米颗粒中物相组成、氧空位浓度与介电性能之间的内在关联，并解释不同化学计量比组分间介电常数差异的物理来源。

Abstract: In this work we analyze correlations of X-ray photoelectron and diffraction spectra, and dielectric properties of hafnia-zirconia nanoparticles (the chemical compositions Hf0.4Zr0.6O2 and Hf0.6Zr0.4O2, and the average size of 7.5 nm) prepared by the solid-state organo-nitrate synthesis and annealed in air. The phase state of the nanoparticles, determined by the X-ray diffraction spectroscopy, is the coexistence of the nonpolar monoclinic (42 - 76 mass %) and orthorhombic (56 - 24 mass %) phases. </details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BZT作为高性能无铅电子元件（lead-free electronic components）的领先候选材料，其铁电-弛豫转变（ferroelectric-to-relaxor transition）高度可调；然而纳米尺度化学成分非均匀性如何影响其结构与功能特性尚需定量阐明，本文即针对局部化学团簇效应展开研究。

Result: Zr团簇在两种组分下均显著增强局域极化，该增强并非单纯体积效应，而源于结构相空间重分布：Zr偏析形成更大、连续的富Ti区域，扩展高极性菱方畴并促进偶极协同取向。此协同耦合在热学上稳定局域极性有序，系统性提升居里温度（Curie temperature）、最大介电常数（maximum permittivity）与伯恩斯温度（Burns temperature）。在x = 0.4组分中，化学偏析促使形成稳定的涡旋极性拓扑织构（vortices与interstitial vortices），这些结构作为钉扎势垒（pinning barriers）抑制极化翻转，导致矫顽场升高与明显的电学硬化（electrical hardening）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了纳米尺度成分异质性、极性畴拓扑与宏观性能之间的定量联系，为通过局部化学有序调控（local chemical order control）工程化无铅钙钛矿（lead-free perovskites）提供了稳健框架。

Abstract: Barium zirconate titanate, Ba(Zr$_x$Ti$_{1-x}$)O$_3$ (BZT), is a leading lead-free candidate for high-performance electronic components due to its highly tunable ferroelectric-to-relaxor transition. This cooperative coupling thermally stabilizes the local polar order, systematically elevating the Curie, maximum permittivity, and Burns temperatures. Focusing on the relaxor composition (x = 0.4), chemical segregation promotes the formation of resilient, swirling polar topological textures,categorized into vortices circulating around Zr-rich clusters and interstitial vortices localized within the Ti-rich matrix. These topological structures act as effective pinning barriers against polarization reversal, driving pronounced electrical hardening with elevated coercive fields. Ultimately, these findings establish a quantitative link between nanoscale compositional heterogeneity, polar domain topology, and macroscopic performance, providing a robust framework for engineering lead-free perovskites via local chemical order control.

</details>

Abstract: We report the structural and optical properties of nanoscale Sr2IrO4 freestanding thin-films fabricated using a water-soluble Sr3Al2O6 layer. Our experimental results show that this freestanding thin-film approach of layered oxides can provide techniques for tuning or realizing unprecedented states beyond conventional thin-film methods, suggesting a pathway in achieving flexible layered-oxide electronics.

Motivation: 控制吸收光能的空间分布是纳米尺度光热化学、等离激元学（plasmonics）与超快材料调控的核心问题。研究旨在探究金属超晶格中电子能量在两种组分间如何重新分配并最终定域，以寻找飞秒尺度调控纳米能量分布的途径。

Conclusion: 这种由耗散驱动的能量定域（dissipation-driven localization of energy）源于Pt的大电子热容（electronic heat capacity）与超晶格中快速的电子输运，为纳米尺度能量分布的飞秒调控提供了新路径。

This dissipation driven localization of energy arises from the large electronic heat capacity of Pt and rapid electronic transport through the superlattice, providing a route to femtosecond control of nanoscale energy distributions.

Abstract: Resolving how ensemble growth kinetics emerge from particle- and grain-level dynamics is essential for predictive control of nanocrystal synthesis. Here, time-resolved four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4DSTEM), extended into five dimensions (5DSTEM) through continuous acquisition, is used to simultaneously resolve morphology, crystallographic orientation, and lattice strain during Au nanocrystal growth in aqueous HAuCl4. Although ensemble growth follows surface-reaction-limited kinetics, individual nanoparticles exhibit distinct pathways, including continuous growth and discrete coalescence through oriented attachment. These pathways display characteristic orientation dynamics, with stable or gradually selected orientations during continuous growth and abrupt reconfiguration followed by alignment during coalescence. At the ensemble level, the out-of-plane orientation distribution is established early and remains stable, whereas in-plane orientations remain broadly distributed without global alignment. Tensile strain develops progressively from particle surfaces and interfaces, directly linking lattice distortion with evolving morphology and crystallographic structure. By connecting ensemble kinetics with particle- and grain-resolved structural evolution, 5DSTEM provides a general framework for uncovering heterogeneous growth mechanisms and for relating synthesis pathways to structure-dependent properties in functional nanomaterials.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性量子自旋霍尔绝缘体受时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）保护，可抵抗单粒子背散射（single-particle backscattering），在无耗散量子电子学中具有前景。实现具有带隙螺旋边缘态（gapped helical edge states）的 QSHI 是可编程拓扑电路的关键需求，可实现边缘通道电导的确定性开/关切换。

Result: 在外延单层 HfTe5 中识别出重构诱导的周期性超晶格调制，并直接观测到边缘通道中的多重类能隙特征与态密度中的一系列尖锐峰。这些尖锐峰在磁场下表现出清晰的塞曼分裂（Zeeman splitting），与拓扑边缘态的螺旋性质一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了在 QSHI 中工程化带隙螺旋边缘态的可行路径，并为具有所需开/关切换能力的拓扑器件提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: The functional quantum spin Hall insulators (QSHI), protected by time-reversal symmetry against single-particle backscattering, hold great promise for dissipationless quantum electronics. Using scanning tunneling microscopy and spectroscopy, we identify a reconstruction-induced periodic superlattice modulation in epitaxial monolayer HfTe5 and directly observe multiple gap-like features in the edge channel, accompanied by a series of sharp peaks in the density of states. Combined with theoretical modelling, we attribute the observed edge gap to the finite-width coupling between the two edges significantly enhanced by the superlattice modulation, whereas the sharp peaks are the manifestations of mini-gaps opening at the reduced Brillouin zone boundaries by the periodic modulation of spin-orbit coupling. Notably, these sharp peaks exhibit clear Zeeman splitting under magnetic fields, consistent with the helical nature of the topological edge states. Our results establish a viable route to engineering gapped helical edge states in QSHI and provide a promising platform for topological devices with desired on/off switchability.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 净磁化强度为零但兼具自旋劈裂电子结构与反常输运的非传统磁体（unconventional magnets）受到广泛关注，其潜在机制与对称性判据仍需系统化的理论框架支撑。

Method: 采用自旋群对称性分析推导二维与三维共面非共线反铁磁体中奇宇称自旋劈裂的普遍条件，识别关键自旋群对称性并讨论其相对论对应物与时间反演奇输运的关系；通过紧束缚（tight-binding）计算验证对称性论证，并筛选三维磁性材料数据库及构建二维异质结（heterostructures）。

Result: 紧束缚计算证实了对称性论证，并在无外场下于完全补偿的奇宇称磁态中展示了反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）；材料数据库筛选与二维异质结构建进一步识别出具有显著自旋取向依赖的反常霍尔与磁光效应（magneto-optical effects）的现实平台。

Abstract: Unconventional magnets combining vanishing net magnetization with spin-split electronic structures and anomalous transport have attracted considerable attention. We identify a key spin-group symmetry whose relativistic counterpart forbids time-reversal-odd transport and show that breaking it allows such transport in the presence of spin-orbit coupling. Tight-binding calculations validate these symmetry arguments and demonstrate an anomalous Hall effect in a fully compensated odd-parity magnetic state without an external field. By screening a three-dimensional magnetic materials database and constructing two-dimensional heterostructures, we further identify realistic platforms exhibiting pronounced spin-orientation-dependent anomalous Hall and magneto-optical effects. Our results establish a general symmetry framework for time-reversal-odd responses in odd-parity magnets and highlight fully compensated odd-parity magnets as a promising platform for controlling anomalous transport.

We find a wide range of magnetic ground states, among them over 20 new antiferromagnetic candidates.

Abstract: Understanding how growth conditions govern structural order and ion transport in Prussian blue analogues (PBAs) thin films is essential for optimizing their electrochemical performance.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期第一性原理（first-principles）研究预测岩盐矿（rocksalt）TiC中的钛空位并非空的八面体（octahedral）位点，而可通过形成碳弗仑克尔对（Frenkel pairs）及短C-C键发生重构；需确定这些局域结构的C 1s谱特征并评估其在无序碳存在时是否仍具特异性。

Result: 未重构空位仅使其最近邻C 1s能级移动-0.36 eV；而每个重构模型的最大正移范围为2.34 eV至2.80 eV，该大位移出现在参与重构C-C网络的碳原子上，并与显著较不负的Mulliken电荷相关。但TiC/非晶碳模型显示无序碳环境可延伸至相同的高结合能区间。

Conclusion: 所计算的位移为实验搜寻TiC中重构钛空位提供了参考能量，但重构指纹的特异性可能因无序碳环境的干扰而受限。

The large shifts occur at carbon atoms participating in the reconstructed C-C network and correlate with markedly less negative Mulliken charges, providing candidate local fingerprints of vacancy reconstruction. However, an exploratory TiC/amorphous-C model shows that disordered carbon environments can extend into the same high-binding-energy range. Overall, the calculated shifts provide reference energies for experimental searches for reconstructed Ti vacancies in TiC.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电解液组成如何影响普鲁士蓝类似物（Prussian blue analogues, PBAs）的电荷存储，需要厘清结构无序（structural disorder）与离子传输之间的耦合效应，而这一问题此前尚不明确。

Method: 在相同电化学条件下，分别从 KCl、NaCl、NH₄Cl、LiCl 电解液中电沉积 Fe、Co、Ni 基六氰合铁酸盐（hexacyanoferrate）薄膜，并采用结构表征、拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与电化学阻抗谱（electrochemical impedance spectroscopy, EIS）进行对比分析。

Conclusion: 电解液身份不仅决定晶格尺寸，还决定缺陷网络的组织与连通性；结构一致性（structural coherence）与可及的传输路径（accessible transport pathways）是决定 PBA 薄膜电化学性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Understanding how electrolyte composition influences charge storage in Prussian blue analogues (PBAs) requires clarifying the coupled effects of structural disorder and ion transport. Na+ promotes lattice expansion accompanied by increased vacancy formation, microstrain, and structural heterogeneity, whereas K+ and NH4+ yield more structurally coherent frameworks. Raman spectroscopy shows that increasing structural disorder broadens the distribution of local coordination environments and correlates with increasingly dispersed electrochemical behavior. Electrochemical impedance spectroscopy further reveals coupled ion-electron transport, with impedance increasing by more than one order of magnitude from FeHCF to NiHCF. Notably, the CPE exponent decreases systematically with Raman band broadening, establishing a direct correlation between structural disorder and transport dispersion. These results demonstrate that electrolyte identity controls not only lattice dimensions but also the organization and connectivity of defect networks, highlighting structural coherence and accessible transport pathways as key factors governing electrochemical performance in PBA thin films.

### [38] [Intrinsic switching leads to oxygen diffusion and breakdown in hafnia ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26030)
*Xabier Diaz de Cerio,Iñigo Robredo-Magro,Jorge Íñiguez-González*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用机器学习分子动力学（machine-learning molecular dynamics）研究氧化铪（hafnia）铁电体在室温下的本征翻转与扩散动力学，揭示两种可共存于真实时间尺度的翻转机制，并指出其雪崩式串联会导致氧传导与本征击穿，同时提出通过设计场脉冲规避该后果的可能路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统铁电体（ferroelectrics）具有明确的极化态并通过电翻转相互关联，而萤石结构（fluorite-structured）铁电体如氧化铪中，翻转与氧扩散似乎共存，使铁电性本质更为复杂。为厘清其本征行为，需要在室温与真实时间尺度上考察翻转与扩散动力学。

Method: 采用机器学习分子动力学（machine-learning molecular dynamics）对氧化铪铁电体进行模拟，考察其本征的室温翻转与扩散动力学过程。

Result: 模拟识别出两种不同的翻转机制，二者在真实时间尺度上均处于活跃状态。由于晶格无法足够快地耗散局域翻转事件所产生的热量，这两种过程以雪崩式（avalanche-like）方式串联，进而导致氧传导（oxygen conduction）。

Conclusion: 本征翻转会导致氧化铪铁电体发生击穿（breakdown）；同时，结果提示可通过合理设计场脉冲（field pulses）来避免这一后果。该结论为氧化铪铁电体的可靠性理解与调控提供了本征层面的依据。

Abstract: Conventional ferroelectrics exhibit well-defined polarization states linked through electric switching. In fluorite-structured ferroelectrics like hafnia, though, switching and oxygen diffusion seem to coexist, enriching the nature of ferroelectricity. Here we address the intrinsic, room-temperature switching and diffusion kinetics of hafnia ferroelectrics using machine-learning molecular dynamics. We identify two distinct switching mechanisms that are both active at realistic time scales. Critically, our simulations reveal that, because the lattice does not dissipate fast enough the heat originating from localized switching events, these two processes concatenate in an avalanche-like manner leading to oxygen conduction. Our results thus show that intrinsic switching leads to breakdown in hafnia ferroelectrics. They also suggest how this outcome might be avoided through suitably designed field pulses.

</details>


### [39] [Surface-bulk hybridization enhances the Berry curvature dipole in GaAs(110)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26043)
*Gastón Blatter,Jorge I. Facio,Jeroen van den Brink*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理计算研究 GaAs(110) 表面的贝里曲率偶极（Berry curvature dipole, BCD），发现表面弛豫通过促进表面态与体态的杂化显著增强 BCD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体边界处的对称性破缺可激发体态中被禁戒的电子响应，因此需要探究表面 BCD 的来源及其受结构弛豫影响的机制。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），对比未弛豫与弛豫 GaAs(110) 板模型的表面态能级分布与 BCD 贡献。

Conclusion: 表面弛豫对准确描述表面—体态杂化至关重要，而该杂化在晶体边界的贝里曲率驱动响应中起关键作用。

Abstract: Symmetry breaking at crystalline boundaries can enable electronic responses that are forbidden in the bulk. Here, we investigate the Berry curvature dipole (BCD) at GaAs(110) surfaces using first-principles calculations. While the BCD vanishes in bulk GaAs due to symmetry constraints, the reduced surface symmetry permits a finite dipole. We show that surface relaxation promotes hybridization between bulk and surface states, which strongly influences the surface BCD. Specifically, in unrelaxed slabs, the BCD arises predominantly from interband coupling among exponentially localized surface states lying within the projected bulk band gap. Surface relaxation shifts these states into the energy range of the bulk continuum, transforming them into surface resonances and promoting hybridization with bulk-like states. This hybridization generates pronounced Berry-curvature hot spots, resulting in an overall enhancement of the BCD. Our results highlight the importance of surface relaxation for accurately describing surface--bulk hybridization, which in turn plays a key role in Berry-curvature-driven responses at crystalline boundaries.

</details>


### [40] [Detection of acoustic phonons in carbon by Raman spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26170)
*Konstantin Iakoubovskii,Andrey Katrusha,Weihua Peng,Jianguo Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，在石墨（graphite）与金刚石（diamond）中探测到声学声子（acoustic phonons），并通过晶格非晶化（lattice amorphization）与硼掺杂（boron doping）激活了通常禁戒的拉曼模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声学声子（acoustic phonons）在常规拉曼光谱中通常因选择定则而被禁戒，难以直接探测。该工作旨在通过引入晶格无序或掺杂来突破这一限制，从而获得碳材料中声学声子的光谱信息。

Method: 采用拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算；在石墨中通过晶格非晶化（lattice amorphization）激活禁戒模式，在金刚石中则通过硼掺杂（boron doping）实现激活，并依据激发波长与偏振依赖性鉴定信号来源。

Result: 金刚石中掺杂诱导的拉曼信号被归属于具有四面体对称性（tetrahedral symmetry）的替位硼（substitutional boron）；非晶化石墨与重硼掺杂金刚石的拉曼光谱对比表明，金刚石晶格中出现了类石墨（graphitic-like）无序。

Conclusion: 非晶化与掺杂可有效激活原本禁戒的声学声子拉曼模式，且该方法不限于碳材料，可推广至其他多种材料体系。

Abstract: We detected acoustic phonons in graphite and diamond by Raman spectroscopy supported by density functional theory calculations. The activation of these normally forbidden Raman modes was achieved via lattice amorphization in case of graphite and by boron doping in case of diamond. The doping-induced Raman signal in diamond was identified with substitutional boron of tetrahedral symmetry via its dependences on excitation wavelength and polarization. Comparison of the Raman spectra of amorphized graphite and heavily boron-doped diamond suggests the emergence of graphitic-like disorder in the diamond lattice. The reported approach is not limited to carbon and can be extended to a wide range of other materials.

</details>


### [41] [Observation of the Nernst effect driven by longitudinal spin fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26407)
*Fuyuki Ando,Hiroto Adachi,Hossein Sepehri-Amin,Takamasa Hirai,Keisuke Hirata,Ken-ichi Uchida*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在铁磁(Mn,Cr)Sb体系中，能斯特效应（Nernst effect）在居里温度（Curie temperature）附近反而出现峰值，而反常霍尔电阻率（anomalous Hall resistivity）同时消失，表明莫特关系（Mott relation）失效。理论计算将该现象归因于自旋的纵向涨落（longitudinal fluctuations of spins），无需引入奇异自旋织构、非常规电子结构或磁子-声子拖曳效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能斯特效应传统上分为外磁场驱动的普通效应与磁化驱动的反常效应（anomalous Nernst effect），因此按预期，随磁化消失，反常能斯特效应在居里温度处应当消失。本文挑战这一预期，探讨磁序消失时能斯特效应的来源。

Method: 对一系列铁磁(Mn,Cr)Sb样品测量能斯特系数与反常霍尔电阻率随温度的演化，并引入自旋纵向涨落进行理论建模以复现实验结果。

Result: 能斯特系数在跨越居里温度时仍保持有限值，而反常霍尔电阻率消失，导致横向热电导率（transverse thermoelectric conductivity）在居里温度附近出现峰值；该峰可由仅含自旋纵向涨落的理论模型重现。

Conclusion: 磁性材料中能斯特效应的驱动力不仅限于静态磁序，自旋涨落亦可成为驱动源，这为增强横向热电转换（transverse thermoelectric conversion）提供了新途径。

Abstract: The Nernst effect, which converts a heat current into a charge current in the orthogonal direction, is generally classified into the ordinary effect due to an external magnetic field applied to conductors and the anomalous effect due to static magnetization in magnetic materials. Thus, one expects that the anomalous Nernst effect disappears at the Curie temperature following the magnetization. Here, we observe the Nernst effect which rather manifests around the Curie temperature. In a series of ferromagnetic (Mn,Cr)Sb samples, the Nernst coefficients exhibit finite values even across the Curie temperatures, whereas the anomalous Hall resistivity disappears, suggesting the breakdown of the Mott relation. These surviving Nernst and vanishing Hall effects result in a peak behavior of the transverse thermoelectric conductivity around the Curie temperature, which we theoretically reproduce by introducing longitudinal fluctuations of spins without accounting for an exotic spin texture, a non-trivial electronic structure, or magnon- and phonon-drag effects. The Nernst effect driven by the longitudinal spin fluctuations reveals that not only the static magnetic order but also the spin fluctuations can be a driving force of the Nernst effect in magnetic materials, opening another way for boosting transverse thermoelectric conversion.

</details>


### [42] [Deep Generative Crystal Structure Prediction: A Benchmark Study and a Controlled Test of Prototype Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26502)
*Lai Wei,Rongzhi Dong,Ying Feng,Madeline Miklos,Jianjun Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究系统评测了12种代表性生成式晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP）模型与模板基方法TCSP 2.0的对比，发现模板检索仍是最强单一方法，而生成模型所正确预测的结构大多亦可由模板替换获得，其性能高度依赖原型家族，本质更接近隐性原型库而非真正的从头预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型被广泛宣称可实现从头晶体结构预测（de novo CSP），但其能力尚未与模板基方法进行一致、可控的横向衡量。论文旨在厘清生成式CSP相对于模板检索的真实增益及其性能来源。

Method: 在180个测试结构及46个泄漏控制子集上，用统一的匹配、对称性与共识准则，评测12种生成式CSP模型（涵盖潜变量、扩散、流匹配、自回归与流形随机游走架构）与TCSP 2.0的对比；并进一步从训练集中整体移除若干化学计量原型家族、重训最强生成模型以探测性能来源。

Result: 模板检索为最强单一方法，top-1成功率达68.3%；对称感知方法EquiCSP（66.4%）与Uni-3DAR（62.9%）位列次席。生成模型正确预测的结构大多同样可被模板替换正确预测，独有可达集合较小；移除四个原型家族后，最强生成模型准确率下降50%–78%，仅极少数结构在原型家族被移除后仍被预测成功。

Conclusion: 当前生成式CSP模型在很大程度上充当隐性、边界更柔性的原型库，而非真正的从头预测器；其对现有原型库之外结构的发现优势有限。核心开放问题在于扩展这一残余的检索无关预测能力，而非仅提升整体匹配率。

Abstract: Deep generative models are widely reported to enable de novo crystal structure prediction (CSP), but their capability has not been measured consistently against template-based methods. We evaluate 12 representative generative CSP models, spanning latent-variable, diffusion, flow-matching, autoregressive, and manifold random-walk architectures, against TCSP 2.0 on 180 test structures and a leakage-controlled subset of 46. All methods use identical structure-matching, symmetry, and consensus criteria. Template retrieval is the strongest single method, reaching 68.3% top-1 success; symmetry-aware EquiCSP (66.4%) and Uni-3DAR (62.9%) form the next tier. However, comparison with TCSP 2.0 shows that most structures correctly predicted by generative models are also correctly predicted by template substitution. Thus, the set of structures uniquely reachable by generation is small, limiting its practical advantage for discovering structures outside existing prototype libraries. To test the source of this performance, we removed entire stoichiometric prototype families from the training set and retrained the strongest generative model. Accuracy declined by 50-78% across four families, establishing that performance is substantially prototype-dependent. A small minority of structures survived removal of their prototype family, demonstrating a real but limited retrieval-independent predictive capacity. Present generative CSP models therefore function largely as implicit, softer-edged prototype libraries rather than genuinely de novo predictors. Enlarging this residual capacity, rather than aggregate match rate alone, is the central open problem.

</details>


### [43] [Topology-Stratified Materials Discovery with A Flow-Based Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26547)
*Jingyi Zhou,Oyshee Chowdhury,Noah Oyeniran,Chongze Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 UFO-MGen，一种基于流（flow-based）的通用生成模型，通过学习 Wyckoff 表示的拓扑特征，在广阔的结构与化学空间中生成晶体，并在多项评测中取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极环境应用（如航空航天、增材制造、聚变能源系统）中高性能材料的发现依赖晶体结构的准确生成；尽管生成建模已成为晶体设计的潜在路径，但受复杂晶体结构与多样化学组成所限，其性能仍然有限。

Method: 开发 UFO-MGen 通用流式生成模型，学习 Wyckoff 表示的拓扑特征，并利用该信息在广泛的结构与化学空间中进行晶体生成；同时在其上实现微调（fine-tuning）模块，用于性质约束的晶体生成。

Result: 与最先进生成模型相比，UFO-MGen 在严格的多稳定性（multi-stability）评测框架下取得最高晶体生成成功率、最高 SUN（stable, unique, novel，即稳定、独特、新颖）率，并具备此前模型未报道的显著外推（extrapolation）能力；微调模块支持面向目标性质的反向材料设计（inverse materials design）。

Conclusion: UFO-MGen 为加速材料发现开辟了新途径，并为通用材料智能（universal materials intelligence）奠定基础。

Abstract: Accurate generation of crystal structures is the foundation to the discovery of high-performance materials for extreme-environment applications, such as aerospace, additive manufacturing, and fusion energy systems. Although generative modeling has emerged as a promising approach for crystal design, its performance remains limited by the complex crystal structures and diverse chemical compositions. In this work, we develop UFO-MGen, a universal flow-based generative model that learns topological features of Wyckoff representations and leverages this information to accurately generate crystals across vast structural and chemical spaces. Compared with state-of-the-art generative models, UFO-MGen achieves the highest crystal generation success rate under a rigorous multi-stability evaluation framework, the highest SUN (stable, unique, novel) rate, and a remarkable extrapolation capability that has not been reported by previous models. Furthermore, a fine-tuning module is implemented to UFO-MGen for property-constrained crystal generation, enabling the inverse materials design toward target properties. The UFO-MGen opens a new avenue for accelerated materials discovery and providing a foundation for universal materials intelligence.

</details>


### [44] [Superfluorescence as a cooperative amplifier of hidden anisotropy in a ferroelectric hybrid perovskite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26554)
*Changhao Gao,Daniel Sandner,Talia Ruhrberg Estévez,Jeron Timmer,Sophia Klubertz,Martijn Kemerink,Felix Deschler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 多晶铁电二维钙钛矿薄膜在无外场偏置下展现强线偏振超荧光，偏振度（DOP）最高达86%，表明协同增益机制可将微弱局域各向异性放大数个数量级并转化为宏观相干偏振光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超荧光（superfluorescence, SF）作为自同步偶极子产生的皮秒强脉冲，有望用于室温量子光源；但自同步过程能否将微弱结构各向异性转化为稳健宏观序，仍属未解问题。

Method: 以多晶铁电二维钙钛矿（2D perovskite）薄膜为研究对象，测量其超荧光的偏振特性（线偏振度），并考察在微观畴取向随机、无外部偏置条件下的宏观偏振行为。

Result: 观测到强线偏振超荧光，偏振度（degree of polarization, DOP）最高达86%；尽管微观畴随机取向且无外部偏置，仍出现宏观偏振，提示协同增益机制可将弱局域各向异性放大数个数量级。

Conclusion: 协同超荧光可作为一种将微观各向异性转化为稳健宏观序的一般性概念（以线偏振为例），可用作探测隐藏材料对称性的灵敏手段，并为偏振量子光源提供框架。

Abstract: Superfluorescence (SF), intense picosecond bursts from self-synchronizing dipoles, is promising for room-temperature quantum sources. Yet, whether this synchronization can convert weak structural anisotropies into robust macroscopic order remains an open question. Here, we demonstrate that polycrystalline ferroelectric 2D perovskite thin films exhibit strongly linearly polarized SF with a degree of polarization (DOP) up to 86%. Remarkably, macroscopic polarization emerges without external bias despite randomly oriented microscopic domains, suggesting a cooperative gain mechanism that amplifies weak local anisotropies by several orders of magnitude into coherent, linearly polarized light bursts. Our results establish cooperative superfluorescence as a general concept for translating microscopic anisotropy into robust macroscopic order, exemplified here for linear polarization, offering a sensitive probe of hidden material symmetries and a framework for polarized quantum light sources

</details>


### [45] [Microstructure, tensile behavior and cyclic bendability of directly extruded biodegradable Zn, Zn-Mg and Zn-Mg-Sr wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26582)
*L. Hlodák,K. Tesař,M. Lebeda,J. Duchoň,J. Kubásek,J. Čech,A. Školáková,J. Pinc*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过单步直接挤压（direct extrusion）制备直径约290 μm的纯Zn及Zn-Mg、Zn-Mg-Sr细丝，系统表征其微观组织、织构、拉伸与弯曲行为，指出细化晶粒显著提升强度与塑性，但循环弯曲性能差是弯曲关键型生物医学器件的核心障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锌基合金（zinc-based alloys）是可降解植入物的有前景材料，但其细丝形态下强度、塑性与弯曲性能（bendability）之间的权衡关系尚不清晰，需要系统研究以评估其生物医学应用潜力。

Method: 采用单步直接挤压（direct extrusion）制备纯Zn（PZ）、Zn-0.15Mg（ZM）和Zn-0.8Mg-0.2Sr（ZMS）三种约290 μm直径细丝，并对其微观组织、织构（texture）、拉伸和弯曲行为进行表征，结合EBSD观察孪生行为，并开展37 °C下最长20天的时效（aging）测试。

Result: Mg2Zn11与SrZn13相促进粒子激发形核（particle-stimulated nucleation），使晶粒尺寸从PZ的35.8 μm细化至ZM的6.8 μm和ZMS的3.3 μm，弱化基面纤维织构（basal-fiber texture），并使37 °C下抗拉强度（ultimate tensile strength）由PZ约119 MPa升至ZM的291 MPa和ZMS的334 MPa；ZMS在37 °C下断裂延伸率达19%。PZ通过跨弱取向晶粒的孪晶传递（twin transmission）变形并出现锯齿状应力降，ZM表现为单一且对时效敏感的应力降，ZMS则为无应力降的分布式颗粒开裂。力学性能在37 °C时效20天后基本稳定，7天后纳米级析出物无显著变化，但所有成分的循环弯曲性能均明显较差。

Conclusion: 单步直接挤压是制备高强度、细晶生物可降解Zn丝的有效途径；然而循环弯曲性能差（与首次在锌中直接由EBSD观测到的孪生-去孪生（twinning-detwinning）机制相关）是其在弯曲关键型生物医学器件中应用的主要障碍。

Abstract: Zinc-based alloys are promising biodegradable implants, yet the trade-off between strength, ductility, and bendability in thin-wire form remains poorly understood. To address this, pure Zn (PZ), Zn-0.15Mg (ZM), and Zn-0.8Mg-0.2Sr (ZMS) wires of ~290 um diameter were produced by direct extrusion, and their microstructure, texture, tensile, and bending behavior were characterized. Mg2Zn11 and SrZn13 phases promoted particle-stimulated nucleation, refining the grain size from 35.8 um in PZ to 6.8 and 3.3 um in ZM and ZMS, respectively, weakening the basal-fiber texture, and raising the ultimate tensile strength at 37 °C from about 119 MPa for PZ to 291 and 334 MPa for ZM and ZMS, respectively. PZ deformed through twin transmission across weakly misoriented grains, producing serrated stress drops in tensile curves, with further straining accommodated by non-basal slip. ZM showed a single, aging-sensitive stress drop from localized twin nucleation and particle cracking, while ZMS showed distributed particle cracking without associated stress drops, consistent with its higher elongation to failure of 19 % at 37 °C. Mechanical properties remained broadly stable after 20 days of aging at 37 °C, with no measurable change in the nanoscale precipitates after 7 days. All compositions, however, showed markedly poor cyclic bendability accompanied by a twinning-detwinning mechanism directly observed by EBSD for the first time in zinc. These results establish single-step direct extrusion as a viable route to strong, fine-grained biodegradable Zn wires, while identifying poor bendability as the principal obstacle to bending-critical biomedical devices.

</details>


### [46] [Agent-E2MD: Autonomous Translation of Interatomic Potential Equations into Physically Validated Pair Styles for Molecular Dynamics in LAMMPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26657)
*Bilvin Varughese,Orcun Yildiz,Aditya Koneru,Henry Chan,Tom Peterka,Subramanian Sankaranarayanan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出 Agent-E2MD——一种知识引导的智能体工作流（agentic workflow），可将用户给定的原子间势（interatomic potential）模型自动翻译为可执行的 LAMMPS pair style，并在七组材料-势组合上完成构建、测试与物理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子间势虽支撑分子动力学（MD）并决定金属、半导体、氧化物、液体及反应体系的原子尺度预测保真度，但仅有方程并不具备实用价值，必须可靠地嵌入生产级 MD 代码。其实现过程缓慢且高度依赖专家经验，除方程到 C++ 的翻译外，还涉及邻接表（neighbor-list）架构选择、多体导数求解与分发、处理器间通信管理，以及宿主代码在能量、力与维里（virial）约定上的一致性保持。

Method: Agent-E2MD 采用知识引导的智能体工作流，融合架构分类（architectural classification）、代码生成、自主“构建-测试-修复”循环、模拟执行与分层物理验证（hierarchical physical validation），将用户指定的原子间模型转换为 LAMMPS pair styles。测试集包含七组材料-势配对：Lennard-Jones Ar、EAM/FS Ag、MEAM Bi、Tersoff Si、GAP Ni、ReaxFF C/H/N/O，以及当前 LAMMPS 尚未支持的符号回归 EAM（Symb EAM）Al 模型。

Result: 单点（single-point）计算结果在全部七个模型上与参考能量和力一致；五项晶体多体基准测试复现了参考的弛豫晶格性质、空位形成能与弹性常数。Ag、Bi、Si、Ni 在纳秒尺度有限温度模拟中保持稳定；ReaxFF 进一步将验证拓展至含键级演化与电荷平衡（charge equilibration）的反应动力学。

Conclusion: 结果表明，原子间势的软件架构可依据其物理与数学结构进行推断。Agent-E2MD 并不替代科学判断，而是将用户精力从常规实现转移到模型定义、严格验证与物理解释；该框架为新兴 AI 驱动的势发现方法与生产级原子尺度模拟之间提供了实用且可追溯的桥梁。

Abstract: Interatomic potentials underpin MD and govern predictive atomistic-model fidelity for metals, semiconductors, oxides, liquids, and reactive systems. A potential has limited practical value until reliably implemented in production MD code. Slow, expertise-intensive implementation requires more than equation-to-C++ translation: it must select the neighbor-list architecture, evaluate and distribute many-body derivatives, manage interprocessor communication, and preserve host-code energy, force, and virial conventions. We introduce Agent-E2MD, a knowledge-guided agentic workflow that translates user-specified interatomic models into executable LAMMPS pair styles. It combines architectural classification, code generation, autonomous build-test-fix cycles, simulation execution, and hierarchical physical validation. We test Agent-E2MD on seven material-potential pairs: Lennard--Jones Ar, EAM/FS Ag, MEAM Bi, Tersoff Si, GAP Ni, ReaxFF C/H/N/O, and a recently developed Symbolic Regression EAM (Symb EAM) model for Al, currently unavailable in LAMMPS. Five crystalline many-body benchmarks recover reference relaxed lattice properties, vacancy formation energies, and elastic constants. Ag, Bi, Si, and Ni are stable at finite temperature in nanosecond-scale simulations; ReaxFF extends validation to reactive dynamics with evolving bond order and charge equilibration. Results demonstrate that the software architecture for an interatomic potential can be inferred from its physical and mathematical structure. Agent-E2MD does not replace scientific judgment; it shifts users' effort from routine implementation to model definition, rigorous validation, and physical interpretation. The framework provides a practical, traceable bridge between emerging AI-driven potential discovery methods and production-scale atomistic simulations.

</details>

Method-dependent differences, in particular those associated with explicit electrolyte ions, become apparent only in more local structural descriptors. The hydrogen-bond topology reveals charge-dependent chain-to-ring rearrangements, with explicit ions enhancing ring populations near the potential of zero charge. Layer-resolved vibrational density of states (VDOS) assigns the strongest O-H stretching perturbation to chemisorbed first-layer water and identifies high-frequency signatures of ion-coordinated water, while computed vibrational sum-frequency generation (VSFG) spectra show that the physisorbed, electrolyte-facing region is particularly sensitive to the counter-charge representation. These results suggest that surface charge controls the average structural response, whereas electrolyte ions and their solvation shells become visible only when locally refined descriptors are investigated or probed, e.g. the H-bond network topology and vibrational fingerprints of the interfacial bilayer.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文为含轨道间配对与能带 Hatsugai-Kohmoto（HK）相互作用的两带超导体建立了精确理论，并将其应用于 Chern dartboard 绝缘体，解析计算了配对磁化率与配对关联。结果表明 HK 相互作用可通过抬升双占据能带使其穿越化学势，在原本的绝缘相中诱导超导，并在特定参数区间产生绝缘体-超导体-绝缘体复现转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 BCS 理论预测在弱配对强度 g 且化学势 μ 位于能隙内时体系为绝缘相，该框架未纳入强关联型 HK 相互作用的影响。本文动机在于探究 HK 相互作用与轨道间配对共同作用是否会改变该绝缘相的物理图像。

Method: 作者为两带超导体（two-band superconductor）构建了精确理论（exact theory），同时包含轨道间配对（inter-orbital pairing）与能带 Hatsugai-Kohmoto 相互作用；随后将该理论应用于 Chern dartboard 绝缘体，并对配对磁化率（pair susceptibility）与配对关联（pair correlations）进行解析计算。

Result: 在 BCS 理论先前预言的绝缘相中（配对强度 g 较小、化学势 μ 位于能隙内），HK 相互作用可通过抬升双占据能带（doubly-occupied band）使其穿越化学势，从而诱导超导，并可能形成 Chern dartboard 超导体。在该 HK 诱导超导相中，于较大参数范围（HK 相互作用强度 U = 1、μ = 0.5）内存在绝缘体-超导体-绝缘体复现转变（re-entrant transition）。

Conclusion: 复现转变被归因于 HK 相互作用与轨道间配对之间的相互混杂（conflation），表明两类相互作用的耦合是产生该复现现象的关键机制。

Abstract: We formulate an exact theory for a 2-band superconductor with inter-orbital pairing and band Hatsugai-Kohmoto (HK) interactions. We apply this theory to a Chern dartboard insulator and compute the pair susceptibility and pair correlations analytically. We find that in the insulating phase previously predicted by BCS theory (small pairing strength $g$, chemical potential $μ$ in the gap), the HK interaction can induce superconductivity, and possibly a Chern dartboard superconductor, by lifting the doubly-occupied band and making it cross the chemical potential. From this HK-induced superconductor, we notice that in a significant parameter range (HK interaction strength $U = 1$, $μ= 0.5$), an insulator-superconductor-insulator re-entrant transition exists. We isolate the re-entrance to a conflation between HK interactions and inter-orbital pairings.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev 蜂窝模型是量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）的典型模型，但实验实现面临困难；已有方案多依赖自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling），而本文旨在提出不依赖该机制且实验可及的实现路径。

Result: 数值计算识别出一个具有受控修正的操作区间，其相关能量与长度尺度处于实验可达范围内；面外磁通与半导体费米能分别实现对键方向的干涉调控与对称性调控。

Conclusion: 结果确立了在工程化超导网络（engineered superconducting networks）中实现平衡态量子自旋液体的可行路径。

Abstract: We propose a realization of the Kitaev honeycomb Hamiltonian -- an archetypal spin-liquid model -- in a superconducting metamaterial. The architecture consists of Cooper-pair boxes coupled through depleted semiconductor--superconductor heterostructures that do not require spin--orbit coupling. The Cooper-pair boxes encode effective spin degrees of freedom, while normal and anomalous virtual propagation through the heterostructures mediate bond-directional interactions. Two key control parameters are an out-of-plane magnetic flux and the semiconductor Fermi energy. The former controls interference and distinguishes the bond directions, while tuning the latter close to the bottom of the band gives rise to an emergent Nambu-exchange symmetry that enforces the required bond directionality. Through numerical calculations, we identify an operating regime with controlled corrections, with associated energy and length scales within experimental reach. These results establish a route toward equilibrium quantum spin liquids in engineered superconducting networks.

</details>

*Tiago Jorge,Jens Paaske,Pedro Ribeiro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在电压偏置的电子 Lipkin–Meshkov–Glick 模型中，通过受控大-$N$ 处理研究非平衡量子临界稳态是否可由有效温度描述，发现稳态涨落与强驱动状态选择均由有效温度 $T_{\rm eff}$ 组织，但状态选择遵循非玻尔兹曼规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡量子临界稳态能否用有效温度（effective temperature）描述尚属开放问题；作者希望在一个可控的驱动-耗散模型中检验该问题。

Method: 采用电压偏置的电子 Lipkin–Meshkov–Glick 模型（一种集体自旋与金属电极耦合的最小驱动-耗散模型），并进行受控的大-$N$（large-$N$）处理。

Result: 计算揭示出区别于封闭模型（closed model）的过阻尼开放量子临界区（overdamped open quantum-critical regime）；由温度与电压组织的量子临界扇（quantum critical fan）中，涨落由有效温度 $T_{\rm eff}$ 支配；强偏置下经三临界点（tricritical point）转变为一阶相变；同一 $T_{\rm eff}$ 也支配强驱动区，但在整个序参量图景（order-parameter landscape）中发生变化。

Conclusion: 驱动临界性仍由有效温度组织，但稳态由非玻尔兹曼规则（non-Boltzmann rule）选择，使一阶相变偏离确定性势的等深点，从而展现出非热态选择（non-thermal state selection）。

Abstract: Whether a nonequilibrium quantum-critical steady state can be described by an effective temperature remains an open question. We address it in a voltage-biased electronic Lipkin--Meshkov--Glick model, a minimal driven-dissipative model of a collective spin coupled to metallic leads. A controlled large-$N$ treatment reveals an overdamped open quantum-critical regime distinct from the closed model. The surrounding quantum critical fan, organized by temperature and voltage, is explored and fluctuations are found to be governed by an effective temperature $T_{\rm eff}$. At strong bias, the transition becomes first-order through a tricritical point. Remarkably, the same $T_{\rm eff}$ governs the strongly driven regime, but now varies across the entire order-parameter landscape. The steady state is therefore selected by a non-Boltzmann rule, shifting the first-order transition away from the equal-depth point of a deterministic potential. Driven criticality thus remains organized by an effective temperature while revealing non-thermal state selection.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究受二维双层系统（two-dimensional bilayer systems）启发，旨在探究蜂窝晶格上不对称次近邻耦合所导致的非磁性基态及其性质。

Abstract: Motivated by two-dimensional bilayer systems we numerically study an anti-ferromagnetic spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg model on the honeycomb lattice with a nearest-neighbour exchange coupling $J_1$, and a next-nearest-neighbour exchange coupling $J_2'$ for the B sublattice sites only. Using infinite Projected Entangled-Pair States (iPEPS), variational uniform matrix-product states on cylinders, and exact diagonalization, we find an extended symmetric regime $0.4\lesssim J'_2/J_1\lesssim0.6$, in which local observables show no magnetic, valence-bond, or chiral spin order. At $J'_2/J_1=0.5$, the PEPS correlation length grows systematically with bond dimension, and an inverse-correlation-length extrapolation favors a vanishing limit. We also find that various observables scale algebraically with the finite bond-dimension-induced correlation length, which points to a gapless spin liquid ground state. We propose a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ Dirac spin liquid parton state, with Dirac points that are protected by translation, time-reversal and three-fold rotation symmetry, as a promising candidate state to explain the numerical results. We also discuss the possibility that the symmetric ground state is a featureless, short-range entangled state with a small gap.

</details>


### [53] [Thermally melted quadrupolar order and intrinsically quantum phases in 5$d^1$ double perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25283)

  <summary>Details</summary>
