Abstract: Spin-orbit-coupled $d^1$ double perovskites exhibit a rich interplay of spin, orbital, and quadrupolar degrees of freedom (DOF), giving rise to competing magnetic and multipolar phases. Although quantum mean-field theories predict many exotic phases, their stability against thermal fluctuations and reproducibility within a classical framework remain an open question. Here, we surpass the mean-field limitations by deploying large-scale classical Monte Carlo simulations on the projected $j=3/2$ manifold of the FCC lattice, allowing complex ordering patterns to emerge spontaneously without preassigned magnetic symmetries. Our thermodynamic mapping reveals that thermal fluctuations melt the intermediate-temperature quadrupolar phase over part of the phase diagram, while it survives intact elsewhere. Crucially, by systematically isolating the boundary between classical and quantum stability, we demonstrate that while the four-sublattice antiferromagnetic and ferromagnetic (FM) phases are robustly classical, the coplanar canted FM[110] state completely destabilizes. This identifies the FM[110] phase as an intrinsically quantum state born out of quantum fluctuations. Our results demonstrate that the dominant magnetic phases are robust within a classical description, where the essential physics of the system is captured by weakly entangled, short-range correlated DOF and highlight the role of thermal fluctuations in determining the stability of different types of magnetic and quadrupolar order.

</details>


### [54] [Lattice in Line: Optimized DMRG ordering for complex lattice geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25384)
*Roman Rausch*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何将二维/三维周期团簇与分子"编译"为一维排列，以用于密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）计算。作者比较了图带宽 B、割宽 C 与平均相互作用距离 R 三个代理损失函数，发现按字典序优化 (C, R) 可获得最佳能量，并提出基于布尔可满足性（Boolean satisfiability, SAT）与约束规划（constraint programming, CP）的分阶段优化方法，优于经典启发式与 QUBO 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMRG 本质是一维张量网络技术，但可用于周期性 2D/3D 团簇与分子，前提是先将格点沿一条线枚举（即"格点编译"）。该枚举顺序显著影响计算效率与精度，因此需要寻找最优排列。

Method: 作者构造了受挫磁性（frustrated magnetism）研究中大量团簇的哈密顿量矩阵乘积算符（matrix-product operator, MPO），用以评估三种代理损失函数：图带宽（graph bandwidth）B、割宽（cutwidth）C 与平均相互作用距离 R。优化方面先测试经典启发式（如 reverse Cuthill–McKee）与 QUBO 形式化，随后提出基于 SAT 与 CP 求解器（主要为 Google OR-Tools 的 CP-SAT）的分阶段优化方案。

Result: C 决定 SU(2) Heisenberg MPO 的峰值键维度，R 决定其平均值；按字典序优化 (C, R) 得到最佳能量。优化 B 可间接降低 C，但效率不及直接优化 C；B 本身影响有限，良好 DMRG 能量也可能对应较大的 B。经典启发式即使在小团簇上也不可靠，QUBO 改进有限，而所提出的 SAT/CP 分阶段方法给出显著更优的排列并提供严格界。

Conclusion: 将格点枚举视为格点编译问题，并以 (C, R) 为字典序目标进行优化，是提升 DMRG 能量表现的有效策略。基于 SAT 与 CP 的分阶段优化方法在质量与可验证性上均优于经典启发式与 QUBO。相应代码 Lattice in Line 已开源，且开发过程中大量使用了 Fable 5 大语言模型。

Abstract: The density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) is a one-dimensional tensor-network technique, but it is not limited to one-dimensional systems: it can be applied to periodic 2D and 3D clusters and molecules, provided their sites are first enumerated along a line; a step one may call "lattice compilation".
  This paper discusses three proxy loss functions for finding this optimal enumeration: the graph bandwidth $B$ (maximum interaction range), the cutwidth $C$ (maximum number of bonds crossing a cut), and the average interaction range $R$. Constructing the Hamiltonian MPO (matrix-product operator) for a large set of clusters that are of interest in frustrated magnetism, I find that $C$ determines the peak SU(2) Heisenberg MPO bond dimension and $R$ the average one. Targeting $(C,R)$ lexicographically yields the best energies. Targeting $B$ indirectly reduces $C$, but not as efficiently as targeting $C$ directly. Otherwise, the value of $B$ itself is largely irrelevant in the sense that good DMRG energies can have large $B$.
  To perform the optimization, classic heuristics (e.g. reverse Cuthill--McKee) prove unreliable even for small clusters, and I find that a QUBO formulation improves them only marginally. Instead I propose a staged optimization built on Boolean satisfiability (SAT) and constraint-programming (CP) solvers, chiefly CP-SAT of Google's OR-Tools, a hybrid of CP propagation and SAT clause learning. This approach yields significantly better orderings together with rigorous bounds.
  The corresponding Lattice in Line code is available at https://github.com/spinflip/lattice_in_line and was designed with extensive use of the Fable 5 large language model.

</details>


### [55] [Coherent lattice dynamics distinguish Holstein and Su-Schrieffer-Heeger electron-phonon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25418)
*Debshikha Banerjee,Jinu Thomas,G. Alvarez,T. P. Devereaux,T. A. Maier,S. Johnston*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用时间依赖密度矩阵重整化群（time-dependent density matrix renormalization group, t-DMRG）模拟抽运（pump）后的一维莫特绝缘体（Mott insulator），比较 Holstein 与 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）两类电子-声子（electron-phonon, e-ph）耦合下的非平衡响应，指出晶格动力学可区分耦合的微观形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快抽运-探测光谱（pump-probe spectroscopy）是研究强关联体系中电子-声子相互作用的有力手段，但不同微观电子-声子耦合机制如何影响晶格的非平衡响应尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 对抽运后的一维莫特绝缘体进行时间依赖密度矩阵重整化群模拟，分别采用 Holstein 耦合与 Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）耦合连接声子。

Result: 在莫特绝缘区，两模型的电子响应定性相似，均由 Hubbard 模型相同的激发与共振条件支配；但晶格动力学差异显著：Hubbard-Holstein 模型在抽运与裸声子频率共振时呈现长寿命相干声子振荡，而 Hubbard-SSH 模型未观测到此类振荡，且该对比在声子频率与抽运参数变化下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 相干晶格动力学可作为一种直接的非平衡探针，用于辨别电子-声子耦合的微观形式。

Abstract: Ultrafast pump-probe spectroscopy provides a powerful means to investigate electron-phonon (e-ph) interactions in strongly correlated systems. Nevertheless, the question of how different microscopic e-ph coupling mechanisms influence the lattice's nonequilibrium response has not been widely addressed. We perform time-dependent density matrix renormalization group simulations on a pumped one-dimensional Mott insulator coupled to phonons through either Holstein or Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) interactions. The electronic responses of the two models are qualitatively similar in the Mott-insulating regime and are governed by the same excitations and resonance conditions of the Hubbard model. In contrast, the lattice dynamics of the two models differ substantially. While the Hubbard-Holstein model exhibits long-lived coherent phonon oscillations when the pump resonates with the bare phonon frequency, no such oscillations are observed for the Hubbard-SSH model. These contrasting responses remain robust against changes in phonon frequency and pump parameters, suggesting that coherent lattice dynamics can provide a direct nonequilibrium probe of the microscopic form of e-ph coupling.

</details>


### [56] [Real-Frequency Diagrammatics with Pole Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26703)
*Rayan Farid,Lei Zhang,Aiman Al-Eryani,Agnieszka Jażdżewska,Emanuel Gull*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于极点和矩展开（pole and moment expansions）的多体微扰理论表示，并配合可控重压缩（controlled recompression）程序，在实轴上直接计算图形贡献，从而避免解析延拓（analytic continuation）与昂贵的数值求积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度多体理论通常在虚时间（imaginary time）中表述以便数值处理，但虚轴到谱性质的提取需数值解析延拓（numerical analytic continuation），而直接在实轴上表述又需极其昂贵的数值求积（numerical quadrature）。

Method: 采用可系统改进的极点与矩展开表示多体微扰理论（many-body perturbation theory）中的对象，建立直接在实轴上求值图形贡献的框架，并引入可控重压缩程序以抑制极点（pole）在代数与图形运算中的增殖。

Result: 在典型量子杂质问题（quantum impurity problems）上进行了基准测试，并应用于均匀电子气（uniform electron gas）的自洽计算；所得谱量系统性地收敛至数值精度。

Conclusion: 该工作为图形多体理论（包括自洽守恒方法）中谱量的可控且可系统改进计算提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Finite-temperature many-body theories are typically formulated in imaginary time, where they are amenable to efficient numerical treatment. However, the extraction of spectral properties from the imaginary axis is difficult and requires numerical analytic continuation, while a direct formulation on the real axis requires prohibitively expensive numerical quadrature. In this paper, we represent the objects of many-body perturbation theory using systematically improvable pole and moment expansions and develop the corresponding framework for evaluating diagrammatic contributions directly on the real axis. We introduce a controlled recompression procedure that prevents the proliferation of poles under algebraic and diagrammatic operations. We benchmark the approach on paradigmatic quantum impurity problems and apply it to self-consistent calculations of the uniform electron gas. The resulting spectral quantities converge systematically towards numerical accuracy. This work provides a practical framework for controlled and systematically improvable calculations of spectral quantities within diagrammatic many-body theories, including self-consistent conserving approaches.

</details>


### [57] [Efficiency of Continuous-Time Quantum Monte Carlo Updates in the Ferromagnetic State of the Doped SU(3) Fermi-Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25494)
*Juntaro Fujii,Kazuki Yamamoto,Akihisa Koga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作系统考察了动态平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory, DMFT）框架下、基于片段的连续时间杂化展开量子蒙特卡洛（continuous-time hybridization-expansion quantum Monte Carlo）求解器在掺杂 SU(3) 费米-哈伯德模型（Fermi-Hubbard model）中的采样效率，比较了多种更新方案在不同磁序区间内的自相关表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究聚焦于低温与强耦合区域，此时在偏离三分之一填充（one-third filling）进行空穴掺杂会出现铁磁态，该区间的采样效率问题较为突出，因此需要评估不同更新方案以提升求解器性能。

Method: 在基于片段的连续时间杂化展开量子蒙特卡洛求解器中，比较了若干更新方案：在基础局域更新（local update）基础上分别加入双翻转更新（double-flip update）与味置换更新（flavor-permutation update）；并通过分析被接受更新所引起的特征占据数变化来探究差异来源。

Result: 在铁磁区（ferromagnetic regime），加入双翻转更新可使多数味占据数（majority-flavor occupation）的积分自相关时间（integrated autocorrelation time）最小；而在顺磁区（paramagnetic regime），加入味置换更新最为有效。

Conclusion: 不同更新方案的有效性依赖于磁序区间：双翻转更新适合铁磁区，味置换更新适合顺磁区；这一差异可归因于被接受更新所导致的特征占据数变化的不同。

Abstract: We investigate the sampling efficiency in a segment-based continuous-time hybridization-expansion quantum Monte Carlo solver within dynamical mean-field theory for the doped SU(3) Fermi-Hubbard model. Focusing on the low-temperature and strong-coupling regime, where a ferromagnetic state appears upon hole doping away from one-third filling, we compare several update schemes. Adding the double-flip update to the basic local update yields the smallest integrated autocorrelation time of the majority-flavor occupation in the ferromagnetic regime, whereas adding the flavor-permutation update is the most effective in the paramagnetic regime. We further clarify the origin of this difference by analyzing the characteristic occupation changes caused by accepted updates.

</details>


### [58] [Model Fractional Quantum Hall States on Lattices: Exact Parent Hamiltonians and Routes to Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25585)
*Guangyue Ji,Jinjie Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作为格点分数量子霍尔（FQH）模型态系统构建厄米母哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonian），并以此为定量参照，研究短程相互作用对Laughlin与Moore–Read态稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化量子平台已实现ν=1/2的玻色Laughlin态，使非阿贝尔拓扑相（non-Abelian topological phases）进入实验可达范围；核心理论挑战在于建立统一框架，联系格点FQH模型态、精确母哈密顿量与实验可及相互作用。

Method: 将连续最低朗道能级（lowest Landau level, LLL）模型态采样到格点上，系统构造以其为精确零模的厄米母哈密顿量；在投影格点LLL中考察短程密度相互作用与两体在位排斥，并进行完整多带（multiband）计算。

Result: 在所研究通量密度下，短程密度相互作用稳定ν=1/3与1/4的Laughlin基态流形，两体在位排斥支持ν=1的Moore–Read三重态；多带计算显示Laughlin流形在所用环面（tori）上对带间混合稳健，而Moore–Read三重态谱隔离性下降并在带间能标附近发生能级交叉。

Conclusion: 该框架为利用格点模型态指导寻找实验可及的阿贝尔与非阿贝尔FQH态提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in engineered quantum platforms have enabled the realization of bosonic Laughlin states at $ν=1/2$ and brought non-Abelian topological phases within experimental reach. A central theoretical challenge is to develop a unified framework connecting lattice fractional quantum Hall (FQH) model states, exact parent Hamiltonians, and experimentally accessible interactions. We systematically construct Hermitian parent Hamiltonians for which continuum lowest Landau level (LLL) model states sampled on lattice sites are exact zero modes. Using these model manifolds as quantitative references, we find that, within the projected lattice LLL at the flux densities studied, short-range density interactions stabilize Laughlin ground-state manifolds at $ν=1/3$ and $1/4$, while two-body onsite repulsion supports a Moore--Read triplet at $ν=1$. Full multiband calculations reveal a sharp contrast: the Laughlin manifolds remain robust against interband mixing on the studied tori, whereas the Moore--Read triplet becomes less spectrally isolated and eventually undergoes a level crossing with competing states near the interband scale. Our framework provides a theoretical foundation for using lattice model states to guide the search for experimentally accessible Abelian and non-Abelian FQH states.

</details>


### [59] [Strong coupling polarons in cavity quantum materials: limits of cavity-induced electron mass renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25829)
*D. M. Basko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作探讨腔诱导电子质量重整化（cavity-induced electron mass renormalization）的极限问题，指出该效应本质上源于电子与腔镜中可极化自由度（polarizable degrees of freedom）之间经由场介导的相互作用，并通过数值精确量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）直接计算二维电子的费曼路径积分（Feynman path integral）。所求得的上限由平面几何下的朗道-佩卡尔极化子（Landau-Pekar polaron）给出，结果在腔控制（cavity control）与介电工程（dielectric engineering）之间建立了联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过真空场修饰（vacuum field modification）调控材料性质的多项方案中，腔诱导电子质量重整化是关键要素，但其最终极限尚不明确。该研究旨在回答这一极限问题，并弥合腔控制与介电工程之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 方法包含两个要点：(i) 论证该质量重整化本质上归因于场介导的电子与腔镜可极化自由度之间的相互作用；(ii) 针对二维电子直接评估相应的费曼路径积分，采用数值精确量子蒙特卡洛方法。

Result: 所寻求的质量重整化上限由平面几何中的朗道-佩卡尔极化子给出。

Conclusion: 该计算将腔控制与材料性质的介电工程联系起来，为真空场修饰下的质量重整化提供了上限判据。

Abstract: Cavity-induced electron mass renormalization is an important ingredient of many proposals to control material properties through vacuum field modification. What is its ultimate limit? Here this question is answered by (i)~noting that this mass renormalization is essentially due to the field-mediated interaction between the electron and the polarizable degrees of freedom in the cavity mirrors, and (ii)~directly evaluating the corresponding Feynman path integral for a 2D electron by numerically exact quantum Monte Carlo. The sought upper limit is provided by the Landau-Pekar polaron in the planar geometry. The presented calculation bridges the gap between cavity control and dielectric engineering of material properties.

</details>


### [60] [Nullspace-guided Adaptive Bootstrap of Quantum Many-body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25834)
*Xu-Cheng Wang,Yang Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出零空间引导的自适应（nullspace-guided adaptive, NGA）自举（bootstrap）方法，通过动态增量精化自举基改进量子多体基态能量下界。该方法在横向场 Ising 链上近乎精确，在 Hubbard 链上将现有最优下界改进至多两个数量级，误差低至 $10^{-5}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体基态能量下界的自举方法受限于自举基的选择，静态或人工设计的基难以高效捕捉强关联体系中的关键算符结构，因此需要一种能够自动、动态精化基的自适应框架。

Method: 在每次迭代中，从优化后的矩量矩阵（moment matrix）中提取饱和正定方向的零空间（nullspace），将其解释为近似基态子空间的湮灭算符（annihilators）；随后剪除零空间杠杆（nullspace leverage）较小的算符，并沿这些零方向的下降算符方向扩展基，形成动态增量的自适应自举流程。

Result: 在横向场 Ising 链（transverse-field Ising chain）上获得近乎精确的能量下界，算法从最小局域自举基自动发现 Jordan-Wigner 费米子（Jordan-Wigner fermions）形式的本征算符结构；在 Hubbard 链（Hubbard chain）上，于强关联区间将当前最优能量下界改进至多两个数量级，误差范围为 $10^{-3}$ 至 $10^{-5}$。此外，该框架可改进一般可观测量（observables）的经认证双侧界（certified two-sided bounds），且自举误差随计算资源增加近似呈幂律下降。

Conclusion: NGA 自举通过零空间引导的基精化为一般量子多体系统的精确自举提供了实用且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: We introduce a nullspace-guided adaptive (NGA) bootstrap method that improves the energy lower bounds of quantum many-body ground states by refining the bootstrap basis in a dynamic and incremental way. At each iteration, the optimized moment matrix reveals a nullspace of saturated positivity directions, which is intuitively interpreted as annihilators of the approximate ground-state subspace. The NGA bootstrap then prunes operators with small nullspace leverage and grows the basis along descendants of these null directions. By applying the NGA bootstrap to the transverse-field Ising chain, we obtain nearly exact energy lower bounds because the algorithm automatically discovers the eigenoperator structure in terms of Jordan-Wigner fermions from a minimal local bootstrap basis. For the Hubbard chain, it improves upon state-of-the-art energy lower bounds by up to two orders of magnitude, reaching errors ranging from $10^{-3}$ down to $10^{-5}$ in the strongly correlated regimes. We further show that the NGA framework can be used to improve the certified two-sided bounds on general observables. In addition, the bootstrap error decreases approximately as a power law with increasing computational resources. These results suggest that our method provides a practical and scalable route toward accurate bootstrap of general quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [61] [Fractional Quantum Geometry in Topological Mott Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25922)
*Junyu Tang,Hongquan Lv,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出在二维拓扑莫特绝缘体（topological Mott insulator，即手性自旋液体 chiral spin liquid）中，通过电荷扇区的虚极化与涌现 U(1) 规范场，将电磁驱动传递给中性自旋子（spinon），从而实现对其能带拓扑与量子几何的定量探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受电子能带中量子几何（quantum geometry）研究兴趣的启发，作者希望探测拓扑莫特绝缘体中分数化准粒子（自旋子）能带的拓扑与量子几何性质，因为这类中性激发无法直接被电磁场耦合。

Method: 利用有隙电荷扇区的虚极化（virtual polarization）经涌现 U(1) 规范场（emergent U(1) gauge field）将电磁驱动传递至自旋子，产生张量形式的 Ioffe-Larkin 响应；据此设计两种互补的光学测量方案：离壳（off-shell，低频）与在壳（on-shell，共振）机制。

Result: 在离壳低频区，物理纵向与霍尔电导率的自校准比值可给出自旋子陈数（spinon Chern number），无需电荷响应的微观知识；在在壳共振区，结合逆 Ioffe-Larkin 响应与 Kramers-Kronig 关系可重构量子度规与贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的谱密度。

Conclusion: 该方法可直接应用于莫尔（Moiré）体系中的三角晶格 Hofstadter-Hubbard 模型，并可经适当修改推广至 Nb$_3$Br$_8$ 等三角晶格反铁磁体，为定量探测拓扑莫特区间中分数化准粒子的量子几何建立了统一框架。

Abstract: Inspired by recent interests in quantum geometry in electronic bands, we consider a two-dimensional topological Mott insulator, i.e. chiral spin liquid, whose spinon bands develop nontrivial band topological and quantum geometry. The virtual polarization of the gapped charge sector transfers the electromagnetic drive to the charge-neutral spinons through the emergent U(1) gauge field, producing a tensor Ioffe-Larkin response and enabling two complementary optical protocols for measurements. In the off-shell (low-frequency) regime, a self-calibrated ratio of the physical longitudinal and Hall conductivities yields the spinon Chern number without the microscopic knowledge of the charge response. In the on-shell (resonant) regime, combining the inverse Ioffe-Larkin response with the Kramers-Kronig relation enables reconstruction of the quantum-metric and Berry-curvature spectral densities. Our approach directly applies to the triangular lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model in Moiré systems, can be well adapted to other triangular lattice antiferromagnets such as Nb$_3$Br$_8$ with appropriate modifications. We establish a unified framework for quantitatively probing the quantum geometry of fractionalized quasiparticles in topological Mott regime.

</details>


### [62] [Effect of interaction range on the phase diagram of magnetic hedgehog lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26373)
*Midori Yamada,Kotaro Shimizu,Shun Okumura,Yasuyuki Kato,Yukitoshi Motome*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过系统调节交换相互作用（exchange interactions）的作用范围与空间衰减，结合大规模蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations），发现磁刺猬晶格（magnetic hedgehog lattices, HLs）可在广泛相互作用范围内稳定存在，其中中等程相互作用尤为有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLs 已在 MnSi₁₋ₓGeₓ 与 SrFeO₃ 等金属化合物中被发现，理论模型分别以短程（绝缘体）与长程（金属）相互作用描述，但相互作用程长对 HLs 稳定性的作用尚不完全清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立从局域自旋绝缘模型到巡游电子金属模型的统一描述。

Method: 系统改变交换相互作用（exchange interactions）的作用范围与空间衰减，从而在传统上与局域自旋绝缘模型及巡游电子金属模型相关联的相互作用剖面之间进行插值，并基于大规模蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）考察 HLs 的稳定性。

Result: HLs 在广泛的相互作用范围内均可稳定存在；在所考察的相互作用剖面中，中等程相互作用（intermediate-range interactions）对稳定 HLs 尤为有利，可产生两类不同的 3Q-HLs，二者在每磁胞内单极-反单极对（monopole-antimonopole pairs）数目上存在差异，其相对稳定性对相互作用程长高度敏感。

Conclusion: 研究结果为从微观相互作用模型调控相图（phase diagrams）提供了统一框架，并为候选材料的实验解释提供了指导准则。

Abstract: Topological spin textures have attracted considerable attention through their emergent electromagnetic properties, including the topological Hall effect and the topological Nernst effect. Among them, magnetic hedgehog lattices (HLs) are three-dimensional topological spin textures that host emergent magnetic monopoles and antimonopoles. HLs have been identified in metallic compounds, such as $\mathrm{MnSi}_{1-x}\mathrm{Ge}_x$ and $\mathrm{SrFeO_3}$. Various theoretical models have been proposed, with either short-range or long-range interactions, which are typically regarded as effective descriptions of insulating and metallic systems, respectively. However, the role of the interaction range in stabilizing the HLs remains only partially understood. To address this gap, we investigate the stability of HLs by systematically varying the range and spatial decay of the exchange interactions, thereby interpolating between interaction profiles commonly associated with localized-spin insulating models and itinerant-electron metallic models. Based on extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we find that HLs are stabilized over a broad interaction range. Intriguingly, among the interaction profiles examined, intermediate-range interactions are particularly favorable for stabilizing HLs. They give rise to two distinct types of $3Q$-HLs that differ in the number of monopole-antimonopole pairs per magnetic unit cell, and their relative stability depends sensitively on the interaction range. Our results provide a unified framework for tuning phase diagrams from microscopic interaction models, serving as a guideline for interpreting experiments in candidate materials.

</details>

Abstract: The excitonic insulator (EI) is an electronic phase of condensed excitons. However, in many prototypical materials the presence of a concurrent structural transition complicates the identification of a purely electronic origin of the ordered state. Here, we investigate Ta2Pd3Te5, in which an EI phase develops below TC ~ 100 K in the absence of a detectable structural transition. We characterize the excitonic condensate via its coherent phonon-amplitude response. Most strikingly, an extraordinarily strong lifetime enhancement of these modes sets in below TC which we establish as a new and robust fingerprint linked to exciton condensation. We discuss possibilities to capture the enhancement when taking into account a coupling of excitonic and lattice effects in line with the coupled phonon-amplitude response. Within an order-parameter polaron picture coupling to the excitonic state may dress the phonon mode and thereby suppress its relaxation and a phenomenological model combines the anharmonic phonon response and a reduced electronic scattering due to the gap opening in the EI phase.

*Tianshu Huang,Xiaowei Ou,Vidvuds Ozolins*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出以神经网络回流变换（neural network backflow transformation）为核心的变分框架，结合深度学习与变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo），用于模拟强关联费米子体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联费米子（strongly correlated fermions）模拟长期受限于希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）的指数复杂度与多体波函数的复杂符号结构（sign structure）。

Method: 采用中心为神经网络回流变换的变分框架，将深度学习与变分蒙特卡洛相结合；其拟设（ansatz）基于多行列式展开（multi-determinant expansion）并引入低秩移位（low-rank shifts），以刻画非局域关联与复杂符号结构。

Abstract: Simulating strongly correlated fermions remains a long-standing challenge due to the exponential complexity of the Hilbert space and the intricate sign structure of many-body wavefunctions. The proposed ansatz employs a multi-determinant expansion with low-rank shifts to capture non-local correlations and complex sign structures. Applied to the two-dimensional Hubbard model at both half-filling and $1/8$ doping, the method achieves energies within $0.45\%$ of auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo at half-filling and captures intertwined charge- and spin-density stripe patterns at $1/8$ doping. These results demonstrate the potential of this framework as a scalable and interpretable approach to variational simulations of strongly correlated fermionic systems.

Result: 在 T_C = 7.5 K 观察到明确铁磁转变；磁有序态低温下磁阻为正；在电阻率、磁阻与低温热容中确认磁振子（magnon）激发能隙。CEF 分析给出三个二重态（doublet）能级方案，基态及 491 K、671 K 两个激发态，并被热容磁贡献中的 Schottky 效应所支持。

Conclusion: CeRu₂Ge₂ 的磁化行为与 CEF 导出的基态波函数完全一致，易磁化轴沿 [001] 方向，区别于多数易轴偏离 CEF 基态的 Ce 基近藤晶格铁磁体。

Abstract: We report a detailed anisotropic studies on the magnetic and transport properties of a CeRu$_2$Ge$_2$ single crystal. A clear ferromagnetic transition is observed at $T_{\rm C} = 7.5$~K in the magnetic susceptibility, electrical resistivity and heat capacity measurements. The magnetoresistance (MR) is positive at low temperature within the magnetically ordered state. We confirmed the presence of a magnon excitation gap in the resistivity, MR and the low temperature heat capacity data. The pronounced anisotropy observed in the magnetic susceptibility and magnetization along the [100] and [001] directions is quantitatively explained within the crystal electric field (CEF) framework using the point-charge model. The analysis reveals a CEF level scheme comprising three doublets, with a ground state and two excited states at 491 and 671~K, that was further confirmed from the Schottky effect in the magnetic part of the heat capacity data. Unlike most Kondo lattice ferromagnets of Ce compounds, where the magnetic easy axis deviates from the expected CEF ground state, CeRu$_2$Ge$_2$ exhibits magnetization, which is fully consistent with the CEF derived ground state wave function, with the easy axis of magnetization aligned along the [001] direction.

</details>


### [66] [Berry-Landau Fermi-liquid theory: transport in presence of quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26632)
*Shuai A. Chen,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文构建了"Berry-Landau 费米液体理论（Berry-Landau Fermi-liquid theory）"，将量子几何（quantum geometry，含 Berry 相位与量子度规）作为低能有效理论的基本要素，并推导出朗道泛函与量子几何修正的输运系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统朗道费米液体理论（Landau Fermi-liquid theory）通过准粒子及其剩余相互作用描述相互作用金属，但难以将非平庸量子几何纳入基本框架，这是需要解决的挑战。

Method: 针对穿越费米面的孤立能带中的无自旋费米子，采用 Nozières–Luttinger 构造推导朗道泛函；对布洛赫波（Bloch waves）微扰的瞬时响应产生作用量中的反常 Berry 联络势（anomalous Berry-connection potential）与相互作用诱导的量子几何准粒子电流贡献，并通过电磁 Ward 恒等式（electromagnetic Ward identity）得到守恒物理电荷流。

Result: 本征反常霍尔电导率（intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity）由重整化准粒子能带的 Berry 曲率积分确定；Drude 权重同时包含常规贡献与量子几何贡献，且在伽利略对称性（Galilean symmetry）下免受相互作用重整化；在平带/窄带极限（flat/narrow-band limit）下输运以量子几何为主导，并可被热增强。

Conclusion: 结果表明，准粒子占据数、朗道相互作用与准粒子携带的量子几何共同构成 Berry-Landau 费米液体的基本低能要素。

Abstract: Landau Fermi-liquid theory characterizes interacting metals through quasiparticles and their residual interactions. It is a challenge to incorporate non-trivial quantum geometry -- as encoded by Berry phases and the quantum metric -- as a fundamental ingredient. We formulate a Berry-Landau Fermi-liquid theory for spinless fermions within an isolated band crossing the Fermi surface and derive the Landau functional through the Nozières--Luttinger construction. The instantaneous response to a perturbation of the Bloch waves generates both an anomalous Berry-connection potential in the action and an interaction-induced quantum-geometric contribution to the quasiparticle current. The conserved physical charge current is then obtained via the electromagnetic Ward identity as a combinati of this quantum-geometric current and the bare drift current. Therefore, the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity is fixed by the Berry-curvature integral of the dressed quasiparticle band while the Drude weight contains both conventional and quantum-geometric contributions. In the presence of Galilean symmetry, the Drude weight is protected against interaction renormalization. In the flat/narrow-band limit, transport is dominantly quantum-geometric and can be thermally enhanced. These results establish quasiparticle occupations, Landau interactions, and the quantum geometry carried by quasiparticles as the fundamental low-energy ingredients of a Berry-Landau Fermi liquid.

</details>

*Wei Li,Jing-Rong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究三维倾斜节线半金属（tilted nodal-line semimetal）的电荷响应各向异性，发现倾斜诱导的低能相空间变形使纵向与横向 Drude 权重呈现不同的标度行为，并进一步修正等离激元（plasmon）色散，预测在带内截止（cutoff）正则化下存在低频声学型分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾斜（tilt）作为模型控制参量，在能谱中表现为恒等项（identity term），会改变低能相空间结构，因此有必要厘清其对纵向与横向电荷响应的差异化影响，以及对集体激发（collective mode）的修正。

Method: 采用含线积分顶点（line-integral vertices）的 Kubo 公式（Kubo formula），计算纵向与横向 Drude 响应以及带间极化（interband polarization），并自洽求解等离激元色散（plasmon dispersion），正则化采用带宽（Brillouin-zone-like）截止。

Result: 纵向权重在对数窗口内为 D_zz = B ln(1/δ)，系数 B = g/(2π)^2，固定物理截止下饱和为截止设定的平台值；横向权重遵循更强的代数标度 D_⊥ ~ δ^{-1}；两者的主导 √δ 修正可解析获得。带间极化保留 q^2 长波结构，其系数在有限掺杂下经泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）红外可积，但带有缓慢的紫外对数。吸收阈值为 ω_c = 2μ/(1+|η_z|)。

Conclusion: 倾斜通过乘性重整化集体模式并限定无阻尼窗口（由带间连续谱界定），在 η_z ≳ 1.2 时于有限动量转移窗口内，II 型（type-II）扩展在该截止正则化下给出粒子—空穴连续谱之外的低频类声学分支候选。

Abstract: We study the anisotropic charge response of a three-dimensional tilted nodal-line semimetal in which the tilt enters the spectrum through an identity term and acts as the model control parameter. Using the Kubo formula with line-integral vertices, we show that the tilt-induced deformation of the low-energy phase space produces parametrically different longitudinal and transverse Drude responses: the longitudinal weight grows logarithmically as $D_{zz}=B\ln(1/δ)$ with the analytically determined coefficient $B=g/(2π)^2$---within the window in which the Fermi-surface tail lies inside the ultraviolet cutoff, while at a fixed physical cutoff the weight saturates instead at a cutoff-set plateau---whereas the transverse weight follows a stronger algebraic scaling $D_\perp\simδ^{-1}$; the leading $\sqrtδ$ corrections to both asymptotic forms are obtained analytically. The interband polarization retains the $q^2$ long-wavelength structure, with a coefficient that is infrared-integrable through Pauli blocking at finite doping yet carries a slow ultraviolet logarithm; it renormalizes the collective mode multiplicatively and sets the interband absorption threshold $ω_c=2μ/(1+|η_z|)$. A self-consistent solution of the plasmon dispersion identifies a tilt-dependent damping-free window bounded by the interband continuum. We predict that the type-II extension of this window, within the stated bandwidth (Brillouin-zone-like) cutoff regularization, contains a candidate low-frequency acoustic-like branch outside the particle--hole continua for $η_z\gtrsim1.2$ over a finite window in momentum transfer.

</details>

### [68] [System-size dependence of bottomonium suppression from Pb-Pb to light-ion collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25182)
*Nora Brambilla,Tom Magorsch,Antonio Vairo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在统一的开量子系统（open-quantum-system）输运框架下，比较 Pb-Pb、O-O 与 Ne-Ne 碰撞中底偶素（bottomonium）压低（suppression）的系统尺寸依赖性。结果显示 QGP 诱导演化可再现 O-O 的序列层级与幅度，并支持不同尺寸系统中存在共同的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出需检验底偶素压低是否随碰撞系统尺寸变化，以判断其微观机制是否在不同尺寸系统中保持一致。为此需在保持微观输运系数（transport coefficients）不变的前提下，对轻离子与 Pb-Pb 进行统一比较。

Result: QGP 诱导的演化再现了 CMS 与 LHCb 测得的 O-O 压低中的序列层级（sequential hierarchy）及总体幅度。Ne-Ne 测量显示压低略强于理论预测，但当前实验不确定度较大。结果对输运系数与终止温度均表现出一定敏感性。

Conclusion: 对于受冷核物质效应（cold nuclear matter effects）影响较小的观测量，从 Pb-Pb 扩展到轻离子的结果支持：在解禁闭物质（deconfined matter）中底偶素压低具有共同的微观起源，且尽管碰撞系统尺寸变化显著，仍可由相同的温度依赖输运系数刻画。

Abstract: We test the system-size dependence of bottomonium suppression using the same open-quantum-system transport framework for Pb-Pb, O-O, and Ne-Ne collisions. The microscopic transport coefficients are retained from previous Pb-Pb analyses. Using QTraj, we solve the next-to-leading-order pNRQCD Lindblad equation in anisotropic hydrodynamic backgrounds and calculate integrated $Υ$ double ratios. The QGP-induced evolution reproduces the observed sequential hierarchy and overall magnitude of the O-O suppression measured by CMS and LHCb. We quantify the sensitivity to both transport coefficients and to the termination temperature of the in-medium evolution. The Ne-Ne measurement favors somewhat stronger suppression than predicted, although the present experimental uncertainty is large. The extension from Pb-Pb to light ions supports, at least for observables less sensitive to cold nuclear matter effects, a common microscopic origin of bottomonium suppression in deconfined matter, characterized by the same temperature-dependent transport coefficients despite the large change in collision-system size.

</details>


### [69] [Limitations of quantum tomography in Higgs-boson decays to four leptons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25217)
*Morgan Del Gratta,Fabio Maltoni,Davide Pagani,Giovanni Pelliccioli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究双量子三态（two-qutrit）量子层析（quantum tomography）在希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）衰变为四带电轻子过程中的局限性。在次领头阶（next-to-leading order, NLO），标准重建可能给出非半正定（not positive semidefinite）算符，根源在于光子与 Z 玻色子贡献的相干叠加；但秩二角向扇区仍可稳健提取，从而给出平方并发度（squared concurrence）的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是考察在希格斯玻色子衰变为四带电轻子这一过程中，双量子三态量子层析方法在高阶微扰下的适用边界，特别是标准重建方法在次领头阶是否会失效，以及纠缠（entanglement）判定是否仍可靠。

Method: 采用次领头阶精度下的量子层析重建，并引入辐射改进的自旋分析能力（radiatively improved spin-analysing powers）进行检验；通过分析离壳双轻子系统（off-shell dilepton system）中光子与 Z 玻色子中介贡献的相干组合对重建算符性质的影响来定位失效来源。

Result: 标准重建在次领头阶可能产生非半正定算符，即便使用辐射改进的自旋分析能力也无法避免；该失效归因于光子与 Z 中介贡献的相干叠加，二者不同的产生与衰变结构破坏了所假设的普适衰变分析器（universal decay analysers）。与此同时，秩二角向扇区（rank-two angular sector）仍可访问且在微扰意义上稳定。

Conclusion: 双量子三态量子层析在次领头阶存在原理性限制，但秩二角向扇区的可用性使得可以给出平方并发度的稳健下界，从而实现对超越领头阶（beyond leading order）纠缠的可靠评估。

Abstract: We investigate the limitations of the two-qutrit quantum tomography in Higgs-boson decays to four charged leptons. At next-to-leading order, the standard reconstruction can yield an operator that is not positive semidefinite, even when radiatively improved spin-analysing powers are used. We trace this failure to the coherent combination of photon- and Z-mediated contributions to the off-shell dilepton system, whose distinct production and decay structures invalidate the assumed universal decay analysers. Nevertheless, the rank-two angular sector remains accessible and perturbatively stable, enabling a robust lower bound on the squared concurrence, and hence a reliable assessment of entanglement beyond leading order.

</details>

Abstract: We study cosmological stasis in coupled dark matter and dark energy models in which a scalar field drives the dark energy and sets the dark matter mass, with the Dark Dimension as the motivating realization. Anchoring the cosmology to the measured abundances at matter-radiation equality and today, we show that the stasis region cannot accelerate for any initial conditions, and that the total equation of state is never phantom although the effective dark energy of a $Λ$CDM fit can appear so. For the Dark Dimension, capture into stasis in the future is excluded for geometric sources of the potential once dark fifth-force bounds are imposed, and dark matter production from the Standard Model brane supports a further stasis which the standard scenario does not reach. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有以模块 A₄ 对称性（modular A₄ symmetry）为基础的最小模型（Feruglio 模型）仅由模块形式三重态 Y(τ) 决定中微子质量与混合。作者意图检验在局域超对称下，超场自身是否会带来被忽视的额外质量来源，从而重新评估该最小方案的可行性与预言能力。

Conclusion: 在局域超对称中，模块对称最小模型天然包含来自 Kähler 势的 R 宇称破坏中微子质量贡献，该贡献对重现观测到的混合角与质量比不可或缺。两种质量生成机制可通过中微子质量之和的宇宙学预言加以区分。

Abstract: The possibility of describing neutrino masses through a single multiplet of modular forms in a modular symmetric framework is reanalysed. We point out that, in local supersymmetry, these superfields by themselves allow an additional $R$-parity-violating contribution to neutrino masses originating from the Kähler potential of the model. Abstract: We compute universal scaling exponents and scaling functions for massless scalar field theories far from equilibrium. For massless dispersion $ω= \bar{p}$, we determine the scaling form of the distribution function whose universal power-law tail $f_S(\bar p) \sim 1/\bar{p}^κ$ is found to be a) $κ= d+1$ and b) $κ= d$ in $d=3$ spatial dimensions. Our results establish that relativistic and nonrelativistic theories do not belong to the same universality class in this case, which opens up new applications to far-from-equilibrium phenomena in high-energy and (quantum) many-body physics with linear dispersions.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The Van der Waals black hole (BH) is a non-Kerr model in Anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetime that exhibits characteristic thermodynamic phase transitions. Whether the physical validity of parameters such as the BH molecular volume $b$ and pressure $P$ is feasible still requires observational verification. As a sensitive probe of strong gravitational fields, the BH photon ring directly carries spacetime geometric information, making it an ideal object for model testing. In accordance with the spacetime metric associated with this BH, this study obtains the null geodesic equations for photons and conserved quantities, determines the photon sphere conditions and radii via effective potential analysis, investigates the photon ring classification and radiative properties, and employs measurements from the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) to place constraints on parameters and simulate the optical appearance. The results show that the molecular volume parameter $b$ significantly regulates the radius of the photon sphere radius, photon ring structure, and the luminosity. Within a specific interval, the model exhibits a high degree of agreement with the EHT measurements for M87* and Sgr A*. This interval not only satisfies the energy conditions (weak, strong, and dominant) but also corresponds to a stable Van der Waals-type phase transition region. Furthermore, the photon ring can distinguish between the Van der Waals BH and the Schwarzschild BH: due to the small-black-hole-large-black-hole (SBH-LBH) phase transition and microstructural discontinuities, the photon ring of the Van der Waals BH is particularly sensitive to the parameter $b$, whereas the Schwarzschild BH lacks these features. This work uses the photon ring to probe BH molecular models, linking its microstructure, spacetime geometry and observational signatures, and provides a new way to derive BH microphysics from observations.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用最小几何形变（minimal geometric deformation, MGD）方法，取双参数种子族 $\nu(r)=\mathrm{Ln}[C(1+ar^{2n})^{m}]$，其径向度规势 $\mu(r)=e^{-\lambda(r)}$ 由各向同性条件以闭式确定。通过单一源函数 $g(r)$ 对 MGD 的 $\theta$-扇区作一次性闭合，用高斯超几何函数 ${}_2F_1$ 闭式积分解耦方程，适用于任意 $(n,m)$；并导出有效各向异性物质内容、与外部 Schwarzschild 真空的 Darmois–Israel 匹配及物理可接受性判据。

Conclusion: 基于同一闭式物质内容计算四极潮汐 Love 数（quadrupolar tidal Love number）与无量纲潮汐形变率（dimensionless tidal deformability），发现在固定紧凑度下解耦系统性增大二者，且在 Heintzmann 与 Durgapal 分支接近因果性上限时急剧上升。该方案以单一主公式统一了多类种子解的各向异性延伸，并明确了各分支的物理可接受边界。

Abstract: We present a unification scheme for anisotropic compact stars generated via minimal geometric deformation (MGD), built on a two-parameter seed family $ν(r)=\text{Ln}[C(1+ar^{2n})^{m}]$ whose radial metric potential $μ(r)=e^{-λ(r)}$ is fixed in closed form by the isotropy condition. At $n=1$ the family reproduces Tolman IV, Korkina--Orlyanskii, Heintzmann IIa, and Durgapal IV--V as the particular cases $m=1,2,3,4,5$. The key result is a single closure of the MGD $θ$-sector, through one source function $g(r)$, that integrates the decoupler equation in closed form, in terms of the Gauss hypergeometric function ${}_2F_1$, for \emph{arbitrary} $(n,m)$: the anisotropic extension of the $n=1$ family is thus obtained from one master formula. We derive the effective anisotropic matter content, the Darmois--Israel matching to the exterior Schwarzschild vacuum, and the full set of physical acceptability criteria constraining the parameter space, and map the admissible region in compactness and decoupling strength explicitly for the case $n=1$: Tolman IV and Korkina--Orlyanskii admit physically consistent anisotropic deformations at arbitrarily high compactness, while Heintzmann IIa and Durgapal IV--V are bounded by a compactness ceiling beyond which no value of the decoupling parameter restores causality. Using the same closed-form matter content, we compute the quadrupolar tidal Love number and dimensionless tidal deformability across the admissible region, finding that the decoupling systematically enhances both relative to the isotropic seed at fixed compactness, with a sharp rise near the causal ceiling in the Heintzmann and Durgapal branches.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一部分已建立奇点的数学定义、分类与存在性证明，但需要进一步考察其在具体宇宙模型中的实际表现，以理解真空、倾斜流体等条件下的物理实现方式。

Method: 先回顾均匀性的几何基础与Bianchi分类，推导正交与倾斜完美流体（tilted perfect fluid）宇宙的场方程；以Kasner与Bianchi I解为各向异性奇点原型，借助Raychaudhuri聚焦条件判定均匀Cauchy发展（Cauchy development）的有限固有时间边界，并结合Ellis-King分析及后续曲率发散结果。

Result: 区分出以密度与Ricci曲率发散为特征的物质奇点（matter singularities）和与Cauchy视界、极端流体倾斜相关的非标量奇点；获得Bianchi VIII、IX模型的严格曲率发散结果；发现测地完备倾斜时空中的运动学奇点；whimper行为在偶极宇宙学（dipole cosmology）中重新出现。

Conclusion: 即使空间均匀宇宙也呈现异常丰富的奇点边界层级；Hamiltonian cosmology、Kasner跃迁、Mixmaster动力学与BKL猜想等动力学方法将留待第三部分讨论。

Abstract: This article is the second part of a review devoted to spacetime singularities in classical General Relativity. While Primer I developed the mathematical framework for defining, classifying, and proving the existence of singularities, the present work examines their concrete realization in spatially homogeneous cosmological models. After reviewing the geometrical foundations of homogeneity and the Bianchi classification, we derive the field equations for orthogonal and tilted perfect fluid cosmologies and use the Kasner and Bianchi I solutions as elementary prototypes of anisotropic singular behaviour. Raychaudhuri focusing provides general conditions under which the homogeneous Cauchy development reaches a finite proper time boundary. We then distinguish matter singularities, characterized by divergent density and Ricci curvature, from more subtle non-scalar or whimper singularities associated with Cauchy horizons and extreme fluid tilt. The classical Ellis-King analysis is reconsidered in the light of later developments, including rigorous curvature-blow-up results for Bianchi VIII and IX models, kinematic singularities in geodesically complete tilted spacetimes, and the recent reappearance of whimper behaviour in dipole cosmology. The resulting picture shows that even spatially homogeneous cosmologies exhibit a remarkably rich hierarchy of singular boundaries. The detailed dynamical approach to these boundaries - Hamiltonian cosmology, Kasner transitions, Mixmaster dynamics and the BKL conjecture - is reserved for Primer III.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该模型为研究可调规则核心与 RN 型外部形变如何共同影响规则黑洞的热力学与测地线性质提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We introduce a family of static and spherically symmetric regular black-hole geometries characterized by a discrete core index $n$, a regularization scale $a$, and a dimensionless deformation parameter $η$. The construction starts from a positive, normalized effective density profile with a tunable central behavior. The lowest member contains the Bardeen geometry as a limiting case, whereas a nonzero deformation parameter generates a Reissner--Nordström-like asymptotic correction. The parameter $n$ controls the order of central depletion: the lowest member possesses a de Sitter-type core, whereas higher members approach a Minkowski-like center with vanishing density and curvature. We determine the horizon structure, extremality condition, Hawking temperature, entropy, and the horizon thermodynamic identity implied by the radial Einstein equation. Null geodesics are analyzed to obtain the photon sphere, critical impact parameter, and shadow radius, including analytic weak-deformation approximations, the exact Schwarzschild and Bardeen limits, and comparison with the leading Reissner--Nordström-like behavior. The photon-orbit angular frequency and Lyapunov exponent are then used to separate orbital and instability timescales; their ratio provides information not already contained in the shadow radius. Weak gravitational lensing and magnification are discussed through a controlled sector-isolated expansion of the asymptotic metric. We further study timelike geodesics, circular motion, the marginally bound orbit, and the innermost stable circular orbit, deriving exact implicit relations and perturbative expressions. The model provides a unified framework for investigating how a tunable regular core and an RN-like exterior deformation affect the thermodynamic and geodesic properties of regular black holes.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞轨道的数值分类结果可能依赖于捕获边界（capture boundary）或吸收边界的实现方式。作者希望厘清这种边界条件的离散评估对轨道分类的影响，以避免将数值效应误判为真实的动力学阈值（dynamical thresholds）。

Method: 将无质量粒子（massless particles）在孤立牛顿点质量（Newtonian point mass）引力场中积分，初始位置设在近星点（periapsis），并设置吸收边界 rsink = 10^4 RS。由于开普勒近星点（Keplerian periapsis）q 为常数，预期分类可由解析方法确定，从而与不同网格分辨率下的数值分类进行对比。考察了分类器是否在初始化时（t = 0）进行评估的两种情形。

Result: 忽略初始化评估的分类器会遗漏那些已经满足移除判据的粒子；而在 t = 0 进行评估时，在所有采样网格上都能复现解析分类。随着网格分辨率提高，推断出的临界近星点趋近于所设定的吸收半径。

Conclusion: 表观临界近星点可能同时取决于所施加的边界条件及其数值评估方式。因此，在轨道分类研究中需区分数值效应与真实动力学阈值，避免将离散化引起的伪临界现象误认为物理阈值。

Abstract: Numerical classification of black-hole orbits can depend on how prescribed inner capture or absorbing boundaries are implemented. This study investigated how discrete evaluation of such a boundary affects orbit classification. Massless particles were integrated about an isolated Newtonian point mass and initialized at periapsis with an absorbing boundary at rsink = 104RS. Because the Keplerian periapsis q is constant, the expected classification can be analytically determined. A classifier omitting evaluation at initialization can miss particles that already satisfy the removal criterion, but evaluation at t = 0 reproduced the analytic classification on every sampled grid. As the grid resolution increased, the inferred critical periapsis approached the imposed sink radius. These results demonstrate that an apparent critical periapsis can depend on both the imposed boundary and its numerical evaluation, emphasizing the need to distinguish numerical effects from genuine dynamical thresholds.

</details>


### [86] [The BTZ black hole, the Third Law and gravitational collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25218)
*Aidan M. McSharry,Harvey S. Reall,Jorge E. Santos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明在满足主导能量条件的三维引力中，BTZ黑洞遵循黑洞力学第三定律：含俘获面的初值不能有限时间内演化至事件视界上为极端BTZ的黑洞；但无质量标量场的引力坍缩仍可在有限时间内形成极端BTZ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在检验黑洞力学第三定律（Third Law of black hole mechanics）在三维引力与BTZ黑洞情形下是否成立，并厘清其与引力坍缩形成极端黑洞之间的关系。

Method: 通过定义准局域能量（quasilocal energy）与角动量，并采用旋量方法（spinorial methods）建立被极端BTZ解饱和的BPS不等式。

Result: 证明含俘获面（trapped surface）的初值不能在有限时间内演化至事件视界上与极端BTZ重合的黑洞，且与无穷远处边界条件选择无关；同时表明无质量标量场的引力坍缩可在有限时间内形成极端BTZ。

Conclusion: 两结果差异源于引力坍缩发生于Neveu-Schwarz扇区（sector），而极端BTZ在Ramond扇区中是超对称的；文中亦讨论了沿事件视界在无限时间中趋近极端BTZ的解。

Abstract: We prove that a Third Law of black hole mechanics holds for the BTZ black hole in 3d gravity coupled to matter obeying the dominant energy condition. This law states that initial data containing a trapped surface cannot evolve in finite time to a black hole that coincides with extremal BTZ on the event horizon, for any choice of boundary conditions at infinity. The result is proved by defining a quasilocal energy and angular momentum and using spinorial methods to establish a BPS inequality that is saturated by the extremal BTZ solution. Nevertheless, we show that gravitational collapse of a massless scalar field can result in the formation of an extremal BTZ black hole in finite time. The difference between these results arises because gravitational collapse occurs in the Neveu-Schwarz sector, whereas extremal BTZ is supersymmetric in the Ramond sector. Solutions that settle down to extremal BTZ in infinite time along the event horizon are also discussed.

</details>


### [87] [Gravity-Informed Neural Networks for Post-Newtonian Binary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25222)
*Gabriele Barbagallo,Javier Matulich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出重力启发神经网络框架（GravINNs），将后牛顿（post-Newtonian, PN）运动方程嵌入训练过程，在单轨道、四维参数空间及含2.5PN领头辐射反作用（radiation reaction）的耗散动力学三个层次上，实现了保守与耗散后牛顿轨道动力学的精确代理建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是为后牛顿双星轨道动力学提供准确且灵活的学习式表示，减少对重复数值积分（numerical integration）的依赖，并探索在训练中无需耗散参考轨迹（dissipative reference trajectories）即可刻画耗散效应的可能性。

Method: 构建重力启发神经网络（gravity-informed neural-network, GravINNs）框架，将运动方程直接纳入训练阶段；分为三个互补层次：保守动力学（conservative dynamics）的单轨道代理模型、覆盖由初始间距（initial separation）、径向与切向动量及对称质量比（symmetric mass ratio）定义的参数模型，以及纳入2.5PN阶领头辐射反作用的耗散动力学模型。

Result: 单轨道代理模型在不同轨道构型下评估了精度；参数模型以单一训练网络表示整族轨道并替代重复数值积分；耗散情形下，网络在不使用耗散参考轨迹训练的情况下，准确再现了轨道演化（orbital evolution）与相关的长期能量损失（secular energy loss）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，物理启发神经网络（physics-informed neural-networks, PINNs）能够为保守与耗散后牛顿动力学提供准确且灵活的表示；完整实现与所用模型通过GravINNs仓库公开。

Abstract: We introduce a gravity-informed neural-network (GravINNs) framework for learning post-Newtonian binary orbital dynamics. The method incorporates the equations of motion directly into the training phase and is developed at three complementary levels. First, we construct single-orbit surrogates for conservative post-Newtonian dynamics and assess their accuracy across different orbital configurations. We then extend the approach to a parametric model spanning a four-dimensional parameter space defined by the initial separation, radial and tangential momenta, and symmetric mass ratio, allowing a single trained network to represent entire families of orbits and replace repeated numerical integrations. Finally, we consider dissipative dynamics by including the leading radiation-reaction contribution at $2.5$PN order. In this regime, the network accurately reproduces both the orbital evolution and the associated secular energy loss without using dissipative reference trajectories in the training. These results show that physics-informed neural-networks (PINNs) can provide accurate and flexible representations of both conservative and dissipative post-Newtonian dynamics. The complete implementation and the models used in this work are publicly available through the \texttt{GravINNs} repository.

</details>


### [88] [Gravitational wave signatures of subsolar neutron star formation in collapsar disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25225)
*Vishal Baibhav,Valerio De Luca,Loris Del Grosso,Emanuele Berti,Brian D. Metzger,Lam Hui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作刻画了坍缩星（collapsar）环境中形成的亚太阳质量双星并合的引力波特征，指出其低组分质量与环境印记的组合可区分该形成通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准恒星核心坍缩模型通常预言中子星质量超过一个太阳质量，而坍缩星模型中盘外部区域碎裂可形成亚太阳质量中子星，其并合信号与形成通道尚待系统刻画。

Method: 围绕坍缩星环境特征，分析环绕中心黑洞轨道运动产生的多普勒及其他相位调制、盘内气体驱动迁移，以及旋进阶段的潮汐相互作用。

Result: 异常低的组分质量与上述环境印记相结合，产生独特的波形特征，可将坍缩星通道与其他亚太阳质量并合情景区分开。

Conclusion: 当前与下一代探测器有望识别此类天体的内禀性质，并在发现亚太阳质量并合时检验和约束坍缩星形成路径。

Abstract: Standard models for stellar core collapse predict the birth of neutron stars with masses exceeding a solar mass. In the collapsar model, a rapidly rotating massive star collapses to a black hole surrounded by a dense, neutrino-cooled accretion disk, whose outer regions may in some cases fragment into neutron-rich clumps that collapse to form subsolar-mass neutron stars. These disk-formed objects can then merge either with one another or, following possibly hierarchical evolution, with the central black hole. In this work, we characterize the gravitational-wave signatures of these binaries, focusing on distinctive features of the collapsar environment: Doppler and other phase modulations from orbital motion about the central black hole, gas-driven migration in the disk, and tidal interactions during inspiral. We show that the combination of anomalously low component masses with these environmental imprints produces unique waveform signatures that can distinguish the collapsar channel from other subsolar merger scenarios. Current and next-generation detectors have good prospects for identifying the intrinsic properties of such objects and, if subsolar mergers are discovered, for testing and constraining the collapsar formation pathway.

</details>


### [89] [Accurate Black Hole Quasinormal Modes, Regge Poles, and Greybody Factors from Light Ring Ladder Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25228)
*Rajes Ghosh,David Pereñiguez,Jaime Redondo-Yuste,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作揭示了黑洞扰动方程在高频极限下的隐藏阶梯对称性（hidden ladder symmetry），将准正规模（quasinormal modes）、Regge极点（Regge poles）与灰体因子（greybody factors）的计算映射为非简谐振子（anharmonic oscillator）量子力学问题，并给出高精度解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞特征振荡可与不稳定光子环（unstable light ring）附近被捕获的零测地线（null rays）建立对应，该对应在高频极限下严格成立；现有近似方案在计算黑洞响应时收敛性有限，亟需更系统高效的框架。

Method: 在高频极限下发现扰动方程中的隐藏阶梯对称性，将球对称黑洞的准正规模、Regge极点与灰体因子计算映射为非简谐振子问题；继而对Schwarzschild黑洞按逆角动量（inverse angular momentum）展开至第十八阶进行解析计算，并考察不同的重求和方案（resummation schemes）。

Result: 对Schwarzschild黑洞解析求得上述三个量至逆角动量第十八阶，结果高度精确；相比既有近似方案具有更好的收敛性质；重求和方案可进一步提升Regge极点、特别是灰体因子的精度。

Conclusion: 该框架为计算黑洞响应提供了系统且高效的途径，并可方便地推广至旋转黑洞（rotating black holes）与修改引力理论（modified theories of gravity）。

Abstract: The characteristic oscillations of a black hole can be understood in terms of null rays trapped near its unstable light ring, with this correspondence becoming exact in the high-frequency limit. In this regime, we uncover a hidden ladder symmetry in the perturbation equations, which maps the calculation of quasinormal modes, Regge poles, and greybody factors for a spherically symmetric black hole onto the quantum-mechanical problem of an anharmonic oscillator. This provides a systematic, efficient framework for computing the black hole response, with improved convergence properties compared with existing approximation schemes. As an explicit application, we analytically compute all three quantities for a Schwarzschild black hole through eighteenth order in inverse angular momentum, obtaining highly accurate results. We explore different resummation schemes and find that they can further improve the accuracy of the Regge poles and, particularly, the greybody factors. Our framework can be readily extended to rotating black holes and to modified theories of gravity.

</details>


### [90] [Higher derivative Hořava-Lifshitz fermions: from superconductivity to atomic spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25236)
*A. A. Araújo Filho,K. E. L. de Farias,N. Heidari,M. Paganelly,A. F. Santos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究带六维空间导数算符的各向同性 Hořava-Lifshitz 费米子理论，在导数展开有效范围内从 z=2 扇区导出超导配对、交换势散射与氢原子能级移动三类结果，并给出对核子-轻子耦合乘积的光谱学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨在保持一阶时间演化的前提下，引入六维空间导数算符如何修改紫外色散关系（ultraviolet dispersion relation），并系统考察 Hořava-Lifshitz 短距离（short distance）扇区在凝聚态配对、散射与原子光谱中的可观测效应。

Method: 采用 BCS/NJL 平均场（mean field）处理费米子配对；以标量极点分母作为最小自旋无关交换核（exchange kernel）推导有质量势与 Born 散射振幅（Born scattering amplitudes）；并闭式（closed form）计算氢型 1S、2S、2P 能级移动，保留对相互作用程的完整依赖。

Result: 得到粒子与反粒子准粒子谱、有限温度能隙方程（gap equation）及稳定超导相的规范不变判据；修改色散同时改变态密度与电磁顶点，从而影响 Meissner 响应。导出指数衰减并振荡的有质量势，四次动量依赖使前向与总截面有限，且大动量转移行为不同于 Rutherford 散射。氢型能级移动给出核子与轻子耦合乘积的光谱学约束，μ子原子对短距离扇区尤为敏感。

Conclusion: z=2 扇区的六维导数修正可同时影响超导配对、交换势散射与原子能级，其中 μ子原子（muonic atoms）提供了对核子与轻子耦合乘积的直接光谱学限制，从而约束 Hořava-Lifshitz 短距离结构。

Abstract: We investigate an isotropic Hořava-Lifshitz fermion theory supplemented by a dimension-six spatial derivative operator, preserving first order time evolution while modifying the ultraviolet dispersion relation. Within the regime of validity of the derivative expansion, we develop three complementary consequences of the $z=2$ sector. First, a Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer/Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (BCS/NJL) mean field treatment of fermion pairing yields the particle and antiparticle quasiparticle spectra, the finite temperature gap equation, and the gauge invariant criterion for a stable superconducting phase. The modified dispersion alters both the density of states governing the pairing scale and the electromagnetic vertices entering the Meissner response. Second, using the scalar pole denominator as a minimal spin independent exchange kernel, we derive an exponentially damped and oscillatory massive potential and obtain the corresponding Born scattering amplitudes. The quartic momentum dependence renders both the forward and total cross sections finite and produces a large transfer behavior distinct from Rutherford scattering. Finally, we evaluate the induced shifts of the hydrogenic $1S$, $2S$, and $2P$ levels in closed form, retaining the full dependence on the interaction range. In other words, these latter results gives direct spectroscopic constraints on the product of nuclear and leptonic couplings, with muonic atoms, showing the respective sensitivity to the short distance Hořava-Lifshitz sector.

</details>


### [91] [Frozen-In Gravitational Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25240)
*Felipe A. Asenjo,Maricarmen A. Winkler,Luca Comisso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文基于与连续介质非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）类比的爱因斯坦方程（Einstein field equations）表述，揭示广义相对论（general relativity）中存在由时空动力学维持连通性的引力场连接结构，并导出守恒的“引力磁”通量与引力螺旋度（gravitational helicity）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空可依据爱因斯坦场方程发生复杂非线性演化，理解其演化过程中产生、持续并相互作用的几何结构是核心挑战。研究动机在于寻找支配这种非线性动力学的组织原则与拓扑约束。

Method: 采用与连续介质非线性电动力学相平行的爱因斯坦方程表述，在此基础上分析引力场的理想欧姆型条件（ideal Ohm-type condition），并考察相应的守恒量构造。

Result: 广义相对论容许引力场连接——即二维曲面（two-surfaces）及与之关联的场线，其连通性由时空动力学维持，表现出引力“冻结”行为；同一框架自然给出守恒的“引力磁”通量（gravitational magnetic flux），并涌现出具有清晰拓扑解释的守恒引力螺旋度。

Conclusion: 这些结果识别出对容许时空演化成立的明确拓扑约束，并为时空非线性动力学提供了一个组织原则。

Abstract: Spacetime can undergo complex nonlinear evolution, as governed by the Einstein field equations, and a central challenge is to understand the geometric structures that arise, persist, and interact throughout its evolution. Using a formulation of Einstein equations that parallels nonlinear electrodynamics of continuous media, we show that general relativity admits gravitational field connections---two-surfaces and associated field lines whose connectivity is maintained by the spacetime dynamics. This gravitational frozen-in behavior is enabled by an ideal Ohm-type condition for the gravitational field. We further show that the same framework naturally leads to a conserved ``gravitational magnetic'' flux. A conserved gravitational helicity also emerges, with a clear topological interpretation in terms of gravitational field-line structures. These results identify well-defined topological constraints on admissible spacetime evolution and provide an organizing principle underlying the nonlinear dynamics of spacetime.

</details>


### [92] [Reconnection of Gravitational Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25241)
*Luca Comisso,Felipe A. Asenjo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在广义相对论（general relativity）的四面体（tetrad）表述下，将引力场类比为理想导体，提出“引力重联（gravitational reconnection）”理论，用以描述引力场片层连通性（connectivity）的破坏及其对能量-动量交换和引力螺旋度（helicity）演化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在引力场的四面体表述中，理想欧姆型条件（Ohm-type condition）意味着引力通量守恒与引力场片层连通性的保持。若该理想“冻结”演化被破坏，其后果尚缺乏定量描述框架，这构成本文研究动机。

Method: 在广义相对论的四面体表述下，将引力重联定义为引力场片层连通性的改变，即理想冻结演化（frozen-in evolution）失效所致；据此构建引力重联理论，并推导重联率的定量度量。

Result: 文中给出了引力重联率的定量度量，表明引力重联会改变引力与物质部分之间的能量-动量交换（energy-momentum exchange），并确定了其对引力螺旋度演化（evolution of gravitational helicity）的修正方式。

Conclusion: 结果为描述引力连通性变化及其在动力学时空（dynamical spacetimes）中的后果提供了定量框架。

Abstract: In a tetrad formulation of general relativity, an ideal Ohm-type condition for the gravitational field implies the conservation of gravitational fluxes and the preservation of the connectivity of gravitational field sheets. We investigate the consequences of departures from ideal gravitational evolution by developing a theory of gravitational reconnection, defined as a change in the connectivity of gravitational field sheets arising from the breakdown of the ideal frozen-in evolution of the gravitational field. We derive quantitative measures of the gravitational reconnection rate, show that gravitational reconnection alters the energy-momentum exchange between the gravitational and matter sectors, and determine how it modifies the evolution of gravitational helicity. Our results provide a quantitative framework for describing changes in gravitational connectivity and their consequences in dynamical spacetimes.

</details>


### [93] [Kerr-Degenerate Shadows and Distinct Strong-Deflection Lensing in Rotating Hayward-like and Bardeen-like Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25258)
*Chen-Hung Hsiao,Limei Yuan,Yidun Wan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在统一的 Kerr 型轴对称拟设下比较旋转 Hayward 型与 Bardeen 型几何，识别视界数目参数域，证明其违反零能量与弱能量条件，并指出阴影边界与 Kerr 完全退化，而强偏折极限量可区分不同几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转正则（regulari）黑洞候选几何多沿用 Kerr 型拟设，但 Hayward 型与 Bardeen 型在相同框架下的系统比较及其可观测差异尚不清晰，需要判定能否借助阴影与强透镜观测量区分它们与 Kerr。

Method: 采用统一的 Kerr 型轴对称拟设（axisymmetric ansatz），以面积半径函数 R_i(ρ) 区分两类模型；在选定径向分支上分析曲率不变量与视界结构，将 Einstein 张量视为有效应力张量检验能量条件，并计算完整光子族的阴影边界以及赤道面顺行强偏折极限（strong-deflection limit, SDL）系数。

Result: 两类几何具有相同渐近 Komar 荷但有限半径处取值不同；对任意非零正则长度 ℓ，该拟设违反零能量与弱能量条件。黑洞分支上阴影边界与同质量、自旋、观测倾角的 Kerr 完全一致，两类正则几何之间也相同；M87* 与 Sgr A* 的面积等效阴影直径与所采用 EHT 区间相符，未给出对 ℓ 的进一步限制。强偏折极限仅部分退化：固定自旋下 u_m^+ 与 θ_∞^+ 与 Kerr 相同，而 ā_+、b̄_+、像间距、相对亮度及次领头时延修正依赖 ℓ 与面积半径剖面。

Conclusion: 阴影观测量无法区分这些旋转正则几何与 Kerr，也不约束 ℓ；但在给定强偏折近似下，ΔT_{2,1}、s^+ 与 r_mag^+ 的未来测量有望检验这些几何，并区分 Hayward 型、Bardeen 型与 Kerr 的预言，包括所采用分支不含 Kerr 型内视界的单视界参数域。

Abstract: We study and compare rotating Hayward-like and Bardeen-like geometries within a common Kerr-like axisymmetric ansatz, with the models distinguished by their areal-radius functions $R_i(ρ)$. On the adopted radial branches, the positive minimum areal radius excludes the Kerr ring locus, and the curvature invariants examined here remain finite. We identify the parameter domains containing two, one, or no horizons. The two geometries have the same asymptotic Komar charges but different finite-radius values. If we treat the Einstein tensor as an effective stress tensor, then for every nonzero regularity length $\ell$, this ansatz violates the null and weak energy conditions, as demonstrated by the equatorial radial-null contraction. For the complete photon family on a black-hole branch, the shadow boundary is identical to that of Kerr at the same mass, spin, and observer inclination, and is also identical for the two regular geometries. The corresponding area-equivalent shadow diameters for M87* and Sgr~A* are consistent with the approximate Event Horizon Telescope (EHT)-based intervals adopted here, and we do not obtain further constraints on $\ell$. By contrast, the prograde equatorial strong-deflection limit (SDL) calculations are only partly degenerate. At fixed spin, $u_m^+$ and $θ_\infty^+$ are common to Kerr and the two regular geometries, whereas $\bar a_+$, $\bar b_+$, the image separation, the relative magnitude, and the subleading time-delay correction depend on $\ell$ and on the areal-radius profile. Within the stated strong-deflection approximation, future measurements of $ΔT_{2,1}$, $s^+$, and $r_{\rm mag}^+$ may help test these rotating geometries and distinguish the Hayward-like and Bardeen-like predictions from Kerr, including in the one-horizon parameter domain where the adopted branch contains no Kerr-like inner horizon.

</details>


### [94] [Circular acceleration in Minkowski spacetime: thermality versus finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25277)
*Cameron R D Bunney,Jorma Louko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在 $2+1$ 维 Minkowski 时空中研究空间限制（spatial confinement）如何改变 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器在圆周轨道上的响应，并针对大边界（large-boundary）情形给出了解析结果与共振峰特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆周运动 Unruh 效应（circular motion Unruh effect）的实验兴趣日益增长，而实际模拟时空系统中必然同时存在边界与环境温度；因此需要理解空间限制对探测器响应的影响。摘要指出圆周运动下的有效温度不仅依赖加速度，还依赖轨道速度与激发能量，故有必要研究受限场中的探测器行为。

Method: 考虑 $2+1$ 维 Minkowski 时空中被圆形边界（circular boundary）限制的无质量标量场（massless scalar field），其初态取真空或热态（thermal state）；由置于圆周轨道上的 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器进行探测，并在大边界（large-boundary）极限下导出探测器响应的解析结果。

Result: 在大边界区域建立了探测器响应的解析结果，并识别出共振峰（resonance peaks）；当场具有环境温度（ambient temperature）时，这些共振峰更为显著。

Conclusion: 空间限制会改变 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器在圆周轨道上的响应，其受限场中的共振结构在存在环境温度时更加突出；该结果针对可检验 Unruh 效应的模拟时空系统（analogue spacetime systems）提出。

Abstract: The Unruh effect predicts that a uniformly linearly accelerated observer with proper acceleration $a$ reacts to the Minkowski vacuum through excitations and de-excitations with the characteristics of a thermal state at temperature $T_U=a/(2π)$. An observer in uniform circular motion will experience similar excitations and de-excitations that we may use to operationally define an effective temperature, which however depends not only on the acceleration but also on the orbital speed and excitation energy. Motivated by the experimental interest in the circular motion Unruh effect, we investigate how spatial confinement modifies the response of an Unruh-DeWitt detector in $2+1$ Minkowski spacetime. We consider a massless scalar field confined within a circular boundary prepared in either the vacuum or a thermal state, probed by an Unruh-DeWitt detector on a circular orbit, a setting that describes proposed analogue spacetime systems for testing the effect, and in which both a boundary and an ambient temperature will necessarily be present. We establish analytic results for the detector response in the large-boundary regime and identify resonance peaks, which are more prominent when the field has an ambient temperature.

</details>


### [95] [Black Hole Perturbation Toolkit: Low frequency and post-Newtonian expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25281)
*Jakob Neef,Chris Kavanagh,Adrian Ottewill*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为黑洞扰动工具箱（BHPT）中 Teukolsky 与 SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics 软件包的配套论文，介绍其新增与改进的弱场解析功能，重点支持后牛顿（post-Newtonian, PN）展开与自作用力计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Teukolsky 方程（Teukolsky equation）的数值求解是黑洞扰动理论的重要工具，但需要补充弱场解析能力，以支持高阶后牛顿自作用力（self-force）计算及小质量比双黑洞波形建模。

Method: 在 BHPT 框架内扩展 Teukolsky 与 SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics 软件包的解析功能，允许独立提取 MST 系数、重整化角动量（renormalized angular momentum）、径向函数振幅、相移等量作为低频/PM 展开，并提供径向函数与非齐次振幅的 PN 展开，以及无穷远与视界处的能量和角动量通量。

Result: 所有量均可在普通笔记本电脑（regular laptops）上以当前最先进精度计算；计算支持特定及任意自旋权重（spin weight）s 与谐波模式数 ℓ、m；软件包同时提供后牛顿自作用力计算工具集并更新了 SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics 的解析功能。

Conclusion: 该工作为小质量比双黑洞波形前沿的高阶后牛顿自作用力密集计算铺路，并为黑洞扰动理论提供广泛适用的低频展开；相关函数已在 Teukolsky v.1.2 与 SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics v.1.1 中发布。

Abstract: The Teukolsky and SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics packages of the black hole perturbation toolkit (BHPT) are one of the main public libraries to compute numerical solutions to the Teukolsky equation and related quantities. This work is a companion paper presenting their new and improved weak-field analytic functionality focusing on post-Newtonian expansions. The Teukolsky package now allows users to independently extract multiple useful quantities, such as the so called MST coefficients, renormalized angular momentum, amplitudes of the radial functions or the phase shift as a low frequency/PM expansion. It also provides PN expansions of the homogeneous radial functions or - for circular orbits in Kerr - inhomogeneous amplitudes, as well as energy and angular momentum fluxes at infinity and the horizon. All quantities can be computed to state of the art precision on regular laptops. It also provides a suite of tools for post-Newtonian self force computations and updates the analytic functionality of the SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics package. Computations are possible both for specific and generic spin weight $s$ and harmonic mode numbers $\ell$ and $m$. This package paves the way for intensive high-order post-Newtonian self-force calculations at the forefront of waveform modelling of small mass ratio black hole binaries, as well as giving widely useful low-frequency expansions for black hole perturbation theory. All functions described in this paper are available in BHPT versions Teukolsky v.1.2 and SpinWeightedSpheroidalHarmonics v.1.1.

</details>


### [96] [Thin-Shell Black Bounce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25354)
*Leandro A. Lessa,Renan B. Magalhães,Gonzalo J. Olmo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出 Lessa–Olmo 提出的黑反弹（black-bounce）解在反弹处隐含薄壳结构，并由此构造一类静态球对称、带有最小面积反弹薄壳的新解；该结构源于反弹处度规退化（metric degeneracy），导致必须存在违反能量条件的 δ 函数薄壳，并进一步讨论其热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lessa 与 Olmo 近期提出的由各向异性流体（anisotropic fluids）支撑的黑反弹解，其外部一直延伸到最小面积反弹处均复原 Reissner-Nordström 几何。本文受"该解恰在反弹处暗含薄壳结构"这一观察启发，意在显式构造以薄壳支撑最小面积反弹的新一类黑反弹解。

Method: 采用静态球对称假设，利用量子引力（quantum gravity）预言的基本最小长度尺度，以有效方式引入编码量子引力效应的能量密度剖面（energy density profiles），将最小长度尺度下物质的点状描述替换为弥散（smeared）描述；具体考察 Lorentzian 型与 Gaussian 型两种剖面来生成新解。

Result: 该非平凡拓扑结构源于反弹处度规退化——逆度规（inverse metric）不再良定义，使曲率张量在该点出现分布型不连续（distributional discontinuities）。其结果是反弹处必然存在一个违反能量条件（energy-condition-violating）物质的 δ 函数薄壳。所构造的解呈现正则黑洞（regular black holes）或虫洞（wormholes）特征，其中心隐藏一个薄壳反弹；同时给出了这些致密天体的热力学（thermodynamics）分析。

Conclusion: 在量子引力启发的最小长度框架下，通过 Lorentzian 与 Gaussian 型弥散能量密度剖面，可得到一类中心为薄壳反弹的紧凑天体解，其性质介于正则黑洞与虫洞之间，并伴随必要的违反能量条件的薄壳及相应热力学行为。

Abstract: A recently proposed class of black-bounce solutions sourced by anisotropic fluids, whose exterior reproduces the Reissner-Nordström geometry all the way down to a minimal-area bounce, was put forward by Lessa and Olmo. In this work, we show that this solution conceals a thin-shell structure precisely at the bounce. Motivated by this observation, we construct a new class of black-bounce solutions with static, spherical symmetry featuring the emergence of a minimal-area bounce supported by a thin shell. This nontrivial topological structure is shown to arise from the degeneracy of the metric at the bounce, where the inverse metric becomes ill-defined, leading to curvature tensors with distributional discontinuities precisely at that point. As a consequence, a delta-function thin shell of energy-condition-violating matter is necessarily present at the bounce. To generate such solutions, we draw on proposals from quantum gravity that predict the existence of a fundamental minimum length scale, employing energy density profiles that encode these quantum-gravitational effects in an effective manner, replacing the point-like description of matter at minimal-length scales with a smeared one. We consider two well-motivated profiles, of Lorentzian and Gaussian type, to generate a novel class of solutions exhibiting the features of either regular black holes or wormholes, whose center conceals a thin-shell bounce. In addition, we investigate the thermodynamics of these compact objects.

</details>


### [97] [Light Cone Thermodynamics: Quasi-Local Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25477)
*Matheus G. Barbosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作面向广义相对论与热力学的内在联系，在一般时空中建立准局域（quasi-local）热力学形式体系，并给出霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）与"不可逆不等式"等结果，进而提出公理化框架以推广经典热力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受黑洞热力学（black hole thermodynamics）及其他暗示广义相对论（general relativity）与热力学存在内在联系的结果启发，旨在在一般时空中建立准局域热力学形式体系，以纳入时空动力学。

Method: 将 Bondi-Sachs 形式体系推广至与任意世界线（worldline）相关联的过去光锥（past light cones）；由该推广通过若干算例展示准局域情形下的霍金辐射；并结合由 Landau-Lifshitz 赝张量（pseudotensor）导出的内能定义，构建公理化（postulational）框架。

Result: 表明在温和条件下即可出现霍金辐射；典型引力系统中满足"不可逆不等式"，提示可建立第二定律（second law）的类比；并可由此从热力学变量定义静止质量密度（rest-mass density）。

Conclusion: 所提出的公理化框架推广了经典热力学以包含时空动力学，并使静止质量密度与组分粒子数（constituent numbers）的动力学解耦。

Abstract: Inspired by black hole thermodynamics and other results suggesting that general relativity has some intrinsic connection with thermodynamics, this work aims to establish a quasi-local thermodynamic formalism in general spacetimes. With this purpose, we generalize the Bondi-Sachs formalism to past light cones associated with an arbitrary worldline. From this generalization, we demonstrate the emission of Hawking radiation in a quasi-local context through a few examples, suggesting that it may occur under mild conditions. In addition, we argue that an "irreversible inequality" is satisfied in typical gravitational systems, indicating that an analogue of the second law may be established. Together with a definition of internal energy derived from the Landau-Lifshitz pseudotensor, these results are used to construct a postulational framework that generalizes classical thermodynamics in order to include the dynamics of spacetime. As a consequence, it is possible to define the rest-mass density from thermodynamic variables, decoupling it from the dynamics of constituent numbers.

</details>


### [98] [Operator-Level Quantum-Classical Correspondence in Relativistic Quantum Theory and Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25360)
*Pankaj Sheoran,Gopal Kashyap,Sanjay Siwach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将算符层面量子-经典对应（operator-level quantum-classical correspondence）从非相对论粒子推广至量子场论与线性化引力，证明多种场方程可在不取 ℏ→0 极限的情况下由海森堡方程（Heisenberg equations）在算符层面复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明非相对论粒子的算符运动方程与牛顿方程（Newton's equation of motion）具有相同微分形式，但该对应能否延伸至量子场论（quantum field theory）与线性化引力（linearized gravity）尚待检验。

Method: 直接计算量子场算符的海森堡方程，考察其是否复现对应经典场方程，并推广至弯曲时空（curved spacetime）；在弱场近似（weak-field approximation）下分析度规微扰（metric perturbations）的算符演化，同时考查弯曲时空中量子粒子的位置与动量算符。

Result: 克莱因-戈尔登方程（Klein-Gordon equation）、麦克斯韦方程组（Maxwell's equations）、杨-米尔斯方程（Yang-Mills equations）与线性化爱因斯坦方程（linearized Einstein equations）均可在算符层面出现，且无需取 ℏ→0 极限；弯曲时空中场算符满足相应协变场方程，度规微扰的海森堡演化复现线性化爱因斯坦方程；量子粒子的位置与动量海森堡方程在半经典极限（semiclassical limit）下复现经典测地线运动（geodesic motion）。

Conclusion: 算符层面的量子-经典对应可从场推广至粒子动力学：量子场算符在算符层面满足经典协变场方程，而粒子层面则需在半经典极限下复现测地线运动，从而形成场与粒子两方面的统一图景。

Abstract: In a recent work \cite{Shaikh:2026nsi}, it was shown that for a non-relativistic particle, the operator equations of motion can be cast in the same differential form as Newton's equation of motion. This establishes a correspondence between quantum operator dynamics and classical mechanics at the level of the equations of motion. In this work, we investigate whether this correspondence extends to quantum field theory and linearized gravity. We explicitly show that the Heisenberg equations of motion for quantum field operators reproduce the corresponding classical equations of motion for a broad class of field theories. The Klein-Gordon equation, Maxwell's equations, Yang-Mills equations, and linearized Einstein equations are shown to arise at the operator level without taking the limit $\hbar\rightarrow0$. We then extend the analysis to curved spacetime and show that the quantum field operators satisfy the corresponding covariant field equations at the operator level. In the weak-field approximation, we show that the Heisenberg evolution of the metric-perturbations reproduces the linearized Einstein equations at the operator level. For a quantum particle in curved spacetime, the Heisenberg equations for position and momentum reproduce classical geodesic motion in the semiclassical limit, thereby extending the operator-level quantum-classical correspondence from fields to particle dynamics.

</details>


### [99] [Cosmology in sixth-order higher-derivative theories of gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25387)
*Carlos Aguilar,Manuel Asorey,Diego Muñoz,Miguel Pardina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文分析含二次曲率不变量（quadratic curvature invariants）\(R^2\)、\(C^{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}C_{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}\) 及六阶导数算符（six-derivative operators）\(R\Box R\)、\(C^{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}\Box C_{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}\) 的引力理论的宇宙学含义，涵盖背景解、微扰稳定性与暴胀观测预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于考察高阶导数引力（higher-derivative gravity）能否通过额外自由度规避 Hawking–Penrose 奇点定理（singularity theorems），并寻找可探测紫外引力修正（ultraviolet gravitational corrections）的宇宙学途径。

Method: 方法包括：求均匀各向同性宇宙学解；在爱因斯坦标架（Einstein-frame）表述中厘清规避奇点定理的机制；分析标准背景上张量微扰（tensor perturbations）的完整线性方程组；在 de Sitter 时空中讨论标量微扰（scalar perturbations）稳定性；并以有效场论（EFT）方式将六阶项视为 Starobinsky 背景上的微扰修正。

Result: 结果显示：理论允许膨胀、再坍缩（recollapsing）及非奇异反弹（non-singular bouncing）解；张量微扰的稳定性由传播子（propagator）极点结构与高阶导数耦合间的关系决定，而无奇点背景对张量微扰不稳定；标量微扰在二次与六阶引力中均出现超视界（super-horizon）不稳定性；六阶算符对原初标量与张量功率谱（power spectra）给出受控偏离，并得到标量谱指数与张量—标量比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）的预测。

Conclusion: 结论为：六阶算符在保持 EFT 展开有效的前提下产生对 Starobinsky 模型的受控修正，从而提供一套自洽的现象学框架，用以通过宇宙学观测量检验紫外引力修正。

Abstract: We analyze the cosmological implications of theories of gravity with quadratic curvature invariants $R^2$ and $C^{μνρσ}C_{μνρσ}$, and six-derivative operators $R\Box R$ and $C^{μνρσ}\Box C_{μνρσ}$. We first analyze homogeneous and isotropic cosmological solutions, showing that the theory admits expanding, recollapsing and non-singular bouncing solutions. We clarify in the Einstein-frame formulation the mechanism that circumvents the Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems, and identify the role played by the additional higher-derivative degrees of freedom in that mechanism. We show that the complete set of linear equations governing tensor perturbations on standard backgrounds admit analytically stable solutions. The analysis reveals that the stability properties are determined by the pole structure of the propagator and by specific relations among the higher-derivative couplings, while the singularity-free backgrounds are found to be unstable against tensor perturbations. For scalar cosmological perturbations, the higher-derivative theories presents super-horizon scalar instabilities in de Sitter spacetimes for both quadratic and sixth-order gravity. To extract inflationary observables, we adopt an EFT approach to the sixth-order theory, treating these terms as perturbative corrections around the Starobinsky background. Within this framework, we compute the modification of the primordial scalar and tensor power spectra, obtaining predictions for the scalar spectral index and tensor-to-scalar ratio. We find that sixth-order operators produce controlled deviations from the Starobinsky model while preserving the EFT expansion, thereby providing a consistent phenomenological framework to probe ultraviolet gravitational corrections through cosmological observables.

</details>


### [100] [A Systematic Study of Resonance-Driven Flux Modifications in Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25392)
*Edoardo Levati,Lennox S. Keeble,Alejandro Cárdenas-Avendaño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作量化了极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）中瞬态轨道共振对能量、轴向角动量与卡特常数通量的修正，并在大范围轨道参数空间内计算共振系数，给出了迄今规模最大的基于 Teukolsky 方程的共振系数集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瞬态轨道共振（transient orbital resonances）会对极端质量比旋进（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, EMRIs）的演化引入相位依赖修正，可能改变其长期动力学与引力波信号，因此需定量刻画共振引起的通量修正。

Method: 使用公开的 \texttt{pybhpt} 代码，在频域求解 Teukolsky 方程，以相干组合共振处退化的径向与极向谐波；并解析推导支配共振通量修正的相对径向-极向相位依赖的选择定则。

Result: 计算了能量、轴向角动量（axial angular momentum）与卡特常数（Carter constant）通量的共振修正及对应共振系数，覆盖广泛轨道参数空间；对动力学上重要的 3:2 与 2:1 共振，刻画了共振系数随主黑洞自旋、轨道偏心率和倾角的变化。

Conclusion: 不同共振的相对强度反映了对称性诱导的相消与共振轨道对二维轨道相空间采样程度之间的平衡；所得结果构成迄今最大的基于 Teukolsky 的共振系数集合，为未来 EMRIs 瞬态轨道共振研究提供关键输入。

Abstract: Transient orbital resonances can introduce phase-dependent corrections to the evolution of extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), potentially altering their long-term dynamics and emitted gravitational-wave signals. In this work, we quantify the resonance-induced modifications to the energy, axial angular momentum, and Carter constant fluxes and compute the corresponding resonance coefficients across a broad region of the orbital parameter space. Using the publicly available \texttt{pybhpt} code, we solve the Teukolsky equation in the frequency domain to coherently combine the degenerate radial and polar harmonics that arise at resonance. We analytically derive a selection rule governing the relative radial-polar phase dependence of the resonant flux modifications. We argue that the relative strengths of different resonances reflect a balance between symmetry-induced cancellations and the degree to which the resonant orbit samples the underlying two-dimensional orbital phase space. For the dynamically important $3{:}2$ and $2{:}1$ resonances, we also characterize how the resonance coefficients vary with the primary black-hole spin, orbital eccentricity and inclination. Our results constitute the largest set of Teukolsky-based resonance coefficients calculated to date and provide essential input for future studies of transient orbital resonances in EMRIs.

</details>
