### [101] [Analytical and spectral characterization of floral patterns in higher-order polynomial pp-waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25459)
*F. L. Carneiro,H. P. de Carvalho,L. A. Cabral,M. P. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文解析与数值研究了 pp-波（pp-wave）高阶多项式模式对粒子环产生的“花形形变（floral deformation）”。横向潮汐本征方向决定 m 个径向拉伸与压缩交错的扇区，弱脉冲解给出环的一阶完整响应，测地线积分验证了取向与离散对称性，傅里叶分析表明响应由角模式 n=m 主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 pp-波高阶多项式模式如何通过横向潮汐效应引起粒子环的形变，并厘清其角向模式成分与记忆效应的来源。

Method: 方法包括解析求解弱脉冲（weak-pulse）下环的一阶响应，以及对 m=2,3,4 直接进行测地线积分（geodesic integration）以作验证，并辅以傅里叶分析（Fourier analysis）。

Result: 结果显示：横向潮汐本征方向（transverse tidal eigendirections）确定 m 个径向拉伸与压缩交错扇区；测地线积分证实了预测的取向与离散对称性（discrete symmetry）；响应由角模式 n=m 压倒性主导，m=4 的环状结构源于基模的几何折叠（geometrical folding）而非强谐波混合；脉冲的时间矩（temporal moments）可区分位移记忆与速度记忆，但不改变角向特征。

Conclusion: 结论：pp-波高阶多项式模式诱导的花形形变主要由单一角模式 n=m 主导，高阶谐波混合作用微弱，位移记忆与速度记忆可通过脉冲时间矩区分而不影响该角向模式特征。

Abstract: We investigate analytically and numerically the floral deformation of particle rings induced by higher-order polynomial modes of pp-waves. The transverse tidal eigendirections determine $m$ interlaced sectors of radial stretching and compression, while a weak-pulse solution provides the complete first-order response of the ring. Direct geodesic integration for $m=2,3,4$ confirms the predicted orientation and discrete symmetry. A Fourier analysis shows that the response remains overwhelmingly dominated by the angular mode $n=m$; in particular, the loops found for $m=4$ arise from geometrical folding of the fundamental mode rather than strong harmonic mixing. The temporal moments of the pulse further distinguish displacement from velocity memory without changing this angular signature.

</details>


### [102] [Quadratic Gravitational-Wave Scattering by Kerr Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25522)
*Lennox S. Keeble,Hengrui Zhu,Lawrence E. Kidder,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Mark A. Scheel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用数值相对论与半解析方法，研究Kerr黑洞对近单色(2,±2)入射引力波的二阶响应，测量其对(4,4)子模的复自耦合，并识别出与(4,4,0)准正规模对应的子模共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶黑洞微扰理论是下一代探测器进行精确引力波建模与强场引力检验的重要组成。除准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）之外，一般推迟解还包含实数频率散射态连续谱，而后者在Kerr时空中的非线性相互作用研究相对不足。

Method: 采用数值相对论（numerical relativity）模拟Kerr黑洞对近单色(ℓ,m)=(2,±2)入射引力波的二次响应，测量其在两倍母频处向出射(4,4)子模的复自耦合，自旋至a=0.95；并辅以二阶Teukolsky半解析计算进行对照。

Result: 低频下耦合被强烈压低，且顺行与逆行散射呈现相反的自旋依赖；较高顺行频率处出现子模共振，其频率与宽度与(4,4,0)基频QNM一致；在(2,2,0)QNM频率附近，入射模耦合随自旋增强，与递减的二次QNM耦合相反。半解析二阶Teukolsky计算复现了数值结果。

Conclusion: 非线性响应取决于完整的母模散射态而非仅其频率；该结果及其半解析推广为Kerr在二阶下的普适齐次辐射微扰提供基础，可应用于动力学潮汐、近极端自旋动力学与二阶引力自作用力理论。

Abstract: Second-order black-hole perturbation theory will be an important component of precision gravitational-wave modeling and tests of strong-field gravity with next-generation detectors. Beyond quasinormal modes (QNMs), whose quadratic interactions have been the focus of recent work in black-hole spectroscopy, a generic retarded solution consists of a continuum of real-frequency scattering states whose nonlinear interactions in Kerr remain comparatively less explored. We use numerical relativity to study the quadratic response of Kerr black holes to nearly monochromatic $(\ell,m)=(2,\pm2)$ incident gravitational waves, measuring the complex self-coupling to the outgoing $(\ell,m)=(4,4)$ daughter at twice the parent frequency for black-hole spins up to $a=0.95$. At low frequencies, the coupling is strongly suppressed and exhibits opposite spin dependence for prograde and retrograde scattering. At higher prograde frequencies, we identify a daughter-mode resonance whose frequency and width extracted from the nonlinear response track the fundamental $(\ell,m,n)=(4,4,0)$ QNM. Near the fundamental $(2,2,0)$ QNM frequency, the in-mode coupling grows with spin, in contrast to the decreasing quadratic QNM coupling, demonstrating that the nonlinear response depends on the full parent scattering state rather than on its frequency alone. A complementary semi-analytic second-order Teukolsky calculation reproduces the nonlinear response from our numerical relativity simulations. Our numerical-relativity results and their semi-analytic extension provide a basis for generic homogeneous radiative perturbations of Kerr at second order, with applications to dynamical tides, near-extremal dynamics, and second-order gravitational self-force theory.

</details>


### [103] [Axial Perturbations of Charged Kiselev Black Holes in Rastall Gravity: Matter Response and Quasinormal Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25565)
*Yan Wang,Yun-tao Gu,Wen-Di Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究 Rastall 引力（Rastall gravity）中、被尘埃型 Kiselev 各向异性物质包围的带电黑洞的轴向引力电磁准正规模（gravitoelectromagnetic quasinormal modes）。系统在特定闭合假设下化为两个耦合薛定谔型方程，其代数解耦一般被 Rastall 修正与包围物质的联合效应阻断；数值结果表明 Rastall 耦合与黑洞电荷、物质贡献对振荡频率与阻尼率有不同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察 Rastall 引力修正与周围各向异性物质（Kiselev 型尘埃）对带电黑洞轴向准正规模的联合影响，并检验耦合方程组能否代数解耦，以厘清修改引力与物质环境对振荡与阻尼特性的作用。

Method: 采用忽略各向异性方向扰动的闭合假设，将系统约化为两个耦合的薛定谔型方程（Schrödinger-type equations）；在 Reissner–Nordström 与广义相对论尘埃极限下存在与 r 无关的代数解耦，但两者同时存在时解耦一般受阻。引入辅助的冻结源截断（frozen-source truncation）作为比较，并用切比雪夫伪谱法（Chebyshev pseudospectral calculation）求解，通过 RN 极限与谱收敛测试进行验证。

Result: 轴向偶极情形下，物质响应系统呈单向耦合，而截断参考系统呈双向耦合，但两者代表性基频差异仅在亚百分比（sub-percent）水平。小 γ 区增大无量纲 Rastall 耦合 γ 会同时降低振荡频率与阻尼率；大 γ 区振荡频率上升而阻尼率非单调变化。较大的黑洞电荷总体上提高振荡频率，而更强的周围物质贡献倾向于产生更长寿命的模式；所有考察的基频轴向模式均满足 Imω&lt;0。

Conclusion: Rastall 修正与 Kiselev 型各向异性物质对带电黑洞轴向准正规模的耦合效应一般无法代数解耦，但冻结源截断仍是良好的近似。Rastall 耦合、黑洞电荷与周围物质强度对振荡频率与阻尼率呈现不同乃至非单调的影响，且所考察模式均为稳定衰减（Imω&lt;0）。

Abstract: We investigate axial gravitoelectromagnetic quasinormal modes of a charged black hole surrounded by dust-like Kiselev-type anisotropic matter in Rastall gravity. Under a closure prescription that neglects perturbations of the anisotropy direction, the system reduces to two coupled Schrödinger-type equations. These equations admit an $r$-independent algebraic decoupling in the Reissner--Nordström and general-relativistic dust-like limits, but such a decoupling is generically obstructed when both the Rastall modification and the surrounding-matter contribution are present. For comparison, we introduce an auxiliary frozen-source truncation, whose potential matrix differs from the matter-response matrix in only two entries. For the axial dipole, the matter-response system has one-way coupling, whereas the truncated reference system has two-way coupling. Nevertheless, representative fundamental frequencies of the matter-response system and the reference system differ only at the sub-percent level. A Chebyshev pseudospectral calculation, validated through the Reissner--Nordström limit and spectral-convergence tests, shows that increasing the dimensionless Rastall coupling $γ$ lowers both the oscillation frequency and the damping rate in the small-$γ$ region. In the large-$γ$ region, the oscillation frequency rises, whereas the damping rate varies nonmonotonically. A larger black-hole charge generally raises the oscillation frequency, whereas stronger surrounding-matter contributions tend to produce longer-lived modes. All surveyed fundamental axial modes satisfy $\operatorname{Im}ω<0$.

</details>


### [104] [Lensing Signatures of a Primary Hair Black Hole Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25667)
*Rahul Das,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在引力解耦（gravitational decoupling, GD）框架下研究原初毛发黑洞（primary hair black hole, BH）在弱场与强场下的引力透镜效应，并在弱场得到二阶偏折角、强场分析光子球附近的对数发散及相对论像，最后以 Sgr A* 为透镜计算强透镜可观测量并用 EHT 阴影观测约束参数 Q。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察由引力解耦方法构造、且带有原初毛发特征的黑洞解在引力透镜方面的可观测行为，以检验其与施瓦西时空（Schwarzschild geometry）的偏离及其潜在天文观测效应。

Method: 该方法以施瓦西几何为种子时空，通过引入几何形变并施加主导能量条件（dominant energy condition）确定形变函数，从而获得含原初毛发 Q 与耦合常数 α 的黑洞解；弱场采用 Gibbons-Werner 方法，强场采用 Bozza 形式体系，并基于光子球附近偏折角计算强透镜可观测量。

Result: 弱场极限下推导出含二阶修正的偏折角，发现耦合参数 α 对领头阶偏折的贡献大于原初毛发参数 Q，且原初毛发表现出有效排斥性引力行为；强场下光子球附近偏折角呈对数发散，光子可绕黑洞多圈形成相对论像，并以 Sgr A* 为透镜给出渐近角位置 ϑ∞、角间距 s 与相对星等 r_mag 等强透镜可观测量。

Conclusion: 结论为：该原初毛发黑洞的透镜特征依赖于参数 Q 与 α，利用事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）合作组对 Sgr A* 的阴影观测，可在不同 α 取值下对原初毛发参数 Q 施加约束。

Abstract: We investigate the weak and strong field gravitational lensing of light by a primary hair black hole (BH) solution obtained within the framework of the gravitational decoupling (GD) approach. In this method, the BH solution is constructed by introducing geometric deformations to a known seed spacetime, which, in the present case, is the Schwarzschild geometry. The deformation functions are determined by imposing the dominant energy condition, leading to the emergence of two characteristic parameters, the primary hair $Q$ and the coupling constant $α$, both of which deform the Schwarzschild spacetime. To explore the lensing properties of this BH, we employ the Gibbons-Werner approach in the weak field regime and Bozza's formalism in the strong field regime. In the weak field limit, we derive the deflection angle up to second-order corrections and find that the coupling parameter $α$ contributes more significantly to the leading-order deflection than the primary hair parameter $Q$. The primary hair is also found to exhibit an effectively repulsive gravitational behavior. In the strong field regime, we compute the deflection angle of photons propagating near the photon sphere, where the deflection diverges logarithmically, allowing photons to complete multiple loops around the BH and form relativistic images. Using the supermassive BH Sgr A* as the astrophysical lens, we further investigate the strong lensing observables, namely the asymptotic angular position $\vartheta_{\infty}$, the angular separation $s$, and the relative magnitude $r_{\mathrm{mag}}$. We also constrain the primary hair parameter $Q$ for different values of the coupling constant $α$ using the shadow observations of Sgr A* reported by the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) Collaboration.

</details>


### [105] [Thermodynamics of arbitrary spherical surfaces in FLRW spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25763)
*Xun Gong,Jun Wang,Chen-Yang Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在广义相对论（general relativity）框架下研究了非平坦 FLRW 时空中任意球对称曲面的热力学，证明克劳修斯关系（Clausius relation）与统一第一定律（unified first law）可直接由引力场方程与物质动力学导出，且宇宙学视界热力学为其特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究中宇宙学事件视界（cosmological event horizon）的热力学存在不一致性，本文旨在澄清这些矛盾的来源，并检验引力-热力学对应（gravity-to-thermodynamics perspective）是否为因果视界（causal horizons）所独有。

Method: 采用引力-热力学对应视角，在非平坦 FLRW 时空中考察任意球对称曲面；以由能量-动量守恒（energy-momentum conservation）导出的物质通量测度（matter-flux measure）为核心，从引力场方程与物质动力学直接推导克劳修斯关系与统一第一定律。

Result: 宇宙学表观视界（apparent horizon）与事件视界的热力学均作为特例被重现；由引力动力学导出的事件视界温度无需引入唯象修正即可满足两个热力学关系，并在 de Sitter 极限下退化为 Gibbons-Hawking 温度。先前的不一致源于温度规定（temperature prescriptions）不相容，而非宇宙学事件视界热力学本身的内在缺陷。

Conclusion: 引力-热力学对应是引力动力学的一般特征（generic feature），而非因果视界所独有的性质。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the thermodynamics of arbitrary spherically symmetric surfaces in non-flat FLRW spacetimes within general relativity. Adopting the gravity-to-thermodynamics perspective, the Clausius relation and the unified first law follow directly from the gravitational field equations and matter dynamics, with a central role played by the matter-flux measure derived from energy-momentum conservation. The cosmological apparent-horizon and event-horizon thermodynamics are both recovered as special cases. In particular, the event-horizon temperatures derived from the gravitational dynamics satisfy both thermodynamic relations without introducing phenomenological modifications, and the corresponding temperature reduces to the Gibbons-Hawking temperature in the de Sitter limit. We further show that previous inconsistencies originate from incompatible temperature prescriptions rather than from any intrinsic deficiency in the thermodynamics of the cosmological event horizon. These results demonstrate that the gravity-thermodynamics correspondence is a generic feature of gravitational dynamics rather than a property unique to causal horizons.

</details>


### [106] [Radiation-pressure induced chaos in multipendular Fabry-Perot resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25872)
*V. Pierro,I. M. Pinto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究展示了无光传播延迟的多摆法布里-珀罗谐振器（multipendular FP resonators）中混沌（chaos）的出现，并估计了从稳定平衡点邻域初值出发在给定时间内演化出混沌解的概率及其对关键系统参数的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注多摆法布里-珀罗谐振器在不含光传播延迟情形下的混沌行为，并将其与干涉式引力波天线（interferometric gravitational wave antennas）的潜在相关性联系起来。

Method: 以稳定平衡点邻域内的初始条件为出发点，估计在给定时间内演化出混沌解的概率，并考察该概率对最相关系统参数的依赖关系。

Result: 结果表明，在无光传播延迟的多摆 FP 谐振器中会出现混沌；混沌解的出现概率随系统参数变化而改变，相关依赖关系已被刻画。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了上述混沌结果对干涉式引力波天线可能具有的相关意义。

Abstract: The occurrence of chaos in multipendular FP resonators (without light propagation delay) is illustrated. The probability of evolving a chaotic solution in a given time, starting from initial conditions contained in a neighbourhood of a stable equilibrium point, is estimated, and its dependence on the most relevant system parameters is investigated. The possible relevance of these results for interferometric gravitational wave antennas is discussed.

</details>


### [107] [Spacetimes of maximal proper acceleration and muon mean lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25903)
*Ricardo Gallego Torromé*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文论证在存在最大固有加速度（maximal proper acceleration）的时空中，μ子（muon）的平均寿命（mean lifetime）会缩短；对不同候选最大固有加速度，修正量均较小，但若将其取为施温格加速度（Schwinger's acceleration），则效应可在未来μ子对撞机（muon collider）设施中被探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注最大固有加速度这一时空假设对粒子寿命的可观测影响，并以μ子平均寿命作为检验对象。其动机在于寻找可在实验上区分该假设的效应，并评估未来μ子对撞机设施对其检验的可行性。

Method: 摘要未给出具体推导细节，仅说明作者考虑了几种最大固有加速度（maximal proper acceleration）的候选取值，并据此计算其对μ子（muon）平均寿命（mean lifetime）所产生的诱导修正（induced corrections）。

Result: 结果显示，对所考虑的全部候选值，诱导修正均为小量；但当最大固有加速度取为施温格加速度（Schwinger's acceleration）时，该效应在未来μ子对撞机（muon collider）设施中是可探测的（detectable）。

Conclusion: 最大固有加速度时空会导致μ子平均寿命缩短，该效应在一般情况下微弱，但在施温格加速度取值下具备实验可探测性，因而未来μ子对撞机可作为检验此类时空假设的潜在平台。

Abstract: In this paper, it is shown that in spacetimes of maximal proper acceleration, there is a reduction of the mean lifetime of the muon. We consider several candidates for the maximal proper acceleration. For all of them, the induced corrections are small, but for the case when the maximal proper acceleration is identified with the Schwinger's acceleration, the effect is detectable in future muon collider facilities.

</details>


### [108] [Gravitational Imprints of Dark Energy in Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25910)
*O. P. Jyothilakshmi,Lakshmi J. Naik,V. Sreekanth*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次采用完全广义相对论（general relativity）方法研究具有暗能量（dark energy, DE）核心的中子星（neutron star, NS）的非径向 f 模振荡（f-mode oscillation），并系统计算星体结构、潮汐形变（tidal deformability）及引力波（gravitational wave, GW）能量与应变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗能量核心是否以及如何影响中子星的动力学振荡与引力波辐射，此前缺乏完全广义相对论框架下的系统性研究。

Method: 核物质采用相对论平均场模型（relativistic mean field model），暗能量采用类修正 Chaplygin 流体（modified Chaplygin fluid）描述；通过改变暗能量状态方程（equation of state, EoS）参数，计算恒星结构、f 模振荡、潮汐形变以及引力波能量与应变。

Result: 暗能量的引入使状态方程软化并降低最大质量；所得质量-半径关系与 PSR J0030+0451、PSR J0740+6620 等大质量脉冲星的观测约束一致；暗能量核心的范围由转变密度（transition density）决定，较低转变密度可在更宽质量范围内产生显著变化；f 模频率与阻尼时间在高质量组态下呈系统性修正，而 f 模频率与潮汐形变的相关性与 GW170817、GW190814 的观测约束保持一致；归一化振荡能量分布近乎普适，仅在高质量组态出现极小偏离。

Conclusion: 暗能量核心显著影响中子星的结构与振荡性质，其 f 模特征频率所对应的引力波应变预计落在未来第三代引力波探测器的灵敏度波段内，具备潜在可探测性。

Abstract: We investigate, for the first time, the non-radial $f$-mode oscillations of neutron stars (NSs) with a dark energy (DE) core utilising a fully general relativistic treatment. We construct the stellar profile by considering a relativistic mean field model for nuclear matter and a modified Chaplygin fluid like prescription for DE. We compute the stellar structure, $f$-mode oscillations, tidal deformability, and gravitational wave (GW) energy and strain of the NS with DE core by varying the DE equation of state (EoS) parameters. Our study reveals that the inclusion of DE softens EoS and reduces the maximum mass compared to the pure neutron star. The obtained mass-radius values are consistent with observational constraints from massive pulsars such as PSR J0030+0451 and PSR J0740+6620. We find that the extent of the DE core, determined by the transition density, plays a crucial role in modifying the stellar structure and oscillation properties, with lower transition densities producing appreciable changes over a wider mass range. The $f$-mode frequencies and damping times exhibit systematic modifications, for higher mass configurations, while the correlation between the $f$-mode frequency and tidal deformability remains consistent with observational constraints from GW170817 and GW190814. Further, the normalised oscillation energy distribution remains nearly universal, with only minimal deviations for the higher mass configurations. Finally, we estimate the characteristic GW strain associated with the $f$-mode oscillations and find that the predicted signals lie within the sensitivity band of future third generation GW detectors.

</details>


### [109] [Weakly nonlinear strong-field signatures of spontaneously vectorized Einstein-vector-Gauss-Bonnet black holes near the Schwarzschild bifurcation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26266)
*Sardor Murodov,Bekzod Rahmatov,Islom Egamberdiev,Dilnavoz Kamalova,Javlon Rayimbaev,Bobomurat Ahmedov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在Schwarzschild分岔点附近研究自发矢量化Einstein-vector-Gauss-Bonnet黑洞的静态球对称电场分支，通过弱非线性展开给出临界耦合、高阶度规修正及一系列强场几何量的领头修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自发矢量化黑洞分支在分岔点附近的局部强场几何尚缺乏系统刻画，需要确定临界耦合并量化该分支相对Schwarzschild解的偏离。

Method: 以基本矢量零模（vector zero mode）为中心构建弱非线性展开；用打靶法（shooting）与配点法（collocation）独立确定临界耦合；将二阶度规修正与未约化的协变方程对比校验；由三阶Fredholm可解性条件（Fredholm solvability condition）导出分支对分岔点的领头偏离；并据此度规计算光子球、阴影尺度、边缘束缚轨道、最内稳定圆轨道、轨道与周转频率、3:2频率比半径、圆光子轨道不稳定性及对数强偏折系数等量的领头修正。

Result: 对应的ADM质量修正被解析地证明为零。近分岔处光子球与最内稳定轨道向外移动，而3:2频率比半径向内移动；光子轨道角频率下降，其Lyapunov指数增大。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了电场矢量化分支的局部强场几何，但作者明确声明其不构成观测约束，也不外推至强非线性区域。

Abstract: We study the static, spherically symmetric electric branch of spontaneously vectorized Einstein-vector-Gauss-Bonnet black holes close to its Schwarzschild bifurcation. A weakly nonlinear expansion is built around the fundamental vector zero mode. The critical coupling is determined independently by shooting and collocation, the second-order metric correction is checked against the unreduced covariant equations, and the corresponding ADM-mass correction is shown to vanish analytically. The leading departure of the vectorized branch from the bifurcation point follows from a third-order Fredholm solvability condition. Using the resulting metric, we calculate the leading corrections to the photon sphere, shadow scale, marginally bound orbit, innermost stable circular orbit, orbital and epicyclic frequencies, the geometric three-to-two frequency-ratio radius, the instability of the circular photon orbit, and the logarithmic strong-deflection coefficient. Near the bifurcation, the photon sphere and innermost stable orbit move outward, whereas the three-to-two radius moves inward. The photon-orbit angular frequency decreases and its Lyapunov exponent increases. These results characterize the local strong-field geometry of the electric vectorized branch. They are not intended as observational constraints or as an extrapolation to the strongly nonlinear regime.

</details>


### [110] [Area-product universality in multi-horizon black hole entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26289)
*Francisco Tello-Ortiz,Y. Gómez-Leyton,Jean Báez Cuevas,Emmanuel N. Saridakis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了黑洞熵（black-hole entropy）与多视界面积乘积关系（multi-horizon area-product relations）之间此前未被察觉的结构性联系：通过对多个 Killing 视界（Killing horizons）进行统计离散化，导出一个一般面积乘积规则，并表明对数熵修正可继承经典面积乘积的质量无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞熵与多视界面积乘积关系表面上分属微观统计与经典几何两种不同的普适性（universality）表现。论文旨在探究二者是否存在内在联系，特别是对数修正（logarithmic correction）是否携带关于全局视界结构的信息。

Method: 将最小假设的统计离散化（minimum-assumptions statistical discretization）推广至多个 Killing 视界，并把不同视界扇区（horizon sectors）视为统计独立；据此推导一般面积乘积规则，并在 Reissner-Nordström、Kerr-Newman（基于面积的转动视界扩展）及 Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter 情形中进行检验。

Result: 领头阶 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵（Bekenstein-Hawking entropy）探测视界面积之和，而对数修正探测其乘积，因此当经典面积乘积与质量无关时，对数熵自动继承该普适性。该结论在 Reissner-Nordström 与 Kerr-Newman 中得到验证；但 Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter 提供三视界反例，其面积乘积规则仍成立，而物理视界的质量无关性一般失效。

Conclusion: 统计性的面积乘积选择与经典普适性属于不同性质；微观熵计数与经典多视界几何之间存在结构性联系，该联系在领头阶面积定律层面不可见，表明对数修正编码了关于全局视界结构的独特信息。

Abstract: Black-hole entropy and multi-horizon area-product relations represent two apparently distinct manifestations of universality, arising respectively from microscopic statistics and classical geometry. We show that these structures are unexpectedly connected. Extending a minimum-assumptions statistical discretization to multiple Killing horizons, with distinct horizon sectors treated as statistically independent, we derive a general area-product rule. In particular, while the leading Bekenstein-Hawking entropy probes the sum of the horizon areas, the logarithmic correction probes their product. Consequently, whenever the classical area product is mass independent, the logarithmic entropy automatically inherits this universality. We demonstrate this for Reissner-Nordström and, under an area-based extension to rotating horizons, Kerr-Newman black holes. However, Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter provides a crucial three-horizon counterexample, for which the area-product rule survives while mass independence is generically lost for the physical horizons. Thus, statistical area-product selection and classical universality are distinct properties. Our results uncover a structural connection between microscopic entropy counting and classical multi-horizon geometry that is invisible at the level of the leading area law, suggesting that logarithmic corrections encode distinctive information about the global horizon structure.

</details>


### [111] [Potential-space geometry and scalar rigidity of the Plebański-Demiański ansatz without a cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26391)
*Leonel Bixano,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在零宇宙学常数的稳态轴对称爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-伸缩子理论中，于共形 Carter 类内采用广义 Ernst 方法，证明二次 Stäckel 标量 1-形式类内不存在具有普通伸缩子的局部非常数解；该结论不适用于幽灵标量、更高次共形因子或非 Stäckel 1-形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在刻画零宇宙学常数下稳态轴对称爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-伸缩子（Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton, EMD）理论在共形 Carter 类（conformal Carter class）中的局部解结构，特别是厘清电磁错位、加速度贡献与伸缩子耦合对解存在性的限制。

Method: 在势空间上采用广义 Ernst 方法（generalized Ernst approach），建立 Plebański-Demiański 主坐标与 Weyl 数据（含电、磁、扭转势及 1-形式 A、B、C）的局部对应；通过无源 Weyl 约化中不变量面积密度调和条件分离 Λ=0 扇区，并进行主手征分裂；随后推导一般局部 EMD 构型的爱因斯坦重构方程，并逐层施加 Carter 域、标量场方程与二次 Stäckel 条件。

Result: 在普通标量的物理 Carter 域中，双线性因子 Ω=1−αpq 强制 C=0 与 B₋=0；固定 α₀≠0 时标量场方程进一步排除带电 PD 库仑型 1-形式；假设精确二次 Stäckel 标量 1-形式时，精确性与重构条件将系统限制到单一对齐分支，但与完整势方程的一致性对该分支对任意光滑 Carter 结构函数均不成立。

Conclusion: 在所研究的二次 Stäckel 类内，不存在具有普通伸缩子的局部非常数解。该结论不延拓至幽灵标量（phantom scalars）、更高次共形因子或非 Stäckel 1-形式。

Abstract: We analyze stationary, axisymmetric Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory with zero cosmological constant in the conformal Carter class, employing a generalized Ernst approach on the space of potentials. We begin by establishing a complete local correspondence between Plebański-Demiański principal coordinates and Weyl data, covering the electric, magnetic, and twist potentials as well as the 1-forms \(A\), \(B\), and \(C\). In the source-free Weyl reduction, requiring the invariant area density to be harmonic singles out the \(Λ=0\) sector, and the principal chiral splitting isolates both electromagnetic misalignment and the part due to acceleration. Next, we obtain the Einstein reconstruction equations for general local EMD configurations in this setting. In a physical Carter domain with an ordinary scalar, the bilinear factor \(\Om=1-\acc pq\) enforces \(C=0\) and \(B_-=0\). With fixed \(\alphao\neq0\), the scalar field equation further rules out the charged PD Coulomb-type 1-form. Lastly, assuming an exact quadratic Stäckel scalar 1-form, the conditions of exactness together with reconstruction restrict the system to a single aligned branch, but consistency with the full potential equations removes this branch for arbitrary smooth Carter structure functions. Consequently, within the quadratic Stäckel class studied here, there is no local nonconstant solution with an ordinary dilaton. This conclusion does not extend to phantom scalars, conformal factors of higher degree, or non-Stäckel 1-forms.

</details>


### [112] [Photon sphere for a dyonic black hole corresponding to $A_2$ Toda chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26421)
*Vladimir D. Ivashchuk,Evgeny E. Trubach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维引力模型中带两个电荷的膨胀双荷子黑洞（dilatonic dyon black hole），分析其零测地线圆轨道、光子球半径与黑洞阴影（black hole shadow）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含一个标量场（scalar field）与一个2-形式（2-form）的四维引力模型框架下，膨胀双荷子黑洞的解与 A_2 Toda 链（Toda chain）相关，其光子球（photon sphere）与阴影（shadow）性质值得系统刻画。

Method: 考虑引力半径为 2μ、具电荷 Q_1（电）与 Q_2（磁）的膨胀双荷子黑洞解，膨胀子耦合常数（dilaton coupling constant）λ 由 λ² = 3/2 固定；推导并分析控制光子球半径 R_0 的五阶多项式主方程（master equation），并计算零测地线圆轨道。

Result: 该五阶多项式主方程被证明存在满足 R_0 > 2μ 的唯一解；圆轨道零测地线（circular null geodesics）被证明是不稳定的；进一步得到阴影角（shadow angle）与临界碰撞参数（critical impact parameter）的关系式。

Conclusion: 该膨胀双荷子黑洞在给定耦合下具有唯一的光子球半径且其零测地线圆轨道不稳定，黑洞阴影可由阴影角与临界碰撞参数的关系式描述。

Abstract: We consider a dilatonic dyon black hole solution with a gravitational radius of $2 μ$ and two charges,
  $Q_1$ (electric) and $Q_2$ (magnetic), within the framework of a four-dimensional gravitational model comprising one scalar field and one 2-form. The dilaton coupling constant $λ$ is fixed by $λ^2 = \frac{3}{2}$.
  This solution is related to the $A_2$ Toda chain. The circular orbits of null geodesics are explored, and the fifth-order polynomial master equation governing the photon sphere radius $R_0$ is analyzed. It is shown to admit a unique solution satisfying $R_0 > 2 μ$. The circular null geodesics are proven to be unstable. Finally, the black hole shadow is examined, yielding relations for the shadow angle and the critical impact parameter.

</details>


### [113] [Stochastic motion of massless particles near event horizons in Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26475)
*Yinhan Huang,Lezhen Miao,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用两个随机版本的入射径向零测地线方程（ingoing radial null geodesic equation），研究无质量粒子在施瓦西（Schwarzschild）与莱斯纳-诺德斯特伦（Reissner-Nordström）时空中的随机径向运动，证明其以有限坐标时间（finite coordinate time）进入黑洞的概率仍为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典广义相对论（general relativity）中，径向外部的无质量粒子需无限坐标时间才能抵达事件视界（event horizon）。本文关注量子效应引入的随机扰动是否改变这一经典性质。

Method: 采用两个随机版本的入射径向零测地线方程，对施瓦西与莱斯纳-诺德斯特伦时空中无质量粒子的随机径向运动进行建模与分析。

Result: 结果表明，径向运动的无质量粒子在有限坐标时间内进入黑洞的概率保持为零，与经典情形一致。

Conclusion: 量子效应的随机扰动（stochastic perturbations）保留了经典黑洞的某些性质，即无质量粒子无法以有限坐标时间穿越事件视界。

Abstract: In classical general relativity, it is well known that radially moving massless particles in the exterior region take infinite coordinate time to reach the event horizon in black hole spacetimes. In this short paper, we use two stochastic versions of ingoing radial null geodesic equation to study the stochastic radial motion of massless particles in Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström spacetimes. We show that the probability that a radially moving massless particle enters the black hole in finite coordinate time remains zero. This indicates the stochastic perturbations of quantum effects preserve certain properties of classical black holes.

</details>


### [114] [Regular Cosmologies: Three Distinct Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26566)
*Sergio Bravo Medina,Marek Nowakowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将正则黑洞（regular black hole）以 de Sitter 核替换奇点的思路移植到早期宇宙，提出三种把位置相关宇宙学项 $Λ_{\rm eff}(r)$ 推广到膨胀宇宙的方案，并应用于六个正则黑洞度规。结果显示三种方案在 de Sitter 核处一致而晚期行为分歧，且单调递减的核密度给出的状态方程不会穿越 $w=-1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则黑洞通过引入 de Sitter 核避免中心奇点，本文追问同一思路若用于早期宇宙会导出何种宇宙学。正则度规定义了位置相关的宇宙学项 $Λ_{\rm eff}(r)=8πG ρ_{\rm MS}(r)$，但将其移植到膨胀宇宙并无唯一方式，因此需要比较不同推广方案的后果。

Method: 采用三种方案把位置相关宇宙学项推广到膨胀宇宙：McCrea–Milne 式的牛顿方案、在哈勃半径处取值的 running vacuum（跑动真空）方案、以及基于 Misner–Sharp 质量的准局域（quasi-local）方案。将三种方案分别应用于六个正则黑洞度规，并对牛顿情形求解 Friedmann 方程：Hayward 度规给出闭式解，其余度规用标准特殊函数或数值方法求解。

Result: 牛顿情形下宇宙始于 de Sitter 阶段并终于尘埃态，构成一个暴胀模型而非暗能量模型。三种方案在 de Sitter 核处一致，但在晚期出现分歧，文中针对 DESI DR2 结果量化了该差异。三种方案下，单调递减的核密度给出的状态方程（equation of state）始终不穿越 $w=-1$，因此 DESI 所暗示的 phantom 行为需要存在密度壳层，而正则核不具备该结构。

Conclusion: 由正则黑洞核移植而来的宇宙学无法自然产生 DESI 暗示的 $w<-1$ 行为；单一正则化长度（regularisation length）不能同时充当暴胀尺度与暗能量尺度。

Abstract: Regular black holes avoid the central singularity by replacing it with a de Sitter core. We ask what cosmology follows if the same idea is brought to the early universe. Regular metrics define a position-dependent cosmological term, $Λ_{\rm eff}(r)=8πG ρ_{\rm MS}(r)$, and there is no unique way to carry it over to the expanding universe. We follow three different prescriptions: a Newtonian one following McCrea and Milne, a running vacuum evaluated at Hubble radius and a quasi-local one based on Misner-Sharp mass. We apply each of them to six regular black hole metrics and explore their consequences. In the Newtonian case the Friedmann equations are solved in closed form for the Hayward metric, and in terms of standard special functions or numerically for the others, and describe a universe that starts in a de Sitter phase and ends as dust (i.e. an inflationary model instead of dark energy). We find that in all three prescriptions a monotonically decreasing core density gives an equation of state that never crosses $w=-1$, and thus the phantom behaviour suggested by DESI would require a density shell which a regular core does not have. The three prescriptions agree on the de Sitter core but differ at late times, and we quantify the difference against the DESI DR2 results. We also point out that a single regularisation length cannot serve as both the inflationary and the dark-energy scale.

</details>


### [115] [Radial quantization of the Schwarzschild geometry: relational observables, evaporation, and remnant state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26575)
*David Brizuela,Leonardo Chataignier,Mikel López-Escondrillas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在基于径向叶片分层的微超空间（minisuperspace）框架下对 Schwarzschild 几何进行正则量子化，构建了穿越 Killing 视界仍保持正则的量子理论，并据此导出量子修正时空、视界位移、表面引力增强及对数修正的 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵，最后讨论了可作为稳定遗迹的最小能量态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞量子化多依赖对时间分层，难以统一描述视界内外区域。本文寻求一种在整个 Killing 视界（Killing horizon）上保持正则、并能同时涵盖黑洞外部与内部区域的量子化表述，以研究几何的量子效应及其热力学后果。

Method: 采用以径向变量标记叶片的叶状结构（foliation）构建微超空间（minisuperspace）描述，对 Schwarzschild 几何实施正则量子化（canonical quantization）。由此得到满足径向约束方程的物理 Hilbert 空间（physical Hilbert space），并构造在物理 Hilbert 空间上作为规范不变对称算符的量子关系型可观测量（quantum relational observables）。为分析量子效应，构建了一族半经典智能态（semiclassical intelligent states），其同时饱和黑洞质量与渐近 Killing 范数（asymptotic Killing norm）的 Robertson-Schrödinger 不确定关系且相对涨落较小。

Result: 该量子理论在 Killing 视界上保持正则，其物理 Hilbert 空间可同时描述黑洞外部与内部区域。智能态下关系型可观测量的期望值具有清晰的几何解释，并给出有效的量子修正时空（quantum-corrected spacetime）。结果显示：视界位置可能发生位移且依赖于态的相关性；静态观测者的固有加速度（proper acceleration）与视界表面引力（surface gravity）相对于相同视界面积的经典 Schwarzschild 黑洞均被增强。由表面引力与温度的关系，得到黑洞热力学性质的修正，导致对数修正的 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵（logarithmically corrected Bekenstein-Hawking entropy）。

Conclusion: 论文进一步讨论了最小能量态的定义，该态可被解释为具有有限视界半径的稳定遗迹（stable remnant），提示了一种可能阻止黑洞完全蒸发（complete evaporation）的机制。

Abstract: We consider the canonical quantization of the Schwarzschild geometry in a minisuperspace description based on a foliation with leaves labeled by the radial variable. This formulation remains regular across the Killing horizon. In this way, the resulting quantum theory admits a physical Hilbert space of states that solve the radial constraint equation and simultaneously describe the exterior and interior regions of the black hole. We discuss the construction of quantum relational observables, which act as gauge-invariant symmetric operators on the physical Hilbert space. In order to analyze quantum effects on the geometry, we construct a family of semiclassical intelligent states that saturate the Robertson-Schrödinger uncertainty relation for both the black-hole mass and the asymptotic Killing norm, while exhibiting small relative fluctuations. The expectation values of relational observables in these states admit a clear geometric interpretation, and they lead to an effective, quantum-corrected spacetime. In particular, we find that the position of the horizon may be shifted, depending on the correlation of the state. In addition, both the proper acceleration of a static observer and the horizon surface gravity are enhanced with respect to a classical Schwarzschild black hole with the same horizon area. Using the relation between surface gravity and temperature, we then obtain corrections to the thermodynamic properties of the black hole, leading to a logarithmically corrected Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. Finally, we discuss the definition of a state of minimal energy that could be interpreted as a stable remnant characterized by a finite horizon radius, suggesting a possible mechanism for preventing the complete evaporation of the black hole.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Cross-Bispectrum Constraints from HI Intensity Mapping and Galaxy Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25389)
*Warren Naidoo,Moumita Aich,Kavilan Moodley*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究中性氢（HI）强度映射（IM）与光学巡天的星系密度和宇宙剪切（cosmic shear）的交叉关联，构建 HI-HI-星系与 HI-HI-剪切的交叉双谱（cross-bispectra），以恢复 HI 图中被前景扣除移除的长波长径向模式。结合多探针可打破红移依赖量的简并（如 f-σ8 简并），并提升对宇宙学模型的约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来中性氢强度映射与光学巡天将在空前的体积上探测大尺度结构，为宇宙学模型提供强约束。前景扣除（foreground subtraction）会移除 HI 图中的长波长径向模式，需借助交叉关联恢复这类信息，并利用多探针结合打破红移依赖量的简并。

Method: 构建 HI-HI-星系密度与 HI-HI-宇宙剪切（cosmic shear）交叉双谱，评估其在 HIRAX 与 Rubin-LSST 上的可探测性；同时采用关于 HI 与恒星间随机交叉相关系数的线性尺度依赖模型 r_HIHIδg = r0 + r1 k，断折尺度（break scale）取 0.14 Mpc^-1。

Result: 在六个红移区间内，对 f 的约束为 0.74%–9.8%，对 σ8 为 0.073%–3.5%；一阶、二阶 HI 偏置参数约束分别为 b_HI^(1) 的 0.42%–5.6% 与 b_HI^(2) 的 2.0%–29%，星系偏置 b_gal 为 2.6%–11%。随机交叉相关系数约束中 r0 为 3.8%–13.5%，r1 为 7.5%–19.9%。加入交叉双谱可增强 ΛCDM 约束，尤其经由 HI-HI-剪切交叉双谱；在 w0 wa CDM 模型中，含 Planck 先验时，仅功率谱对 (w0, wa) 的约束从 (0.036, 0.15) 与 (0.032, 0.12)，在加入 HI-HI-星系与 HI-HI-剪切交叉双谱后分别改善至 (0.014, 0.057) 与 (0.015, 0.059)。

Conclusion: HI 强度映射与光学巡天的交叉双谱能够恢复被前景扣除移除的长波长径向模式，打破 f-σ8 等简并，并显著提升 ΛCDM 与 w0 wa CDM 模型的参数约束，其中 HI-HI-剪切交叉双谱的增益尤为突出。

Abstract: Future neutral hydrogen (HI) intensity mapping (IM) and optical surveys probe large-scale structure over unprecedented volumes, offering powerful constraints on cosmological models. We study the cross-correlation between the HI IM field and the galaxy density and cosmic shear fields from optical surveys. By formulating the HI-HI-galaxy and HI-HI-shear cross-bispectra, we recover sensitivity to long-wavelength radial modes otherwise removed by foreground subtraction in HI maps, evaluating their detectability for HIRAX and Rubin-LSST.
  Combining the probes breaks degeneracies in redshift-dependent quantities, including the $f$-$σ_8$ degeneracy. Our constraints range from $0.74\%$ to $9.8\%$ on $f$ and $0.073\%$ to $3.5\%$ on $σ_8$ across the six redshift bins that we consider. Our constraints on the first- and second-order HI bias parameters range from $0.42\%$ to $5.6\%$ on $b_{\rm HI}^{(1)}$ and from $2.0\%$ to $29\%$ on $b_{\rm HI}^{(2)}$, while the galaxy bias constraints range from $2.6\%$ to $11\%$ on $b_{\rm gal}.$ The HI-HI-galaxy density cross-bispectrum measurement allows us to constrain a linear scale-dependent model for the stochastic cross-correlation coefficient between HI and stars, $r_{{\rm HI \,HI\,}δ_g} = r_0 + r_1 k,$ with a break scale of $0.14\,\mbox{Mpc}^{-1}.$ We obtain $r_0$ constraints that range from $3.8\%$ to $13.5\%$ and $r_1$ constraints that range from $7.5\%$ to $19.9\%$.
  For cosmology, adding cross-bispectra enhances Lambda CDM constraints, notably via the HI-HI-shear cross-bispectrum. For the $w_0\, w_a$CDM model, the power-spectrum-only constraints on $w_0$ and $w_a,$ including Planck priors, improve from $(σ_{w_0},σ_{w_a})=(0.036,0.15)$ and $(0.032,0.12)$ to $(σ_{w_0},σ_{w_a})=(0.014,0.057)$ and $(0.015,0.059)$ when adding the HI-HI-galaxy and HI-HI-shear cross-bispectra, respectively.

</details>


### [117] [Gravitational-Susceptibility Dip Behind DESI's results: Addressing the Phantom Crossing by Local limit of Nonlocal Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26517)
*Aria Hemmatian,Yahya Mohammadi,Javad Tabatabaei,Shant Baghram*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: DESI 第二次数据发布（约1400万个星系与类星体）偏好动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy），CPL 参数化下倾向 \(w_0>-1,\ w_a<0\)，表明状态方程在 \(z\simeq0.4\) 穿越幽灵分界（phantom divide）\(w=-1\)。本文指出非局域引力的局域极限（local limit of nonlocal gravity，一种基于引力敏感度 \(S(x)\) 的远平行推广）可自然解释该现象：该模型中宇宙学常数不被允许，暗能量必然为动力学。作者建立对应关系并用 CMB、BAO、SNe 联合数据检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI DR2 数据显示暗能量状态方程可能随时间演化，并在 \(z\simeq0.4\) 处穿越 \(w=-1\)，对标准宇宙学常数图像构成挑战。作者意在寻找一种无需引入宇宙学常数、且能自然产生该动力学行为与幽灵穿越的理论解释，故考察非局域引力的局域极限。

Method: 采用远平行（teleparallel）框架下由标量函数——引力敏感度 \(S(x)\)（gravitational susceptibility）——支配的非局域引力的局域极限。建立对应关系：任意具有状态方程 \(w(z)\) 的广义相对论暗能量模型，都可对应一个具有 \(w=-1\) 及适当敏感度 \(S(z)\) 的修正 TEGR 宇宙学，二者在背景膨胀层面不可区分。据此构造能重现 DESI 所偏好 CPL 行为的敏感度函数，并使用玻尔兹曼求解器（Boltzmann solver）结合 CMB、BAO 与 SNe 数据进行检验。

Result: 所恢复的敏感度 \(S(z)\) 呈现特征性的低红移下凹，在 \(z\simeq0.4\) 处深度为 \(\beta=-0.025\pm0.007\)；其导数改变符号的红移与幽灵穿越时期一致。这表明观测到的幽灵穿越是由 \(S(z)\) 的时间演化所产生的有效现象。

Conclusion: 非局域引力的局域极限为 DESI 偏好动力学暗能量及 \(z\simeq0.4\) 处幽灵穿越提供了一种自然解释。该模型中不存在宇宙学常数，暗能量必然是动力学的；幽灵穿越是观测者所见 \(S(z)\) 演化的有效效应，且构造的敏感度函数可复现 CPL 行为并在 CMB、BAO、SNe 联合数据约束下自洽。

Abstract: The second data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), based on more than fourteen million galaxies and quasars, prefers a dark energy component whose equation of state is dynamical. In the simplest two-parameter description of this behavior, the Chevallier--Polarski--Linder (CPL) parametrization, the data favor the quadrant $w_0>-1$, $w_a<0$, which implies that the equation of state crosses the phantom divide $w=-1$ at a redshift $z\simeq0.4$. We show that the local limit of nonlocal gravity, a teleparallel extension of general relativity governed by a scalar function called gravitational susceptibility $S(x)$, offers a natural explanation. In this model a cosmological constant is not allowed; dark energy is necessarily dynamical. The phantom crossing seen by an observer is an effective phenomenon generated by the time evolution of $S(z)$. We establish a correspondence: to every general-relativistic dark energy model with equation of state $w(z)$, there corresponds a modified TEGR cosmology with $w=-1$ and a suitable susceptibility $S(z)$ that are indistinguishable at the level of the background expansion. We construct the susceptibility functions that reproduce the CPL behavior favored by DESI data, and we confront the model with the combined CMB,BAO, and SNe data using Boltzmann solver codes. The recovered susceptibility displays a characteristic low-redshift dip with depth $β= -0.025 \pm 0.007$ at $z\simeq 0.4 $, and its derivative changes sign at a redshift equal to the phantom-crossing epoch.

</details>


### [118] [To USR or not to USR: An effective description of two-field dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26754)
*Samuel Sánchez López,Eemeli Tomberg*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文对含轻旁观场（spectator field）的拐点暴胀（inflection point inflation）模型提出替代性解读：暴胀子（inflaton）仍经历有效超慢滚（ultra-slow roll, USR），其扰动在抬升后的势中遵循单场动力学并与旁观场扰动解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单场拐点暴胀模型中的超慢滚阶段会放大曲率扰动；Lorenzoni 等人通过引入轻旁观场，认为该场为第一慢滚参数 ε 设定下限从而破坏 USR，但同时指出与等曲率扰动（isocurvature perturbations）的混合仍会放大曲率功率谱。本文旨在提供不同的解释视角。

Method: 论证旁观场以常数方式抬升势能，暴胀子扰动因此在抬升后的拐点势中遵循单场动力学，并与旁观场扰动解耦；模式混合（mode mixing）仅影响功率谱的凹陷（dip），使其变得更平缓。作者对示例模型进行了数值验证以展示这种有效单场行为。

Result: 数值结果表明，所考察的示例模型确实呈现有效单场行为；模式混合的作用局限于使功率谱凹陷变缓，而非主导曲率扰动的放大。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解此类模型的参数依赖性提供了更简便的途径，即通过抬升势中的有效单场描述来研究，而无需将等曲率混合视为核心机制。

Abstract: Single-field models of inflection point inflation experience a phase of ultra-slow roll (USR), leading to amplified curvature perturbations. Recently, Lorenzoni et al. modified the scenario by adding a light spectator field, showing that it ruins USR by setting a floor for the first slow-roll parameter $ε$. Mixing with isocurvature perturbations still amplifies the curvature power spectrum. We offer an alternative point of view, arguing that the inflaton still undergoes effective USR. The spectator lifts the potential by a constant amount, and the inflaton perturbations follow single-field dynamics in the lifted inflection point potential, decoupling from the spectator perturbations. Mode mixing only affects the power spectrum dip, making it milder. We demonstrate this effective single-field behavior numerically for example models. Our results offer a simple way to study the models' parameter dependence.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Universal behavior in diffuse charge dynamics with patterned electrodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25265)
*E. Krucker-Velasquez,E. Kirkinis,M. Olvera de la Cruz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究指出，在具有非均匀带电电极的电解池（electrolytic cell）中，阻抗（impedance）频率色散（frequency dispersion）的起始点约为谐振器自然频率（resonator natural frequency）的两倍，且与驱动方式及双电层（double layer）是否重叠无关，该规律对储能器件与生物材料界面器件均适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子电子学器件（iontronic devices）可复现生理过程，有望提升能效以支撑人工智能架构。但储能与能量转换器件存在无序的界面与体相构型（disordered interfacial and bulk configurations），传统阻抗谱（conventional impedance spectroscopy）无法分辨这些问题。

Method: 采用阻抗谱分析，考察非均匀带电电极电解池在两种驱动条件下的频率色散行为：振荡电荷驱动与电压差驱动，并覆盖双电层重叠与非重叠两种情形。

Result: 频率色散的起始出现在约两倍谐振器自然频率处，且不依赖于电池由振荡电荷还是电压差驱动，对重叠与非重叠双电层均成立，表现出普适性（universal）。

Conclusion: 该普适性结论除用于表征储能器件外，还可推广至与生物材料（biomaterials）形成界面的器件。

Abstract: Iontronic devices that replicate physiological processes will improve energy efficiency to support artificial-intelligence architectures. Energy storage and conversion devices, however, display disordered interfacial and bulk configurations that conventional impedance spectroscopy cannot resolve. Here, we show that the onset of frequency dispersion in the impedance of an electrolytic cell with non-uniformly charged electrodes takes place at approximately twice the resonator natural frequency, independently of whether the cell is driven by oscillating charges or voltage differences, and remains valid for both overlapping and non-overlapping double layers. In addition to characterizing energy storage devices, these universal findings apply to devices interfaced with biomaterials.

</details>


### [120] [Self-limited stacking of non-Euclidean colloidal shells: From saddles to caps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25399)
*Kyle T. Sullivan,Mark J. Stevens,Gregory M. Grason*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究将曲率受挫（curvature-frustrated）胶体壳（"curvamers"）的模型从圆柱形推广至球冠与鞍形等非欧几何（non-Euclidean geometries），发现非欧壳堆叠的弹性能积累受壳厚度（经与Foppl-von Karman数相关的无量纲量）影响，进而调控自限尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同亚单元间的形状失配（shape misfit）会产生几何受挫（geometric frustration），从而诱导有限尺寸的自限组装（self-limitation）。已有curvamer模型仅考虑圆柱形弯曲壳，其弹性代价纯由弯曲产生，未涵盖非欧几何下可能出现的拉伸（stretching）代价。

Method: 构建更一般的曲率受挫胶体壳模型，纳入球冠（spherical caps）与鞍形（saddles）等非欧形状；引入壳堆叠的连续介质力学模型（continuum mechanical model）与离散粗粒化模型（discrete, coarse-grained model），分析各几何所需的装配内应变梯度（intra-assembly strain gradients）如何影响超广延（super-extensive）弹性代价的积累。

Result: 非欧壳堆叠的弹性能积累对壳厚度敏感，其通过一个与Foppl-von Karman数相关的无量纲量表征拉伸与弯曲的相对强度。薄壳对拉伸的惩罚更强，使弹性能增长更快，从而抑制自限：自限尺寸一般以圆柱壳最大、球冠最小、鞍形居中。薄球冠虽受最强弹性惩罚，但鞍形堆叠中弹性惩罚速率的快速增长使其在粒子结合强度（inter-particle binding strength）增大时具备稳健的尺寸选择（size-selection），且自限尺寸分散度（dispersity）相对圆柱与球形curvamer更小。

Conclusion: 将曲率受挫胶体壳推广至非欧几何后，壳厚度经由拉伸-弯曲竞争主导弹性能积累与自限行为；不同几何在自限尺寸及其对结合强度的响应、尺寸分散度上存在显著差异，鞍形壳在稳健尺寸选择方面具有优势。

Abstract: The presence of geometric frustration in self-assemblies, stemming from a shape misfit between identical subunits, can lead to the spontaneous formation of finite-sized structures known as self-limitation. Recently proposed models of curved, colloidal shells, dubbed "curvamers", that form one-dimensional stacks have shown curvature-induced frustration to be a promising class of particle designs for engineering self-limiting assembly. However, existing models of curvamer assembly considered only cylindrically curved shells where elastic costs are derived purely from bending. Here, we study the self-limiting behavior of a more general class of curvature-frustrated colloidal shells, extending shapes to include non-Euclidean geometries such as spherical caps and saddles whose deformations can also incur stretching costs. We introduce continuum mechanical and discrete, coarse-grained models of shell stacks to study how qualitatively different intra-assembly strain gradients required for each geometry affect the buildup of super-extensive elastic costs. We find the accumulation of elastic energies in non-Euclidean shell stacks is sensitive to shell thickness via a dimensionless quantity related to the Foppl-von Karman number that characterizes the relative strength of stretching to bending. In particular, thin shells penalize stretching more causing faster elastic energy growth resulting in the suppression of self-limitation with sizes generally largest for cylindrical shells, smallest for spherical caps and intermediate for saddles. We additionally find that while thin spherical caps experience the strongest elastic penalties, the rapidly increasing rate of elastic penalties in stacks of saddles lead to a robust size-selection as inter-particle binding strength grows as well as relatively smaller dispersity in self-limiting stack size in comparison to cylindrical and spherical curvamers.

</details>


### [121] [Statistical signatures of microorganism motility under environmental stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25403)
*Luiza Treichel Mossi,Nicole Mazzitelli Narvaz,Larissa da Silva Bento,Roger Modena,Bernardo Boatini,Amalia Garcez,Leonardo Gregory Brunnet,Alexandre Arenzon,Carolina Brito*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过视频显微术追踪草履虫（*Paramecium caudatum*）与卤虫（*Artemia salina*）的个体轨迹，在基准与胁迫环境下计算均方位移、速度分布、持续时长、曲折度与回转半径等指标，并用从实验数据参数化的粗粒化随机模型（run-and-tumble 框架）解释运动统计，表明轨迹指标可作为生理胁迫的指示量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物运动为研究活性物质（active matter）及其对环境扰动响应提供了天然框架；研究旨在探究环境胁迫如何改变运动统计特征，并检验轨迹指标能否指示生理胁迫。

Method: 在受控条件下使用视频显微术（video microscopy）重建两个物种的个体轨迹，并设置胁迫环境：草履虫暴露于毒性试剂、卤虫置于蒸馏水。基于轨迹计算均方位移（mean square displacement）、速度分布（velocity distributions）、持续时长（persistence times）、曲折度（tortuosity）与回转半径（radius of gyration）；随后建立由实验数据直接参数化的粗粒化随机模型（coarse-grained stochastic models）。

Result: 草履虫可用 run-and-tumble 框架描述，并在胁迫下包含反转事件（reversal events）；卤虫则需要带持续取向噪声（persistent orientational noise）的类 run-and-tumble 描述，以刻画跑动期间连续的方向涨落。模型复现了用于比较的主要轨迹级观测量，即曲折度与回转半径。

Conclusion: 该联合方法揭示了环境胁迫如何改变运动统计规律，结果显示不同物种与条件下的运动统计特征存在系统性差异，提示轨迹指标（trajectory metrics）或可作为生理胁迫的指示量；该工作为可直接测量并建模个体层面统计的活性物质实验提供了易用框架。

Abstract: The motility of microorganisms provides a natural framework for studying active matter and its response to environmental perturbations. We experimentally investigate the trajectories of \textit{Paramecium caudatum} and \textit{Artemia salina} under controlled conditions. Using video microscopy, we reconstruct individual trajectories over time. Beyond baseline conditions, we analyze stressed environments where \textit{P. caudatum} are exposed to a toxic agent and \textit{A. salina} are placed in distilled water. To characterize the motion, we compute trajectory-based metrics, including mean square displacement, velocity distributions, persistence times, tortuosity, and radius of gyration. These observables quantify changes in motility patterns across species and conditions. To interpret the dynamics, we develop coarse-grained stochastic models parameterized directly from experimental data. \textit{P. caudatum} is described within a run-and-tumble framework, including reversal events under stress. In contrast, \textit{A. salina} requires a run-and-tumble-like description with persistent orientational noise, accounting for continuous directional fluctuations during runs. The models reproduce the main trajectory-level observables used for comparison, namely tortuosity and radius of gyration. This combined approach provides insight into how environmental stress modifies locomotion statistics. Our results highlight systematic differences in the statistical signatures of motion, suggesting that trajectory metrics may serve as indicators of physiological stress. This work provides an accessible framework for active matter experiments where individual-level statistics can be directly measured and modeled.

</details>


### [122] [Linear and nonlinear active microrheology of viscous, viscoelastic, and elastic media: A fluid particle dynamics approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26431)
*Muhammed Muhsin Abdul Azeez,Henning Reinken,Andreas M. Menzel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作将流体粒子动力学（fluid particle dynamics）方法从黏性流体扩展至黏弹性与弹性介质，用于研究平面几何微流变学（microrheology）中探针粒子的线性与非线性响应；发现非线性会引发应力-应变曲线畸变及探针净漂移现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动微流变学（active microrheology）可通过探针粒子对外加振荡驱动力的位置响应，间接表征微观尺度下黏性、黏弹性或弹性介质的流变性质。本文旨在探究平面几何微流变装置中探针粒子的线性与非线性响应。

Method: 将流体粒子动力学（fluid particle dynamics）计算方法从黏性类流体扩展至黏弹性与弹性介质，并涵盖非线性区域；考虑受固体壁面约束的系统，以Jeffreys模型量化线性响应进行验证，并进一步考察无壁面条件下两探针粒子受往复成对振荡驱动的设置。其驱动力实现方式与光镊（optical tweezers）或主动磁微流变学（active magnetic microrheology）实验装置相一致。

Result: 在线性响应方面，受壁约束系统的结果以Jeffreys模型得到验证；随驱动力振幅增大，出现明显非线性效应，包括应力-应变（stress-strain）曲线畸变，以及初始靠近壁面的探针粒子逐渐产生净漂移（net drift）。该漂移在黏性类流体与弹性类固体极限下消失，但在中间黏弹性体系中显著。无壁面条件下，两探针粒子的往复成对振荡驱动亦因黏弹性非线性而产生使粒子相互远离的净漂移。

Conclusion: 扩展后的流体粒子动力学方法可用于描述平面微流变装置中黏弹性与弹性介质的线性及非线性探针响应，其驱动力实现契合光镊或主动磁微流变学实验；非线性漂移是黏弹性体系区别于纯黏性与纯弹性极限的特征现象。

Abstract: Active microrheology is an effective tool to determine the rheological properties of viscous, viscoelastic, or elastic materials on microscopic length scales. The positional response of an embedded probe particle to an externally applied oscillating driving force allows to indirectly characterize the properties of the surrounding media. We aim to explore the linear and nonlinear response of probe particles in a microrheological setup of planar geometry. For this purpose, we extend the computational method of fluid particle dynamics from viscous fluid-like to viscoelastic and elastic media, including nonlinear regimes. We consider a system confined by solid walls. In this case, we validate the approach by quantifying the linear response in terms of a Jeffreys model. Increasing the amplitude of the driving force, we observe distinct nonlinear effects. They include distorted stress-strain curves and a gradual net drift of probe particles initially positioned close to a wall. This drift vanishes in the viscous fluid-like and elastic solid-like limits, but is manifest for intermediate viscoelastic systems. We further address a setup of two probe particles in the absence of walls. They experience reciprocal pairwise oscillatory forcing. Here, nonlinearities in viscoelastic systems induce a net drift gradually moving the particles further apart from each other. Comparing with real setups, our implementation of the driving force is in line with experimental setups of optical tweezers or active magnetic microrheology.

</details>


### [123] [Reinterpreting ultrafast experiments on supercooled water: Glass transition versus liquid-liquid criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26442)
*Florian Pabst,Ali Hassanali*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究对近期报道的“过冷水中液-液临界点（liquid-liquid critical point）证据”提出替代解释：快速加热低密度非晶冰（low-density amorphous ice）中热容的显著升高，可由玻璃化转变中的非平衡动力学效应解释，无需引入液-液转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水的异常性质被认为源于过冷区存在液-液临界点，但快速结晶使实验验证极为困难；近期基于快速加热低密度非晶冰热容升高的研究声称观察到该转变，因此有必要检验这一解释的必然性。

Method: 结合在先进 MB-pol 水模型上训练的机器学习势（machine-learning potential）模拟，并引入描述玻璃化转变的 Tool-Narayanaswamy-Moynihan（TNM）模型，对超快加热过程的动力学进行分析。

Result: 模拟表明，所观测的热容信号可源于超快加热过程中分子转动与平动自由度被激活所引发的动力学效应，而非液-液转变；该结果与近期电子衍射（electron diffraction）测量显示的、在预测玻璃化转变温度附近过冷水结构被冻结的现象一致。

Conclusion: 结果为相关实验观测提供了替代性解释，凸显了非平衡玻璃态动力学（nonequilibrium glassy dynamics）在超短时间尺度上解读过冷水行为时的重要性。

Abstract: Water's anomalous properties have been hypothesized to originate from a liquid-liquid critical point in the supercooled regime, separating high- and low-density liquid states. Experimental verification remains challenging due to rapid crystallization under these conditions. A recent study reported evidence for such a transition, based primarily on a pronounced increase in the heat capacity of rapidly heated low-density amorphous ice. Here, we show that this heat capacity increase can be explained without invoking a liquid-liquid transition. By combining simulations using a machine-learning potential trained on the state-of-the-art MB-pol water model, combined with the Tool-Narayanaswamy-Moynihan (TNM) model of the glass transition, we demonstrate that the observed signal can arise instead from a dynamical effect induced by the mobilization of rotational and translational molecular degrees of freedom during ultrafast heating. We further show that our findings are fully consistent with recent electron diffraction measurements showing structural arrest of supercooled water close to our predicted glass-transition temperature. These results provide an alternative interpretation of the experimental observations and highlight the importance of nonequilibrium glassy dynamics in the interpretation of the behavior of supercooled water on ultra-short time scales.

</details>


### [124] [Adsorption of the low density lipoproteins on the micropatterned polymer brush: computer simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26613)
*J. Ilnytskyi,D. Yaremchuk,O. Komarytsia*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文将介观模型（mesoscale model）从均匀接枝链扩展到簇状（bunch-like）与网格状（grid-like）接枝微图案（micropatterns）；簇状排列吸附效率最高，其低密度峰为均匀接枝的两倍，高密度峰仅此微图案具备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是降低低密度脂蛋白（low density lipoproteins, LDLs）水平，此前模型仅考虑均匀接枝链（uniformly grafted chains），未涵盖非均匀接枝微图案的情形，故需考察接枝排列方式对吸附效能的影响。

Method: 在先前介观模型基础上，将分析扩展至簇状与网格状接枝微图案，并通过吸附等温线（adsorption isotherms）拟合考察吸附行为。

Result: 簇状排列效率最高，呈现低密度与高密度两个吸附效能峰；低密度峰源于LDLs尺寸与微图案间距（micropattern pitch）的匹配，较均匀接枝高出一倍。等温线可由Langmuir或logistic生长形式较好拟合，显示更高生长速率；高密度峰仅存在于该微图案，扩展了吸附剂适用范围。在最高LDL浓度下，LDLs自组装（self-assembly）为密堆积立方相（packed cubic phase）而阻碍吸附。

Conclusion: 接枝微图案（尤其簇状排列）可显著提升光再生聚合物吸附剂（photorenewable polymer adsorbents）对LDLs的吸附效能并拓宽适用浓度范围，但高浓度下LDLs自组装成密堆积立方相会限制吸附。

Abstract: Photorenewable polymer adsorbents, aimed at reducing the low density lipoproteins (LDLs) level, are reported experimentally. Previously we developed a mesoscale model for this setup with uniformly grafted chains, and examined the role played by chains length and grafting density [Ilnytskyi, et al., Processes, 2023, 11, 2913]. The analysis is extended here to the cases of bunch-like and grid-like grafting micropatterns. The bunch-like arrangement is the most efficient one displaying low- and high-density peaks for adsorbing effectiveness. The former peak is twice higher compared with uniform grafting, as a consequence of a match between LDLs dimensions and the micropattern pitch. Adsorption isotherms are fitted well by either Langmuir or logistic growth forms, indicating a higher growth rate compared with uniform grafting. The high-density peak exists only for this micropattern and extends the applicability of an adsorbent. At the highest LDL concentration, adsorption is hampered by self-assembly of LDLs into a packed cubic phase.

</details>


### [125] [Dense Packing of Tetrahedra in Cylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26627)
*Gabrielle N. Jones,Adil Mughal,Philipp W. A. Schönhöfer,Sharon C. Glotzer*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文首次对圆形圆柱内密堆的正四面体（regular tetrahedra）进行系统研究，将其最密构型归类为手性四面体螺旋（chiral Tetra Helices）与非手性二聚体链（achiral Dimer Chains）两个可解析处理的连续族，并揭示 Boerdijk-Coxeter 螺旋（Boerdijk-Coxeter Helix）是柱面约束的自然结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对圆柱内四面体密堆缺乏系统研究，且著名的 Boerdijk-Coxeter 螺旋通常被视为孤立特例；本文旨在厘清其来源与整体构型分类。

Method: 结合数值模拟（numerical simulations）与精确解析理论（exact analytical theory），对圆形圆柱内正四面体的密堆构型进行分类与分析。

Result: 最密构型被归为两个连续且数学上可处理的族：手性四面体螺旋与非手性二聚体链；Boerdijk-Coxeter 螺旋被证明是柱面约束的自然产物而非孤立异常；并在某一临界直径处发现对称性破缺分岔（symmetry-breaking bifurcation），此时手性螺旋与非手性二聚体链在完全相同的填充率（packing fraction）下共存。

Conclusion: 柱面约束为四面体密堆提供了统一的构型框架，其中 Boerdijk-Coxeter 螺旋并非特例；临界直径处的对称性破缺分岔表明手性与非手性结构可在相同填充率下并存。

Abstract: We present the first systematic investigation of regular tetrahedra densely packed within a circular cylinder. Combining numerical simulations with exact analytical theory, we classify the densest configurations into two continuous, mathematically tractable families: chiral Tetra Helices and achiral Dimer Chains. Crucially, we reveal that the celebrated Boerdijk-Coxeter Helix is not an isolated anomaly, but a natural consequence of this cylindrical confinement. We further identify a striking symmetry-breaking bifurcation at a critical diameter, where the chiral helix coexists with an achiral dimer chain at a strictly identical packing fraction.

</details>


### [126] [Mesoscale heterogeneity in protein hydrogels induced by dynamic unfolding and post-gelation rearrangements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26681)
*Victoria Byelova,Lorna Dougan,David Head*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究构建粗粒化（coarse-grained）计算模型，模拟化学交联蛋白水凝胶（protein hydrogel）中蛋白去折叠（unfolding）的动力学过程，揭示凝胶化（gelation）期间及之后介观尺度（mesoscale）的异质网络结构，并发现体系对去折叠势垒高度敏感性较弱，而对持续构象弛豫（conformational relaxation）响应更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白水凝胶在组织工程、伤口愈合与生物传感等医疗应用中需实现理性设计，而蛋白去折叠的动态过程会同时影响单分子构筑单元与整体凝胶行为，二者存在跨尺度耦合。要表征并控制此类材料，需建立多尺度（multi-lengthscale）图景以理解这种复杂相互作用，现有认识尚不充分。

Method: 采用粗粒化计算模型，将蛋白去折叠简化为单一步骤的动态过程，研究化学交联蛋白水凝胶中动态去折叠的基本特征，重点关注凝胶化过程中及之后的介观结构。通过系统性改变单个蛋白去折叠的势垒高度（barrier height），并以分形维数（fractal dimension）和相关长度（correlation length）表征介观结构变化。

Result: 模型生成了由粗粒化蛋白构成的异质网络：高密度蛋白簇区域因局部拥挤效应（crowding effects）而去折叠程度较低，并通过低密度链状区域相互连接，后者富含去折叠蛋白。系统性改变去折叠势垒高度对介观结构影响较弱，而凝胶经历持续构象弛豫后，分形维数与相关长度等参数的变化更为显著。

Conclusion: 粗粒化建模能够有效刻画交联可去折叠蛋白的异质介观网络。该模型提升了对蛋白水凝胶结构与行为的预测性理解，有助于加速新型生物材料的设计。

Abstract: The rationalization of protein hydrogel design is essential in creating bespoke and functional materials for a range of medical and healthcare applications, such as tissue engineering, wound healing and bio-sensors. The dynamic process of protein unfolding can significantly influence the resultant gel structure and behaviour - conformational changes in individual protein building blocks affect the bulk gel, whilst changes to bulk conditions influence how individual proteins move and respond. In order to characterize and control protein hydrogels, a multi-lengthscale picture must be built so that this complex interplay can be understood. We develop a coarse-grained computational model to investigate fundamental aspects of dynamic unfolding within chemically crosslinked protein hydrogels, focussing on the mesoscale structure during and after gelation. In simplifying protein unfolding to a single dynamic step, we are able to create a heterogeneous network formed of coarse-grained proteins. We observe evolving hydrogels consisting of regions of high-density protein clusters with a low degree of unfolding due to local crowding effects, interconnected by low-density stranded regions with a higher concentration of unfolded proteins. Systematically varying the barrier height for single protein unfolding demonstrates a weak influence on the mesoscale structure as characterised by fractal dimension and correlation length. Conversely, the measured changes in these parameters are greater after the gel experiences sustained conformational relaxation. This work demonstrates the effectiveness of coarse-grained modelling in capturing the heterogeneous mesoscale network of crosslinked unfoldable proteins. A greater predictive understanding of the structure and behaviour of protein hydrogels is gained through this model and will accelerate the design of novel biomaterials.

</details>


### [127] [Entropy-Driven Initiation and Cytoskeletal Viscoelasticity in Endocytosis: An Onsager Variational Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26734)
*Jinjie Liu,Zhongcan Ouyang,Hao Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作将熵驱动的耗尽力（entropic depletion force）作为颗粒接近细胞膜的一种非特异性贡献纳入连续介质模型，并结合配体-受体结合、膜变形与细胞骨架黏弹性耗散，预测内吞（endocytosis）包裹的动力学窗口与时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受体介导内吞（receptor-mediated endocytosis）要求颗粒先接近细胞膜至数纳米内才能发生配体-受体结合，但既有连续介质模型多从已建立接触出发，未显式描述胞外侧拥挤颗粒如何影响颗粒在膜附近的分布。因此需要考察非特异相互作用在特异性结合之前的潜在贡献。

Method: 对理想耗竭剂（ideal depletants）采用 Asakura–Oosawa 排体积构造，在接触前的平面几何中给出精确的耗竭势（depletion potential）；再在约化的连续介质模型中耦合耗竭吸引、配体-受体结合、膜变形与细胞骨架黏弹性耗散（viscoelastic dissipation），其中黏弹性接触通过遗传积分（hereditary integral）与标准线性固体（standard linear solid）表述。

Result: 动力学模型给出完全包裹（complete engulfment）所需的条件性最小配体密度、有限的颗粒尺寸窗口以及依赖刚度的上限；当稳态半径落在有限正包裹时间域内时，估计包裹时间在某一半径处取极小值，且该半径随结合能密度增大而减小；在黏度等参数固定时，该近似预测细胞刚度增大会使包裹变慢；定义尺寸窗口的两个正根在极限参数值处合并，标志可容许尺寸区间的闭合。

Conclusion: 耗竭吸引可解释为颗粒与膜结合的一种可能贡献；精确的排体积结果仅适用于接触前平面几何，推广到弯曲包裹几何需额外近似。

Abstract: Receptor-mediated endocytosis requires a particle to approach the cell membrane to within a few nanometers before ligand--receptor binding can occur. Existing continuum models often start from an already established contact and do not explicitly describe how crowding particles on the extracellular side influence the distribution of the particle near the membrane. We examine entropic depletion forces as one possible nonspecific contribution to this initial approach. For ideal depletants, the Asakura--Oosawa excluded-volume construction gives an exact depletion potential for the planar geometry before contact. The potential and force vanish continuously at the onset of excluded-volume overlap. This interaction provides a possible contribution to membrane proximity before specific binding, while its extension to curved wrapping geometries requires additional approximation. Within a reduced continuum model, we combine depletion attraction, ligand--receptor binding, membrane deformation, and cytoskeletal viscoelastic dissipation. The viscoelastic contact is formulated through a hereditary integral and a standard linear solid. The kinetic model gives a conditional minimum ligand density for complete engulfment, a finite particle-size window, and a stiffness-dependent upper limit. When the stationary radius lies inside the domain of finite positive wrapping times, the estimated wrapping time has a minimum at a radius that decreases with increasing binding energy density. At fixed viscosity and other independent parameters, the same time approximation predicts slower wrapping as cell stiffness increases. The two positive roots defining the size window merge at a limiting parameter value, which characterizes closure of the admissible size interval. Depletion attraction is interpreted as one possible contribution to particle-membrane association

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Schrödinger operators with accretive potentials in weighted spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25826)
*Borbala Gerhat,Petr Siegl*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文在加权空间（weighted spaces）中分析具有增生势（accretive potentials）的薛定谔算子（Schrödinger operators），给出狄利克雷实现（Dirichlet realisation）预解集非空的条件，并研究其预解式的紧性与 Schatten 类等性质，进而应用于特征系统完备性与算子矩阵问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将标准空间中关于增生势算子的既有结果推广到加权空间（weighted spaces），以便刻画预解集非空、紧性及特征系统完备性等谱性质在更一般框架下的成立条件。

Method: 采用广义形式方法（generalised form methods）构造狄利克雷实现（Dirichlet realisation），并据此分析势与权满足的条件，建立定义域与图范数分离性质，以及预解式紧性与 Schatten 类的充分条件。

Result: 获得使预解集非空的势与权条件；建立定义域与图范数分离性质；给出预解式紧性及属于 Schatten 类的充分条件；并刻划标准空间与加权空间中离散谱（discrete spectra）与特征函数之间的关系。

Conclusion: 作为应用，将增生势算子特征系统完备性的结果从标准空间推广至加权空间，并分析具有 Schur 支配（Schur dominance）性质的算子矩阵，特别是与带强增生阻尼的波动方程（wave equation with strong accretive damping）相关的情形。

Abstract: We analyse Schrödinger operators with accretive potentials in weighted spaces. We find conditions on potentials and weights for which the Dirichlet realisation, introduced by generalised form methods, has non-empty resolvent set. We establish a domain and graph norm separation property, as well as sufficient conditions for the compactness and Schatten class of the resolvent. Moreover, we investigate the relation between discrete spectra and eigenfunctions of operators in standard and weighted spaces. As applications we extend results on the completeness of eigensystems of operators with accretive potentials from standard to weighted spaces and analyse operator matrices exhibiting a Schur dominance property, in particular, related to a wave equation with strong accretive damping.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [An Exploratory Replica-Overlap Probe of the Grokking Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25634)
*A. C. Opus,J. Q. Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以RSB启发的权重重叠分布为探针，检验grokking转变，但因对齐实现缺陷与数值精度问题导致预注册结果无法裁决（UNDETERMINED, C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID）；事后分析中仅重叠标准差比值具备检验效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在检验grokking转变过程中，是否存在RSB（replica symmetry breaking，复本对称破缺）启发的成对权重重叠分布变化，并尝试对Parisi序参量（Parisi order parameter）作出验证性解读。

Method: 训练四种配置下64个独立随机种子的网络，直至持续收敛或达40,000轮上限；随后计算成对权重重叠统计量q_wt与基于预测的q_fn，并依据预注册规则在检查点完整性要求下进行评估。

Result: 仅frac40配置满足12/16检查点完整性。其对齐步骤置换隐单元时未同步置换对应偏置与头内部单元，破坏网络函数，故q_wt全部继承该缺陷，q_fn不受影响；数值精度要求亦未达标，审计发现协议偏离。预注册结果为UNDETERMINED（C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID）。事后Hartigan-dip区间含零（Delta dip的95% CI = [-0.017, 0.034]），但校准显示dip检验在模拟分离度下功效为零，该区间不具信息量；重叠标准差增大约5.6倍，是唯一在观测效应下具功效的统计量。

Conclusion: 数据既未提供确证性零结果，也未给出Parisi序参量的有效解读。grokking率0/16、11/16、16/16仅为描述性，因训练比例与数据划分身份相混淆。提示rsb-inspired探针的结论依赖对齐与校准的有效性，而非统计量本身。

Abstract: We trained 64 independently seeded networks in four configurations, continuing each to sustained convergence or a 40,000-epoch ceiling. We then asked whether an RSB-inspired distribution of pairwise weight overlaps changes across the grokking transition. It is the alignment step, not the overlap statistic, that determines what this registered probe can report. The registered implementation permutes hidden units without the corresponding bias and head-internal permutations and therefore does not preserve the network function. Every q_wt value computed through this alignment inherits the defect; q_fn does not, because it is computed from predictions of the unpermuted models. The numerical-precision requirement also failed, and an audit found protocol deviations. Consequently, the pre-registered rule gives no verdict: registered outcome UNDETERMINED (reason code C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID). These data provide neither a confirmatory null nor a validated reading of the Parisi order parameter. Only frac40 cleared the 12/16 checkpoint-completeness requirement. For this configuration, a post-hoc criterion applied to the same data gave a Hartigan-dip interval containing zero (95% CI for Delta dip = [-0.017, 0.034]), whereas the overlap standard deviation increased by a factor of about 5.6. A post-hoc calibration assigns the dip test zero power at the simulated separations; the interval is therefore uninformative, not evidence of no change. The standard-deviation ratio is the only statistic here with power at the observed effect. Ensemble loss was near-flat only under the pre-specified 1% threshold. Finally, grokking rates of 0/16, 11/16 and 16/16 remain descriptive because train fraction is confounded with split identity.

</details>


### [130] [Double Descent and Malign Overfitting in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26392)
*Raphaël Urfin,Tony Bonnaire,Giulio Biroli,Marc Mézard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究解释了扩散模型（diffusion models）中过拟合为何是灾难性的而非良性的悖论：插值峰值出现在 p∼nm 而非 p∼n，但测试损失的上升早在 p∼n 就已开始；这种恶性过拟合（malign overfitting）源于估计器偏向记忆训练集的经验分数（empirical score），且只有在配合正则化（regularization）时过参数化才有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习（deep learning）中普遍认为过参数化（overparameterization）是良性的，但扩散模型的训练虽归结为回归问题却表现出灾难性过拟合，作者旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 结合在 CelebA 上训练的 U-Net 实验与一个可推导闭式学习曲线（closed-form learning curves）的随机特征模型（random-features model），并进行偏差-方差分解（bias-variance decomposition）来定位机制。

Result: 每个训练样本固定 m 个噪声实现时，插值峰值出现在 p∼nm 而非 p∼n；测试损失上升在 p∼n 即出现且与 m 无关；偏差自 p∼n 起增长，峰值后方差衰减但偏差持续增长并饱和于较大值；由于扩散模型用 m≫1 训练，峰值被推至极大模型规模，模型实际处于其前的上升分支。

Conclusion: 过拟合是恶性的，因为隐式正则化虽起作用却将模型推向记忆训练集的经验分数而非真实分数；但过参数化与正则化配合时仍有益，经最优正则化（岭惩罚 ridge penalty 或早停 early stopping）的大模型优于任何未正则化模型。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom in deep learning holds that overparameterization---having more parameters $p$ than training samples $n$---is benign: larger models generalize better and, even without regularization, interpolating models generalize well, the test error following a double-descent curve. One might expect the same benign overfitting for diffusion models, whose training reduces to regression, i.e. to minimizing a quadratic score-matching loss. Yet the opposite is observed: overfitting here is catastrophic, driving the model into a memorization regime. We resolve this paradox by combining experiments on U-Nets trained on CelebA with a random-features model for which we derive closed-form learning curves. We show that with a fixed number $m$ of noise realizations per training sample, an interpolation peak does occur, but at $p\sim nm$ rather than at $p\sim n$ as in standard regression. The rise of the test loss, however, sets in much earlier, at $p\sim n$, independently of $m$. This overfitting is malign because, although the implicit regularization of training is fully at work, it drives the model toward the empirical score, which memorizes the training set, rather than toward the true score. A bias-variance decomposition pinpoints the mechanism: the bias of the score estimator starts to grow at $p\sim n$; past the peak the variance decays, as in regression, whereas the bias keeps growing and both saturate at a large value. Since diffusion models are trained with $m\gg1$, the peak is pushed to very large model sizes, and therefore sit on the rising branch that precedes it, where malign overfitting is already in play. Nevertheless, overparameterization remains beneficial when paired with regularization: in the random-features theory and in U-Net experiments, optimally regularized large models---via a ridge penalty or early stopping, respectively---outperform any unregularized models.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.SR'></div>

# astro-ph.SR [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Super-Chandrasekhar white dwarfs by the evolution of magnetized main-sequence stars: New mass-limits from STARS simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22437)
*Zenia Zuraiq,Banibrata Mukhopadhyay,Achal Kumar,Arnab Sarkar,Alexander J. Hackett,Projjwal Banerjee,Christopher A. Tout*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该研究利用改进的剑桥恒星演化程序 STARS，引入磁场效应与冷却过程，模拟磁化主序星向磁化非自转白矮星的演化，探讨超钱德拉塞卡极限的磁化白矮星（B-WD）存在的可能性及其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 某些异常高光度的 Ia 型超新星（如 SN2003fg）观测暗示可能存在稳定的、大质量的、超钱德拉塞卡极限（super-Chandrasekhar）的近似球形白矮星，这促使作者探究磁场在其中的作用。

Method: 采用剑桥恒星演化程序（Cambridge Stellar evolution code, STARS），通过引入磁场效应与冷却过程对其进行修改，构建一系列模型追踪磁化主序星（main-sequence stars, MSs）向磁化非自转白矮星（white dwarfs, WDs）的时间演化。

Result: 结果表明磁场的经典效应显著影响主序星及其向白矮星的演化；磁场可能对解释某些亚钱德拉塞卡白矮星（如 SDSS J123204.19+522548.2）观测到的异常大半径起决定性作用，并与有限温度效应共同作用；同时确认在钱德拉塞卡极限上下均存在稳定的磁化白矮星，其质量上限取决于磁场几何构型、伴星质量吸积与冷却速率。

Conclusion: 尽管“钱德拉塞卡极限（Chandrasekhar limit）”本身可能依然成立，但其具体数值严重依赖于底层物理，在此即磁场；超钱德拉塞卡磁化白矮星（B-WDs）的稳定性得到支持。

Abstract: We explore the time evolution of the magnetized main-sequence stars (MSs) to magnetized non-rotating white dwarfs (WDs) with a series of models, capturing modified Hertzsprung-Russell diagram. We use the Cambridge Stellar evolution code, STARS, with modifications by introducing magnetic effects and cooling. We investigate the possible existence of stable, magnetized, massive, super-Chandrasekhar, approximately spherical WDs, away from Chandrasekhar's mass-radius curve. These we refer to as B-WDs. They are inferred from the observations of peculiar over-luminous type Ia supernovae, e.g. SN2003fg. We show that the classical effects of magnetic fields significantly impact MSs and their time evolution to WDs. Moreover, the magnetic field could be decisive in explaining the unusually large radius observed in some sub-Chandrasekhar WDs, e.g. SDSS J123204.19+522548.2, along with the finite temperature effect. We further investigate the stability of magnetized WDs, both below and above the Chandrasekhar limit and confirm the possibility of super-Chandrasekhar B-WDs with new mass limits that depend on the magnetic field geometry, mass accretion from a companion and cooling rates. Therefore, while the "Chandrasekhar limit" may be sacrosanct, its value severely depends on the underlying physics, here the magnetic field.

</details>

### [132] [On Haagerup-Izumi fusion categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25185)
*Terry Gannon,Andrew Schopieray,Harshit Yadav*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文对每个奇数 $N$ 用 Barnes 双正弦函数（Barnes double-sine functions）构造 $\mathbb Z/N$ 的 Haagerup-Izumi（HI）范畴，并通过等变化（equivariantization）得到 $((\mathbb Z/N)^2,N^2)$ 型近群范畴。文章分类了 HI 范畴中连通可分代数（connected separable algebras）的 Morita 等价类并刻画其对偶范畴，证明 Evans-Gannon 猜想，并在任意特征下给出 HI 方程与重构以及特征二下的 Frobenius 对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在系统构造并分类循环群 $\mathbb Z/N$（$N$ 为奇数）的 Haagerup-Izumi 范畴，并解决该领域若干悬而未决的猜想与分类问题。具体动机包括验证 Evans-Gannon 关于中心模数据（modular data）结构的猜想，以及反驳 Davidovich-Hagge-Wang 关于 $\mathbb Z/5$ HI 范畴中心可在分圆域（cyclotomic field）上定义的相关猜想。

Method: 方法上，利用 Barnes 双正弦函数构造 $\mathbb Z/N$ 的 HI 范畴，并通过等变化（equivariantization）生成近群范畴（near-group categories）。对 HI 范畴中连通可分代数按 Morita 等价进行分类，刻画其对偶范畴；同时在任意特征下建立 HI 方程与重构，证明特征二下的 Frobenius 对称性。

Result: 结果表明，对偶范畴常为非循环群（noncyclic groups）带非平凡 pointed associator 的 HI 范畴；中心模数据总为涉及阶为 $N^2+4$ 的有限度量群（finite metric group）的 smashed sum，且该群及其二次型可从 HI 范畴数据直接恢复。此外给出 $\mathbb C$ 上奇数 $N\le 43$ 的全部（非扭曲、子因子型）HI 范畴清单，并找到三个两两非 Morita 等价的 $\mathbb Z/15$ 范畴其中心模数据相同。

Conclusion: 结论包括：证明 Evans-Gannon 猜想成立；$\mathbb Z/5$ HI 范畴的中心无法在分圆域上定义（即不存在 split ribbon 形式），从而反驳 Davidovich-Hagge-Wang 的猜想；并给出 HI 方程在任意特征下的重构框架与特征二下的 Frobenius 对称性，为 HI 范畴分类提供工具。

Abstract: For every odd $N$, we construct a Haagerup-Izumi (HI) category for $\mathbb Z/N$, using Barnes double-sine functions. Equivariantization gives near-group categories of type $((\mathbb Z/N)^2,N^2)$ for every odd $N$. We classify connected separable algebras up to Morita equivalence in such HI categories and describe their dual categories. These duals are often HI for noncyclic groups with nontrivial pointed associators. We prove the Evans-Gannon conjecture that the modular data of the center is always a smashed sum involving a finite metric group of order $N^2+4$ and recover this group and its quadratic form directly from the data of the HI category. We also give the HI equations and reconstruction in arbitrary characteristic and prove a Frobenius symmetry in characteristic two. Using this, we obtain the complete list of (untwisted, subfactor type) HI categories over $\mathbb{C}$ for odd $N\le 43$. Other applications include finding three pairwise non-Morita-equivalent $\mathbb Z/15$ categories whose centers have the same modular data. We show that the center of a $\mathbb Z/5$ HI category cannot be defined (i.e., admits no split ribbon form) over a cyclotomic field, disproving a conjecture of Davidovich-Hagge-Wang.

</details>

### [133] [Non-Monotonicity of $p_{\rm T}$ Correlations in Au + Au Collisions at RHIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26449)
*Rutik Manikandhan*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 首次测量 Au+Au 碰撞中 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$ = 3.0–7.7 GeV 下中快度带电粒子的双粒子横动量关联，发现其随参与核子数呈近似幂律标度，并在中心碰撞中呈现非单调能量依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为扩展 STAR 束流能量扫描（Beam Energy Scan, BES）低能区间的双粒子横动量（$p_{\rm T}$）关联测量，检验其在更低碰撞能量下与既有 BES-I 结果及输运模型（transport model）计算的相符程度。

Method: 利用 STAR 实验记录的 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$ = 3.0、3.2、3.5、3.9、4.5、5.2 和 7.7 GeV 的 Au+Au 碰撞数据，测量中快度带电粒子的双粒子横动量关联量（two-particle $p_{\rm T}$ correlators），并与先前 STAR BES-I 测量结果及输运模型计算进行比较。

Result: 测得的双粒子 $p_{\rm T}$ 关联量随参与核子数（$N_{\rm part}$）呈近似幂律标度（power-law scaling），与独立源（independent-source）情景的预期一致；该标度在最低碰撞能量 $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}$ = 3.0 GeV 处描述最差。此外，在中心碰撞（central collisions）中观察到 $p_{\rm T}$ 关联的非单调（non-monotonic）能量依赖。

Conclusion: 该结果构成了随碰撞能量变化的此类行为的首次系统性观测，并指出在最低能量下独立源标度假设的适用性最弱。

Abstract: We report the first measurements of two-particle transverse momentum correlations for mid-rapidity charged particles in Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} =$ 3.0, 3.2, 3.5, 3.9, 4.5, 5.2 and 7.7 GeV recorded by the STAR experiment. The results are compared with previous STAR measurements from the Beam Energy Scan Phase I (BES-I) and with transport model calculations. The measured two-particle $p_{\rm T}$ correlators exhibit an approximate power-law scaling with the number of participating nucleons ($N_{\rm part}$), consistent with expectations from an independent-source scenario. This scaling is least well described at the lowest collision energy, $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}} = 3.0$~GeV. Furthermore, a non-monotonic energy dependence of the $p_{\rm T}$ correlations is observed in central collisions, representing the first systematic observation of such behavior as a function of collision energy.

</details>
