### [134] [String-Monodromy Rigidity from Six-Point Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25025)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作表明，弦散射（string scattering）的特征单值性（monodromy）可仅由 S 矩阵（S-matrix）一致性导出，无需预设世界面（worldsheet）：有限粒子一致性将解析四点零核（null kernel）约束为 $q''=\kappa q$，仅留线性、三角、双曲三支。交叉对称目标与正无减除色散进一步选出三角支，从而把 Veneziano 胚（Veneziano germ）确定到尺度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是检验弦散射的特征单值性能否不依赖世界面假设、仅从 S 矩阵一致性推导出来，并找出实现第一性原理推导所缺失的原则。该问题关涉有限多粒子因子化与通常归因于世界面几何的全阶约束之间的关系。

Method: 在无质量双序恒等模型（massless doubly ordered identity model）中，利用两个精确六点留数（six-point residues）生成标量五边形（scalar pentagon）与奇 Fay 恒等式（odd Fay identity）。对相反 KLT 模块（KLT module）中的交叉对称目标，施加非零重谱权重的正无减除色散（positive unsubtracted dispersion），并以矩确定性（moment determinacy）加以约束；另构造双尺度反例。

Result: 奇 Fay 恒等式迫使每个解析四点零核满足 $q''=\kappa q$，从而只保留线性、三角与双曲三支；这一过程将有限多重数因子化（finite-multiplicity factorization）转化为通常与世界面几何相关联的全阶约束。在相反 KLT 模块中，正无减除色散与矩确定性选取三角支，并将 Veneziano 胚固定到尺度不定。双尺度反例证明模块进入（module entrance）是独立输入。

Conclusion: 结果同时识别出弦散射的有限到无限刚性机制（finite-to-infinite rigidity mechanism），以及实现第一性原理推导所需的精确缺失原则。模块进入不能被一致性条件所吸收，须作为独立输入单独给定。

Abstract: Can the characteristic monodromy of string scattering be derived from S-matrix consistency alone, without assuming a worldsheet? We show that finite-particle consistency fixes it to a sharply defined extent. In a massless doubly ordered identity model, two exact six-point residues generate a scalar pentagon and the odd Fay identity, forcing every analytic four-point null kernel to satisfy $q''=κq$ and leaving only the linear, trigonometric, and hyperbolic branches. This converts finite-multiplicity factorization into an all-orders constraint normally associated with worldsheet geometry. For a crossing-symmetric target in the opposite KLT module, positive unsubtracted dispersion with nonzero heavy spectral weight selects the trigonometric branch, and moment determinacy fixes the Veneziano germ up to scale. A two-scale counterexample proves that module entrance is an independent input. Our result therefore identifies both a finite-to-infinite rigidity mechanism for string scattering and the precise missing principle needed for a first-principles derivation.

</details>


### [135] [Eigenvectors and Eigenvalues of $p$-Curvature Operators for KZ--type Differential Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25269)
*Vitaly Tarasov,Alexander Varchenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究特征 $p$ 下 KZ 型联络（KZ-type connection）的 $p$-曲率算子（$p$-curvature operators），给出其本征向量与本征值的 Bethe-ansatz 型构造，并证明本征向量可由特征 $p$ 超几何积分（characteristic $p$ hypergeometric integral）给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征 $p$ 下 KZ 型联络的 $p$-曲率算子是一族相互交换的自同态，其本征结构尚缺乏显式构造，作者希望借助 Bethe-ansatz 型方法给出本征向量与本征值的显式描述。

Method: 采用 Bethe-ansatz 型（Bethe-ansatz-type）构造方法，将本征向量表示为特征 $p$ 超几何积分（characteristic $p$ hypergeometric integral）的取值，并考察其在相应 Bethe 点（Bethe point）的 Frobenius 邻域（Frobenius neighborhood）上的延拓性质。

Result: 获得了 $p$-曲率算子的本征向量与本征值；本征向量可由特征 $p$ 超几何积分取值给出，并可延拓为相应 Bethe 点的 Frobenius 邻域上的平坦本征截面（flat eigensection）。

Conclusion: 特征 $p$ 情形下的 KZ 型联络 $p$-曲率算子具有 Bethe-ansatz 型可积结构，其本征向量以特征 $p$ 超几何积分实现，且在 Bethe 点的 Frobenius 邻域上具平坦延拓。

Abstract: A KZ-type connection in characteristic $p$ has $p$-curvature operators. These operators are commuting endomorphisms of the connection. We present a Bethe-ansatz-type construction of their eigenvectors and eigenvalues. The eigenvector is constructed as the value of a characteristic $p$ hypergeometric integral and extends to a flat eigensection over the Frobenius neighborhood of the corresponding Bethe point.

</details>


### [136] [Asymptotics for partition functions of random normal matrix models with singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25307)
*Kohei Noda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有光滑径向势（smooth radial potential）、光滑径向扰动（radial perturbation）、跳跃型奇异性（jump-type singularities）以及硬壁区域（hard wall region）的行列式平面库仑气体（determinantal planar Coulomb gases），给出了配分函数（partition function）至 $1$ 阶项的显式渐近展开（asymptotic expansions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在统一并推广此前多项关于平面库仑气体渐近展开的研究，因而将势、扰动、奇异性类型与硬壁几何纳入同一分析框架。摘要未进一步说明具体应用动机。

Method: 研究对象为行列式平面库仑气体（determinantal planar Coulomb gases），设定包含光滑径向势（smooth radial potential）与光滑径向扰动（smooth radial perturbation），并涵盖跳跃型奇异性（jump-type singularities），其中包括体相（bulk）、半硬（semi-hard）、硬（hard）与软边（soft-edge）区域。硬壁区域（hard wall region）取为中心圆盘、圆盘补集或圆环。分析方法为推导配分函数（partition function）的显式渐近展开。

Result: 获得了配分函数（partition function）的显式渐近展开（explicit asymptotic expansions），展开精度达到并包含 $1$ 阶项（the term of order $1$）。

Conclusion: 所得结果统一并推广了此前的多项工作（unify and generalize many of the earlier works），表明上述多种势、奇异性与硬壁设定可纳入统一框架处理。

Abstract: We study determinantal planar Coulomb gases with a smooth radial potential, a smooth radial perturbation, jump-type singularities (including the bulk, semi-hard, hard, and soft-edge regimes), and a hard wall region given by a centered disk, a disk complement, or an annulus. We obtain explicit asymptotic expansions of the partition functions up to and including the term of order $1$. Our results unify and generalize many of the earlier works.

</details>


### [137] [Lee-Huang-Yang Energy for Dilute Bose Gases: an Upper Bound for General Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25314)
*Giulia Basti,Morris Brooks,Serena Cenatiempo,Alessandro Olgiati,Benjamin Schlein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文为稀薄玻色气体（dilute Bose gas）的基态能量密度（ground state energy density）建立了一个上界，所用相互作用为一般短程排斥势（general short-range, repulsive potential），方法上推广了此前针对硬球（hard spheres）情形所引入的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前相关工作 [1] 的处理框架仅针对硬球势，适用范围受限。本文旨在将这一方法推广至更一般的短程排斥势，从而在更普遍设定下获得稀薄玻色气体基态能量密度的上界。

Method: 基于参考文献 [1] 中为硬球情形最近引入的方法，将其推广至一般短程排斥势的情形，并据此推导基态能量密度的上界。

Result: 在所设定的一般短程排斥势下，成功建立了稀薄玻色气体基态能量密度的上界。

Conclusion: 该结果表明参考文献 [1] 的方法具有可推广性，不局限于硬球势，可用于处理更广泛的短程排斥相互作用。

Abstract: We establish an upper bound for the ground state energy density of a dilute Bose gas, interacting through a general short-range, repulsive potential. To reach this goal, we extend the approach recently introduced in [1] for hard spheres.

</details>


### [138] [Comparing the Lee-Yang gap with the mass gap in the sub-critical planar Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25348)
*Noam Borgnia,Jui-Hui Chung,Jacob Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明在自由边界方形盒子（free-boundary square boxes）上，亚临界质量间隙（subcritical mass gap）与 Lee-Yang gap 的 $8/15$ 次幂相当，并给出压强场导数的统一阶乘界（uniform factorial bounds）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于建立亚临界质量间隙与 Lee-Yang gap 之间的定量比较关系，从而刻画临界点附近相关长度与 Lee-Yang 奇点尺度之间的联系。摘要未进一步说明更广泛的应用背景。

Method: 上界（upper bound）由随机流矩界（random-current moment bounds）、一个关联不等式（correlation inequality）与有限块展开（finite block expansion）得到；下界（lower bound）则依赖 Lee-Yang 乘积（Lee-Yang product）、磁化率（susceptibility）与临界磁化强度（critical magnetization）。

Result: 主要结果为：亚临界质量间隙与自由边界方形盒子上 Lee-Yang gap 的 $8/15$ 次幂可相比（comparable）。此外，还获得压强（pressure）的场导数（field derivatives）的统一阶乘界。

Conclusion: 论文确立了质量间隙与 Lee-Yang gap 之间的幂律对应关系（指数为 $8/15$），并附带得到压强场导数的统一阶乘界，为相关临界现象分析提供了定量工具。

Abstract: We prove that the subcritical mass gap is comparable to the Lee-Yang gap over free-boundary square boxes raised to the power $8/15$. Random-current moment bounds, a correlation inequality and a finite block expansion give the upper bound. The lower bound follows from the Lee-Yang product, the susceptibility and the critical magnetization. We also obtain uniform factorial bounds on the field derivatives of the pressure.

</details>


### [139] [On soliton asymptotics for Maxwell--Lorentz equations with rotating particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25391)
*Elena Kopylova*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究带扩展带电旋转粒子的二维麦克斯韦–洛伦兹方程（Maxwell–Lorentz equations），证明其孤子解（soliton solutions）具有渐近稳定性（asymptotic stability）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统存在对应于粒子以恒定速度平动并绕恒定角速度旋转的孤子解，其稳定性是理解此类带电旋转粒子动力学行为的关键问题，但摘要未进一步说明具体动机来源。

Method: 基于二维麦克斯韦–洛伦兹方程（2D Maxwell–Lorentz equations）与扩展带电旋转粒子的耦合模型进行数学分析；摘要未给出具体技术手段（如泛函分析或色散估计）。

Result: 主要结果为该孤子解（soliton solutions）的渐近稳定性（asymptotic stability）。

Conclusion: 在二维麦克斯韦–洛伦兹方程框架下，描述粒子匀速平动与匀速旋转的孤子解是渐近稳定的。

Abstract: We consider the 2D Maxwell--Lorentz equations with an extended charged rotating particle. The system admits soliton solutions corresponding to a particle moving with a constant velocity and rotating with a constant angular velocity. Our main result is the asymptotic stability of the solitons.

</details>


### [140] [Finite Rodriguez-Villegas Approximants to the Riemann $ξ$-Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25564)
*Alisson M. Silva*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文构造了一列有限的 Rodriguez-Villegas 变换，在临界带内局部一致收敛到 Riemann ξ-函数；其输入来自 Riemann theta 核经双曲正割核与 logistic 坐标卷积得到的单位区间上的正对称 profile。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅呈现构造目标：建立有限变换序列以在临界带内逼近 Riemann ξ-函数。

Method: 以单位区间上的正对称 profile 为输入，该 profile 由 Riemann theta 核与双曲正割核（hyperbolic-secant kernel）卷积并经 logistic 坐标得到，且是 x(1-x) 的 Stieltjes 函数；其 Bernstein 多项式（Bernstein polynomials）生成倒数分子与精确有限函数方程。

Result: 所得有限 Rodriguez-Villegas 变换序列在临界带（critical strip）内局部一致收敛到 Riemann ξ-函数；相应的分子具有作为费米子超迹（fermionic supertraces）的精确实现，而 Bernstein 多项式为归一化 Gibbs 迹（Gibbs traces）。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出明确结论，仅表明上述构造将 ξ-函数的逼近、精确有限函数方程、费米子超迹与 Gibbs 迹表示统一于同一分子结构之中。

Abstract: We construct a sequence of finite Rodriguez-Villegas transforms converging locally uniformly to the Riemann $ξ$-function in the critical strip. The input is a positive symmetric profile on the unit interval obtained from the Riemann theta kernel through convolution with the hyperbolic-secant kernel and the logistic coordinate. The profile is a Stieltjes function of $x(1-x)$. Its Bernstein polynomials produce reciprocal numerators and exact finite functional equations. The same numerators admit an exact realization as fermionic supertraces, while the Bernstein polynomials are normalized Gibbs traces.

</details>


### [141] [Finite images of braid group representations and algebraic solutions of KZ-type equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25805)
*Haru Negami*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究 Katz-Long-Moody 构造下有限单值群（finite monodromy）与微分方程代数解之间的联系，对固定有限像输入系统地分类了使输出表示具有有限像的参数取值，并据此给出相应 KZ 型方程全部解为代数解的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限单值群（finite monodromy）可充当群表示与微分方程代数解（algebraic solutions）之间的桥梁，但针对 Katz-Long-Moody 构造这一具体机制，尚缺乏对参数取值的系统刻画，本文即针对该空白展开分类研究。

Method: 以 Katz-Long-Moody 构造为对象，该构造将自由群（free group）与辫群（braid group）半直积（semidirect product）的表示变换为同一群的新表示，并与 Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov（KZ）型方程相关；对固定的有限像（finite-image）输入，分类使所得表示在半直积及其自由群、辫群子群上均具有限像的参数取值。

Result: 分类结果表明，辫群像的有限性独立于可容许参数（admissible parameter）；同时，该分类给出了相应正则奇异（regular-singular）KZ 型方程全部解为代数解的充要条件。

Conclusion: 限制到自由群时，所得结果刻画了相关 Fuchsian 系统（Fuchsian systems）的有限单值性与全部解的代数性，从而将上述分类与代数超几何函数（algebraic hypergeometric functions）的经典问题联系起来。

Abstract: Finite monodromy provides a bridge between group representations and algebraic solutions of differential equations. We study this connection for the Katz-Long-Moody construction, which transforms representations of the semidirect product of a free group and a braid group into new representations of the same group and is related to Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov (KZ)-type equations. For a fixed finite-image input, we classify the parameter values for which the resulting representations have finite image, both on the semidirect product and on its free-group and braid-group subgroups. In particular, finiteness of the braid-group image is independent of the admissible parameter. These results give necessary and sufficient conditions for all solutions of the corresponding regular-singular KZ-type equations to be algebraic. On restriction to the free group, they also characterize finite monodromy and algebraicity of all solutions of the associated Fuchsian systems, connecting the classification to classical questions about algebraic hypergeometric functions.

</details>


### [142] [Disentangling Measurement Protocol from Material Physics in Isotypic and Anisotypic SiC/Si Heterojunctions: A Metrology-Driven Study for Betavoltaic Energy Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26277)
*Mikhail V. Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过开尔文探针力显微镜、扫描隧道谱与精密电学测量，对比同型（isotypic, n-SiC/n-Si）与异型（anisotypic, n-SiC/p-Si）SiC/Si 异质结，指出四线开尔文感测可消除引线与接触压降，从而揭示异型结才是β辐照下更优的贝塔伏打（betavoltaic）转换平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于辨析SiC/Si异质结中同型与异型结构在电学响应上的差异，并厘清两线探测所得增强电流是否真实反映体区与界面特性。同时需要建立可靠的测量—解释框架，以正确评估贝塔伏打转换性能。

Method: 采用开尔文探针力显微镜（Kelvin probe force microscopy, KPFM）、扫描隧道谱（scanning tunnelling spectroscopy, STS）、基于Keithley 2450 SourceMeter的精密电学测量，以及0.1法拉超级电容器储能测试，并对比两线探测与四线开尔文感测（four-wire Kelvin sensing）结果。测量框架强调四线感测、发生器极性（阴极接LO）与高阻输出关断状态。

Result: 两线探测显示同型结构低偏压电流增强，但四线开尔文感测去除外接引线与接触压降后该电流降至受体区与界面限制的更低水平。KPFM与STS在富位错区域测得高界面态密度，最高达5×10^12 cm^-2 eV^-1，可解释局部响应的增强。异型结呈现真实类二极管电流—电压特性，β辐照下开路电压约1.2 V；在专利电荷泵转换器电容测试中，30分钟后异型结构达0.25 V，而同型仅为0.05 V，能量比约25。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在正确测量条件下，异型SiC/Si异质结是贝塔伏打转换的优选平台，而两线探测易因引线与接触压降造成误导性结论。所提出的基于四线开尔文感测的测量—解释框架对同类器件的性能评估具有方法学意义。

Abstract: We compare isotypic (n-SiC/n-Si) and anisotypic (n-SiC/p-Si) heterojunctions using Kelvin probe force microscopy, scanning tunnelling spectroscopy, precision electrical measurements with a Keithley 2450 SourceMeter, and a 0.1 farad supercapacitor charge-storage test. Two-wire probing shows an enhanced low-bias current in the isotypic structures; four-wire Kelvin sensing removes the voltage-drop contribution of external leads and contacts and collapses this current to a much lower bulk and interface limited level. The high density of interface states detected by KPFM and STS, up to five times ten to the twelfth per square centimetre per electronvolt in dislocation-rich regions, provides a plausible origin for the enhanced local response. Anisotypic junctions exhibit a genuine diode-like current-voltage characteristic and an open-circuit voltage of about 1.2 volts under beta irradiation. In the capacitor test with the patented charge-pump converter, the anisotypic structure reaches 0.25 volts versus 0.05 volts for the isotypic one after 30 minutes, giving an energy ratio of about 25. We formulate a measurement-interpretation framework based on four-wire Kelvin sensing, generator polarity with cathode to LO, and high-impedance output-off state. Our results show that anisotypic SiC/Si heterojunctions are the preferred platform for betavoltaic conversion when measured correctly.

</details>


### [143] [Asymptotics of Hankel determinants for potentials with singular edge points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26412)
*Dan Dai,Jia-Hao Du,Chenhao Lu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了具有高阶边缘奇性的酉随机矩阵系综（unitary random matrix ensembles）中 Hankel 行列式（Hankel determinants）的大 $n$ 渐近（large-$n$ asymptotics）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究聚焦于平衡测度（equilibrium measure）支撑在单一区间、且极限特征值密度以 $2k+\frac{1}{2}$ 阶消失（$k \in \mathbb{N}$）的系综中的高阶边缘奇性（higher-order edge singularity），旨在刻画其精细渐近行为。

Method: 方法基于正交多项式（orthogonal polynomials）的 Riemann-Hilbert 问题（Riemann-Hilbert problem）并采用 Deift-Zhou 非线性最速下降分析（nonlinear steepest descent analysis）。

Result: 显式求出了渐近展开中的常数项（constant term），该项包含与 Painlevé I（$P_{\rm I}^{2k}$）层级（hierarchy）相关的哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的正则化积分（regularized integral）；同时证明了奇异边缘附近特征值关联核（eigenvalue correlation kernel）的普适性（universality），并导出了由 $P_{\rm I}^{2k}$ 方程的特殊解相关函数表示的极限核（limiting kernel）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了高阶边缘奇性下 Hankel 行列式的完整大 $n$ 渐近结构，并同时确立了关联核的普适性与 Painlevé I 层级描述的极限核形式。

Abstract: We establish the large-$n$ asymptotics of Hankel determinants for unitary random matrix ensembles possessing a higher-order edge singularity. We focus on ensembles where the equilibrium measure is supported on a single interval and the limiting eigenvalue density vanishes to order $2k+\frac{1}{2}$ for $k \in \mathbb{N}$. Notably, we explicitly evaluate the constant term in the asymptotic expansion, which involves a regularized integral of the Hamiltonian associated with the Painlevé I ($P_{\rm I}^{2k}$) hierarchy. As a by-product, we also prove the universality of the eigenvalue correlation kernel near this singular edge and derive a limiting kernel expressed through functions related to a special solution of the $P_{\rm I}^{2k}$ equation. Our method relies on the Deift-Zhou nonlinear steepest descent analysis for the Riemann-Hilbert problem of orthogonal polynomials.

</details>


### [144] [An upper-symbol approach to $Φ^4_d$ limits of Bose gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26518)
*Hao Liang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在二维与三维环面上，从巨正则玻色气体（grand-canonical Bose gas）出发，于高温下推导 Φ⁴_d 测度。基于上符号变分方法（upper-symbol variational approach），利用相对熵（relative entropy）给出相对自由能收敛与固定阶重整化约化密度矩阵 Hilbert–Schmidt 收敛的统一证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在高温区域从玻色气体严格导出 Φ⁴_d 测度，并统一处理相对自由能收敛与约化密度矩阵向 Hartree 对应物的收敛。二维情形意在改进既有结果，使有效指数可逼近稀释阈值（diluteness threshold）；三维情形意在将有限阶弱收敛强化为逐阶 Hilbert–Schmidt 收敛。

Method: 沿用作者早期工作的上符号变分方法（upper-symbol variational approach），并引入相对熵（relative entropy）作为工具，对固定阶重整化约化密度矩阵（rescaled reduced density matrices）的收敛进行统一证明。相互作用程长设为 ε=λ^η，其中 λ 为逆温度。

Result: 结果对每一固定指数成立：二维为 0<η<1/2，三维为 0<η<1/26。二维改进了 Jougla 与 Rougerie (2026) 的结果及 Fröhlich、Knowles、Schlein 与 Sohinger (2025) 的局部 Φ⁴_2 推导；结合经典近似，给出首个对每一固定 0<η<1/2 均成立的局部 Φ⁴_2 推导。三维将 Nam、R. Zhu 与 X. Zhu [定理 2.8] (2025) 的有限阶弱收敛强化为每一固定阶的 Hilbert–Schmidt 收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作通过相对熵方法实现了相对自由能收敛与 Hilbert–Schmidt 收敛的统一刻画，并在二维放宽了有效指数范围至任意接近稀释阈值，在三维将弱收敛升级为逐阶 Hilbert–Schmidt 收敛。

Abstract: We study the high-temperature derivation of $Φ^4_d$ measures from grand-canonical Bose gases on two- and three-dimensional tori. Building on the upper-symbol variational approach of the author's earlier work, we use relative entropy to give a simple, unified proof of relative free-energy convergence and Hilbert--Schmidt convergence of all fixed-order rescaled reduced density matrices to their Hartree counterparts. For interaction ranges $\varepsilon=λ^η$, where $λ$ is the inverse temperature, these results hold for every fixed $0<η<1/2$ in two dimensions and $0<η<1/26$ in three. In two dimensions, we improve the result of Jougla and Rougerie (2026) and the local $Φ^4_2$ derivation of Fröhlich, Knowles, Schlein and Sohinger (2025). Combined with classical approximation, the two-dimensional result gives the first local $Φ^4_2$ derivation valid for every fixed exponent $0<η<1/2$, allowing polynomial exponents arbitrarily close to the diluteness threshold from below. In three dimensions, we strengthen the weak convergence at finitely many orders established by Nam, R.~Zhu and X.~Zhu [Theorem~2.8](2025) to Hilbert--Schmidt convergence at every fixed order.

</details>

# cs.FL [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Causal graph rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25188)
*Pablo Arrighi,Marin Costes,Luidnel Maignan*

Main category: cs.FL

TL;DR: 本文提出因果图重写（causal graph rewriting）这一计算模型，在有向无环图上以异步方式应用局部规则，并证明局部性可从业余单步规则推广到任意有效序列（* -局部与 * -扩张性），同时形式化了一维元胞自动机（cellular automaton）的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算模型难以统一刻画异步性、非确定性（non-determinism）与局部性约束。本文以有向边同时承载计算依赖（computational dependencies）与局部性约束（locality constraints），并允许边自身被重写，旨在构建一个兼具表达力与良定义性的异步计算框架。

Method: 模型定义：在带方向的有向无环图（directed acyclic graph）上以异步方式施加局部重写规则。分析工具：研究诱导子图（induced subgraphs）与图组合（graph composition）的良定义性，用以分离并重组受重写影响的区域；随后针对这些构造研究局部性，考察单步局部重写如何保持位置、边界与上下文。

Result: 通过粒子系统（particle system）与类广义相对论的时间膨胀（time-dilation）两个示例展示模型表达力。证明单步局部重写保持位置、边界与上下文；核心结果给出顺序组合（sequential composition）定理：任何局部规则自动满足 * -局部与 * -扩张性，因此局部性从单次规则应用扩展至任意有效序列。此外，形式化并证明了对任一维元胞自动机的模拟。

Conclusion: 因果图重写为异步、局部的图计算提供了良定义的形式化基础，其局部性在顺序组合下封闭，并足以模拟一维元胞自动机，表明该模型在表达力上具备通用计算意义上的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce causal graph rewriting, a model of computation in which local rules are applied on directed acyclic graphs in an asynchronous manner. The non-determinism arising from asynchrony is disciplined by the oriented edges, which must be understood as both computational dependencies and locality constraints---and are themselves subject to the rewriting. We illustrate the model through two examples: a particle system, and a time-dilation example---reminiscent of general relativity. We study the well-definedness and properties of induced subgraphs and graph composition, which isolate and recombine the region affected by a rewrite. We then study locality with respect to these constructions, showing how a local rewrite preserves positions, borders, and context. Our main result concerns sequential composition: locality extends from single rule applications to arbitrary valid sequences, as any local rule is automatically $*$-local and $*$-extensive. We also formalise and prove the simulation of any one-dimensional cellular automaton.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Effects of High-Temperature Preconditioning on Li Plating During Low-Temperature Cycling of the COTS LG Chem HG2 Cell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25005)
*Lukas Lehnert,Keith B. Chin,Frederick C. Krause,John P. Ruiz,Simon Hein,John Bescup,Gil Garteiz,Arnulf Latz,Erik J. Brandon,Birger Horstmann*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究针对商用（COTS）锂离子电池在航天低温环境下的可逆性与安全性问题，考察低温循环中的锂沉积（Li plating）行为，并揭示其与前期高温预处理协议之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用现成（COTS）锂离子电池是可充电电源，但通常设计用于0 °C以上充电，在低于零度的飞行工况下可能产生不可逆退化与安全隐患，因此需理解影响其低温性能的因素。

Method: 以COTS LG Chem 18650 HG2电芯为对象，在−20 °C下进行恒电流循环（galvanostatic cycling），比较三种条件：30 °C下两周恒电流循环预处理、30 °C下存储预处理、以及无预处理直接−20 °C循环；并结合电极微结构的计算机断层扫描（computed tomography）与三维空间分辨电池建模进行仿真分析。

Result: 循环实验显示，不同预处理条件下电芯的锂金属沉积（Li metal deposition）存在明显差异；通过断层扫描与三维建模相结合，进一步分析了这些差异的来源。

Conclusion: 前期高温预处理协议会显著影响COTS锂离子电池在−20 °C循环时的锂沉积行为；结合微结构成像与空间分辨建模可用于追溯该差异的成因。

Abstract: Commercial off-the-shelf~(COTS) Li ion cells offer an attractive and rechargeable power source for spacecraft applications. However, since these cells are typically designed for charging above 0\,\textdegree C, challenging sub-zero temperatures could inflict irreversible degradation and safety hazards during in-flight operation. To address these challenges, this work explores strategies affecting their low-temperature performance. Specifically, we investigate the Li plating behavior of the COTS~LG~Chem~18650~HG2 cell during galvanostatic cycling at $-20\,$\textdegree C and its dependence on prior high-temperature preconditioning protocols, involving either two-week galvanostatic cycling or storage at 30\,\textdegree C, as well as a reference case without preconditioning, i.e., direct cycling at $-20\,$\textdegree C. Experimental cycling tests reveal distinct differences in Li metal deposition between cells subjected to these conditions. To identify the origin of these discrepancies, we combine computed tomography of the electrode microstructure with three-dimensional, spatially resolved cell modeling and analyze the simulation results.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Weyl Chirality and Kitaev Topology through Bargmann Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25278)
*Swarup Sangiri,A. Taraphder*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文将基于 Bargmann 不变量的有限态几何表述（finite-state geometric formulation）应用于两带 Weyl 哈密顿量与一维 Kitaev p 波超导模型，展示通过量子态重叠即可提取手性敏感、拓扑及高阶几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何描述通常依赖对态空间几何的连续刻画，本文旨在提供一种无需连续描述的、以量子态重叠为基础的替代路径，用于探测手性与拓扑信息。

Method: 对 Weyl 体系，构造对称-反对称化的三阶 Bargmann 组合以选取局域赝自旋几何（local pseudospin geometry）中的手性敏感分量，并推广至两带格点 Weyl 模型；对 Kitaev 链，采用三阶 Bargmann 不变量与二阶重叠因子的归一化比值以消除中间态依赖。

Result: 该归一化比值复现了两能隙相之间的 Z_2 区分；进一步构造了连接局域 Majorana 边缘模剖面与有限能量 BdG 态的四阶回路，以及涉及相反 Weyl 赝自旋扇区的交替四阶回路。

Conclusion: 合适的 Bargmann 组合可直接通过量子态重叠探测手性敏感、拓扑与高阶几何信息，为态空间几何提供一种基于量子态的替代途径。

Abstract: We apply a finite-state geometric formulation based on Bargmann invariants to a two-band Weyl Hamiltonian and the one-dimensional Kitaev model of a p-wave superconductor. For the Weyl system, a symmetry-antisymmetrized third-order Bargmann combination selects the chirality-sensitive component of the local pseudospin geometry and extends to a two-band lattice Weyl model. For the Kitaev chain, a normalized ratio involving a third-order Bargmann invariant and a second-order overlap factor removes the dependence on an intermediate state and reproduces the $Z_2$ distinction between its two gapped phases. We further construct a fourth-order loop connecting localized Majorana edge-mode profiles with finite-energy BdG states and an alternating fourth-order loop involving opposite Weyl pseudospin sectors. These constructions illustrate how suitable Bargmann combinations can probe chirality-sensitive, topological, and higher-order geometric information directly through quantum-state overlaps, providing an alternative state-based route without requiring a continuous description of the underlying state-space geometry.

</details>


### [148] [Comment on "Andreev Reflection to Probe Momentum-Dependent Spin Polarization in Altermagnet CrSb"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25357)
*Igor I. Mazin,Maxim Khodas,Boris Nadgorny*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文是对 Zhang 等 [PRL 137, 016701 (2026)] 关于交替磁体 CrSb 动量依赖自旋极化测量结果的批评性评论，指出其宣称的安德烈夫自旋极化在对称性上被禁止，且源于对弹道安德烈夫自旋极化的错误定义，同时弹道区间未被证明、拟合流程存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhang 等报告在交替磁体（altermagnet）CrSb 中沿 (0001)、(-1-120)、(10-10) 三个方向通过点接触安德烈夫反射（point-contact Andreev reflection）测得动量依赖的自旋极化（spin polarization）。本文旨在检验该测量结论在对称性与理论定义层面是否成立。

Method: 基于对称性分析（symmetry analysis）与理论论证：分别考察扩散区（diffusive regime）与弹道区（ballistic regime）中安德烈夫自旋极化的允许条件，并指出安德烈夫选择定则（Andreev selection rule）仅对平行于界面的动量分量具有局域性；同时复核所测三个晶向与计算能带结构。

Result: 所称的安德烈夫自旋极化在 CrSb 中对称性上恒为零：扩散区对所有方向均为零；弹道区在三个取向中的两个亦为零，仅在一个方向上被允许，该方向由计算能带结构给出约 50%。报告中大且近乎各向同性（nearly isotropic）的数值，源于在固定三维动量下评估自旋不对称性的错误弹道安德烈夫自旋极化定义。

Conclusion: Zhang 等所报道的安德烈夫自旋极化由对称性禁止或仅在单一方向允许，其数值来源为定义错误；此外弹道区间未被证实，拟合流程（fitting procedure）亦存在缺陷，故该实验结论不成立。

Abstract: Zhang et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. 137, 016701 (2026)] report momentum-dependent spin polarization in the altermagnet CrSb from point-contact Andreev reflection along the (0001), (-1-120), and (10-10) directions. We show that the Andreev spin polarization they claim to measure is forbidden by symmetry: it vanishes identically in the diffusive regime for all directions in CrSb, and in the ballistic regime for two of the three orientations measured. It is allowed only along one direction, where the calculated band structure gives ~50%. The large, nearly isotropic values reported follow from an incorrect definition of ballistic Andreev spin polarization that evaluates the spin asymmetry at fixed three-dimensional momentum; the Andreev selection rule is local only in the momentum component parallel to the interface. Moreover, the ballistic regime is not demonstrated, and the fitting procedure is flawed.

</details>


### [149] [Analysis of Josephson junctions switching time distributions for the detection of single microwave photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25751)
*A. S. Piedjou Komnang,C. Guarcello,C. Barone,C. Gatti,S. Pagano,Vincenzo Pierro,A. Rettaroli,G. Filatrella*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究利用约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）的切换时间分布（switching time distribution）来优化单微波光子（single microwave photon）探测方案，通过比较有/无脉冲序列下的切换概率，评估探测效率并建立光子场特性、量子效率与结切换特性之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该分析旨在为判断光子场是否存在提供支持，在稀有事件（rare events）情形下尤为重要。

Method: 在假定腔（cavity）与传输线（transmission line）为理想、且光子源为周期性的条件下，将暴露于模拟弱光子场的电流脉冲序列的约瑟夫森结切换概率，与无脉冲时同一器件的切换概率进行对比；两种情形下热噪声（thermal noise）均可诱发热激活切换（thermal activated switchings）。

Result: 通过考察两种情形下切换事件数目的不平衡（unbalance），可估计探测效率；在采用 Kramers 模型描述逃逸（escapes）的假设下，可给出光子场特性、探测过程量子效率（quantum efficiency）与有限温度下约瑟夫森结切换特征之间的关系。

Conclusion: 基于切换时间分布的分析为单微波光子探测提供了优化方案，并为光子场存在性的判定及探测效率的定量评估建立了理论联系。

Abstract: We investigate an optimal scheme for the detection of single microwave photons by a Josephson junction through the analysis of its switching times distribution. The proposed analysis is of support for the decision about the existence of the photon field, which is important in the case of rare events. We assume that the cavity and the transmission line are ideal (each photon absorbed to the cavity gives a current pulse as the output of the transmission line) and the photon source is periodic. The employed methodology consists in comparing the switching probabilities of a Josephson junction exposed to a train of current pulses, simulating a weak photon field, with that of the same device in absence of pulses. In both cases, thermal noise can induce thermal activated switchings. The investigation of the unbalance in the number of switching events in the two cases, gives an estimate of the efficiency of the detection. Furthermore, in the assumption of escapes described by Kramers model, it is possible to provide a relationship between the properties of the photons field, the quantum efficiency of the detection process, and the Josephson junctions switching features at finite temperatures.

</details>


### [150] [Different reconstruction pathways toward superconductivity in TaRhTe4 and TaIrTe4 Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25899)
*Jinyu Zhao,Yang Fu,Congcong Le,Shuaihang Sun,Shu Cai,Yang Ding,Qi Wu,Hechang Lei,Jiangping Hu,Lili Zhang,Cedomir Petrovic,Liling Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 首次在压缩的 TaRhTe4 中观察到超导性，其起始转变温度 Tc 在 65.2 GPa 时升至约 2.6 K；该结果与 TaIrTe4 对比，揭示 TaXTe4 家族中通向超导的多条材料特异路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压力可通过晶格与正常态电子结构的重构驱动外尔半金属（Weyl semimetal）进入超导态，但不同材料中重构路径是否一致尚不明确；本文旨在通过 TaRhTe4 与已研究的 TaIrTe4 的直接对比，检验超导是否依赖单一临界压力或单一费米面重构。

Method: 对压缩 TaRhTe4 开展高压电阻、霍尔效应（Hall effect）与磁阻（magnetoresistance）测量，并结合同步辐射 X 射线衍射（synchrotron X-ray diffraction）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）进行分析。

Result: TaRhTe4 在约 20 GPa 附近出现超导转变，起始 Tc 在 65.2 GPa 增至约 2.6 K，63 GPa 以上实现零电阻；超导出现与渐进晶格畸变、正磁阻强烈抑制及霍尔系数持续趋零同步，计算显示压缩下有额外电子型能带穿过费米能级（EF）且 N(EF) 增大。与此不同，TaIrTe4 的霍尔系数先增大、后在超导阈值附近反转压力依赖，结构异常限于更窄压力区间。

Conclusion: TaXTe4（X = Rh, Ir）家族的超导并不绑定于唯一临界压力或单一费米面重构，而可在压力充分将低载流子外尔半金属衍生态重构为多带金属态后，经由材料特异的路径出现。

Abstract: Pressure can drive Weyl semimetals toward superconductivity through qualitatively distinct reconstructions of their lattices and normal-state electronic structures. Here, we report the first observation of superconductivity in compressed TaRhTe4. This finding enables a direct comparison of the distinct reconstruction pathways leading to superconductivity in TaRhTe4 and the previously studied TaIrTe4, both of which belong to the TaXTe4 (X = Rh, Ir) family of type-II Weyl semimetals. For TaRhTe4, high-pressure electrical-resistance, Hall effect, and magnetoresistance measurements, together with synchrotron X-ray diffraction and first-principles calculations, reveal a superconducting transition that emerges near 20 GPa, with onset Tc increasing to approximately 2.6 K at 65.2 GPa and zero resistance achieved above 63 GPa. The onset of superconductivity coincides with a progressive lattice distortion, a strong suppression of the positive magnetoresistance, and a continuous decrease of the Hall coefficient toward zero. Calculations further show that additional electron-like bands cross the Fermi level (EF) and that N(EF) increases upon compression. This evolution contrasts with TaIrTe4, where the Hall coefficient initially increases before reversing its pressure dependence near the superconducting threshold, while the structural anomaly is confined to a narrower pressure interval. This comparison indicates that superconductivity in the TaXTe4 family is not tied to a unique critical pressure or a single Fermi-surface reconstruction, but can emerge through distinct material-specific pathways once pressure sufficiently reconstructs the low-carrier Weyl-semimetal-derived state into a multiband metallic regime.

</details>


### [151] [Electromagnetic Proximity Effects and Spontaneous Currents in Clean Superconducting Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26005)
*Jian-Lin Li,Chien-Te Wu,Klaus Halterman*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过自洽求解 Bogoliubov-de Gennes 与 Maxwell 方程，研究可调自旋阀铁磁/铁磁/超导异质结构中电磁邻近效应与超导邻近效应的相互作用，揭示自发电流的产生机制及其对奇频三态关联的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁体与超导体接触时会产生超导邻近效应（superconducting proximity effect），但以往研究多关注配对振幅与自旋关联，从电动力学角度出发理解自发电流（spontaneous currents）何时及如何产生同样重要。因此需要探究电磁邻近效应与常规超导邻近效应之间的相互作用。

Method: 研究洁净的铁磁/铁磁/超导（ferromagnet/ferromagnet/superconductor）自旋阀异质结构，其中两层铁磁的相对角度可调；采用耦合格博-德热纳方程（Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations）与麦克斯韦方程（Maxwell equations）的自洽数值求解方法，实现可分辨原子尺度物理的微观描述。

Result: 在弱交换场与强交换场两种情形下，中间铁磁层在交换场强度变化时对产生显著自发电流起主导作用；电磁响应延伸至整个超导层，与短程的反向邻近效应（inverse proximity effect）形成鲜明对比。在非共线构型中，电磁邻近效应重塑局域磁场取向并减小两铁磁层磁场间的角度失配；长程奇频三态振幅（odd-frequency triplet amplitudes）因轨道响应而改变，其准粒子态因动量空间结构的改变而受到修正。

Conclusion: 该框架可自然推广至其他超导自旋电子学（superconducting spintronics）系统，可能包括约瑟夫森结（Josephson junctions）及交变磁体/超导异质结构（altermagnet/superconductor heterostructures）。

Abstract: When ferromagnets are brought into contact with a superconductor, superconducting proximity effects give rise to a variety of interesting phenomena, including oscillatory singlet Cooper-pair amplitudes and long-ranged odd-frequency triplet correlations induced by the exchange interactions in the ferromagnets. From an electrodynamic perspective, however, it is equally important to understand when and how spontaneous currents can emerge. To investigate the interplay between electromagnetic and conventional superconducting proximity effects, we study clean ferromagnet/ferromagnet/superconductor spin-valve heterostructures in which the relative angle between the two ferromagnetic layers can be tuned. Our approach is based on a self-consistent numerical solution of the coupled Bogoliubov-de Gennes and Maxwell equations, providing a microscopic description capable of resolving physics on atomic length scales. Several notable features emerge. In both the weak- and strong-exchange-field regimes, the central ferromagnetic layer plays a dominant role in generating sizable spontaneous currents when its exchange-field strength is varied. We further find that the resulting electromagnetic response extends across the entire superconducting layer, in sharp contrast to the short-ranged inverse proximity effect. In noncollinear configurations, the electromagnetic proximity effect also reconfigures the local magnetic-field orientation and reduces the angular mismatch between the fields in the two ferromagnetic layers. The long-ranged odd-frequency triplet amplitudes are consequently modified by the orbital response, which alters the underlying quasiparticle states by changing their momentum-space structure. Finally, our framework can be naturally generalized to other superconducting spintronic systems, possibly including Josephson junctions and altermagnet/superconductor heterostructures.

</details>


### [152] [The observation of bulk superconductivity in Rhombohedral ReO3 under pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26628)
*S. Huyan,R. F. S. Penacchio,L-L. Wang,J. Schmidt,D. Zhang,B. Lavina,Z. Li,R. A. Ribeiro,T. J. Slade,J. Zhao,S. L. Morelhão,P. C. Canfield,S. L. Bud'ko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在高达 80 GPa 的静水压条件下，ReO3 经历由立方 Pm-3m 到 Im-3、再到菱方 R-3c 的结构演化，并在 R-3c 主导的压力区间内呈现体超导性，最高起始 Tc 约 17.5 K；进一步加压导致晶格重构并抑制超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化物中超导性如何由晶格几何所促成，仍是核心未解问题；本研究以 ReO3 为对象，试图揭示压力诱导的结构转变与超导电性之间的关联机制。

Method: 结合同步辐射 X 射线衍射（synchrotron X-ray diffraction）、拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）、电输运（electrical transport）、直流磁化率（dc magnetic susceptibility）测量与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），并采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）与焓（enthalpy）计算分析电子-声子耦合。

Result: 观测到由立方 Pm-3m 到 Im-3、再到菱方 R-3c 的连续压力诱导结构转变，R-3c 相伴随体超导，onset Tc 最高约 17.5 K；磁化率与俘获磁通（trapped-flux）测量表明体超导局限于 R-3c 主导的压力区间。DFT 显示 hR24-R-3c 结构存在强电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling），低频 Re 振动与高频氧相关声子共同贡献，计算 Tc 与实验相符。约 35-40 GPa 以上出现对称性降低的相变，衍射谱可由 R32 类平均畸变描述，而焓计算指向由声子不稳定性驱动的 mP16-P2/c 结构；该高配位相态密度（density of states）降低、电子-声子耦合减弱、计算 Tc 显著下降，解释了高压下体超导的消失。

Conclusion: 体超导性稳定于菱方结构之中，压力诱导的晶格重构通过协同的 Re-O 晶格动力学（Re-O lattice dynamics）增强电子-声子耦合，从而支撑超导；更高压力下发生的高配位重构则削弱该耦合并抑制超导。

Abstract: Understanding how lattice geometry enables superconductivity in oxides remains a central challenge. Here, we report a systematic study of ReO3 up to 80 GPa. Synchrotron X-ray diffraction, Raman spectroscopy, electrical transport, dc magnetic susceptibility, and first-principles calculations establish a sequence of pressure-induced structural transitions, from cubic Pm-3m to Im-3 followed by the emergence of a rhombohedral R-3c phase accompanied by bulk superconductivity with a maximum Tc, onset ~17.5 K. DC magnetic susceptibility and trapped-flux magnetization measurements demonstrate that bulk superconductivity is confined to the pressure range where R-3c phase is dominant. Density functional theory calculations show strong electron-phonon coupling in the hR24-R-3c structure, with substantial contributions from both low-frequency Re vibrations and high-frequency oxygen-related phonon modes, yielding a calculated Tc comparable with the experiment. Upon further compression above ~35-40 GPa, powder X-ray diffraction results indicate a symmetry-lowering structural transition. Whereas the experimental diffraction patterns can be best described by a rhombohedral-derived R32-like average distortion with effective enlargement of the crystallographic unit cell, enthalpy calculations identify a lower-symmetry mP16-P2/c structure driven by phonon instability of the R-3c phase. This reconstructed higher-coordination phase has a reduced density of states at the Fermi level, weaker electron-phonon coupling, and a much lower calculated Tc, providing a microscopic explanation for the loss of bulk superconductivity in the higher-pressure phase. These results show that bulk superconductivity is stabilized within the rhombohedral structure, where pressure-induced lattice reconstruction supports enhanced electron-phonon coupling through cooperative Re-O lattice dynamics.

</details>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [How do incorrect ligands help detect a correct ligand?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25708)
*Yan-Ru Chen,Kwan-tai Leung,Hsuan-Yi Chen*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个由少量不可移动受体组成的最小模型（minimal model），通过最近邻结合态耦合（binding-state coupling）与动力学校对（kinetic proofreading）机制，实现对极少量正确配体（correct ligand）的快速且特异性响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于T细胞受体（T cell receptor）在仅存在少量激动剂配体（agonist ligand）时仍能产生响应的现象，作者希望构建能实现类似性能的简化模型。

Method: 模型由一小组不可移动受体构成，受体与环境中的两类配体（正确/错误）可逆结合，错误配体结合强度略弱；受体间存在最近邻结合态耦合，且结合态/游离态受体被特定酶激活/去激活，其速率支持动力学校对（kinetic proofreading）。

Result: 在一定结合态耦合强度范围内，仅错误配体无法激活受体；而仅一个正确配体结合即足以通过诱导其他受体结合错误配体来促进其激活。响应时间（response time）与信号放大（signal amplification）随耦合强度增加而增大，直至达到实现最快速、最灵敏响应的最优区间。

Conclusion: 结果提示了一种可能机制，可用于解释生物与人工系统中亚细胞尺度（subcellular scale）受体对极少量正确配体的快速且特异性响应。

Abstract: Intrigued by the response of T cell receptors to the presence of a few agonist ligands, we propose a minimal model that can achieve similar performance. The model consists of a small cluster of immobile receptors that bind reversibly to two types (correct/incorrect) of ligands in the environment, with slightly weaker binding strength for the incorrect one. It features binding-state coupling between nearest-neighbor receptors, and receptors in the bound/free states are activated/deactivated by specific enzymes, with rates that allow kinetic proofreading. It is found that, for a range of binding-state coupling strength, incorrect ligands alone cannot activate the receptors, but the binding of merely one correct ligand to a receptor is sufficient to promote the activation of other receptors via induced binding to incorrect ligands. Both response time and signal amplification increase as the receptor binding-state coupling strength increases until it reaches an optimal range to achieve the most rapid and sensitive response. These results suggest a possible mechanism for a speedy and specific response of receptors to very few correct ligands in biological and artificial systems at the subcellular scale.

</details>


<div id='math.AC'></div>

# math.AC [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Poincaré Duality and Quadratic Refinements over Laurent Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25767)
*Błażej Ruba,Bowen Yang*

Main category: math.AC

TL;DR: 该文为洛朗多项式环上非退化半双线性配对（sesquilinear pairings）的缺陷发展了一套庞加莱对偶（Poincaré duality）理论，以扇（fan）诱导的无穷远球面三角剖分为构造核心，并将其应用于平移不变泡利稳定子码（Pauli stabilizer codes），证明了高维编织配对（braiding pairings）的非退化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对洛朗多项式环上配对缺陷缺乏系统性对偶理论的问题，本文旨在建立相应的庞加莱对偶框架，并为高维拓扑码中的编织配对与拓扑自旋（topological spin）提供理论基础与几何解释。

Method: 构造特征模（character module）的一种新型平坦消解（flat resolution），其来源为与扇（fan）相关联的无穷远球面三角剖分；在该消解上利用杯积（cup product）将球面上的庞加莱对偶转化为缺陷模之间的典范配对。在中间次数上，借助球面在対径作用（antipodal action）下的等变上同调（equivariant cohomology）构造特异的二次加细（quadratic refinements），并以射影空间（projective space）有效替代球面，作为除以二的几何替代。

Result: 该理论应用于平移不变泡利稳定子码时，确立了高维编织配对的非退化性；同时将二维的 T 型结（T-junction）拓扑自旋公式推广到高维，并赋予其几何解释。

Conclusion: 所构建的庞加莱对偶（Poincaré duality）理论为洛朗多项式环上的配对缺陷提供了统一框架，其消解构造与等变方法使得球面对偶可转化为缺陷模配对，并在高维稳定子码中给出编织配对非退化性与拓扑自旋公式的推广及几何诠释。

Abstract: We develop a Poincaré duality theory for defects of nondegenerate sesquilinear pairings over Laurent polynomial rings. A key ingredient is a novel flat resolution of the character module, constructed from a triangulation of the sphere at infinity associated with a fan. The cup product on this resolution turns Poincaré duality on the sphere into canonical pairings between the resulting defect modules. In middle degrees, we construct distinguished quadratic refinements using equivariant cohomology of the sphere with the antipodal action. The effective replacement of the sphere with a projective space provides a geometric substitute for division by two. Applied to translation-invariant Pauli stabilizer codes, our results establish the nondegeneracy of higher-dimensional braiding pairings. They extend the two-dimensional T-junction formula for topological spin to higher dimensions, while giving it a geometric interpretation.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Experiment $\leftrightarrow$ lattice QCD: understanding high-temperature QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25191)
*Bedangadas Mohanty*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述相对论重离子碰撞（relativistic heavy-ion collisions）与格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）之间的互动，讨论QCD物态方程、输运性质、守恒荷涨落及临界点寻找等议题，并强调平衡态格点计算与有限、动态实验系统对比时的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论重离子碰撞可实验研究极端温度与密度下的强相互作用物质，而格点QCD为非微扰区平衡性质提供第一性原理方法；二者结合对确定QCD物质性质与相结构至关重要。

Method: 以综述方式讨论若干格点QCD与实验联系尤为有效的领域，包括QCD物态方程及其在重离子碰撞流体动力学描述中的作用、夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）的输运性质、守恒荷涨落与实验累积量（cumulants）的关系，以及QCD相图中临界点（critical point）的寻找；并特别关注将平衡态格点计算与实验系统对比时的局限。

Result: 近期进展日益允许在更宽的温度与重子化学势（baryon chemical potential）范围内对QCD热力学进行定量检验；RHIC与LHC的持续实验计划，以及FAIR、NICA与电子-离子对撞机（Electron-Ion Collider）的未来测量，为格点QCD、现象学与实验之间更紧密的互动提供重要机会。

Conclusion: 格点QCD、现象学与实验的互动已成为确立QCD物质性质与相结构的核心；尽管将平衡态格点计算与重离子碰撞所产生有限、动态且受实验约束的系统对比存在局限，未来实验计划有望进一步深化这一互动。

Abstract: Relativistic heavy-ion collisions provide a unique experimental opportunity to study strongly interacting matter at extreme temperature and density, while lattice quantum chromodynamics (QCD) offers a first-principles approach to the equilibrium properties of such matter in the non-perturbative regime. The interplay between experiment and lattice QCD has therefore become central to establishing the properties and phase structure of QCD matter. Selected areas where this connection is particularly informative are discussed, including the QCD equation of state and its role in hydrodynamic descriptions of heavy-ion collisions, transport properties of the quark-gluon plasma, conserved-charge fluctuations and their relation to experimental cumulants, and the ongoing search for a critical point in the QCD phase diagram. Particular attention is given to the limitations involved in confronting equilibrium lattice calculations with the finite, dynamical and experimentally constrained systems produced in heavy-ion collisions. Recent developments increasingly allow quantitative tests of QCD thermodynamics over an extended range of temperature and baryon chemical potential. The continuing experimental programmes at RHIC and the LHC, together with future measurements at FAIR, NICA and the Electron-Ion Collider, provide important opportunities for an increasingly close interplay between lattice QCD, phenomenology and experiment.

</details>

### [156] [Search for boosted vector boson scattering $W^{\pm}W^{\pm}H$ production in final states with same-sign leptons and $b$-jets in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=$13 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25153)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS 利用 LHC Run 2 的 140 fb$^{-1}$ 质子-质子碰撞数据，搜索同号 W 玻色子对与希格斯玻色子伴随两个前向喷流的电弱产生过程（$W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$），未观测到显著超出标准模型的信号，并对夸克四次相互作用耦合修正参数 $\kappa_{WW}$ 给出排除区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该过程对四重 $WWHH$ 相互作用（quartic $WWHH$ interaction）具有直接敏感性，可检验标准模型（Standard Model）预测并约束可能的偏离。

Method: 使用 ATLAS 探测器在质心能量 $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV 下记录的完整 Run 2 数据集，积分亮度为 140 fb$^{-1}$。选取两个 W 玻色子均轻子衰变的末态，要求两个同号带电轻子、缺失横向动量及一个希格斯玻色子候选者，后者由高动量衰变为 $b\bar{b}$ 对并重建为单个大半径喷流（large-radius jet）。偏离通过耦合修正参数 $\kappa_{WW}$ 参数化，$\kappa_{WW}=1$ 对应标准模型。

Result: 未观测到显著超出标准模型预测的过量。在 95% 置信水平（confidence level）下给出 $W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$ 产生截面随 $\kappa_{WW}$ 变化的上限；被排除的观测（预期）$\kappa_{WW}$ 区间为 $-2.1 < \kappa_{WW} < 4.1$（$-1.9 < \kappa_{WW} < 3.9$）。

Conclusion: 结果与标准模型预测一致，未发现四重 $WWHH$ 相互作用显著偏离标准模型的证据，并对 $\kappa_{WW}$ 施加了实验约束。

Abstract: A search for the electroweak production of a Higgs boson in association with a same-sign $W$ boson pair and two forward jets ($W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$) is presented. The full Run 2 proton-proton collision dataset recorded at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider is used, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The search targets events where both $W$ bosons decay leptonically, yielding a final state with two same-sign charged leptons, missing transverse momentum, and a Higgs boson candidate. The high-momentum Higgs-boson candidate decaying into a $b\bar{b}$ pair is reconstructed as a single large-radius jet. This process provides direct sensitivity to the quartic $WWHH$ interaction. Possible deviations from the Standard Model prediction are parameterized through the coupling modifier $κ_{WW}$, where $κ_{WW}=1$ corresponds to the Standard Model. No significant excess over the Standard Model prediction is observed. Upper limits at the 95% confidence level are set on the $W^{\pm}W^{\pm}Hjj$ production cross-section as a function of $κ_{WW}$. Observed (expected) values of $κ_{WW}$ outside the interval $-2.1 < κ_{WW} < 4.1$ ($-1.9 < κ_{WW} < 3.9$) are excluded.

</details>

Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 探测器收集的 2712.4 ± 14.3 百万 ψ(3686) 事件，对 ψ(3686) 辐射衰变中的 ΛΛ̄ 系统进行模型无关分波分析，发现阈值增强结构并观测到新赝标量共振 η(2600)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 ψ(3686) 辐射衰变中产生的 ΛΛ̄（baryon-antibaryon）系统，以理解重子-反重子阈值动力学（threshold dynamics）及赝标量轻强子谱（pseudoscalar light hadron spectroscopy）。

Method: 使用 BESIII 探测器收集的 2712.4 ± 14.3 百万 ψ(3686) 事件，对 ψ(3686) 辐射衰变中的 ΛΛ̄ 系统开展模型无关的部分波分析（model-independent partial wave analysis）。

Result: 发现一个显著的阈值增强结构（threshold enhancement structure），主要由 ¹S₀ 与 ³P₀ 分波主导，分别对应 J^{PC} = 0^{-+} 与 0^{++}。此外，在 ¹S₀ 分波中观测到新赝标量共振 η(2600)，其质量与先前报道的 X(2600) 态一致，为迄今观测到的最重轻介子。

Conclusion: 上述结果增进了对重子-反重子阈值动力学与赝标量轻强子谱的理解。

Abstract: Using $2712.4 \pm 14.3$ million $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, the $Λ\barΛ$ system produced in $ψ(3686)$ radiative decays is studied. A model-independent partial wave analysis reveals a significant threshold enhancement structure dominated by the $^1S_0$ and $^3P_0$ partial waves, corresponding to $J^{PC} = 0^{-+}$ and $0^{++}$, respectively. In addition, a new pseudoscalar resonance, designated as $η(2600)$, is observed in the $^1S_0$ partial wave with a mass value consistent with the previously reported $X(2600)$ state, which represents the heaviest light meson observed to date. These results enhance our understanding of baryon-antibaryon threshold dynamics and the pseudoscalar light hadron spectroscopy.

</details>

Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于LHCb实验的质子-质子对撞数据，在美-奇异（beauty-strange）扇区观测到一个新共振态，全局显著性超过七个标准差。该共振态具有窄自然宽度与显著的质量亏损，是首个被发现的非传统单美夸克强子（nonconventional single-beauty hadron），为强相互作用手征动力学（chiral dynamics）与重夸克自旋对称性（heavy-quark spin symmetry）提供关键证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统夸克模型（quark model）将强子（hadrons）仅归类为夸克-反夸克对构成的介子（mesons）与三个价夸克构成的重子（baryons），无法解释强力的复杂手征动力学（chiral dynamics）及奇异多夸克强子（exotic multi-quark hadrons）的出现。此类非常规现象的实验证据此前主要出现在粲（charm）扇区，而开美（open-beauty）系统一直是检验这些机制普适性的待探索前沿。

Method: 利用欧洲核子研究组织（CERN）大型强子对撞机底夸克实验（LHCb）记录的质子-质子对撞数据，在美-奇异扇区中搜寻新共振态。分析报告了一个全局显著性超过七个标准差的共振信号。

Result: 观测到一个新共振态，其具有窄自然宽度（narrow natural width），且相对于传统夸克模型预言存在显著的质量亏损（mass deficit）。

Conclusion: 该结果为首次观测到的非传统单美夸克强子，为理解强相互作用的手征动力学以及奇异（exotic）领域中的重夸克自旋对称性提供了关键线索。

Abstract: As the essential building blocks of visible matter, hadrons have traditionally been classified by the quark model as mesons composed of quark-antiquark pairs and baryons built from three valence quarks. Yet, this classical picture does not account for the intricate chiral dynamics of the strong force and the emergence of exotic multi-quark hadrons. While experimental evidence for such unconventional dynamics has surfaced in the charm sector, the open-beauty system remains the long-sought frontier for testing the universality of these mechanisms. Here the observation of a new resonance in the beauty-strange sector with a global significance exceeding seven standard deviations is reported using proton-proton collision data recorded by the Large Hadron Collider beauty (LHCb) experiment at the European Organization for Nuclear Research (CERN). It exhibits a narrow natural width and a substantial mass deficit compared to the conventional quark model predictions. This result marks the first observation of a nonconventional single-beauty hadron, providing crucial insights into the chiral dynamics of the strong interaction and heavy-quark spin symmetry in the exotic domain.

</details>


### [159] [Fast Timing at Colliders: From Scintillator Hodoscopes to MicroPattern Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25358)
*Sebastian N. White*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该报告综述了对撞机（collider）中带电粒子定时（charged particle timing）技术的发展，并将其划分为三个时代。当前高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity LHC, HL-LHC）时代，双阈值定时（dual-threshold timing）技术可显著简化幅度游走（amplitude walk）校正，并基于为 CMS 升级设计的 TOFHIR2C ASIC 验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定时阵列（timing arrays）的演进受光度（luminosity）与多重数（multiplicity）驱动，同时需重新审视信号采集与处理方式及校准条件的变化。高辐射环境下工作条件多变，校准不再是可一次完成的操作，因此需要适应新时代的定时技术。

Method: 采用选择性综述（select overview）方式梳理对撞机带电粒子定时的三个时代，重点讨论当前时代（HL-LHC 升级）的双阈值定时技术；并利用可为 CMS 升级设计的 TOFHIR2C ASIC 的配置能力，演示该技术的效果。

Result: 双阈值定时能够显著简化幅度游走的关键校正，该校正通常跨越比目标分辨率（target resolution）大一个数量级的时间范围。TOFHIR2C ASIC 可被配置以提供双阈值定时功能。

Conclusion: 双阈值定时技术适用于当前的 HL-LHC 时代，TOFHIR2C ASIC 的该能力为其提供了实现途径。

Abstract: We give a select overview of charged particle timing at colliders. Roughly speaking, three eras capture the evolution of timing arrays. While luminosity and multiplicity dictate the detector technologies, there is also a need to review the various approaches to signal capture and processing, and their role in changing conditions for calibration.
  For the current era--the High-Luminosity LHC upgrades--the technique of dual-threshold timing can significantly simplify the crucial correction for amplitude walk.This correction often spans a time range an order of magnitude larger than the target resolution. Furthermore, because operating conditions vary in the high radiation environment, calibration can no longer be thought of as a one-time procedure.
  The TOFHIR2C ASIC designed for a CMS upgrade can be configured to provide dual threshold timing. In this report, we use this capability to demonstrate the power of this technique suitable for our new era.}

</details>


### [160] [Measurement of the $Ω_b^-$ baryon lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25370)

Zhang,J. Zhao,M. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 LHCb 实验 Run 2 的 6 fb⁻¹ pp 碰撞数据，测量 Ω_b⁻ 与 Ξ_b⁻ 重子的寿命比 r_τ，得到 r_τ = 1.109 ± 0.055 ± 0.010，并由此推出 Ω_b⁻ 寿命 τ = 1.751 ± 0.089 ± 0.022 ps，精度较此前世界平均值提升约两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ω_b⁻ 重子寿命的实验精度此前相对有限，需要更精确的测量以检验理论预测。重夸克展开（heavy quark expansion, HQE）框架对 Ω_b⁻ 与 Ξ_b⁻ 的寿命比给出预言，因此高精度测量该比值可用于检验理论。

Method: 利用 LHCb 实验在 LHC Run 2（2015–2018）采集的对应积分亮度 6 fb⁻¹ 的 pp 碰撞数据，在两组衰变模式中测量寿命比 r_τ：一组为 (Ω_b⁻, Ξ_b⁻) → (Ω_c⁰π⁻, Ξ_c⁰π⁻)（含 Ω_c⁰/Ξ_c⁰ → pK⁻K⁻π⁺），另一组为 (Ω_b⁻, Ξ_b⁻) → (J/ψΩ⁻, J/ψΞ⁻)（含 Ω⁻/Ξ⁻ → Λ⁰K⁻/Λ⁰π⁻、Λ⁰ → pπ⁻、J/ψ → μ⁺μ⁻）。测量值经平均后与 Run 1（2011–2012）在相同衰变模式下的结果联合。

Result: 联合 Run 1 与 Run 2 数据得到 r_τ = 1.109 ± 0.055 ± 0.010（统计误差与系统误差）。结合已知的 Ξ_b⁻ 寿命，得到 Ω_b⁻ 寿命 τ_{Ω_b⁻} = 1.751 ± 0.089 ± 0.022 ps。该测量将 Ω_b⁻ 寿命的精度较此前世界平均值提升约两倍。

Conclusion: r_τ 的测量值与重夸克展开（heavy quark expansion）框架下最新的理论预测相符，表明该理论框架在描述 Ω_b⁻ 与 Ξ_b⁻ 寿命比方面得到实验支持。

Abstract: The lifetime ratio ${r_τ\equivτ_{Ω_b^-}/τ_{Ξ_b^-}}$ between the ${Ω_b^-}$ and ${Ξ_b^-}$ baryons is measured using a sample of $pp$ collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 6 fb$^{-1}$ and collected by the LHCb experiment during LHC Run 2 (2015$-$2018). The ratio $r_τ$ is measured in two sets of decays modes, ${ {(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)\to(Ω_c^0π^-,Ξ_c^0π^-)}}$ and ${(Ω_b^-,Ξ_b^-)\to(J/ψΩ^-, J/ψΞ^-)}$, with ${(Ω_c^0,Ξ_c^0)\to pK^-K^-π^+}$, ${(Ω^-,Ξ^-)\to(Λ^0 K^-,Λ^0π^-)}$, ${Λ^0\to pπ^-}$ and $J/ψ\toμ^+μ^-$. The measured $r_τ$ values are averaged and combined with Run 1 (2011$-$2012) measurements in the same decay modes to obtain ${r_τ = 1.109\pm0.055\pm0.010}$. Multiplying by the known ${Ξ_b^-}$ lifetime results in the ${Ω_b^-}$ lifetime ${τ_{Ω_b^-} = 1.751\pm0.089\pm0.022~{\rm ps}}$, where the uncertainties are statistical and systematic. This measurement improves on the precision of the $Ω_b^-$ lifetime by about a factor of two over the previous world average. The value of $r_τ$ is in agreement with the most recent theoretical predictions from the heavy quark expansion framework.

</details>


### [161] [Spin-spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ pair production at the SPD experiment at NICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25383)
*D. Gubachev,A. Guskov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对 NICA 上 SPD 实验中 $pp$ 与 $dd$ 碰撞（$\sqrt{s_{NN}}\leq27$ GeV）的 $\Lambda\bar\Lambda$ 自旋关联开展可行性研究，基于 SU(6) 与 Burkardt–Jaffe 模型估算关联量 $P_{\Lambda\bar\Lambda}$，并通过全模拟评估探测器适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STAR 协作组的测量表明 $\Lambda\bar\Lambda$ 对的自旋关联（spin correlations）对 $s\bar{s}$ 对的 spin 结构及强子化（hadronization）过程中的自旋转移具有敏感性，结合 CMS 更高能量结果，为研究 parton 到 hadron 过渡中的 spin 动力学开辟了新途径，SPD 实验在此方向具备研究潜力。

Method: 在 $\sqrt{s_{NN}}\leq27$ GeV 的 $pp$ 与 $dd$ 碰撞条件下，针对 spin-triplet $s\bar{s}$ 对，在 SU(6) 与 Burkardt–Jaffe 模型框架内估算期望关联 $P_{\Lambda\bar\Lambda}$；分析该过程的运动学特性，并借助全模拟（full simulation）评估探测器对该类测量的适用性。

Result: 给出了 $P_{\Lambda\bar\Lambda}$ 在 SU(6) 与 Burkardt–Jaffe 模型下的预期估计，并结合运动学分析与全模拟结果评估了 SPD 探测器开展此类自旋关联测量的可行性。

Conclusion: SPD 实验为研究非微扰（non-perturbative）与微扰（perturbative）$s\bar{s}$ 产生机制的相对贡献提供了机会；若使用极化束流（polarized beams），还可研究初始态极化（initial-state polarization）在自旋相关 partonic 动力学中的作用。

Abstract: Recent measurements by the STAR collaboration have demonstrated that spin correlations of $Λ\barΛ$ pairs provide sensitivity to the spin structure of $s\bar{s}$ pairs and to spin transfer during hadronization.These results, together with recent CMS measurements at higher energies, open a new avenue for studying spin dynamics in the transition from partons to hadrons.
  A feasibility study of spin-spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ pairs at the SPD experiment at NICA is presented for $pp$ and $dd$ collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}\leq27$~GeV. The expected correlation $P_{Λ\barΛ}$ is estimated within the SU(6) and Burkardt--Jaffe models for a spin-triplet $s\bar{s}$ pair. The kinematic properties of the process are analysed, and the suitability of the detector for such measurements is evaluated using a full simulation. The SPD experiment provides an opportunity to investigate the relative contributions of non-perturbative and perturbative $s\bar{s}$-production mechanisms and, with polarized beams, to study the role of initial-state polarization in spin-dependent partonic dynamics.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究利用超级神冈（Super-Kamiokande, SK）探测器 0.401 兆吨·年的曝光数据，对三核子衰变模式 $^{16}\text{O}(ppp) \rightarrow ^{13}\text{C}\, \pi^+ \pi^+ e^+$ 进行搜寻，给出新的部分寿命下限 $4.2 \times 10^{32}$ 年，较此前实验限制提升六个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前实验对该三核子衰变模式的约束相对宽松，需要更灵敏的搜寻以提升寿命下限。SK 的大统计量与深度学习事件分类能力的结合，为获得更严格限制提供了条件。

Method: 基于 SK 探测器五个运行阶段（SK-I 至 SK-V，1996--2020）共 0.401 兆吨·年的曝光数据展开搜寻；采用带有注意力机制（attention mechanism）的卷积神经网络（convolutional neural network, CNN）聚焦切伦科夫环（Cherenkov ring）图案中最相关的区域，以增强事件分类。这是 CNN 首次应用于 SK 的核子衰变（nucleon decay）搜寻。

Result: 得到三核子衰变模式 $^{16}\text{O}(ppp) \rightarrow ^{13}\text{C}\, \pi^+ \pi^+ e^+$ 的部分寿命（partial lifetime）新下限为 $4.2 \times 10^{32}$ 年，相比先前实验限制改善六个数量级。

Conclusion: 带有注意力机制的 CNN 有效提升了事件分类与搜寻灵敏度；SK 的大数据集增强了统计效力，使得该研究能够给出比以往实验更严格的三核子衰变寿命约束。

Abstract: We report a new partial lifetime limit of $4.2 \times 10^{32}$ years for the trinucleon decay mode $^{16}\text{O}(ppp) \rightarrow ^{13}\text{C} π^+ π^+ e^+$, obtained from a search conducted using the Super-Kamiokande detector with 0.401 megaton-years of exposure across five operational periods (SK-I: 1996--2001, SK-II: 2002--2005, SK-III: 2006--2008, SK-IV: 2008--2018, SK-V: 2019--2020). This represents an improvement of six orders of magnitude over previous experimental constraints. The analysis utilizes a convolutional neural network (CNN) incorporating an attention mechanism---a computational technique that enables the model to focus on the most relevant regions of Cherenkov ring patterns---to enhance event classification, thereby improving the sensitivity of the search. This is the first application of a CNN to a nucleon decay search in Super-Kamiokande. Furthermore, the large dataset available in Super-Kamiokande (hereafter "SK") strengthens the statistical power of the study, enabling a more stringent constraint than those set by prior experiments.

</details>


### [163] [Feasibility Study of $e^+e^-\to η_cJ/ψ$ Production and Fully-Charmed Tetraquark Searches at STCF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25870)
*Zhuojuan Dong,Xiaorong Zhou,Xiaorui Lv,Zhengyun You,Jin Zhang,Cong Geng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该文基于STCF的蒙特卡洛模拟，评估在e⁺e⁻→η_c J/ψ道中全粲矢量四夸克候选态T₄c的预期显著性，在三种双电子宽度假设下得到5.1σ、10.6σ与20.5σ的单标记显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟议的高亮度超级陶粲装置（Super Tau-Charm Facility, STCF）为奇异强子（exotic hadrons）的阈值研究提供洁净实验环境，因此有必要评估其对全粲四夸克态（fully-charmed tetraquark）候选者的探测能力。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations），对√s=6.71至6.79 GeV进行能量扫描，每点积分亮度100 fb⁻¹；以J/ψ轻子衰变的高效率单标记（single-tag, ST）重建为主要策略，并以η_c→K⁺K⁻π⁰的双标记（double-tag, DT）重建作为独立交叉检验。

Result: 由于信号截面取决于尚不确定的双电子宽度（dielectron width）Γ_ee，在Γ_ee=0.25、0.5、1 eV三种基准假设下，预期ST显著性分别为5.1σ、10.6σ与20.5σ。

Conclusion: 结果表明STCF可在阈值附近对全粲四夸克态提供有意义的灵敏度（sensitivity）。

Abstract: The proposed high-luminosity Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) offers a clean experimental environment for threshold studies of exotic hadrons. In this Letter, we evaluate the expected significance for the fully-charmed vector tetraquark candidate $T_{4c}$ in the $e^+e^- \to η_c J/ψ$ channel at the STCF. Using Monte Carlo simulations of an energy scan from $\sqrt{s}=6.71$ to $6.79~\mathrm{GeV}$ with $100~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ per point, we adopt a high-efficiency single-tag (ST) reconstruction of $J/ψ$ leptonic decays as the primary strategy, with a double-tag (DT) reconstruction of $η_c \to K^+K^-π^0$ retained as an independent cross-check. Because the signal cross section is governed by the still-uncertain dielectron width $Γ_{ee}$, we consider three benchmark hypotheses, $Γ_{ee}=0.25, 0.5, 1~\mathrm{eV}$. The corresponding expected ST significances are $5.1\,σ$, $10.6\,σ$, and $20.5\,σ$, respectively. These results indicate that the STCF can provide meaningful sensitivity to fully-charmed tetraquark states near threshold.

</details>


### [164] [Future Muon Physics Experiments: Muon Beams and Experimental Apparatus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25943)
*Yi Yuan,Leyun Gao,Jian Tang,Qiang Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了全球缪子束（muon-beam）设施现状与升级计划，并按三类实验方向梳理所需实验装置：带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）搜寻、精密测量（磁偶极矩/电偶极矩、缪素光谱、缪子原子光谱）、以及缪子散射项目（MUonE、NA64μ、PKMu）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缪子（muon）被视为在精度前沿与强度前沿探测基础物理的有力探针，因此有必要系统评估现有及规划的缪子束设施与配套实验条件，以推动下一代灵敏度与精度。

Method: 以综述形式进行，围绕三类实验方向组织内容：CLFV搜寻（稀有缪子衰变、缪子-电子在原子核中转换、缪素-反缪素转换）；精密测量（缪子磁偶极矩与电偶极矩、缪素光谱、缪子原子光谱）；缪子散射（MUonE测定强子真空极化、NA64μ缺失动量搜寻不可见暗区、分阶段PKMu）。

Result: 梳理了各方向的科学目标：CLFV实验检验带电轻子味守恒；精密测量检验标准模型（Standard Model）与束缚态QED，并确定基本常数及核电荷与磁化分布；散射项目涵盖强子真空极化测定、不可见暗区搜寻，以及缪子亲和多暗物质、轻中介子、LFV缪子-电子过程与共振湮灭中的原子效应。

Conclusion: 实现下一代灵敏度与精度依赖于束流强度、时间结构、纯度与相空间控制，以及束线（beamline）与探测器设计、本底抑制（background suppression）和相应实验技术。

Abstract: Muons are powerful probes of fundamental physics at the precision and intensity frontiers. We review the status and planned upgrades of muon-beam facilities worldwide, together with the experimental apparatus required across three experimental categories. Charged-lepton flavor violation (CLFV) searches test charged-lepton flavor conservation through rare muon decays, muon-to-electron conversion in nuclei, and muonium--antimuonium conversion. Precision measurements of the muon magnetic and electric dipole moments, muonium spectroscopy, and muonic-atom spectroscopy test the Standard Model and bound-state QED and determine fundamental constants and nuclear charge and magnetization distributions. Muon-scattering programs include the MUonE determination of hadronic vacuum polarization, NA64$μ$ missing-momentum searches for invisible dark sectors, and the phased PKMu program, which investigates muonphilic dark matter, light mediators, LFV muon--electron processes, and atomic effects in resonant annihilation. We emphasize the beam intensity, timing, purity, and phase-space control, as well as beamline and detector design, background suppression, and experimental techniques needed to achieve next-generation sensitivity and precision.

</details>


### [165] [Improving the CMS High Level Trigger tracking at the HL-LHC with novel and evolved heterogeneous algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26376)
*Mario Masciovecchio*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该摘要聚焦 LHC 与高亮度 LHC（HL-LHC）实验中带电粒子径迹重建（track reconstruction）的计算瓶颈，提出在异构计算系统上组合 mkFit、Patatrack 与 LST 算法，并借助机器学习（machine-learning, ML）技术提升 CMS 高阶触发器（HLT）的径迹重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电粒子径迹重建是 LHC 实验事件重建链中计算量最重的任务之一；HL-LHC 的预测显示，单线程 CPU 算法所需计算资源将超出可预期获得的资源量。因此需要在包含多核 CPU 与 GPU 的异构系统上采用新颖且演进的径迹重建算法，在保持最佳重建效率的同时最大化计算性能。

Method: 在 HL-LHC 的 CMS 高阶触发器（HLT）背景下，利用 mkFit 算法在 CPU 上的并行化与向量化特性执行模式识别（pattern recognition），其种子径迹（seed tracks）由设计为完全可并行且硬件无关的 Patatrack 与线段跟踪（Line Segment Tracking, LST）算法产生。Patatrack 已用于 LHC Run 3 的 HLT 像素径迹重建，LST 则是新近集成于 CMS 软件、面向 HL-LHC CMS 探测器外层径迹仪（outer tracker）的算法。同时采用深度神经网络与多目标粒子群优化（multi-objective particle swarm optimization）等 ML 技术抑制重复与误重建径迹。

Result: 给出了 HL-LHC 条件下 CMS HLT 径迹重建的最新性能（state-of-the-art performance），该性能由 mkFit、Patatrack 与 LST 三种算法组合实现。

Conclusion: 摘要指出还将展示进一步改进的前景，重点关注 ML 技术在 CMS 径迹重建中的应用。整体目标是在异构计算系统中兼顾计算性能与重建效率，以应对 HL-LHC 的计算资源压力。

Abstract: Charged particle track reconstruction is one of the heaviest computational tasks in the event reconstruction chain at LHC experiments. Furthermore, projections for the High Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) show that the required computing resources for single-threaded CPU algorithms will exceed those that are expected to be available. It follows that experiments at the HL-LHC will need to employ novel and evolved track reconstruction algorithms, within heterogeneous computing systems that include many-core CPUs as well as GPUs, in the attempt to maximize the computational performance while retaining the best possible reconstruction efficiency. In the context of the CMS High Level Trigger (HLT) at the HL-LHC, the mkFit algorithm, already in use for the CMS track reconstruction during the LHC Run 3, will exploit its parallelized and vectorized nature on CPUs to perform pattern recognition using seed tracks produced with algorithms that are designed to be fully parallelizable and hardware agnostic, thus suitable for heterogeneous systems: the Patatrack and the Line Segment Tracking (LST) algorithms. Patatrack is an established algorithm, already used for the CMS pixel track reconstruction at HLT during the Run 3 of the LHC, while LST is a novel algorithm, recently integrated in the CMS software, targeting the reconstruction of tracks in the outer tracker of the HL-LHC CMS detector. The state-of-the-art performance for the CMS HLT track reconstruction at the HL-LHC is presented, obtained using the combination of the mkFit, Patatrack, and LST algorithms, that in turn use machine-learning (ML) techniques such as deep neural networks and multi-objective particle swarm optimization to suppress duplicate and misreconstructed tracks. Prospects of further improvements are also presented, with a focus on the usage of ML techniques for track reconstruction at CMS.

</details>


### [166] [An enhanced track reconstruction for the CMS experiment at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26383)
*Mario Masciovecchio*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS 提出 phase-2 基线径迹重建策略，统一多种算法范式并融入机器学习，用于高亮度 LHC 下的在线与离线事件重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）的极端堆积（pileup）条件对径迹重建的效率、分辨率与计算性能构成严峻挑战，需在维持物理性能的同时控制资源需求。

Method: 该策略将多种算法范式统一为连贯流程并集成机器学习（ML）技术，结合 GPU 优化算法（Patatrack、LST）与向量化 CPU 方法（mkFit），以异构计算方式利用现代硬件提升吞吐量。

Result: 该策略在保持高效率和高质量径迹重建的同时，改善了虚假径迹率（fake track rate）与计算性能，并降低了资源需求。

Conclusion: 通过引入全局位移径迹重建（global displaced track reconstruction）并扩展对长寿命粒子等稀有信号的灵敏度，该异构策略提升了物理覆盖范围，对 HL-LHC 尤为关键。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new phase-2 CMS baseline tracking strategy currently proposed for online event reconstruction, which can be similarly useful in offline reconstruction under the extreme pileup conditions of the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC). By unifying diverse algorithmic paradigms and integrating machine learning (ML) techniques into a coherent sequence, CMS can maintain high-efficiency and high-resolution tracking while improving the fake track rate and the computational performance, a crucial aspect especially for the HL-LHC. The approach combines GPU-optimized algorithms (Patatrack, LST) with vectorized CPU methods (mkFit), exploiting modern hardware to maximize throughput. This heterogeneous strategy reduces the resource requirements and enhances the physics reach by incorporating a global displaced track reconstruction and extending sensitivity to rare, interesting signatures, such as long-lived particles.

</details>


### [167] [Complementary Time- and Distance-Based Methods for Cosmogenic $^{9}$Li/$^{8}$He Background Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26538)
*Cailian Jiang,Qishan Liu,Liangjian Wen,Gaosong Li,Zeyuan Yu,Wanlei Guo,Lei Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 提出将传统时间拟合扩展为按缪子分类的事件级联合时间拟合（J-MuCAT），并结合候选事例到缪子径迹距离分布（TraDiTS）约束，构建 DCJ-MuCAT 拟合，用于估计低能中微子实验中残余的宇生 $^{9}$Li 与 $^{8}$He 本底，在模拟中统计不确定度降低逾 40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇生 $^{9}$Li 与 $^{8}$He 的 β 延迟中子衰变（β-delayed neutron decay）特征可模拟逆 β 衰变（inverse beta decay）信号，构成低能中微子实验的重要关联本底。基于“距上次缪子时间”的传统估计在高缪子率下变得困难，且缪子相关 vetoe 会进一步削减残余同位素统计量。

Method: 将传统时间拟合扩展为事件级、按缪子分类的联合时间拟合（J-MuCAT），使用各能量损失类别中距最近前序缪子的时间；同时由候选事例到缪子径迹的距离分布（TraDiTS）获得互补估计，从远距离区确定缪子无关成分并扣除，再以此约束 J-MuCAT 拟合，形成距离约束的 J-MuCAT（DCJ-MuCAT）拟合。

Result: 在带连续宇生本底 vetoe 的探测器级模拟中，DCJ-MuCAT 相对 J-MuCAT 将统计不确定度降低超过 40%，相对 TraDiTS 降低超过 10%；拟合结果与模拟真值保持一致。适用性研究进一步表明，在较大范围的缪子率与同位素分数下均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该框架为在大型中微子探测器中估计残余 $^{9}$Li/$^{8}$He 本底提供了一种实用方法。

Abstract: Cosmogenic $^{9}$Li and $^{8}$He isotopes constitute an important correlated background in low-energy neutrino experiments because their $β$-delayed neutron decay signatures can mimic inverse beta decay signals. Conventional estimates based on the time since the last muon become challenging at high muon rates, while muon-related vetoes further reduce the residual isotope statistics. We extend the conventional time fit to an event-level muon-categorized joint time (J-MuCAT) fit, which uses the time to the most recent preceding muon in each energy-loss category. A complementary estimate is obtained from the candidate-to-muon-track distance distribution (TraDiTS). The muon-uncorrelated component is determined from the far-distance region and subtracted. This estimate is then used to constrain the J-MuCAT fit, defining the distance-constrained J-MuCAT (DCJ-MuCAT) fit. In detector-level simulation with successive cosmogenic-background vetoes, DCJ-MuCAT reduces the statistical uncertainty by more than $40\%$ relative to J-MuCAT and by more than $10\%$ relative to the TraDiTS. The fitted results remain consistent with the simulation truth. Applicability studies further show good performance over a broad range of muon rates and isotope fractions. The proposed framework provides a practical approach for estimating residual $^{9}$Li/$^{8}$He backgrounds in large neutrino detectors.

</details>


### [168] [Extending the reconstruction of displaced tracks at CMS during the HL-LHC with Line Segment Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26695)
*Jade Chismar*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作面向高光度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）高堆积（pileup）环境下的带电粒子径迹重建，在已集成于CMS软件的线段径迹（LST）算法中引入新对象四联体（T4），用于提升位移径迹（displaced tracks）的重建接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC升级为高光度LHC（HL-LHC）将大幅增加每次束团交叉的质子-质子同时相互作用数（pileup），对带电粒子径迹重建的计算资源提出显著需求。同时，HL-LHC相关物理场景需要高效重建具有位移特征的径迹，而传统序列在径向位移接受度上受限。

Method: 采用高度可并行化、可在GPU上高效运行的线段径迹（LST）算法，该算法已集成至CMS软件并仅使用外层径迹探测器（outer tracker）击中信息构建径迹候选。本文在LST中引入新对象四联体（T4），其定义为共享同一条线段的两个三联体（triplet）的链接对，专门针对位移径迹特征进行重建。

Result: 在不使用LST的HL-LHC径迹重建序列中，位移径迹重建在径迹原点径向位移约8 cm处终止；引入LST后，位移径迹效率提升，径向位移接受度扩展超过4倍。进一步引入T4后，在多个物理场景中，相对不含T4的LST，径向位移接受度由40 cm扩展至60 cm。

Conclusion: 引入四联体（T4）的改进型LST算法能够有效拓展位移径迹的径向位移接受度至60 cm，为HL-LHC高堆积条件下位移径迹重建提供了更优性能。

Abstract: The upgrade of the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) to the High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) will greatly increase the average number of simultaneous proton-proton interactions per bunch-crossing (pileup), and will place significant demands on computing resources for the reconstruction of charged-particle tracks. Line Segment Tracking (LST) is a novel, highly parallelizable algorithm that can run efficiently on GPUs and has been integrated into the CMS software to enable the reconstruction of displaced tracks in the high pileup scenarios of the HL-LHC. The LST algorithm is designed to build track candidates using only outer tracker hits, naturally allowing for high acceptance of displaced track signatures. In the HL-LHC tracking reconstruction sequence without LST, displaced tracking ends at a radial displacement of the track origin of approximately 8 cm. The usage of LST in track reconstruction enhanced efficiency for displaced tracks, extending the acceptance in radial displacement from the track origin by more than a factor of 4. In this work, we introduce a new LST object, the quadruplet (T4), defined as a linked pair of triplets with a common line segment, targeting displaced track signatures. We present the performance of this improved implementation of the LST algorithm in several physics scenarios, extending the displaced-track acceptance from 40 cm to 60 cm in radial displacement compared to LST without T4s.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Approach to Steady-State in Nested Resetting Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25252)
*Callum Britton,Ben Le Jeune,Henry Alston,Paul C. Bressloff,Thibault Bertrand*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文通过推导累积时间（accumulation times），刻画嵌套重置过程（nested resetting processes）趋近非平衡稳态（nonequilibrium steady state）的行为，揭示弛豫以波前（wavefront）形式传播，并最终由系统最小重置速率（resetting rate）支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解层级耦合随机重置系统（hierarchically coupled stochastic resetting systems）中稳态如何随时间局部建立，并将首次通过（first-passage）概念拓展至非平衡稳态的建立过程。

Method: 方法为推导系统累积时间，用以量化局部稳态建立的有效首次通过时间；先处理等重置速率情形并获得闭式解（closed-form expressions），再推广至异质重置速率（heterogeneous resetting rates），推导简并与非简并情形下的精确稳态分布、空间矩（spatial moments）与累积时间。

Result: 等重置速率下，弛豫以波前形式传播，其延迟沿重置链（resetting chain）线性增加；异质情形下，弛豫最终由系统最小重置速率决定，且在简并系统中还取决于该速率的重数（multiplicity）。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了嵌套重置系统中非平衡稳态首次通过过程的完整解析描述，并指出其在生物系统弛豫与输运（transport）过程中的潜在应用。

Abstract: We characterise the approach to nonequilibrium steady state in nested resetting processes by deriving accumulation times for the system, which quantify the effective first-passage time for the local establishment of steady state. For equal resetting rates, we obtain closed-form expressions showing that relaxation propagates as a wavefront with a delay that increases linearly along the resetting chain. We then extend this analysis to heterogeneous resetting rates, deriving exact steady-state distributions, spatial moments and accumulation times for both degenerate and non-degenerate resetting rates. We show that relaxation to steady state is ultimately governed by the minimum resetting rate in the system and, in degenerate systems, its multiplicity. These results provide a comprehensive analytical description of first-passage to nonequilibrium steady state in hierarchically coupled stochastic resetting systems, with potential applications to relaxation and transport processes in biological systems.

</details>


### [170] [Order-parameter-dependent mobility in the Convective-Viscous Cahn-Hilliard equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25263)
*P. O. Mchedlov-Petrosyan,L. N. Davydov,O. A. Osmaev*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 针对 Cahn-Hilliard 方程中迁移率（mobility）依赖序参量（order parameter）导致求解困难的问题，本文考虑迁移率对序参量的"倒数"线性与二次依赖形式，并获得了精确的行波解（traveling wave solutions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cahn-Hilliard 方程中的迁移率原则上可依赖于序参量（order parameter），但显式引入该依赖会造成巨大的计算困难，因此常采用常数迁移率近似；然而在某些情形下该依赖至关重要，需采用更实际的表达式（通常为正幂次或多项式），而此类依赖往往仅有近似解或数值解。

Method: 考虑迁移率对序参量的"倒数"线性依赖与倒数二次依赖两种形式，并在该依赖设定下求解 Cahn-Hilliard 方程。

Result: 对上述倒数线性与倒数二次的迁移率依赖，均获得了精确的行波解（traveling wave solutions）；此外，在有限的参数区间内，倒数二次迁移率可作为多项式迁移率的良好近似。

Conclusion: 倒数形式的迁移率依赖具有可精确求解的优势，且在一定参数范围内可有效逼近多项式迁移率，为处理迁移率依赖序参量的 Cahn-Hilliard 问题提供了可行的解析途径。

Abstract: Generally the mobility in the Cahn-Hilliard equation may depend on the order parameter; this was evident already from the original derivation. However, explicit introduction of such dependence results in tremendous calculation difficulties, so the constant mobility approximation was commonly used. On the other hand, in several cases this dependence appeared to be crucial, so more realistic expressions were applied, usually positive powers or polynomials; still, for such dependencies only approximate and/or numerical solution exist. We consider 'reciprocal' linear and quadratic dependencies of mobility on the order parameter; for these dependencies exact traveling wave solutions are obtained. Even more, in a limited interval of the parameters reciprocal quadratic mobilities are rather good approximations to polynomial mobilities.

</details>


### [171] [Kinetic Interference in Translational Control: A Path-Measure Framework for Collision-Triggered Transcript Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25536)
*Shlomo Segal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 将非平衡统计力学（non-equilibrium statistical mechanics）的路径测度表述（path-measure formulation）与信使RNA（messenger RNA）核糖体交通的随机建模相连接，针对反义寡核苷酸（antisense oligonucleotide, ASO）干扰摆动尿苷（wobble-uridine, U34）修饰这一拟议干预提出一个可证伪的研究纲领，而非报告结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所连接的两个文献方向此前独立发展：一是轨迹作用量（trajectory action）分解为时间反对称（熵性）与时间对称（frenetic）两部分的路径测度形式，二是核糖体在mRNA上交通的随机模型。生物学目标是ASO干扰tRNA的U34修饰，其意图不是废止翻译（translation）而是扰动其时序，优先在高通量、密码子偏好（codon-biased）转录本上驱动核糖体碰撞（ribosome collisions）及碰撞触发的转录本降解。

Method: 针对核糖体交通队列、翻译延伸（translation elongation）与碰撞触发的no-go降解，形式化其动力学活性与大偏差（large-deviation）分析；借鉴已在对称及完全非对称简单排他过程（symmetric and totally asymmetric simple exclusion processes）中确立的格子排他模型方法，填补该特定应用的形式化空白。

Result: 未报告新的实验、计算或生物信息学结果。论文明确说明两项承重且未经检验的主张——选择性（selectivity）与非冗余性（non-redundancy）——均未被本文确立，仅将其设定为带有预注册决策规则的可证伪检验，并给出应放弃或收窄该框架的结局条件。

Conclusion: 本文为一项研究纲领提议（research programme proposal），非研究结果。其贡献在于明确界定两项可证伪主张及其判定规则，并预先声明在何种结果下应放弃或收窄该框架。

Abstract: I connect two literatures developed independently: the path-measure formulation of non-equilibrium statistical mechanics, where a trajectory action decomposes into a time-antisymmetric (entropic) and time-symmetric (frenetic) sector, and the stochastic modelling of ribosomal traffic on messenger RNA. The biological target is a proposed intervention -- antisense oligonucleotide (ASO) interference with wobble-uridine (U34) modification of transfer RNA -- whose intended effect is not to abolish translation but to perturb its timing, driving ribosome collisions and collision-triggered transcript decay preferentially on high-flux, codon-biased transcripts.
  Dynamical-activity and large-deviation analyses of generic lattice exclusion models -- notably the symmetric and totally asymmetric simple exclusion processes -- are well established. To my knowledge their formalization specifically for ribosomal traffic queues, translation elongation, and collision-triggered no-go decay remains unoccupied; this paper addresses that narrower gap, not the general one.
  Two claims here are load-bearing and untested. First, selectivity: transcripts whose loss is therapeutically desirable are separable, by vulnerable-codon-pair burden, from transcripts whose loss is toxic. Second, non-redundancy: the frenetic decomposition yields predictions, specific to ribosomal queueing and collision-triggered decay, not already obtainable from rate-level exclusion-process models or existing activity/large-deviation analyses of exclusion processes. This paper establishes neither. It specifies both as falsifiable tests with pre-registered decision rules, including outcomes under which the framework should be abandoned or narrowed. It is a research programme proposal, not a result. No new experimental, computational, or bioinformatic results are reported.

</details>


### [172] [Thermodynamic efficiency of communication channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25934)
*Nahuel Freitas,Pedro E. Harunari,Massimiliano Esposito*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究界定了一类涵盖人工与自然系统中常见物理约束的通信信道（communication channels），推导其热力学代价（thermodynamic cost）下界，并揭示热力学效率（thermodynamic efficiency）最大化的最优输入策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于能量受限通信信道的研究未充分刻画热力学代价的完整来源，本文旨在给出更一般的信道类并厘清其热力学成本结构。

Method: 通过识别一类广泛的通信信道，推导每次信道使用熵产（entropy production）的下界，并将其与输入-输出互信息（input-output mutual information）建立联系，从而定义热力学效率。

Result: 每次信道使用的熵产下界由输入-输出互信息给出；热力学成本不仅归属于输入符号本身，也归属于相邻符号间的跃迁（transitions）。因此最大化热力学效率倾向于偏置输入（biased input）且极少切换，而非容量可达输入（capacity-achieving input）；在二元对称信道（binary symmetric channel）中该偏好经叉式分岔（pitchfork bifurcation）自发破坏信道对称性，细胞感知（cellular sensing）最小模型也呈现相同现象。

Conclusion: 热力学效率最优的通信并不等同于信息容量最优；成本须计入符号间跃迁，导致稀疏切换的偏置输入占优，该机制在二元对称信道与细胞感知模型中普遍出现。

Abstract: We identify a broad class of communication channels that captures common physical constraints in both artificial and natural systems and derive bounds on their thermodynamic cost.
