We find that the entropy production per channel use is bounded from below by the input-output mutual information, and their ratio -mutual information divided by entropy production- defines the thermodynamic efficiency. Unlike previous studies of energy-constrained communication channels, our analysis shows that thermodynamic costs must be assigned not only to the input symbols themselves, but also to transitions between successive symbols. As a result, maximizing thermodynamic efficiency favors a biased input that switches only rarely, rather than the capacity-achieving input. For the binary symmetric channel, this preference emerges through a pitchfork bifurcation that spontaneously breaks the symmetry of the channel. A minimal model of cellular sensing exhibits the same phenomenon.

</details>


### [173] [Intersection Exponents of Simple Random Walks in Two and Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25968)
*Qiyuan Shi,Runsheng Liu,Xinyi Li,Ming Li,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究多个独立随机游走（random walks）相互回避的概率衰减指数（intersection exponent）ξ，在二维验证已知精确公式，在三维对多组构型给出一系列新的数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立随机游走互不相交的概率以幂律衰减，其相交指数（intersection exponent）在二维有精确公式、在三维缺乏一般解析表达式，因此需要数值方法填补三维结果的空白并为解析研究提供参考值。

Method: 考虑若干组独立随机游走，组内允许相交、组间禁止相交；先在二维情形与已知精确公式对照以检验数值方法，再在三维计算两组（分别含 k 与 m 条游走）的指数 ξ(k,m)，并将 m 连续化为参数 λ 进行数值求解，同时考察若干三组构型。

Result: 三维下给出 k=1,2 且 m=1,…,7 的整数指数 ξ(k,m)，以及 k=1,2,3、0.25≤λ≤3 的连续指数 ξ(k,λ)；这些函数随 λ 增大而递增且斜率递减，与布朗相交指数（Brownian intersection exponents）严格凹性（strict concavity）的预期一致。

Conclusion: 所得整数与连续指数为不相交随机路径（non-intersecting random paths）的解析研究提供了数值数值支持，二维结果与精确公式吻合验证了方法的可靠性。

Abstract: The probability that several independent random walks avoid one another decays algebraically with the number of steps $N\to\infty$ in two and three dimensions, $P_N\sim N^{-ξ/2}$, where the intersection exponent $ξ$ depends on the number of random walks. More generally, one may consider several groups of independent random walks, with intersections allowed within each group and forbidden between different groups. We first study intersection exponents in two dimensions, where our results agree with the known exact formulas and test the numerical approach. In three dimensions, where no general exact expression is known, we determine $ξ$ for a range of cases. For two groups containing $k$ and $m$ random walks, respectively, we obtain the exponents $ξ(k,m)$ for $k=1,2$ and $m=1,2,\ldots,7$. We further extend $m$ to a continuous parameter $λ$, allowing us to obtain numerical values for $ξ(k,λ)$ for $k=1,2,3$ and $0.25\leqλ\leq3$. Away from the smallest moment orders, these functions increase with $λ$ with decreasing slopes, as expected from the strict concavity of Brownian intersection exponents. We also investigate three-group configurations for several representative cases. The resulting integer and continuous exponents supply numerical values for analytical studies of non-intersecting random paths.

</details>


### [174] [Statistical mechanics of classical fractons on a line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25999)
*Ylias Sadki,Abhishodh Prakash,S. L. Sondhi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维经典 Machian fracton（马赫式分形子）在非紧支撑（non-compact support）相互作用核下的平衡态统计力学与遍历性（ergodicity），并用事件链蒙特卡洛（ECMC）算法采样其位置—动量耦合相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究聚焦于紧支撑（compact support）相互作用核，发现其导致 Gibbs 配分函数发散、破坏遍历性与对称性、形成粒子团簇的非平衡稳态，从而规避 Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman 定理。本文旨在考察具有非紧支撑核的 fracton 体系的遍历性质与平衡态描述是否成立。

Method: 论文考虑非紧支撑相互作用核，包括指数衰减核与均匀非局域核。对指数衰减核，使用图拉普拉斯（graph-Laplacian）与矩阵树（matrix-tree）界估计自由能；对均匀非局域核，指出其自由能超广延（super-extensive）并需 Kac 重标度（Kac rescaling）。采用推广的 Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman 论证并辅以有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析对称性。为采样平衡系综，构造了保持偶极矩与总动量的非可逆事件链蒙特卡洛（event-chain Monte Carlo, ECMC）算法。

Result: 指数衰减核给出广延（extensive）自由能，暗示统计力学描述成立；均匀非局域核则表现出超广延自由能。推广的 HMWC 论证表明对称破缺密度序参量在所有波矢下消失，熔化紧支撑核原有的长程平移破缺密度序。ECMC 采样的结果与哈密顿动力学长时间平均量定量吻合。平衡液态呈现短程团簇倾向，以及与非高斯单粒子动量尾部相关的动量和位置强关联特征。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了非紧支撑核区域的平衡液态性质，表明在该区域统计力学描述有效、长程平移破缺密度序被熔化；而与之配套的姊妹论文则研究使该液体松弛的机制。

Abstract: We study the equilibrium statistical mechanics of one-dimensional classical Machian fractons: particles whose dynamics conserves a global dipole moment and whose Hamiltonian couples momentum differences through a position-dependent pair-inertia kernel. Compactly supported interaction kernels have a divergence in the Gibbs partition function, and have been shown to break ergodicity and symmetry by forming non-equilibrium steady states with particle clusters, evading the Hohenberg-Mermin-Wagner-Coleman theorem. In this paper, we consider kernels with non-compact support and study their ergodic properties. For exponentially decaying kernels, graph-Laplacian and matrix-tree bounds provide an extensive free energy suggesting that a putative statistical mechanical description is valid. Similarly, uniform non-local kernels have a super-extensive free energy and require a Kac rescaling. A generalized Hohenberg--Mermin--Wagner--Coleman argument, supported by finite-size scaling, implies symmetry-breaking density order parameter vanishes at all wave vectors melting the long-range translation-breaking density order of compact kernels. To study the resulting equilibrium ensemble, we construct a nonreversible event-chain Monte Carlo (ECMC) algorithm that samples the coupled position-momentum phase space while preserving the dipole moment and total momentum. The ECMC sampling is shown to quantitatively match long time-averaged quantites in Hamiltonian dynamics. The equilibrium liquid exhibits preferred short-range clustering and strongly non-Gaussian single-particle momentum tails associated with the correlated nature of positions and momenta. This paper provides a detailed investigation into the equilibrium liquid properties of the non-compact regime, whilst the companion paper investigates the mechanisms that relax the liquid.

</details>


### [175] [Counterions between walls with surface charge modulations: Exact Poisson-Boltzmann solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26356)
*Ladislav Šamaj*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究带位置依赖表面电荷密度的平行壁间经典点电荷系统的热平衡，在 Poisson-Boltzmann 极限下考察表面电荷调制（surface charge modulation）对壁间有效压力（effective pressure）的影响。基于高温 Monte Carlo 模拟预测调制会降低压力，但由二维 Liouville 方程（two-dimensional Liouville equation）精确解给出的解析结果表明调制在小间距增大压力、大间距减小压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有讨论多针对均匀带电壁面，而实际壁面电荷往往存在空间调制，其对壁间有效力的影响尚不明确。本文旨在厘清位置依赖的表面电荷密度如何改变壁间压力。

Method: 采用 Poisson-Boltzmann 极限下的理论分析与高温 Monte Carlo 模拟；限定表面电荷仅沿一个方向调制，利用二维 Liouville 方程的一般解构造静电势的精确解，并由该势反推壁面上的表面电荷密度。

Result: Monte Carlo 模拟预测，在相同平均表面电荷密度下，表面电荷调制会降低壁间压力；而精确解析结果显示，调制在小距离 d 时可增大压力、在大距离 d 时则减小压力。

Conclusion: 表面电荷调制对壁间压力的作用取决于壁间距 d，且精确结果仅适用于表面电荷密度随距离 d 呈特定变化的有限一类模型。

Abstract: We study the thermal equilibrium of a classical system of identical point charges moving between two walls with parallel surfaces at a distance $d$, charged symmetrically with position-dependent surface charge densities. Specifically, we study the effect of surface charge modulation on the effective force (pressure) between the walls in the Poisson-Boltzmann limit. Based on Monte Carlo simulations at high temperatures, it is predicted that surface charge modulation reduces the pressure between the walls compared to uniformly charged wall surfaces with the same average surface charge density. We restrict ourselves to surface charge modulations in only one direction and, using a general solution of the two-dimensional Liouville equation, we construct exact solutions for the electrostatic potential. The surface charge density on the walls is generated inversely from this potential, which means that our exact results apply to a limited set of models with a specific variation of the surface charge density with distance $d$. Explicit analytical results show that surface charge modulation can both increase (small distances $d$) and decrease (large $d$) the pressure between the walls.

</details>


### [176] [Unraveling the Emergence of Slow Dynamics in U(1) Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26599)
*Riccardo Andreoni,Zala Lenarčič,Zeno Bacciconi,Antonello Scardicchio,Marcello Dalmonte,Devendra Singh Bhakuni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一个统一框架，通过将含拓扑θ角的U(1)格点规范理论与具有涌现线性Stark势的非局域动力学约束费米子模型精确映射，解释了一维U(1) LGT中观测到的慢弛豫现象，并为在Rydberg实验中探索亚扩散分形子流体动力学提供新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论动力学是基础性课题，低维系统展现出超越QCD与非阿贝尔理论快速热化之外的丰富现象。近期量子模拟实验在一维U(1) LGT中报告了稳健且意外的慢弛豫信号，其起源尚不清楚，本文旨在提供统一解释。

Method: 核心方法是建立从含拓扑θ角的U(1) LGT到带涌现线性Stark势的非局域动力学约束费米子模型的精确解析映射（exact analytical mapping），并结合数值模拟与未来实验方案进行验证。

Result: 无θ项时，该框架将实验中的非热动力学解释为靠近可积自由费米子模型所产生的预热化（prethermal）行为；θ非零时，涌现的Stark势解释了另一类实验中的冻结动力学（freezing dynamics）；该对应关系还可能因涌现守恒律导致亚扩散输运（subdiffusive transport）。

Conclusion: 所预测现象应在更广泛的一类模型中出现，作者以数值模拟与未来实验方案支持这一预测，并指出该框架为在Rydberg实验中探索亚扩散分形子流体动力学（fractonic hydrodynamics）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The dynamics of gauge theories is of fundamental interest, with low-dimensional systems displaying a rich phenomenology beyond the rapid thermalization typical of QCD and non-Abelian theories. In particular, recent quantum simulation experiments have reported robust and unexpected signatures of slow relaxation in generic regimes and at large volumes in one-dimensional U(1) lattice gauge theories (LGTs), whose origin remains poorly understood. Here, we present a framework that provides a unified perspective on such slow dynamics and establishes a new route for exploring subdiffusive fractonic hydrodynamics in Rydberg experiments. The backbone of our theory is an exact analytical mapping from U(1) LGTs with a topological $θ$ angle to non-local kinetically constrained fermionic models with an emergent linear Stark potential. In the absence of the $θ$ term, this framework interprets the non-thermal dynamics reported in recent experiments as prethermal behavior arising from proximity to an integrable free-fermion model. For non-zero $θ$, the emergent Stark potential instead explains the freezing dynamics observed in other recent experiments. We argue that this correspondence could also lead to subdiffusive transport due to emergent conservation laws. We predict that these phenomena arise in a much broader class of models, and support this prediction with numerical simulations and proposals for future experiments.

</details>


### [177] [A Statistical Analysis of Diffusion Dynamics in Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26759)
*A. Talebi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文从拉普拉斯矩阵（Laplacian）特征谱的统计性质出发研究网络扩散（network diffusion），避免逐一计算全部特征值，并提出系统扩散性较弱的一个条件，同时考察网络结构变化对整体扩散动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往网络扩散研究主要依赖网络拉普拉斯矩阵的次小特征值（second-smallest eigenvalue）来确定最慢衰减模式的时间尺度，该做法在大规模网络中计算代价较高，且对特征谱整体统计性质关注不足。

Method: 研究转而考察网络拉普拉斯矩阵特征谱（eigenspectrum）的统计性质，以此刻画网络扩散的统计特征，从而无需计算全部特征值；并引入一个系统扩散性较弱（less diffusive）的条件，分析网络结构变化对整体扩散动力学的影响。

Result: 基于特征谱统计性质的方法可在不计算全部特征值的情况下研究网络扩散的统计特征，因而适用于大规模网络；研究进一步给出了系统扩散性较弱所满足的条件，并揭示了网络结构变化与整体扩散动力学之间的关系。

Conclusion: 通过特征谱统计性质刻画网络扩散，为大规模网络提供了一种计算可行的分析途径，并借助扩散性较弱条件深化了对网络结构与扩散动力学关系的理解。

Abstract: Network diffusion is a well-known model for studying how a quantity in a network propagates to reach a steady state, where its value becomes uniformly distributed across all nodes. Previous studies of network diffusion have mainly focused on the second-smallest eigenvalue of the network Laplacian to determine the timescale associated with the slowest decaying mode. However, this study investigates the eigenspectrum of the network Laplacian in terms of its statistical properties. This method is useful for studying the statistical properties of network diffusion without calculating all eigenvalues, which can be useful for large networks. Additionally, this study introduces a condition under which the system is less diffusive and investigates how changes in the network structure influence the overall diffusion dynamics.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Loop Equations for Multi-Matrix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25095)
*Aravinth Kulanthaivelu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重访组合环方程方法（combinatorial loop-equation method），用于计算具有多项式势的多矩阵模型（multi-matrix models）中的平面圆盘振幅（planar disc amplitudes）。通过按边界插入层级组织 Schwinger-Dyson 约束，在系统闭合时导出平面圆盘函数的代数方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多矩阵模型中平面圆盘振幅的计算通常依赖鞍点方法（saddle-point methods）等途径，而标准方法多局限于单矩阵与双矩阵情形。本文旨在发展一种可推广的组合环方程流程，以识别并求解超出标准单、双矩阵情形的多矩阵模型扇区。

Method: 构建一套流程，将 Schwinger-Dyson 约束按其边界插入（boundary insertions）的层级进行组织；当所得系统闭合时，生成关于平面圆盘函数（planar disc function）的代数方程。该方法被应用于三态 Potts 模型（three-state Potts model）以及若干未解决的双矩阵与三矩阵模型。

Result: 该方法复现了三态 Potts 模型中固定（fixed）、混合（mixed）与自由（free）边界条件对应的圆盘配分函数（disc partition functions），与先前鞍点方法所得结果一致；并新导出了二次与三次耦合不相等时三态 Potts 模型固定边界预解式（fixed-boundary resolvent）的代数方程。此外，方法还可计算若干此前未解决的双矩阵与三矩阵模型的圆盘配分函数。

Conclusion: 这些例子表明，环方程（loop equations）为识别和求解标准单矩阵、双矩阵情形之外的多矩阵模型扇区提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: We revisit the combinatorial loop-equation method for computing planar disc amplitudes in multi-matrix models with polynomial potentials. We develop a procedure which organises the Schwinger-Dyson constraints by the level of their boundary insertions and, when the resulting system closes, produces an algebraic equation for the planar disc function. Using this method we recover the disc partition functions associated with fixed, mixed, and free boundary conditions in the three-state Potts model, previously obtained by saddle-point methods. As a novelty we derive an algebraic equation for the fixed-boundary resolvent of the three-state Potts model with unequal quadratic and cubic couplings. We further show one can compute the disc partition function for several unsolved two- and three-matrix models. These examples demonstrate that loop equations provide a practical route to identifying and solving sectors of multi-matrix models beyond the standard one- and two-matrix cases.

</details>


### [179] [On Thermodynamic Universalities and Optics of B-deformed Reissner--Nordström--AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25161)
*Adil Belhaj,Maryem Jemri*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习（machine learning）方法研究源于非交换几何（non-commutative geometry）的B-形变Reissner–Nordström–AdS黑洞，考察其热力学范德华行为与光学阴影，并借助EHT观测数据约束形变参数B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在将弦论非交换几何启发的时空导数修正（形变参数与NS-NS B场之逆相关）引入Reissner–Nordström–AdS黑洞，并借助机器学习手段刻画其热力学与光学特征，同时用观测数据检验该弦论形变黑洞模型的合理性。

Method: 通过高精度数值计算求解P-V临界性（P-V criticality）的普适比值与Joule–Thomson膨胀特征，据此构建训练数据，训练全连接神经网络（fully connected neural network）判别该黑洞是否呈现热力学范德华行为；并结合事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope）观测数据，用机器学习方法研究其光学阴影并约束包括弦论参数B在内的模型参数。

Result: 经训练的全连接神经网络能够准确识别热力学范德华行为；同时借助M87*与Sgr A*黑洞经验数据，对包括弦论参数B在内的相关参数施加了约束，从而给出经观测佐证的模型。

Conclusion: 机器学习可有效用于识别弦论形变AdS黑洞的热力学范德华特征并分析其光学阴影；结合EHT观测数据可对B-形变Reissner–Nordström–AdS黑洞参数进行限制，为该类弦论黑洞构型提供观测一致性支持。

Abstract: Using machine learning techniques, we study the Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes in the framework of extended space-time derivatives inspired by non-commutative geometry in string theory where the deformation parameters are related to the inverse of the NS-NS B-field. We refer to these solutions as B-deformed Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes. Precisely, we examine the thermodynamic and the optical behaviors of such stringy deformed AdS black holes. Exploiting advanced numerical computations, we first examine the corresponding thermodynamic Van der Waals behaviors by calculating the universal ratios describing the P-V criticality and the Joule-Thomson expansion features. Applying such numerical results, we construct reliable training data for a fully connected neural network being developed to determine whether such stringy black hole configurations exhibit thermodynamic Van der Waals aspects. Concretely, we reveal that the fully connected and trained neural network accurately detects Van der Waals behaviors. Supported by the observational data reported by the Event Horizon Telescope international collaborations, we investigate the optical shadows of such B-deformed Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes using machine learning methods. In order to provide corroborated models, we constraint the involved parameters including the stringy one B by making use of M87* and Sgr A* black hole empirical data.

</details>


### [180] [The landscape and the swampland of O(3,3) gauged supergravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25203)
*Mariana Graña,Diego Perugini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了七维半极大规范超引力的完整目录，通过建立轨道折叠扭转与广义通量之间的对应关系，判定哪些规范化可实现为（非）对称轨道折叠，并将无法实现者列为规范超引力沼泽地的候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通量群流形上通过Scherk-Schwarz机制紧致化弦论可得到规范超引力，而借助双重场论可使该过程具有T对偶协变性，由此产生几何与非几何通量，但其弦论起源往往不明确，故需系统厘清哪些规范化具有弦论实现。

Method: 系统考察七维半极大规范超引力的完整目录，在轨道折叠扭转（orbifold twists）与广义通量（generalized fluxes）之间构造字典式对应关系。

Result: 借助该对应关系，识别出哪些规范化（gaugings）可实现在（非）对称轨道折叠（(a)symmetric orbifolds）上，哪些无法实现。

Conclusion: 无法实现为轨道折叠的规范化被确立为规范超引力沼泽地（gauged supergravity swampland）的候选对象。

Abstract: Compactifications of string theory on group manifolds with fluxes, performed via the Scherk-Schwarz mechanism, give rise to gauged supergravities. Using double field theory, this procedure can be made T-duality covariant, yielding a set of geometric and non-geometric fluxes whose string theory origin is often unclear. In this paper, we systematically analyze the complete catalog of half-maximal gauged supergravities in seven dimensions. By constructing a dictionary between orbifold twists and generalized fluxes, we identify which gaugings admit a realization as (a)symmetric orbifolds, and which do not, establishing the latter as candidates for the gauged supergravity swampland.

</details>


### [181] [Quantum Clausius relation beyond de Sitter equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25206)
*Jinn-Ouk Gong,TaeHun Kim,Junghwan Lee,Chang Sub Shin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在准德西特时空（quasi-de Sitter spacetime）视界内部，为共形场（conformal fields）建立了量子版克劳修斯关系（quantum Clausius relation），并给出超越精确德西特平衡的动力学视界热力学（dynamical horizon thermodynamics）的量子场论实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有视界热力学多依赖引力运动方程或在精确德西特平衡下成立，缺乏不依赖引力方程、可推广至非平衡情形的量子场论层面的验证。

Method: 从重整化应力-能量张量（renormalized stress-energy tensor）计算穿越视界的净热流，并独立用复本方法（replica method）确定重整化冯·诺依曼熵（renormalized von Neumann entropy）的时间演化，在哈勃流展开（Hubble-flow expansion）中逐阶比较两者。

Result: 在哈勃流展开领头阶，两种计算完全一致，且未使用引力运动方程；在次领头阶之外，得到一个包含相应瓦尔德熵（Wald entropy）的、与方案无关的熵平衡关系。

Conclusion: 结果提供了动力学视界热力学在精确德西特平衡之外的量子场论实现，表明该热力学关系可由量子场论自身导出而非依赖引力方程。

Abstract: We establish a direct quantum Clausius relation for conformal fields inside the apparent horizon of a quasi-de Sitter spacetime. We compute the net heat flow across the horizon from the renormalized stress-energy tensor, and independently determine the time evolution of the renormalized von Neumann entropy using the replica method. At leading order in the Hubble-flow expansion, the two calculations agree exactly, without using the gravitational equations of motion. Beyond leading order, we find a scheme-independent entropy balance that includes the corresponding Wald entropy. Our results provide a quantum field theoretic realization of dynamical horizon thermodynamics beyond exact de Sitter equilibrium.

</details>


### [182] [A Quantum-Mechanical Model for the M5-Brane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25207)
*Manuel Artime,Ralph Blumenhagen,Thomas Raml*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于 5-Lie 代数（5-Lie algebra）$\mathcal{A}_6$ 的 BFSS 矩阵模型（BFSS matrix model）的最大超对称扩展（maximally supersymmetric extension），其拉格朗日（Lagrangian）形式将 BFSS 的 2-括号（2-bracket）结构常数提升为动力学对象，并展现出 M5-膜（M5-brane）理论所预期的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是构造 BFSS 矩阵模型的最大超对称扩展，以获得符合 M5-膜理论预期的理论框架，并为 M5-膜最低模式提供量子力学描述。

Method: 基于 5-Lie 代数 $\mathcal{A}_6$ 建立拉格朗日理论，将 BFSS 的 2-括号结构常数推广为受类 Chern-Simons（Chern-Simons-like）动能项支配的动力学对象；并从空间五球面（five-sphere）上的单光锥（lightcone）M5-膜作用量出发，经向时间坐标的截断（truncation）得到该理论。

Result: 该理论可用拉格朗日形式表述，具有最大超对称性；其可由五球面单光锥 M5-膜作用量经时间坐标截断导出，且该截断是自洽的（consistent truncation）。

Conclusion: 与 $U(2)$ BFSS 模型类似，$\mathcal{A}_6$ 理论可解释为 M5-膜最低模式（lowest-lying modes）的量子力学模型。

Abstract: We present a maximally supersymmetric extension of the BFSS matrix model based on the 5-Lie algebra ${\mathcal A}_6$. The theory is Lagrangian, promotes the BFSS 2-bracket structure constants to dynamical objects governed by a Chern-Simons-like kinetic term, and displays features expected from a theory of M5-branes. We show that it can also be obtained from the single lightcone M5-brane action on a spatial five-sphere after a truncation to the time coordinate. The resulting theory is a consistent truncation and, analogously to the $U(2)$ BFSS model, supports an interpretation of the $\mathcal{A}_6$ theory as a quantum-mechanical model for the lowest-lying modes of the M5-brane.

</details>


### [183] [The Double Copy of the Black Ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25209)
*Damien A. Easson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文为单自旋五维 Emparan–Reall 黑洞环（black ring）构造了精确的 Weyl 双重拷贝（Weyl double copy），将完整非线性 Weyl 曲率表示为两个标量加权 Maxwell 平方之和，从而把五维精确 Weyl doubling 推广至同时含 type-G 与 type-I_i 区域的时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有精确五维 Weyl doubling 案例仅覆盖 type-D 与 type-N 时空，尚缺乏对外部含更复杂代数类型（如 type-G 与 type-I_i）的黑洞解（如黑洞环）的验证，因此需检验该结构是否可扩展至此类更一般的背景。

Method: 以单自旋五维 Emparan–Reall 黑洞环为背景，构造其精确 Weyl 双重拷贝：两个无源 Maxwell 场分别为静止 Killing 矢量（Killing vector）生成者与携带穿过环绕环之球面的拓扑磁通者，并各自对应满足同一有源波动方程（sourced wave equation）的标量伙伴；同时分析 Weyl 多项式的因式分解结构。

Result: 完整非线性 Weyl 曲率为两个标量加权 Maxwell 平方之和；两 Maxwell 场在黑洞环时空上均无源。Weyl 多项式分解为两个不同的不可约二次式，对应真正的 De Smet type 22，排除了约化为单一 Maxwell 平方的可能。

Conclusion: 该工作将精确五维 Weyl doubling 从 type-D 与 type-N 案例扩展至外部同时包含 type-G 和 type-I_i 区域的黑洞，并证明其 Weyl 多项式为真正的 De Smet type 22，表明该结构并非单一 Maxwell 平方所能刻画。

Abstract: We construct an exact Weyl double copy of the singly spinning five-dimensional Emparan--Reall black ring. Its complete nonlinear Weyl curvature is the sum of two scalar-weighted Maxwell squares. One field is generated by the stationary Killing vector; the other carries topological magnetic flux through a sphere linking the ring. Both are source-free Maxwell fields on the black-ring spacetime, with corresponding scalar partners obeying a common sourced wave equation. This extends exact five-dimensional Weyl doubling beyond the type-$\mathrm D$ and type-$\mathrm N$ examples to a black hole whose exterior contains both type-$\mathrm G$ and type-$\mathrm{I}_i$ regions. The Weyl polynomial factorizes into two distinct irreducible quadratics, establishing genuine De Smet type $22$, thereby ruling out a reduction to a single Maxwell square.

</details>


### [184] [Twisted \texorpdfstring{$A_{2N}$}{A(2N)} Argyres--Douglas theories: vertex algebras, Higgs branches, and double covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25212)
*Christopher Beem,Harshal Kulkarni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究扭曲 \(A_{2N}\) 与扭曲 \(D_{N+1}\) 两族广义 Argyres–Douglas 理论（generalised Argyres–Douglas theories），提出 \(A\)-系列可由 \(D\)-系列经幂零 Higgsing（nilpotent Higgsing）实现，并据此给出顶点算子代数与 Higgs 分支的结构性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两族理论密切相关，其关联顶点算子代数（vertex operator algebras）与 Higgs 分支（Higgs branches）的具体刻画尚需统一框架；作者试图通过 \(A\)-系列与 \(D\)-系列的对应关系来解释 \(A\)-系列代数并非单纯仿射 Kac–Moody 代数这一现象。

Method: 提出 \(A\)-系列理论是 \(D\)-系列理论幂零 Higgsing 的结果，并收集多方面的证据支持该提议；同时分析 \(\mathbb{Z}_2\) 规范化（\(\mathbb{Z}_2\) gaugings）后的理论及其关联顶点代数结构，并讨论 \(\mathfrak{R}\)-filtration 与分级幺正性（graded unitarity）的相容性。

Result: \(A\)-系列的关联顶点算子代数为非边界容许水平（non-boundary admissible levels）下仿射 Kac–Moody 顶点代数的有限扩张（finite extensions），而非该 Kac–Moody 代数本身；其 Higgs 分支在许多情形下为幂零轨道闭包（nilpotent orbit closures）的双重覆盖（double covers）；\(A\)-系列理论的 \(\mathbb{Z}_2\) 规范化给出关联顶点算子代数恰为非边界容许水平仿射 Kac–Moody 代数的幺正 SCFT 例子。

Conclusion: 所提出的 \(A\)-系列为 \(D\)-系列幂零 Higgsing 的图景可统一解释顶点代数与 Higgs 分支特征；相关顶点代数被提议带有非标准 \(\mathfrak{R}\)-filtration，并需进一步考察其与分级幺正性的相容性。

Abstract: We investigate the associated vertex operator algebras and Higgs branches of two closely related families of generalised Argyres--Douglas theories, arising from the twisted $A_{2N}$ and twisted $D_{N+1}$ series, respectively. We propose that the $A$-series theories are realised as nilpotent Higgsings of the $D$-series, and we assemble a variety of pieces of evidence for this proposal. A consequence is that the associated vertex operator algebras of the $A$-series are finite extensions of affine Kac--Moody vertex algebras at (non-boundary) admissible levels, rather than those Kac--Moody algebras themselves, and that their Higgs branches are in many cases double covers of nilpotent orbit closures. The $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gaugings of the $A$-series theories then furnish examples of unitary SCFTs whose associated vertex operator algebras are precisely affine Kac--Moody algebras at non-boundary admissible levels. We propose that these vertex algebras are equipped with a non-standard $\mathfrak{R}$-filtration, and comment on compatibility with graded unitarity.

</details>


### [185] [Classification of order-two T-duality orbifolds at the SO(12) free fermionic point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25213)
*Alon E. Faraggi,Stefan Groot Nibbelink,Benjamin Percival*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对六维非几何构造T-fold进行系统分类：辨识全部Z2点群，在SO(12)格点上对II型弦理论的费米子点处分类二阶T-fold构型，给出谱的一般化GSO相位参数化，并讨论镜像对称推广、orientifold构型、自旋3/2态与超对称性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称orbifold（asymmetric orbifold）提供了T-fold这类非几何构造（non-geometric construction）的具体实例，但六维情形下的点群与构型尚缺乏系统分类，需明确其谱、对偶性与超对称性质。

Method: 在六维非对称二阶orbifold中辨识所有Z2点群；于II型弦理论（type II string theories）的费米子点（fermionic point）上，对SO(12)格点上的二阶T-fold构型进行分类；采用一般化GSO相位（generalised GSO phases）参数化给出各模型谱；并借助基本世界sheet宇称（worldsheet parity）识别orientifold化构型，同时通过选取一般化GSO投影以保持最大超对称。

Result: 谱以一般化GSO相位参数化形式给出；六个T-fold构型的最小有效Hodge数（Hodge numbers）为(h_{11},h_{12})=(1,1)；分类中识别出可使用基本世界sheet宇称的orientifold化构型；纯非对称twist对应的twisted sector可能含带自旋3/2态（spin-3/2 states）的Rarita-Schwinger多重态（Rarita-Schwinger multiplets）；并指出部分非超对称T-fold可增强至N=1甚至N=2超引力（supergravity）。

Conclusion: 非对称orbifold/T-fold存在系统的Z2分类与参数化谱结构，且含自旋3/2态的情形必然要求谱以超对称方式重排，从而使真空能（vacuum energy）为零；文中还猜想非对称的镜像对称映射（mirror symmetry map）推广。

Abstract: Asymmetric orbifolds provide concrete examples of non-geometric constructions dubbed T-folds. All Z2 point groups of six dimensional asymmetric order-two orbifolds are identified. The order-two T-fold configurations on the SO(12) lattice are classified at the fermionic point of type II string theories for all Z2 point groups. The spectra of the models on these configurations are presented parametrically, in terms of certain generalised GSO phases. The minimal effective Hodge numbers were found to be (h_{11},h_{12})=(1,1) for six T-fold configurations. Asymmetric generalisations of the mirror symmetry map are conjectured. The orientifoldable configurations using the basic worldsheet parity were identified within the classification. Twisted sectors corresponding to pure asymmetric twists may contain Rarita-Schwinger multiplets with spin-3/2 states. Throughout the paper, generalised GSO projections are chosen to preserve the maximal amount of supersymmetry possible. Relaxing this, the order-two point groups were identified for which non--supersymmetric T-folds can be constructed. Since some of them may enhance to N=1 or, even, N=2 supergravities, we argue that appearance of spin-3/2 states necessarily implies that the spectrum has reorder itself in a supersymmetric fashion and hence that the vacuum energy vanishes.

</details>


### [186] [Bulk OPE Coefficients of the $E$-Series Virasoro Minimal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25215)
*Amaury Lhoste,Jiaxin Qiao,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作给出具有 \(E_6\)、\(E_7\)、\(E_8\) 模不变量（modular invariant）的全部 Virasoro 极小模型（Virasoro minimal model）的体原算子乘积系数（bulk-primary operator-product coefficients）。通过除以手征广义极小模型 OPE 系数的普适乘积，共形自举（conformal bootstrap）问题被约化为由单位根量子群 \(6j\) 符号（root-of-unity quantum-group \(6j\) symbols）支配的有限代数问题。作者给出约化后代表系数的数量（\(E_6\)、\(E_7\)、\(E_8\) 分别为 18、32、107），并通过有限计算机辅助计算获得 \(s=1\) 种子数据与高精度数值证据，同时证明种子数据的存在唯一性可推广至所有 Kac 标号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上直接求解 Virasoro 极小模型的共形自举涉及连续、无限的 OPE 系数自由度，难以系统处理。本文旨在利用模不变量分类下的特殊结构，将相关问题压缩为有限代数问题，从而给出所有 \(E_6\)、\(E_7\)、\(E_8\) 型极小模型的体原 OPE 系数。

Method: 将 OPE 系数除以手征广义极小模型 OPE 系数的普适乘积，把自举问题约化为由单位根量子群 \(6j\) 符号控制的有限代数问题；证明例外因子可独立于第二 Kac 指标 \(s\) 选取，其中心荷（central charge）依赖归结为有限个参考情形与显式相位。随后进行有限计算机辅助计算以获得候选代数 \(s=1\) 种子数据。方法仅假设 Virasoro 对称性、体局域性（bulk locality，即置换对称性）与 OPE 结合性（crossing equations）。

Result: 得到候选代数 \(s=1\) 种子数据及高精度数值证据，支持其在原场符号（primary-field signs）模意义下的存在性与唯一性；约化后的代表 OPE 系数数量在 \(E_6\)、\(E_7\)、\(E_8\) 三类模型中分别为 18、32、107。并提供适用于幺正与非幺正模型的系数重构约定与系数表。

Conclusion: 作者证明：在亏格为零的体局域性与交叉方程（genus-zero bulk locality and crossing equations）范围内，种子数据的精确存在唯一性可推出所有 Kac 标号（Kac labels）下的相应结论；但该有限种子步骤本身尚未被严格证明（uncertified），仍依赖计算机辅助验证。

Abstract: We present bulk-primary operator-product coefficients for every Virasoro minimal model with an $E_6$, $E_7$, or $E_8$ modular invariant. Dividing by a universal product of chiral generalized-minimal-model OPE coefficients reduces the conformal bootstrap problem to a finite algebraic problem governed by root-of-unity quantum-group $6j$ symbols. The exceptional factors of the OPE coefficients can be chosen independent of the second Kac indices $s$, and their dependence on the central charge reduces to finitely many reference cases and explicit phases. A finite computer-assisted calculation yields candidate algebraic $s=1$ seed data and high-precision numerical evidence for existence and uniqueness modulo primary-field signs. We prove that exact existence and uniqueness for these seeds imply the corresponding result for all Kac labels within the genus-zero bulk locality and crossing equations; the finite seed step remains uncertified. The three families require $18$, $32$, and $107$ representative reduced OPE coefficients for $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$, respectively. We provide reconstruction conventions and coefficient tables for both unitary and nonunitary models. Our calculation assumes only Virasoro symmetry, bulk locality (permutation symmetry) and OPE associativity (crossing equations).

</details>


### [187] [Functional Dimensional Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25238)
*Piero Beretta,Alessandro Codello*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出泛函维度正则化（Functional Dimensional Regularization, FDR），将维度正则化（dimensional regularization, DR）原理推广至微扰 $\varepsilon$ 展开之外，构建可在任意连续维度 $d$ 定义 beta 函数的泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group, RG）框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有维度正则化主要局限于微扰 $\varepsilon$ 展开，而泛函 RG 方法虽灵活却缺乏与维度正则化的直接衔接；工作动机在于融合二者的技术简洁性与泛函方法的多功能性。

Method: 将 FDR beta 函数定义为各临界维度上 DR 所得方案无关（scheme-independent）beta 函数之和，并以阈值函数（threshold functions）刻画；在局域势近似（local potential approximation）下进行不动点分析，并推广至导数展开（derivative expansion）二阶。

Result: 在局域势近似下重现所有多临界模型（multi-critical models）的 $\varepsilon$ 展开，并建立与泛函标度解、spike plots 及本征微扰谱的联系；在 $d=3$ 下计算 Ising 普适类的临界指数，给出异常维度 $\eta$ 与 RG 谱的快速收敛估计，与最新结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 通过与不可微扰 RG 及 proper-time RG 的详细比较，揭示结构性异同，并提出“蒸馏”（distillation）程序以从任意单圈精确 RG 方案系统导出 FDR 流，确立 FDR 为兼具维度正则化简洁性与泛函方法通用性的自洽且有竞争力的 RG 框架。

Abstract: We introduce and develop Functional Dimensional Regularization (FDR), a novel functional renormalization group (RG) framework that extends the principles of dimensional regularization beyond the perturbative $\varepsilon$-expansion. The key insight is to define the FDR beta functions, valid in any continuous dimension $d$ and characterized by threshold functions, as a sum over all critical dimensions of the corresponding scheme-independent beta functions computed in DR. We demonstrate that this formalism exhibits all the hallmarks of a fully-fledged functional RG. Within the local potential approximation, we perform a general fixed point analysis, recovering the $\varepsilon$-expansion for all multi-critical models and establishing a direct connection to functional scaling solutions, spike plots, and eigen-perturbation spectra. Extending the framework to the second order of the derivative expansion, we compute the critical exponents for the Ising universality class in $d=3$, providing estimates for the anomalous dimension $η$ and RG spectrum that converge rapidly and show favorable agreement with state-of-the-art results. We present a detailed comparison with non-perturbative and proper-time RG approaches, highlighting structural similarities and key differences. In particular, we introduce a ``distillation'' procedure that allows one to systematically derive the FDR flow from any other one-loop-exact RG scheme. Our results establish FDR as a self-contained and competitive RG framework that combines the technical simplicity of dimensional regularization with the versatility of the functional approach.

</details>


### [188] [Anyon Crystallization by Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25249)
*Zohar Komargodski,Xuzixiang Lou,Ivri Nagar,Domenico Orlando,Susanne Reffert,Amit Sever*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视任意子气体（anyon gas）问题，在固定 \(N/k\)、大 \(N\) 的近玻色极限下发现基态为晶态，统计相互作用被完全屏蔽，由此提出晶态—超流态量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子气体长期被普遍认为具有超流性，本文旨在检验“仅含统计相互作用（statistical interaction）的任意子气体必然超流”这一传统认知，并借助可解极限给出明确判断。

Method: 在固定 \(N/k\)（其中 \(1/k\) 表征统计相互作用强度）下，研究大粒子数 \(N\) 的近玻色极限；该体系中统计相互作用由长程规范场（gauge field）传递；并开展任意子—任意子相互作用的重整化群（renormalization-group）演化分析。

Result: 该极限下基态为具有 \(N/k\) 个格点的晶体，统计相互作用在晶相内被完全屏蔽，形成无超流性的常规晶体；文中确定了其单胞尺寸、基态能量、声学激发（acoustic excitation）、角动量交叉（angular momentum crossing）等性质，并给出 \(k>k_c\) 晶态与 \(k<k_c\) 超流态之间的量子相变，临界值 \(k_c\simeq2\text{--}5\)。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅含统计相互作用的任意子气体不一定超流，与传统认知相反；重整化群分析还识别出多个新的潜在不动点（fixed point）并阐明了有限尺寸效应（finite-size effect）。

Abstract: We revisit the long-standing problem of the anyon gas. We study the solvable near-bosonic limit of a large number of anyons $N$ at fixed $N/k$, where $1/k$ characterizes the statistical interaction. In this limit, the ground state is a crystal with order $N/k$ lattice sites. The statistical interactions, mediated by a long-range gauge field, are completely screened within the crystalline phase. The resulting state is a conventional crystal without superfluidity. We characterize this anyon crystal by determining its unit-cell size, ground-state energy, acoustic excitations, angular momentum crossings, and other properties.
  Our results indicate a quantum phase transition between the crystal at $k>k_c$ and a superfluid at $k<k_c$, with a critical value $k_c\simeq2\text{--}5$. These results imply that an anyon gas with only statistical interactions is not necessarily a superfluid, contrary to common lore.
  We also present a detailed analysis of the renormalization-group evolution of anyon--anyon interactions, identifying many new potential fixed points and elucidating finite-size effects.

</details>


### [189] [Primary Decompositions in Lorentz-Covariant Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25253)
*Giuseppe De Laurentis,David Tai*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文针对散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）积分系数紧凑表示的构造问题，将奇点结构显式化的任务转化为多项式商环分式域中的理想初等分解（primary decomposition）计算问题，提出基于理想饱和（ideal saturation）的改进方法，解决了生成元选取破坏洛伦兹协变性（Lorentz covariance）的问题，并给出数值点生成与素性检验算法的实质性改进，最终给出了五点多质量与无质量振幅在更高维度调节子幂次下的新余维二初等分解结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射振幅积分系数的紧凑表示依赖于奇点结构的显式化，而奇点对应于代数消没轨迹（algebraic vanishing loci），其部分分式分解（partial fraction decomposition）由不可约余维二簇（irreducible codimension-two varieties）上的行为主导，这些簇可通过关联理想的初等分解识别。然而初等分解在计算上极具挑战，通常需要针对性策略。

Method: 扩展一种基于理想饱和的方法，识别出理想生成元选取过程中破坏洛伦兹协变性的问题，并提出通过特殊运动学极限下的拟合拟设（fitting ansätze）加以解决。同时改进两类算法：一是在给定簇附近生成数值点的算法，二是检验非混合理想（unmixed ideals）素性的算法，二者均依赖一种半数值程序来计算独立集与簇维度。

Result: 给出了五点无质量振幅在维度调节子（dimensional regulator）更高幂次下的新余维二初等分解，以及五点单质量运动学（five-point one-mass kinematics）下有限余项（finite remainders）的初等分解，其中出现了一个新的伪奇点轨迹（spurious singular locus）。

Conclusion: 基于理想饱和、辅以运动学极限拟合拟设的方法可有效克服洛伦兹协变性破坏问题，配合半数值化算法改进，能够完成此前计算困难的新余维二初等分解，并揭示出五点单质量情形下额外的伪奇点结构。

Abstract: An effective strategy for constructing compact representations of integral coefficients in scattering amplitudes is to make their singularity structure manifest. This is naturally achieved by treating them as elements of fraction fields of polynomial quotient rings, where poles correspond to algebraic vanishing loci. Simplification via partial fraction decomposition relies on exploiting the structure of the numerators and is governed, at least in a first approximation, by their behaviour on irreducible codimension-two varieties. These are identifiable through primary decompositions of the associated ideals. Despite the availability of general algorithms, primary decompositions remain computationally challenging and often require tailored strategies. In this work, we extend an approach based on ideal saturation, identify an issue related to the breaking of Lorentz covariance in the choice of ideal generators, and present a solution based on fitting ansätze in special kinematic limits. We also provide substantial improvements to an algorithm for the generation of numerical points in proximity of given varieties and to one for testing primality of unmixed ideals, both of which rely on a semi-numerical procedure for computing independent sets and variety dimensions. Finally, we present new codimension-two primary decompositions for five-point massless amplitudes at higher powers in the dimensional regulator, and for finite remainders with five-point one-mass kinematics, where a new spurious singular locus appears.

</details>


### [190] [Dual symmetry breaking and magnetic charge screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25324)
*Saulo Carneiro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将电、磁希格斯标量场引入对偶电动力学的拉格朗日表述，研究仅磁标量具有非零期望值的真空相。结果显示磁扇区获得质量、电相互作用在大距离上保持库仑形式，磁荷被屏蔽而电荷不被屏蔽，Dirac 条件作为微观耦合的量子化规则得以继承。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在拓展对偶电动力学（dual electrodynamics）的拉格朗日表述，通过引入电与磁的希格斯标量场来考察非局域耦合下的对称性破缺相及其对电荷屏蔽行为的影响。

Method: 在拉格朗日框架中引入电、磁希格斯标量场，使其耦合到进入电扇区与磁扇区的 Mandelstam 非局域势（Mandelstam nonlocal potentials）；选取仅磁标量具有非零期望值的真空进行谱与相互作用分析。

Result: 广义麦克斯韦方程（generalised Maxwell equations）尽管标量耦合为非局域仍保持局域形式；该相中局域电势保持无质量极点，磁扇区获得质量。因此大距离上电相互作用为库仑型，而极-极与电荷-极相互作用变为短程；按 Swieca 定理（Swieca theorem）磁荷被屏蔽而电荷未被屏蔽，Dirac 条件（Dirac condition）作为微观电、磁耦合的量子化规则从对称相继承而来。

Conclusion: 该非局域耦合的希格斯机制相中，磁荷屏蔽与电荷非屏蔽并存，Dirac 量子化条件保持有效；论文并讨论了被屏蔽的单极子（screened monopoles）作为可能的暗扇区自由度。

Abstract: We extend a Lagrangian formulation of dual electrodynamics by introducing electric and magnetic Higgs scalar fields. The scalars couple to Mandelstam nonlocal potentials which enter the electric and magnetic sectors. We consider the vacuum in which only the magnetic scalar has a nonzero expectation value. The generalised Maxwell equations remain local even though the scalar couplings are nonlocal. In this phase the local electric potential keeps a massless pole while the magnetic sector acquires a mass. The electric interaction is therefore Coulombian at large distances while the pole-pole and charge-pole interactions become short ranged. The magnetic charge is then screened in the sense of the Swieca theorem while the electric charge is not. The Dirac condition is inherited from the symmetric phase as a quantisation rule for the microscopic electric and magnetic couplings. We also comment on screened monopoles as possible dark sector degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [191] [Hidden symmetries and the universality of Hawking radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25475)
*Ivan Pérez-Castro,Abdel Pérez-Lorenzana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作表明，利用量子场论（quantum field theory）中的规范不变性（gauge invariance）与时空隐对称性（hidden symmetries）导致的 Hamilton-Jacobi 方程可分离性，可在不做视界附近度规近似的前提下导出霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）图像为霍金辐射提供了直观的半经典解释，但对满足无毛定理（no-hair theorem）的黑洞（black hole, BH），通行的复路径（Hamilton-Jacobi）方法通常依赖视界附近度规的近似，构成方法论上的局限。

Method: 以量子场论中的规范不变性为出发点，结合时空隐对称性所赋予的 Hamilton-Jacobi（HJ）方程可分离性，构造无需视界附近度规近似的复路径推导路径。

Result: 在避免度规近似的情况下成功导出霍金谱，并由此显式说明黑洞在事件视界（event horizon）处为何表现为有效的 (1+1) 维黑体（blackbody）。

Conclusion: 该视角给出了将霍金辐射推广至高自旋粒子（higher-spin particles）所需的条件，并提供了一个可自然纳入反作用（back-reaction）与量子引力（quantum gravity）效应的框架。

Abstract: Quantum tunneling of particles across a black hole (BH) horizon provides an intuitive interpretation of Hawking radiation (HR), yielding a conceptually simple derivation of the thermal spectrum within a semiclassical framework. However, for BHs obeying the no-hair theorem, usual implementations of the complex-path (Hamilton-Jacobi) method typically rely on approximating the metric near the BH horizon. In this work, we demonstrate that the Hawking spectrum can be derived without such approximations by exploiting gauge invariance in quantum field theory and the separability of the Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) equation arising from hidden symmetries of these spacetimes. However, for BHs obeying the no-hair theorem, usual implementations of the complex-path (Hamilton--Jacobi) method typically rely on approximating the metric near the horizon. This perspective shows explicitly why the BH behaves as an effective $(1+1)$-dimensional blackbody at the event horizon, provides the conditions needed to extend the HR to higher-spin particles, and offers a framework that can naturally incorporate back-reaction and quantum-gravity effects.

</details>


### [192] [Holographic description of RN-like black hole in bumblebee gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25530)
*Jian-Peng Zhang,Bin Chen,Jiaju Zhang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 bumblebee 引力中 RN 型黑洞的全息描述，通过热力学方法与隐藏共形对称性检验，得出对偶 CFT 的左右手移动中心荷相等（$c_R=c_L$），并指出这源于洛伦兹破缺的自发性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: bumblebee 引力是含自发洛伦兹破缺（spontaneous Lorentz violation）的矢量-张量理论，其黑洞的全息对偶性质尚待刻画，尤其是洛伦兹破缺是否在对偶共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）中引入手征不对称结构。与 $(2+1)$ 维拓扑大质量引力（topologically massive gravity）的对比，构成该问题的关键动机。

Method: 结合外视界与内视界的第一定律（first laws），以热力学方法导出对偶二维 CFT 的右行与左行熵、温度与化学势；由与质量无关的熵乘积确定中心荷，并用 Cardy 公式复现内外视界熵。另以带电标量探针的隐藏共形对称性（hidden conformal symmetry）进行独立的动力学检验。

Result: 两种方法相互印证。核心结果为左右手移动中心荷相等，即 $c_R=c_L$。全部 CFT 数据对洛伦兹破缺参数光滑依赖，并在洛伦兹破缺关闭时约化为已知的 RN（Reissner-Nordström）结果。

Conclusion: 中心荷相等可归因于洛伦兹破缺的自发特征：bumblebee 引力作用量保持微分同胚与局域洛伦兹不变性，对称性仅由矢量真空期望值破缺，故对偶理论中不出现手征不对称结构，与 $(2+1)$ 维拓扑大质量引力形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: We study the holographic description of the RN-like black hole in bumblebee gravity, which is a vector-tensor theory with spontaneous Lorentz violation. By combining the first laws of the outer and inner horizons, the thermodynamics method yields the right- and left-moving entropies, temperatures, and chemical potentials of the dual two-dimensional conformal field theory (CFT), determines the central charges from the mass-independent entropy product, and reproduces the outer and inner horizon entropies through the Cardy formula. An independent dynamical test based on the hidden conformal symmetry of a charged scalar probe confirms these results. A central outcome is the equality of the right- and left-moving central charges, $c_R=c_L$, which we trace to the spontaneous character of Lorentz breaking. Because the action of bumblebee gravity preserves diffeomorphism and local Lorentz invariance, and the symmetry is broken only by a vector vacuum expectation value, no chirally asymmetric structure arises in the dual theory, in sharp contrast to topologically massive gravity in $(2+1)$ dimensions. All CFT data depend smoothly on the Lorentz-violating parameter and reduce to the known RN results when Lorentz violation is switched off.

</details>


### [193] [Semi-Universality of $O(N)$ model on $S^1\times S^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25622)
*Vinayak Mishra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究旋转 $S^1\times S^2$ 上临界大-$N$ $O(N)$ 模型（critical large-$N$ $O(N)$ model）的热配分函数（thermal partition function），利用外尔协变流体静力学展开（Weyl-covariant hydrostatic expansion）确定四阶导数阶的配分函数，并探讨近光速极限与相应留数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转使鞍点（saddle）呈现纬度依赖性，其主导轮廓由局部温度控制，因此需要考察旋转背景几何对配分函数及半普适（semi-universal）行为的影响。

Method: 采用外尔协变流体静力学展开（Weyl-covariant hydrostatic expansion），在高温度与半普适极限下确定配分函数至四阶导数阶（four-derivative order）。在近光速极限下，赤道附近几何约化为 pp-wave，并在该极限几何上直接计算依赖理论的留数（theory-dependent residue）。

Result: 高温结果与球面计算（sphere calculation）的近光速极限一致；低温展开揭示来自鞍点对热源响应的相互作用依赖修正（interaction-dependent corrections）。

Conclusion: 赤道附近的 pp-wave 几何可捕捉配分函数的预期半普适行为，且该极限几何可用于计算高低温下的理论依赖留数，低温修正体现鞍点对热源的响应。

Abstract: We study the thermal partition function of the critical large-$N$ $O(N)$ model on rotating $S^1\times S^2$. Rotation makes the saddle latitude dependent, with its leading profile governed by the local temperature. Using a Weyl-covariant hydrostatic expansion, we determine the partition function through four-derivative order in high-temperature and semi-universal limits. In the near-light-speed limit, the geometry around the equator reduces to a pp-wave and captures the expected semi-universal behavior of the partition function. We compute the corresponding theory-dependent residue directly on this limiting geometry, both at high and low temperatures. The high-temperature result agrees with the near-light-speed limit of the sphere calculation, while the low-temperature expansion reveals interaction-dependent corrections arising from the response of the saddle to the thermal source.

</details>


### [194] [Negative Rank Relations for Giant Graviton Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25846)
*Qi Chen,Zhongjie Huang,Xinan Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究了 \(\mathcal{N}=4\) 超杨–米尔斯理论（super Yang–Mills theory）中巨引力子（giant graviton）与对偶巨引力子（dual giant graviton）的关联函数（correlation functions），揭示其算子对应转置杨图（transposed Young diagrams）的 Schur 多项式（Schur polynomials），并伴随形式上的秩反射（rank reflection）\(N\to -N\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨引力子与对偶巨引力子在 \(AdS_5\times S^5\) 中对应差异较大的 D3 膜构型，但规范理论算子却由互为转置杨图的 Schur 多项式描述，此对应关系背后的关联函数结构值得澄清。

Method: 通过比较两类算子的 Schur 多项式表示，并利用拉格朗日插入（Lagrangian insertion）技术，在有限 \(N\) 的自由关联函数及分离点弱耦合被积函数（integrands）层面逐阶环路进行分析。

Result: 转置关系伴随形式秩反射 \(N\to -N\)；对 \(U(N)\) 与 \(SU(N)\) 均成立，在有限 \(N\) 自由关联函数中精确，并借助拉格朗日插入延伸至任意环路阶的分离点弱耦合被积函数；关系可推广至任意数量的 Schur 多项式外算子，只要所有杨图均转置。

Conclusion: 该转置–秩反射对应在微扰被积函数层面得到验证，并给出其可延伸至微扰被积函数之外的证据。

Abstract: We study correlation functions of giant and dual giant gravitons in $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory. Although the two objects correspond to rather different D3 brane configurations in $AdS_5\times S^5$, their gauge theory operators are Schur polynomials associated with transposed Young diagrams. We show that this transposition is accompanied by a formal rank reflection $N\to -N$ at the level of correlation functions. For both $U(N)$ and $SU(N)$, the relation is exact for free correlators at finite $N$, and, using Lagrangian insertion, extends to separated point weak coupling integrands at every loop order. More generally, it applies to any number of Schur polynomial external operators, provided all Young diagrams are transposed. We illustrate the relation in several examples and also discuss evidence for its extension beyond perturbative integrands.

</details>


### [195] [Numerical Sasaki--Einstein metrics and harmonic forms on del Pezzo links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25857)
*Nakwoo Kim,Sejin Kim,Hoseob Shin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在二次 del Pezzo 曲面 $\mathrm{dP}_2$ 锥的链（link）上，数值构造了不规则、体积最小的 Reeb 向量处对应的 Sasaki–Einstein 度量及两个原调和基本 $(1,1)$-形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是获得可用于研究 warped 非共形全息 IIB 背景（即 conifold 上 Klebanov–Tseytlin 解之类比）的度量与调和形式数据。为此需在 $\mathrm{dP}_2$ 上以高精度求解 Sasaki–Einstein 条件。

Method: 利用环面（toric）性质，在辛坐标（symplectic coordinates）下将度量编码为多边形上的单一凸函数；对 Guillemin 势的修正分别采用多项式展开与神经网络两种方式逼近。

Result: 多项式拟合在留出集上的 Monge–Ampère 残差均方低于 $10^{-13}$，而体积非最小的正则 Reeb 向量仅停留在 $10^{-2}$ 平台；并借助曲率不变量对 $Y^{p,q}$ 闭式度量、以及低频环面不变模的 Laplacian 谱对 Doran 等（2007）$\mathrm{dP}_3$ 的 Kähler–Einstein 数值结果进行了验证。

Conclusion: 所得到的度量与调和形式数据可支撑 warped 非共形全息 IIB 背景（conifold 上 Klebanov–Tseytlin 解的类比）的研究，方法亦通过已有闭式解与数值解得到交叉验证。

Abstract: We numerically construct the Sasaki--Einstein metric on the link of the cone over the second del Pezzo surface $\mathrm{dP}_2$ and two primitive harmonic basic $(1,1)$-forms at its irregular volume-minimizing Reeb vector. Being toric, the metric in symplectic coordinates is encoded in a single convex function on a polygon. We approximate the correction to the canonical Guillemin potential in two ways: polynomial expansion and neural networks. The polynomial fit achieves a held-out mean-squared Monge--Ampère residual below $10^{-13}$, in contrast to the $10^{-2}$ plateau for the non-volume-minimizing regular Reeb vector. We validate our method against closed-form metrics of $Y^{p,q}$ using curvature invariants, and also against the numerical result of Doran et al. (2007) for the Kähler--Einstein metric of $\mathrm{dP}_3$ using the Laplacian spectrum of low torus-invariant modes. Our data for the metric and harmonic forms can be used to study warped non-conformal holographic IIB backgrounds, the analogues of the Klebanov--Tseytlin solution on the conifold.

</details>


### [196] [Quantum Love Numbers are Non-Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25875)
*Asaad Elkhidir,Godwin Martin,Julio Parra-Martinez,M. V. S. Saketh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 经典层面史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）的静态潮汐勒夫数（static Love numbers）为零；本文在量子理论中表明该结论不成立。通过世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）计算无质量标量与光子散射的首个后闵可夫斯基阶（first post-Minkowskian order）量子修正，发现静态勒夫数重整化伴随对数发散，导致勒夫数被普朗克长度平方与视界半径平方之比 $\ell_{\rm pl}^2/r_{\rm h}^2$ 压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典广义相对论中史瓦西黑洞静态勒夫数的消失长期被视为其潮汐响应的重要特征，但其是否为量子层面依然成立的性质尚不明确。本文动机在于检验该经典消失（classical vanishing）在量子理论中是否被破坏，并给出可能的量子起源解释。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）框架，计算无质量标量（massless scalars）与光子（photons）在史瓦西黑洞上散射的康普顿振幅（Compton amplitude）的首个后闵可夫斯基阶（first post-Minkowskian order）量子修正，并分析其中出现的对数发散（logarithmic divergences）与静态勒夫数重整化（renormalization）的关联。

Result: 计算发现与静态勒夫数重整化相关的对数发散，表明重整化群跑动（renormalization-group running）必然生成勒夫数，其量级被普朗克长度平方除以视界半径平方 $\ell_{\rm pl}^{2}/r_{\rm h}^{2}$ 压低。该结果与经典消失源于静态广义相对论偶然对称性（accidental symmetry）、并被量子圈图（quantum loops）破坏的图像一致。

Conclusion: 经典静态勒夫数的消失并非量子理论中的稳健性质，而是静态广义相对论偶然对称性的产物，被量子圈图破坏。将结果外推至引力扰动（gravitational perturbations），史瓦西黑洞的四极静态勒夫数（quadrupolar static Love numbers）量级约为 $λ\sim M r_{\rm h}^{2} \ell_{\rm pl}^{2}$。

Abstract: The static Love numbers of four-dimensional Schwarzschild black holes vanish classically. We show that this is not true in the quantum theory. Working in the worldline effective field theory, we compute the leading quantum correction to the Compton amplitude for massless scalars and photons scattering off a Schwarzschild black hole at first post-Minkowskian order, and find logarithmic divergences associated with the renormalization of the static Love numbers. We conclude that renormalization-group running necessarily generates Love numbers suppressed by the square of the Planck length in units of the horizon radius, $\ell_{\rm pl}^{2}/r_{\rm h}^{2}$. This is consistent with the classical vanishing being a consequence of an accidental symmetry of static general relativity, broken by quantum loops. Extrapolated to gravitational perturbations, our results suggest that the quadrupolar static Love numbers of Schwarzschild are of size $λ\sim M r_{\rm h}^{2} \ell_{\rm pl}^{2}$.

</details>


### [197] [BRST quantization of the induced gauge theory for the Higgs-branch CFT${}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26571)
*Bogdan Stefański*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 D1-D5 希格斯支 CFT₂ 相关的 Witten 诱导规范理论（induced gauge theory），其中非动力学矢量多重态（non-dynamical vector multiplet）通过积分掉基本超多重态（fundamental hypermultiplets）而被"复活"。作者提出该理论的 BRST 量子化方案，并由此建立微扰计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Witten 诱导规范理论中，原本非动力学的矢量多重态经积分掉基本超多重态后获得动力学行为，但其量子化与微扰计算框架尚待明确。本文旨在为该诱导理论提供一致的量子化与可计算的结构。

Method: 提出该理论的 BRST 量子化（BRST quantisation），并推导其诱导传播子（induced propagators）；同时选取特定的正则化方案（regularization prescription）。

Result: 鬼场部分（ghost sector）在次领头阶（subleading order）才开始贡献；诱导的 Aφ² 顶点满足 Slavnov-Taylor 恒等式；规范不变关联函数（gauge-invariant correlators）与规范参数无关。

Conclusion: 结合所选正则化方案，上述结果给出了一个用于计算诱导理论中可观测量（observables）的微扰框架（perturbative framework）。

Abstract: We study Witten's induced gauge theory associated with the D1-D5 Higgs-branch CFT${}_2$, in which the non-dynamical vector multiplet becomes reanimated by integrating out the fundamental hypermultiplets. We propose a BRST quantisation of this theory and derive its induced propagators. We show that the ghost sector first contributes at subleading order, that the induced $Aφ^2$ vertex satisfies the Slavnov-Taylor identity and that gauge-invariant correlators are independent of the gauge parameter. Together with our chosen regularization prescription, this gives a perturbative framework for computing observables in the induced theory.

</details>


### [198] [Density-driven reversal of flavour-entanglement anisotropy in the D3-D7 holographic conductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26641)
*Fuminori Okabayashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在有限温度与电荷密度下，利用 D3-D7 全息模型研究强耦合导体中探针味（probe-flavour）对等时空间纠缠熵（equal-time spatial entanglement entropy）的首个贡献，发现熵修正之差呈现密度驱动的符号反转（sign reversal）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强耦合、有限温度与电荷密度导体在外加恒定直流电场（DC electric field）下的量子纠缠结构，特别是探针味自由度对空间纠缠熵的贡献，此前缺乏系统性刻画。

Method: 采用全息（holographic）D3-D7 系统实现 3+1 维强耦合导体，施加恒定直流电场；比较沿电场方向与垂直电场方向延伸、具有有限宽度的板状（slab-shaped）子区域，提取两者熵修正之差，并借助互信息（mutual information）作为紫外有限（ultraviolet-finite）检验。

Result: 熵修正之差隔离出均匀、空间无迹（spatially traceless）的响应，并表现出密度驱动的符号反转；在所考察参数范围内，交叉密度（crossover density）随电场强度增大而升高、随条带宽度增大而降低。该效应源于完整 Dirac-Born-Infeld 应力各向异性（stress anisotropy）中的径向符号竞争。

Conclusion: 该反转属于平滑的响应交叉（smooth response crossover），而非热力学相变（thermodynamic phase transition）；互信息给出定性一致的反转并提供了紫外有限性检验。

Abstract: We study the leading probe-flavour contribution to equal-time spatial entanglement entropy in a homogeneous 3+1-dimensional strongly coupled conductor at finite temperature and charge density, realised holographically by the D3-D7 system and driven by a constant DC electric field. We compare slab-shaped subregions that are extended in two spatial directions and have finite width parallel or transverse to the field. The difference between the corresponding entropy corrections isolates a homogeneous, spatially traceless response and exhibits a density-driven sign reversal. Over the investigated parameter range, the crossover density increases with the electric-field magnitude and decreases with the strip width. Mutual information shows the same qualitative reversal and provides an ultraviolet-finite check. We trace the effect to a radial sign competition in the full Dirac-Born-Infeld stress anisotropy. The reversal is a smooth response crossover rather than a thermodynamic phase transition.

</details>


### [199] [Quasinormal modes and quantum black hole interiors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26724)
*Chiara Coviello,Ansh Gupta,Robie A. Hennigar,Kai Shi,Andrew Svesko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过复分析方法计算一类精确 (2+1) 维量子黑洞解的渐近准正规模（asymptotic QNM）谱，揭示其与黑洞内部奇点结构的对应关系，并指出渐近 QNM 可作为探测奇点标度指数的稳健工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大泛音极限下的准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）即渐近 QNM 对黑洞内部几何高度敏感，因此可作为探测奇点结构的探针。作者借此研究一类精确 (2+1) 维量子黑洞解的奇点结构，考察非微扰量子效应主导时黑洞内部的几何转变。

Method: 采用复分析方法，将径向坐标（radial coordinate）解析延拓至复平面，计算该 (2+1) 维量子黑洞解的渐近 QNM 谱；在关于复化径向坐标全局 Stokes 拓扑的一般假设下，由频率偏移量（offset）重构更一般（类空或类时）奇点的标度指数，并进一步计算次领头阶修正。

Result: 领头阶 QNM 频率均符合 $ω=(\text{offset})+n(\text{gap})$ 的通用形式（$n$ 为泛音数），并经数值验证。次领头阶修正提供了提取奇点附近度规函数标度的稳健方法。带电量子黑洞的渐近 QNM 在度规函数中性与电荷贡献分别主导的两个区间之间出现交叉（crossover），交叉区域内 QNM 呈现振荡行为，可能是内视界（inner horizon）的特征。

Conclusion: 研究结果例证了随非微扰量子效应增强、接近类空奇点时“Kasner eons”之间的转变。渐近 QNM 频谱可用于重构奇点标度指数并区分不同几何区间，且振荡行为或可作为内视界的探针。

Abstract: Quasinormal modes (QNMs) of black hole perturbations in the large overtone limit, namely, asymptotic QNMs, are highly sensitive to a black hole's interior geometry. We probe the singularity structure of a class of exact (2+1)-dimensional quantum black hole solutions to semi-classical gravity by computing their asymptotic QNM spectra. We do this using methods of complex analysis in which the radial coordinate is analytically continued to the complex plane. The leading order QNM frequencies all fit the generic form $ω=(\text{offset})+n(\text{gap})$, for overtone number $n$, which we also confirm numerically. Under a general assumption about the global Stokes topology of the complexified radial coordinate, we show how to reconstruct the scaling exponent of more general (spacelike or timelike) black hole singularities from the offset. We then compute subleading corrections to the asymptotic QNMs, from which we provide a robust method for extracting the scaling of the metric function near the singularity. Our findings exemplify the transition between "Kasner eons", successive regimes encountered on approach to a spacelike singularity, as non-perturbative quantum effects become dominant. For charged quantum black holes, the asymptotic QNMs exhibit a crossover between two regimes in which the neutral and charge contributions to the metric function dominate, respectively. Within the crossover region, the QNMs develop an oscillatory behavior that may be a signature of the inner horizon.

</details>


### [200] [Renormalization Group in far-from-equilibrium states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06243)
*Vladimir Rosenhaus,Michael Smolkin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将重整化群（renormalization group, RG）流拓展至远非平衡态（far-from-equilibrium states），聚焦空间均匀、时间无关且尺度不变的状态（$n_k \sim k^{-\gamma}$），指出正 $\gamma$ 会降低算符维度、使边缘相互作用（marginal interactions）变为相关相互作用（relevant interactions）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 RG 分析多针对真空态，其在远非平衡状态下的行为尚不清晰；非平衡态中 RG 流与真空情形存在本质差异。作者希望通过选取可处理的特殊状态类型，使这一问题的研究变得可行。

Method: 针对空间均匀、时间无关、尺度不变且占据数为 $n_k \sim k^{-\gamma}$ 的状态，计算单圈贝塔函数（one-loop beta functions），并借鉴 epsilon 展开（epsilon expansion）构造红外不动点（IR fixed points），其中 epsilon 由相互作用的标度行为而非时空维度设定。

Result: 非平衡态中的 RG 流与真空情形定性不同：正 $\gamma$ 会降低算符维度，把边缘相互作用转化为相关相互作用。依据贝塔函数符号，反作用（backreaction）可能仅使红外态产生微小偏移，也可能完全改变该态的性质；在近边缘相互作用下可构造类 epsilon 展开与红外不动点。

Conclusion: 在 RG 流语言下，临界平衡标度（critical-balance scaling）——广泛应用于天体物理与海洋波等领域——对应于状态沿 RG 流动态自调整，直至相互作用变为边缘相互作用。

Abstract: We study renormalization group flows in far-from-equilibrium states. The study is made tractable by focusing on states that are spatially homogeneous, time-independent, and scale-invariant. Such states, in which mode $k$ has occupation numbers $n_k \sim k^{-γ}$, are well known in nonlinear physics. RG flow in such states is qualitatively different from that in the vacuum -- a positive $γ$ decreases the dimension of an operator, turning marginal interactions into relevant interactions. We compute one-loop beta functions. Depending on the sign of the beta function, backreaction may either cause a minor shift of the state in the IR, or completely change the nature of the state. Focusing on nearly marginal interactions, we construct an analog of the epsilon expansion and IR fixed points, with epsilon now set by the scaling of the interaction rather than the spacetime dimension. In the language of RG flow, critical-balance scaling -- which has applications in fields as varied as astrophysics and ocean waves -- corresponds to the state dynamically adjusting itself along the RG flow until the interaction becomes marginal.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [The Failure of Simultaneous Quantum Typicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25726)
*Benoit Collins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定了同时量子典型性（simultaneous quantum typicality）失效的精确阈值：混合态需三方，纯态需四方，并通过四量子比特 Higuchi–Sudbery 态构造反例，否证了 Dutil 猜想及若干单次同时最小熵光滑化猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在判定同时量子典型性在多体情形下何时失效，即寻找参与方数目的最小阈值，并检验 2011 年提出的 Dutil 多体量子典型性猜想以及 Drescher–Fawzi、Colomer–Winter 的单次同时最小熵光滑化（min-entropy-smoothing）猜想是否成立。

Method: 证明将同时量子光滑化（simultaneous quantum smoothing）与 $\CP^1$ 上的多项式集中（polynomial concentration）相联系；借助对称性把问题约化为 $\Sym^n(\mathbb C^2)$ 上的框架不等式（frame inequality），其中线性熵亏损与多项式集中的二次代价相竞争，并用 Bernstein–Markov 估计与次调和障碍（subharmonic barrier）控制该代价。

Result: 对四量子比特 Higuchi–Sudbery 态，任何满足三个重叠边缘分布纯度界、且带足够小固定正熵余量的序列，其与输入张量幂（tensor powers）的迹距离（trace distance）趋于最大值 2，即渐近完全可区分；该最大分离在四量子比特态空间的某开邻域内以共同正余量持续存在，取掉一方即得混合三体反例。由此得到同时典型性失效的最小参与方数：混合态三方、纯态四方。

Conclusion: 结果否证了 Dutil 的多体量子典型性猜想及 Drescher–Fawzi 与 Colomer–Winter 的单次同时最小熵光滑化猜想；结合较小系统的正面结果，精确确定了同时典型性可能失效的最小参与方数目。

Abstract: We establish exact thresholds for the failure of simultaneous quantum typicality: three parties for mixed states and four for pure states. For the four-qubit Higuchi--Sudbery state, every sequence satisfying the expected purity bounds on three overlapping marginals, with sufficiently small fixed positive entropy slack, is asymptotically perfectly distinguishable from the tensor powers of the input: its trace distance from them tends to the maximum value $2$. This maximal separation persists, with a common positive slack, throughout an open neighborhood in the full four-qubit state space. Tracing out one party yields a mixed tripartite example.
  The proof connects simultaneous quantum smoothing with polynomial concentration on $\CP^1$. Symmetry reduces the problem to a frame inequality on $\Sym^n(\mathbb C^2)$, where a linear entropy deficit competes with a quadratic cost of polynomial concentration. Bernstein--Markov estimates and a subharmonic barrier control this cost, yielding an exponential obstruction to simultaneous typicality.
  These results disprove Dutil's multiparty quantum typicality conjecture, formulated in 2011, and the one-shot simultaneous min-entropy-smoothing conjectures of Drescher--Fawzi and Colomer--Winter. Together with the positive results for smaller systems, they determine the smallest numbers of parties at which simultaneous typicality can fail.

</details>


### [202] [Nonlocal advantage of quantum coherence in tau-lepton pairs from electron--positron collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25214)
*Yoav Afik,Juan Ramón Muñoz de Nova,Sarthak Sharma,Surya Sundar Raman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究电子-正电子湮灭产生τ轻子对（$e^+e^- \to \tau^+\tau^-$）过程中的量子非局域优势（NAQC），并提出专用见证量（witness）与实验方案，用于在Belle II与FCC-$ee$能标下认证NAQC，这将是迄今对撞机上测得的最强量子关联形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联（quantum correlations）近来在粒子对撞物理中受到关注，其中NAQC是目前研究的最强量子关联形式；因其强度更高，仅在更窄的相空间区域存在，故比贝尔非局域性（Bell nonlocality）更难观测。τ轻子对产生被视为理想测试平台，因为它在较大相空间区域展现强量子关联与NAQC。

Method: 在质心能量10.58与91.19 GeV（分别对应Belle II与未来FCC-$ee$）下研究$e^+e^- \to \tau^+\tau^-$产生中的NAQC。为此开发了一个专用NAQC见证量（witness），可通过测量单一量值认证NAQC的存在，并设计了实现导致NAQC定义的引导博弈（steering game）的实验方案。

Result: 估计了确立NAQC存在所需的实验灵敏度：相当大比例的事件数（Belle II为0.18，FCC-$ee$为0.31）贡献于NAQC信号。Belle II实现5$\sigma$观测NAQC需约1%的精度，而FCC-$ee$下几个百分点的精度即足够。所设计的方案是首次在高能对撞机上实现引导博弈。

Conclusion: 若观测成功，将构成迄今在对撞机上测得的最强量子关联形式；结果可方便地推广至不同质心能量的其他电子-正电子对撞机。

Abstract: Quantum correlations have recently attracted significant attention in collider physics, with Nonlocal Advantage of Quantum Coherence (NAQC) representing the strongest form of quantum correlation studied so far. Owing to its strength, NAQC is considerably more challenging to observe than Bell nonlocality since it is only present in narrower regions of phase space. Unlike other systems, tau-lepton pair ($τ^+τ^-$) production in electron--positron ($e^+e^-$) collisions provides an ideal testing ground, as it exhibits strong quantum correlations and, in particular, NAQC over a large region of phase space. In this paper, we investigate NAQC in $e^+e^- \to τ^+τ^-$ production at center-of-mass energies of 10.58 and 91.19~GeV, corresponding to the ongoing Belle~II and future FCC-$ee$ experiments, respectively. Remarkably, for our experimental proposal, we develop a dedicated NAQC witness, which allows to certify NAQC presence by measuring one single magnitude. We estimate the experimental sensitivities needed to establish the presence of NAQC, finding that a large fraction of the number of events (specifically, 0.18 for Belle~II and 0.31 for FCC-$ee$) contribute to the NAQC signal. A 5$σ$ observation of NAQC requires a precision of approximately 1\% at Belle~II, while a precision of a few percent is sufficient at FCC-$ee$. The resulting observation would constitute the strongest form of quantum correlation measured in a collider to date. In addition, we design an experimental scheme to implement the steering game leading to the NAQC definition, representing the first implementation of a steering game in a high-energy collider. Our results are easily extendable to other electron--positron colliders with different center-of-mass energies.

</details>


### [203] [Transversal non-Clifford gates on good quantum locally testable codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26691)
*Yiming Li,Zimu Li,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将既有横向门框架应用于新近的“好”量子局部可测码（quantum locally testable code, qLTC）构造，在量子低密度奇偶校验码（quantum low-density parity check code, qLDPC）与 qLTC 上同时实现渐近最优参数的横向逻辑多控 Z 门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在 qLDPC 码与 qLTC 上同时获得渐近最优参数的横向逻辑多控 Z 门；此前接近“好”码的构造以超图积（hypergraph product）为基空间，其配对方法不适用于当前的算术立方复形（arithmetic cubical complex）基空间，故需新的构造途径。

Method: 采用覆盖空间方法（covering space method），在有限算术立方复形上构造非零杯积配对（cup product pairing）；将配对表示为 Moore 行列式之积中的系数，并通过二部多重图特化（bipartite multigraph specialization）证明其多项式非零性；随后构造覆盖空间以获得“好”qLTC 的渐近族，使拉回配对诱导出所需的非平凡横向逻辑作用。

Result: 在 qLDPC 码与 qLTC 上同时实现了非平凡横向逻辑多控 Z 门，参数达到渐近最优；所构造的非零杯积配对经二部多重图特化证明具有多项式非零性。

Conclusion: 通过覆盖空间方法在算术立方复形上建立非零杯积配对，并将其拉回至覆盖空间诱导横向逻辑作用，从而在 qLDPC 与 qLTC 上同时取得渐近最优参数的横向逻辑多控 Z 门。

Abstract: We achieve nontrivial transversal logical multi-controlled-$Z$ gates with asymptotically optimal parameters simultaneously on quantum low-density parity check codes and quantum locally testable codes, by applying the gate framework of [arXiv:2604.01874] to the recent good qLTC construction of [arXiv:2609.20780]. To this end, we use the covering space method to construct a nonzero cup product pairing on a finite arithmetic cubical complex. This differs from the previous construction of almost-good codes, whose base space is a hypergraph product. We express the pairing as a coefficient in a product of Moore determinants and prove polynomial nonvanishing by a bipartite multigraph specialization. We then construct covering spaces to obtain an asymptotic family of good qLTCs on which the pulled-back pairing induces the desired nontrivial transversal logical action.

</details>


### [204] [When are bosonic Gaussian states classical to learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26705)
*Senrui Chen,Antonio Anna Mele,Francesco Anna Mele,John Preskill*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从学习理论（learning theory）视角研究玻色高斯态（bosonic Gaussian states）何时“可经典地学习”，发现可学习性由热涨落（thermal fluctuations）调控并呈现平滑过渡：冷态本质上非经典、需 $\\Omega(n^3)$ 样本，暖态可经典学习、$\\nu=\\Omega(1)$ 时样本复杂度降至 $\\Theta(n^2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答物理学基本问题：经典行为何时从量子系统中涌现。玻色高斯态同时包含经典场行为与光的量子特性，为探索量子—经典边界（quantum-classical boundary）提供自然框架，作者据此提出“玻色高斯态何时可经典地被学习”这一学习理论问题。

Method: 采用学习理论（learning-theoretic）方法，以样本复杂度（sample complexity）与测量操作复杂度为度量，对比 n 模玻色高斯态与经典 2n 元高斯分布的层析（tomography）。分析区分单拷贝（非纠缠）测量与少拷贝纠缠测量，并以协方差矩阵 $\\Sigma$ 的约束刻画冷、暖两类状态。

Result: 冷态（$\\Sigma\\le(\\frac12+O(\\frac1n))I$，接近真空协方差）非经典：单拷贝测量下层析需 $\\Omega(n^3)$ 拷贝，严格高于经典高斯分布的 $\\Theta(n^2)$，且少拷贝纠缠测量也难以缓解该困难。暖态（$\\Sigma\\ge(\\frac12+\\nu)I$，$\\nu>0$）可经典学习：单拷贝层析需 $N=\\Theta(n^2\\min(n,1+\\nu^{-1}))$ 拷贝，该界紧致且可由简单、非自适应、非纠缠的外差测量（heterodyne measurements）达到；当 $\\nu=\\Omega(1)$ 时降至 $\\Theta(n^2)$，与经典情形一致。

Conclusion: 结果紧密刻画了玻色高斯态可学习性上的量子—经典过渡（quantum-to-classical crossover），揭示了基础物理与统计学习理论（statistical learning theory）之间的新联系，并对实际传感实验（sensing experiments）具有启示意义。

Abstract: A fundamental question in physics is: When does classical behavior emerge from quantum systems? Bosonic Gaussian states provide a natural setting to explore this quantum-classical boundary, as they capture both the classical field behavior and the intrinsic quantum nature of light. Here, we address this problem from a learning-theoretic perspective by asking: When are bosonic Gaussian states classical to learn? That is, under what conditions (if any) can an n-mode bosonic Gaussian state be learned with as few samples, and with operations as simple, as are needed to learn a classical 2n-variate Gaussian distribution? We establish a smooth crossover in learnability governed by the state's thermal fluctuations:
  - Cold Gaussian states are non-classical to learn: When the covariance matrix satisfies $Σ\le(\frac12+O(\frac1n))I$, i.e. close to the vacuum covariance, tomography under single-copy (i.e., non-entangled) measurements fundamentally requires $Ω(n^3)$ copies, strictly exceeding the sample complexity $Θ(n^2)$ of learning classical Gaussian distributions. We show that this hardness persists even when few-copy entangled measurements are allowed.
  - Warm Gaussian states are classical to learn: When thermal fluctuations exceed the vacuum noise, parameterized by $Σ\ge(\frac12+ν)I$ for any parameter $ν>0$, we prove that single-copy tomography requires $N=Θ\left(n^2\min(n,1+ν^{-1})\right)$ copies. This bound is tight and is achieved by simple, non-adaptive, unentangled heterodyne measurements. Crucially, for $ν=Ω(1)$, the sample complexity drops to $Θ(n^2)$, matching the classical case.
  Our results tightly characterize a quantum-to-classical crossover in the learnability of bosonic Gaussian states, reveal a novel connection between fundamental physics and statistical learning theory, and have implications for real-world sensing experiments.

</details>


### [205] [Explicit channels with unbounded gains in classical communication using entangled inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26743)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了一类显式的有限维量子信道（quantum channels），其使用乘积态码字（product-state codewords）与集体解码（collective decoding）时可实现的最优经典通信速率趋于零，而仅在信道输入对之间使用纠缠（entanglement）时，可达速率无界增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在给出 Hastings 概率性构造的去随机化（derandomization）版本，以显式信道证明纠缠对经典通信容量的增强效应，从而替代原有依赖概率方法的非显式构造。

Method: 构造将确定性的 qudit Clifford 酉算子（deterministic qudit Clifford unitaries）与二值测量（binary measurement）及经典前馈（classical feedforward）相结合。关键设计在于测量与前馈过程的精心编排，仅借助矩估计（moment estimates）即可导出所需的单拷贝与双拷贝输出熵界（one-copy and two-copy output entropy bounds），无需依赖底层酉算子族的强收敛性（strong convergence）。

Result: 所构造的信道族满足：乘积态码字配合集体解码时最优经典通信速率趋近于零，而仅利用输入对内部纠缠时可达速率无界增长。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了 Hastings 概率性构造的显式去随机化版本，表明纠缠对经典通信速率的增强可在确定性有限维信道族中实现，且其熵界分析可通过矩估计完成。

Abstract: We construct an explicit family of finite-dimensional quantum channels for which the optimal classical communication rate achievable with product-state codewords and collective decoding tends to zero, while rates achievable using entanglement only within pairs of channel inputs grow without bound.
  Our construction combines deterministic qudit Clifford unitaries with a binary measurement and classical feedforward, providing a derandomization to Hastings' probabilistic construction. The key ingredient is a careful design of measurement and feedforward process that yields the required one-copy and two-copy output entropy bounds from moment estimates alone, without requiring strong convergence of the underlying unitary family.

</details>


### [206] [On the Complexity of Finding Decoherence Free Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26769)
*Evan Borras*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究判定开放量子系统是否具有无退相干子空间（decoherence free subspace, DFS）的计算复杂度。作者将问题置于由时间无关 Lindblad 主方程（Lindblad master equation）描述的马尔可夫开放量子系统（Markovian open quantum systems）框架下，并引入 k-Local Lindbladian 问题以刻画计算纯度衰减率的难度。结果表明，当局域性（locality）k ≥ 5 时，两个问题对 QMA 类均为困难，其中前者在完美完备性（perfect completeness）下成立，后者对 QMA 完备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无退相干子空间是开放量子系统的稳态结构，可保持其内态的量子相干性（quantum coherence），因而在量子信息科学与技术中有广泛应用。本文旨在厘清判定一个开放量子系统是否容许此类子空间的固有计算难度。

Method: 研究在马尔可夫开放量子系统背景下进行，系统由时间无关 Lindblad 主方程（Lindblad master equation）支配。作者同时引入 k-Local Lindbladian 问题，用于刻画 Lindbladian 动力学下纯度衰减率（purity decay rate）的计算难度。

Result: 当局域性 k ≥ 5 时，两个问题对 Quantum Merlin Arthur（QMA）复杂度类均为困难：判定 DFS 存在问题在完美完备性（perfect completeness）下为 QMA-困难，而 k-Local Lindbladian 问题对 QMA 完备。困难性构造将 Kitaev 时钟哈密顿量（clock Hamiltonian）构造推广至开放量子系统，通过将量子电路执行编码进含有纯态与混合历史态（history states）的 Lindbladian 稳态子空间，该子空间依据所编码电路的输出而呈现混合性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使是对于量子计算，判定一般马尔可夫开放量子系统是否容许无退相干子空间也是难以处理的（intractable）。

Abstract: Decoherence free subspaces are a steady-state structure of the open quantum system which preserves quantum coherence between the states lying with in it and thus has found a variety of applications throughout quantum information science and technology. In this paper we study the computational complexity of deciding whether an open quantum system admits a decoherence free subspace or not. More specifically we study this problem with in the context of Markovian open quantum systems, governed by the time-independent Lindblad master equation. Along the way we introduce the $k$-Local Lindbladian problem, which captures the difficulty of computing purity decay rates under Lindbladian dynamics. We show that both problems are hard for the complexity class Quantum Merlin Arthur (QMA) when the locality $k \geq 5$, with the first under perfect completeness and the second being complete for QMA. Our hardness construction generalizes Kitaev's clock Hamiltonian construction to the open quantum system setting by encoding the execution of a quantum circuit into the steady subspace of a Lindbladian containing both pure and mixed history states. This subspace is then mixed depending on the output of the encoded circuit. Our results suggest that deciding whether a generic Markovian open quantum system admits a decoherence free subspace is intractable even for quantum computation.

</details>


### [207] [Probing quantum spin liquids with multiple quantum coherences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25193)
*Lukas Homeier,Simon Linsel,Lode Pollet,Ana Maria Rey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出将多量子相干（multiple-quantum coherences, MQCs）作为探测有能隙 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquids）的相敏诊断量，并在扩展环面码（extended toric code）中通过大规模量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）模拟建立其与任意子（anyon）激发及相变的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟器（quantum simulators）开始制备材料中难以干净实现的长寻相（long-sought phases），但当相的判定性质本质非局域、无法由常规局域测量刻画时，识别这些相仍是重大挑战。

Method: 以扩展环面码为模型，采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟，建立基本任意子激发与不同 MQC 扇区（sectors）权重之间的直接对应；并使用子系统分辨（subsystem-resolved）的 MQC 及基于绝热往返（adiabatic round trips）回流保真度（return fidelity）的提取协议进行基准测试。

Result: MQC 通过特征性信号揭示相变处的任意子凝聚（anyon condensation），且对遮蔽常规诊断量的涨落保持稳健；子系统分辨 MQC 提供二分纠缠见证（bipartite entanglement witness），可区分经典环气体（classical loop gas）与量子相干闭合环气体（quantum-coherent closed-loops gas）；局域相干测量保留实验可及的相变信号。

Conclusion: 结果确立 MQC 为一种实用诊断工具，可用于刻画量子自旋液体的激发、相结构及全局约束（global constraints）。

Abstract: Quantum simulators are beginning to prepare long-sought phases of matter that remain difficult to realize cleanly in materials. Yet identifying such phases remains a major challenge when their defining properties are inherently non-local and cannot be captured by conventional local measurements. Here we establish multiple-quantum coherences (MQCs) as phase-sensitive diagnostics for gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ quantum spin liquids. Focusing on the extended toric code and using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we build a direct correspondence between the elementary anyonic excitations and the weights of different MQC sectors. MQCs thereby reveal anyon condensation across the phase transitions through characteristic signatures that remain robust against fluctuations that obscure conventional diagnostics. Subsystem-resolved MQCs further provide a bipartite entanglement witness that distinguishes a classical loop gas from a quantum-coherent closed-loops gas. We develop and benchmark a practical protocol to extract MQCs based on the return fidelity of adiabatic round trips, and show that local coherence measurements retain experimentally accessible signatures of the phase transitions. Our results establish MQCs as a practical diagnostic for the excitations, phase structure and global constraints of quantum spin liquids.

</details>


### [208] [Adaptive thresholding for scalable measurement-based qubit reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25208)
*Qian Cao,Unnati Akhouri,Samuel Vizvary,Nissim Ofek,Wei Dai,Kater W. Murch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）的自适应测量重置协议，利用完整模拟测量历史动态调整反馈阈值，在超导量子比特（superconducting qubit）上实现高保真度基态初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子信息处理需要快速、高保真度的量子比特初始化，但传统重置协议要么依赖长弛豫时间，要么丢弃连续测量记录中的信息。

Method: 在色散读取的 transmon 量子比特上，借助基于 FPGA 的实时反馈实现自适应协议：每次读取后用贝叶斯推断更新量子态估计，并动态调整反馈阈值，从而利用完整模拟测量历史；同时与固定阈值及重复直到成功（repeat-until-success, RUS）策略进行对比。

Result: 该协议在少量重置轮次后实现 $99.44\pm0.04\%$ 的基态初始化保真度；在多量子比特实验中，自适应阈值提供确定性的重置时长，并保持五量子比特同时重置保真度 $98.78 \pm 0.19\%$。

Conclusion: 结果表明，贝叶斯自适应阈值（Bayesian adaptive thresholding）是超导量子处理器中实现快速量子比特重置的一种实用且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Fast, high-fidelity qubit initialization is a key primitive for scalable quantum information processing, but conventional reset protocols either require long relaxation times or discard information contained in continuous measurement records. We demonstrate an adaptive measurement-based reset protocol for superconducting qubits that uses Bayesian inference to update the qubit-state estimate after each readout and dynamically adjust the feedback threshold. Unlike fixed-threshold or repeat-until-success (RUS) protocols, the method utilizes the full analog measurement history, enabling the reset decision to become progressively more conservative as confidence in ground-state preparation increases. Implemented with real-time FPGA-based feedback on a dispersively readout transmon qubit, the protocol achieves a ground-state initialization fidelity of $99.44\pm0.04\%$ after a small number of reset rounds. We further compare adaptive reset with RUS strategies in multi-qubit experiments and show that adaptive thresholding provides deterministic reset duration while maintaining a five-qubit simultaneous-reset fidelity of $98.78 \pm 0.19\%$. These results establish Bayesian adaptive thresholding as a practical and scalable route to fast qubit reset in superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [209] [Thermalization and dephasing in an isolated system of coupled qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25336)
*Jukka P. Pekola,Bayan Karimi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析孤立耦合量子比特（qubit）系统中单个量子比特子系统的自洽弱耦合主方程（master equation）动力学，论证去相位（dephasing）与热化（thermalization）的出现，并指出需非线性（non-linearity）才能使系统达到热分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体幺正演化（unitary evolution）系统按定义不会去相位或热化，但对其子系统的描述可能呈现不同行为。作者旨在考察孤立耦合量子比特集合中单个量子比特作为子系统时的有效开放系统动力学。

Method: 将孤立耦合量子比特集合中逐个量子比特视为子系统，自洽地写出其弱耦合主方程；分析弱非简谐振子（weakly anharmonic oscillator）与理想量子比特（二能级系统，two-level system）的去相位行为；并将非线性以三波混频（three-wave mixing）形式纳入分析。

Result: 弱非简谐振子和理想量子比特表现出：密度矩阵对角元在长时极限达到稳态，理想量子比特的非对角元呈指数衰减。在线性耦合下只有简并量子比特发生相互作用，系统不达热分布；引入三波混频形式的非线性后，长时极限下系统达到热分布，其温度由初始非热态的能量的唯一确定。

Conclusion: 孤立幺正系统的子系统仍可通过自洽弱耦合主方程展现去相位与热化，非线性相互作用是达到热分布的必要条件，温度由初始能量决定。文中还提出了基于超导量子电路（superconducting quantum circuit）的潜在实验方案。

Abstract: A system evolving unitarily does not dephase or thermalize by definition. Yet, if one considers a part of a unitary system, situation is different. Here we analyze an isolated set of coupled qubits taking one qubit at a time as a subsystem, and writing self-consistently a weak-coupling master equation for it. Weakly anharmonic oscillators and ideal qubits (two-level systems) demonstrate dephasing, i.e. the diagonal elements of the density matrix reach a steady-state in the long-time limit, and the off-diagonal elements of ideal qubits decay exponentially. For the linear coupling between the qubits, only the degenerate ones interact, and the system does not reach thermal distribution.
