However, non-linearity, in our analysis in form of three-wave mixing, ensures a thermal distribution in the long time limit, where the temperature is determined uniquely by the energy in the initial non-thermal state. Finally, we present a potential experimental scheme based on a superconducting quantum circuit.

</details>


### [210] [Random Gaussian Augmented Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25458)
*Stavya Puri,Poetri Sonya Tarabunga,Piotr Sierant*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出并研究高斯增强矩阵乘积态（Gaussian-augmented matrix product states, GAMPS），即在矩阵乘积态（matrix product state, MPS）上施加自由费米子（Gaussian）幺正变换。通过随机 GAMPS 系综的副本张量网络（replica tensor-network）方法，揭示高斯增强可显著扩展张量网络的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强纠缠（strong entanglement）限制了张量网络（tensor network）方法对量子多体系统的描述范围，因此需要探索能突破此限制的态族。作者旨在考察高斯增强这一构造是否能提升表达能力，并理解其结构与能力来源。

Method: 构造 GAMPS：将自由费米子（free-fermionic, Gaussian）幺正变换作用于矩阵乘积态。为探明该态族能力，定义随机 GAMPS（random GAMPS）系综，即独立采样随机矩阵乘积态与高斯幺正变换。利用高斯幺正变换的代数结构，发展副本张量网络方法，用于计算该系综在数百量子比特规模上的平均。

Result: 随机 GAMPS 在较小的键维数（bond dimension）下即表现出接近最大的体积律纠缠（volume-law entanglement）；随键维数增加，其他量子资源量（resource quantifiers）迅速趋近 Haar 随机态的值。纠缠标度表明，与未增强的矩阵乘积态相比，逼近体积律熵所需键维数呈指数级下降。此外，随机 GAMPS 在键维数与系统尺寸无关的固定精度下表现出态二设计（state two-design）行为。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了高斯增强（Gaussian augmentation）如何扩展张量网络的表达能力，表明该构造可在键维数上以更优的纠缠标度逼近体积律纠缠，并在固定精度下实现与系统尺寸无关的二设计行为。

Abstract: Strong entanglement limits the reach of tensor-network descriptions of quantum many-body systems. We study Gaussian-augmented matrix product states (GAMPS), formed by applying a free-fermionic (Gaussian) unitary to a matrix product state. To explore the capabilities of this family, we consider random GAMPS}, a minimally structured ensemble obtained by independently sampling random matrix product states and Gaussian unitaries. Exploiting the algebraic structure of Gaussian unitaries, we develop a replica tensor-network method that computes averages over the random GAMPS ensemble for hundreds of qubits. Random GAMPS exhibit nearly maximal volume-law entanglement already at modest bond dimension, while other quantum resource quantifiers rapidly approach Haar-random state values as the bond dimension increases. The entanglement scaling suggests an exponential reduction in the bond dimension needed to approach the volume-law entropy compared with unaugmented matrix product states. We find a state two-design behavior of random GAMPS at fixed accuracy with a bond dimension independent of system size. Our results reveal how Gaussian augmentation expands the expressive power of tensor networks.

</details>


### [211] [Dynamical, thermal, and ground-state multiparameter quantum metrology: Fundamental limits and their attainability in magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25977)
*Riccardo Pedroni,Víctor Izquierdo,Advay Burte,Paolo Abiuso,John Calsamiglia,Martí Perarnau-Llobet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究形如 H_θ = H_θ^P + H^C 的哈密顿量中多参数的同时估计问题，覆盖动力学、热平衡与基态三种度量框架，将单参数估计的界推广到多参数情形并给出可饱和条件；随后以矢量磁强测量（vector magnetometry）为例，推导磁场分量同时估计的紧界并识别可饱和的最优多体控制哈密顿量 H^C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多集中于单参数估计的界，而实际度量任务（如矢量磁强测量）需要同时估计多个参数，因此有必要将单参数界推广到多参数情形，并明确其在三种基本度量框架下的可饱和条件与最优控制形式。

Method: 考虑哈密顿量 H_θ = H_θ^P + H^C，其中 H_θ^P 编码未知参数、H^C 为控制项；在动力学（参数幺正编码）、热平衡与基态三种度量框架下，将单参数估计的界推广到同时多参数估计，并建立其可饱和性所需条件；进而针对矢量磁强测量推导紧界并识别能使界饱和的最优多体控制哈密顿量 H^C。

Result: 获得了适用于三种度量框架的多参数同时估计的广义界及其可饱和条件；在矢量磁强测量中给出磁场各分量同时估计的紧界，并识别出可实现饱和的最优多体控制哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了幺正动力学、热平衡态与基态三种情形下矢量磁强测量的基本极限（fundamental limits）。

Abstract: We consider the simultaneous estimation of multiple parameters in Hamiltonians of the form $H_{\vecθ}= H_{\vecθ}^P + H^C$, where $H_{\vecθ}^P$ encodes the unknown parameters and $H^C$ is a control term. We consider three basic paradigmatic frameworks for metrology: (i) dynamical, where the parameters are encoded unitarily, (ii) thermal, and (iii) ground state. We generalize previous bounds for single-parameter estimation to simultaneous multiparameter estimation for all three metrological settings and establish the conditions required for their saturability. We then focus on vector magnetometry, deriving tight bounds for the simultaneous estimation of magnetic-field components and identifying optimal many-body control Hamiltonians $H^C$ that saturate them. These results establish the fundamental limits of vector magnetometry for unitary dynamics, thermal equilibrium states, and ground states.

</details>


### [212] [Hyperbolic Restricted Boltzmann Machine Neural Quantum State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26032)
*H. L. Dao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出首个非欧几里得（non-Euclidean）非自回归（non-autoregressive）神经量子态——双曲受限玻尔兹曼机（hyperbolic Restricted Boltzmann Machine, HRBM），并在量子Sherrington-Kirkpatrick（QSK）模型的变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte-Carlo, VMC）框架下验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欧几里得（Euclidean）神经量子态（neural quantum state, NQS）在表示体积律纠缠（volume-law entanglement）量子系统时可能存在表达力局限，作者因此寻求更自然的几何结构以刻画此类系统。

Method: 构建双曲几何下的HRBM，并以非自回归形式在VMC设置中研究量子Sherrington-Kirkpatrick（QSK）模型；将系统规模从N=14扩展至N=24（希尔伯特空间维度增长512倍），与欧几里得版本RBM NQS进行对比。

Result: HRBM NQS在基态能量优化、Renyi-2熵（S_2）与von Neumann熵（S_vN）绝对纠缠熵重建误差上均稳定优于RBM NQS；HRBM能忠实复现跨越15个数量级的完整纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum），而RBM NQS持续高估次主导模式。此外得到RBM型NQS在QSK体积律系统中随希尔伯特空间指数增长的多项式标度结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了概念验证（proof-of-concept），表明得益于双曲几何底层构造的指数体积，双曲非自回归NQS拟设（ansatz）在表示体积律量子系统方面可能比传统欧几里得NQS更自然。

Abstract: We construct the first type of non-Euclidean non-autoregressive neural quantum state (NQS) in the form of the hyperbolic Restricted Boltzmann Machine (HRBM), which is studied in the variational Monte-Carlo (VMC) setting of the Quantum Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (QSK) model whose ground state exhibits volume-law entanglement. Across a 512-fold increase in the Hilbert space dimension corresponding to a system size increase from $N=14$ to $N=24$, HRBM NQS robustly outperforms its Euclidean version, the RBM NQS, in terms of better ground state energy optimization as well as lower Renyi-2 $S_2$ and von Neumann $S_{vN}$ absolute entanglement entropy reconstruction errors. More importantly, for all QSK system sizes, HRBM NQS demonstrates a superior expressivity in faithfully reproducing the entire entanglement spectrum of the QSK model from the top eigenvalues down to the tail end across 15 orders of magnitude, while RBM NQS consistently overestimates the sub-dominant modes. This work furnishes a proof-of-concept demonstrating that hyperbolic non-autoregressive NQS ansatzë, thanks to the exponential volume of the hyperbolic geometry underlying their constructions, might be more natural at representing volume-law quantum systems than conventional Euclidean NQS. Furthermore, an interesting byproduct of this work is the polynomial scaling result of RBM-type NQS ansatzë in the QSK volume-law system as the Hilbert space increases exponentially.

</details>


### [213] [Entanglement Embezzlement from Diffusive Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26362)
*Shi-Xin Zhang,Shuo Liu,Yu-Qin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示了一种守恒律机制：在积分施密特秩坐标（logarithmic Schmidt-rank coordinate）中重新分配施密特谱（Schmidt spectrum）的主导概率质量，从而控制纠缠挪用（entanglement embezzlement）这一操作资源，并给出固定 U(1) 电荷下典型随机纯态的有限误差转换律及其动力学演化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠挪用关注的是：在仅使用局域操作与经典通信（local operations and classical communication）且以小误差归还多体态的前提下，能从多体态中“借取”多少纠缠。该文旨在寻找支配这一操作资源的底层机制，并刻画其在守恒电荷与动力学演化下的行为。

Method: 对固定 U(1) 电荷下的典型随机纯态进行解析证明，给出有限误差转换律；对保持电荷的随机电路（charge-conserving random circuits），结合流体力学分析（hydrodynamic analysis）与大规模复制张量网络计算（replica tensor-network calculations）研究资源的动力学发展。

Result: 在固定 U(1) 电荷的典型随机纯态中，偏离半填充（half filling）时电荷偏置将 $O(\sqrt L)$ 的电荷涨落转化为 $O(\sqrt L)$ 的可借取纠缠，而半填充处的粒子—空穴对称（particle–hole symmetry）完全消除该贡献。动力学上，扩散（diffusion）在 $t^{1/4}$ 尺度上展宽积分施密特秩坐标中操作相关的分布，并增加可借取纠缠量。

Conclusion: 电荷输运（charge transport）在主导的体积律熵（volume-law entropy）饱和之后，仍持续重组纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）并激活纠缠挪用，表明守恒律机制是控制该操作资源的核心。

Abstract: Entanglement embezzlement asks how much entanglement can be borrowed from a many-body state by local operations and classical communication while returning that state with a small error. We uncover a conservation-law mechanism that redistributes the dominant probability mass of the Schmidt spectrum in the logarithmic Schmidt-rank coordinate and thereby controls this operational resource. For typical random pure states at fixed U(1) charge, we prove a finite-error conversion law: away from half filling, the charge bias converts $O(\sqrt L)$ charge fluctuations into $O(\sqrt L)$ borrowable entanglement, whereas particle--hole symmetry at half filling removes this contribution entirely. We then show how the resource develops dynamically in charge-conserving random circuits. Combining hydrodynamic analysis with large-scale replica tensor-network calculations, we find that diffusion broadens the operationally relevant distribution in the logarithmic Schmidt-rank coordinate on the scale $t^{1/4}$ and increases the amount of entanglement that can be borrowed. Charge transport therefore continues to reorganize the entanglement spectrum and activate embezzlement after the leading volume-law entropy has saturated.

</details>


### [214] [Lattice Lindbladian simulation by patching and merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26372)
*Kaoru Mizuta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对局域 Lindbladian 支配的耗散多体动力学，提出基于局域性（locality）的量子模拟算法：稀疏耗散情形达到近最优门数 O(Nt·polylog(Nt/ε))，通用有限程晶格情形实现 O((Nt)^{4/3}·polylog(Nt/ε)) 的门数，是该精度依赖下已知对系统尺寸依赖最小的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟由局域 Lindbladian（local Lindbladian）支配的耗散量子多体动力学是量子计算的基础任务，但哈密顿量模拟（Hamiltonian simulation）已接近最优门数，Lindbladian 模拟的同类结果仍难以实现，因此需要借助局域性来降低门数开销。

Method: 利用局域性的两项技术：拼接（patching）将耗散动力学分解为子系统上的动力学并以指数小误差近似，推广了 Haah-Hastings-Kothari-Low 算法中近最优哈密顿模拟的核心思想；合并（merging）将反向耗散动力学吸收进演化的其他部分，从而显著降低准概率采样（quasi-probabilistic sampling）的额外开销。

Result: 对含边界驱动（boundary-driven）在内的稀疏耗散系统，算法门数为 O(Nt·polylog(Nt/ε))，达到近最优；对具有有限程相互作用与耗散的通用晶格 Lindbladian，每样本模拟时间演化可观测量（time-evolved observables）的门数为 O((Nt)^{4/3}·polylog(Nt/ε))，采样复杂度为 Θ(ε^{-2})，且该门数在保持对 1/ε 的多对数依赖的算法中具有已知最小的系统尺寸依赖。

Conclusion: 结果表明局域性可被充分利用以快速量子模拟耗散多体动力学，为预测非平衡现象（nonequilibrium phenomena）与制备目标量子态等应用开辟了路径。

Abstract: Simulating the dynamics of dissipative quantum many-body systems governed by local Lindbladians is a fundamental task in quantum computation. While the near-optimal gate count has been achieved for Hamiltonian simulation, comparable results for Lindbladian simulation remain elusive. In this work, we develop quantum algorithms for lattice Lindbladians by exploiting their locality. First, we consider sparsely dissipative systems, in which the dissipation is sparsely located, including boundary-driven systems. We establish a near-optimal quantum algorithm for their dynamics with gate count $O(Nt \, \mathrm{polylog}(Nt/\varepsilon))$, where $N$ is the system size, $t$ is the evolution time, and $\varepsilon$ is the allowable error. We then consider generic lattice Lindbladians with finite-range interactions and dissipation, and develop an algorithm for simulating time-evolved observables with gate count $O((Nt)^{4/3} \, \mathrm{polylog}(Nt/\varepsilon))$ per sample with the sampling complexity $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$. The gate count has the smallest known dependence on the system size among algorithms retaining polylogarithmic dependence on $1/\varepsilon$. Our algorithms are based on two techniques that exploit locality: patching and merging. Patching decomposes dissipative dynamics into dynamics on subsystems with exponentially small error, generalizing a key idea underlying the Haah-Hastings-Kothari-Low algorithm for near-optimal Hamiltonian simulation. Merging absorbs reversed dissipative dynamics into other parts of the evolution, substantially reducing the overhead associated with quasi-probabilistic sampling. These results demonstrate that locality can be fully exploited to achieve fast quantum simulation of dissipative many-body dynamics, opening the way to applications such as predicting nonequilibrium phenomena and preparing desirable quantum states.

</details>


### [215] [Hilbert-space fragmentation selects channels of strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26453)
*Mingdi Xu,Kaixiang Lu,Daoguang Li,Lei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究考察强到弱自发对称破缺（strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking, SWSSB）在电荷扇区（charge sector）分裂为动力学不相连碎片（fragment）时的行为，并在一维 $t$-$J_z$ 链（$t$-$J_z$ chain）中给出了判定长程混态序（mixed-state order）的碎片级判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大混合电荷扇区中即使普通带电关联（charged correlations）消失，仍可能出现 SWSSB 序。已有研究多针对完整扇区，而电荷扇区在动力学上分裂为不相连碎片（dynamically disconnected fragments）时的序是否存续尚未明确，故需研究碎片化对混态序的影响。

Method: 采用带物种分辨退相位（species-resolved dephasing）的一维 $t$-$J_z$ 链，每个碎片由守恒自旋串（conserved spin word）标记。通过分析分离的有限区间（finite intervals）之间带电转移（charged transfer）保持该串的条件，导出中间自旋需满足的周期性约束（periodicity constraint），并在全态两点意义（full-state two-point sense）下考察保真度（fidelity）与 Rényi-1 序。

Result: 利用周期性约束，证明典型固定组分（fixed-composition）碎片中不存在长程保真度序与 Rényi-1 序，且该结论对任意给定有限区间内所有范数有界（norm-bounded）带电算子一致成立。具本原自旋串周期（primitive spin-word period）$k$ 的碎片支持一个 $k$ 粒子通道，其长程值恰为 $\nu^k(1-\nu)^k/k^2$，且少于 $k$ 个格点的带电算子无法承载该序。

Conclusion: 横向交换（transverse exchange）可连接各碎片、保持全局荷（global charges）不变，并在有限尺寸下恢复全扇区稳态（full-sector steady state）。因此，守恒自旋串决定了哪些局域通道能够支持混态序。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) can occur in a maximally mixed charge sector even when ordinary charged correlations vanish. We study this order when a charge sector splits into dynamically disconnected fragments. In a one-dimensional $t$-$J_z$ chain with species-resolved dephasing, each fragment is labeled by a conserved spin word. A charged transfer between separated finite intervals preserves that word only if the intervening spins satisfy a periodicity constraint. In the full-state two-point sense, we use this constraint to prove the absence of long-range fidelity and Rényi-1 order in typical fixed-composition fragments, uniformly over all norm-bounded charged operators in any prescribed finite interval. A fragment with primitive spin-word period $k$ supports a $k$-particle channel with exact long-distance value $ν^k(1-ν)^k/k^2$, and no charged operator on fewer than $k$ sites can carry this order. A transverse exchange connects the fragments while preserving the global charges and restores the full-sector steady state at finite size. The conserved spin word therefore selects the local channels that can support mixed-state order.

</details>


### [216] [Quantum Fisher information of driven-dissipative systems from Keldysh path integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26462)
*Malik Jirasek,Igor Lesanovsky,Viktoria Noel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种场论方法，将量子费舍尔信息（quantum Fisher information, QFI）与 Keldysh 路径积分（Keldysh path-integral）联系起来，从而在多体驱动耗散系统中得到 QFI 的半经典表达式，并在三类复杂度递增的系统中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动耗散多体量子系统（driven-dissipative many-body quantum systems）可用于量子增强传感（quantum enhanced sensing），其非经典关联（non-classical correlations）有望使测量精度超越标准量子极限（standard quantum limit）。然而，QFI 作为衡量该量子优势的指标，其计算需知悉完整的系统-环境态，在多体情形下十分困难，因此需要可扩展的计算框架。

Method: 采用适合多自由度系统的场论方法（field-theoretical approach），将 QFI 与 Keldysh 路径积分（Keldysh path-integral）建立联系，并通过对所谓量子场（quantum fields）进行受控展开，得到 QFI 的半经典（semiclassical）表达式。

Result: 在三个复杂度递增的系统中对该框架进行基准测试：驱动耗散谐振子（driven-dissipative harmonic oscillator）、边界时间晶体（boundary time crystal，在大系统尺寸下趋于半经典），以及二维集体发射原子阵列（two-dimensional array of collectively emitting atoms）。研究评估了半经典描述成立的参数区间，并表明其可处理超出精确方法（exact methods）能力范围的系统尺寸。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于 Keldysh 路径积分的半经典方法为多体驱动耗散系统中 QFI 的计算提供了可行途径，能够在特定参数条件下有效刻画量子优势，并拓展可研究的系统规模。

Abstract: Driven-dissipative many-body quantum systems can be exploited in quantum enhanced sensing protocols. Here, non-classical correlations may allow one to reach a measurement precision that surpasses the standard quantum limit. The relevant figure of merit for assessing such quantum advantage is the quantum Fisher information (QFI). In a many-body setting its computation is challenging as it requires knowledge of the full system-environment state. Here we make use of a field-theoretical approach, which is particularly well suited for systems with many degrees of freedom. In particular, we show that the QFI can be linked to a Keldysh path-integral. This route leads to a semiclassical expression of the QFI, which is obtained through a controlled expansion in the so- called quantum fields. We benchmark the framework on three systems of increasing complexity: the driven-dissipative harmonic oscillator, the boundary time crystal - which becomes semiclassical at large system sizes - and a two-dimensional array of collectively emitting atoms. We assess in which parameter regimes the semiclassical description holds and gives access to system sizes beyond the reach of exact methods.

</details>


### [217] [Evidence for a low-gap component in compressively stressed niobium thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26714)
*J. T. Paustian,Maciej Olszewski,Kiichi Okubo,Lingda Kong,B. L. T. Plourde,Ethan G. Arnault,Caleb W. Fink,Valla Fatemi,Ivan V. Pechenezhskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 溅射 Nb 薄膜的双轴应力（biaxial film stress）几乎不改变输运测量的超导转变温度与剩余电阻比，但微波谐振器性能随压应力显著退化；该现象可用附加低能隙组分（low-gap component）随压应力增强来解释，表明常规输运测量会遗漏影响微波性能的应力相关低能激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清薄膜应力（film stress）如何影响 Nb 薄膜的微波响应，并检验常规输运表征（transport measurements）是否足以反映超导微波器件的实际性能退化机制。

Method: 方法为制备一系列不同双轴应力的溅射（sputtered）Nb 薄膜，比较其输运性质与微波谐振器（microwave resonator）的损耗及频移，并用 Mattis–Bardeen 描述进行拟合分析。

Result: 输运结果显示各应力样品的超导转变温度（$9.0\pm0.2$K）与剩余电阻比（residual resistance ratio）几乎一致；但高压缩应力薄膜的谐振器呈现更强的温度依赖损耗（temperature-dependent loss）和更大频移。单相 Mattis–Bardeen 拟合给出的有效临界温度仅为 $2.4\pm0.2$K，约为输运值的四分之一。

Conclusion: 数据与存在附加低能隙组分（low-gap component）的图像一致，该组分贡献随压应力增大；结论是常规输运测量可能掩盖强烈影响微波性能的应力相关低能激发（low-energy excitations）。

Abstract: We investigate the microwave response of sputtered Nb films as a function of biaxial film stress. Although transport measurements show nearly identical superconducting transition temperatures ($9.0\pm 0.2$K) and residual resistance ratios across the stress series, microwave resonators fabricated from highly compressive films exhibit enhanced temperature-dependent loss and larger frequency shifts. A conventional single-phase Mattis--Bardeen description yields an effective critical temperature of $2.4\pm0.2$K, approximately a factor of four below the value obtained from transport measurements. We show that the data are instead consistent with the presence of an additional low-gap component whose contribution increases with compressive stress. These results show that conventional transport measurements can overlook stress-dependent low-energy excitations that strongly influence microwave performance.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Mesoscopic transition for $β$-ensembles at intermediary temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25301)
*Charlie Dworaczek Guera,Gaultier Lambert,Luke Peilen*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文在温度区间 $1/N \ll \beta(N) \le 1$ 下，为 $\beta$-系综（$\beta$-ensemble）或对数气体（log-gas）的线性统计量建立了介观中心极限定理（mesoscopic central limit theorem），并揭示了随机矩阵机制与泊松机制之间在临界尺度 $\eta \asymp 1/(N\beta(N))$ 处的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 $\beta$-系综在有限温度（intermediate temperature）下线性统计量的介观涨落行为，尤其是波动方差如何随温度参数 $\beta(N)$ 与介观尺度变化，并识别不同普适类（universality class）之间的转变。

Method: 方法上依赖于中间温度下的最优局部律（optimal local laws）以及适用于 $\beta$-系综的 Stein 方法（Stein's method）。文中还需构造对经典平衡测度（equilibrium measure）的新修正项，以实现线性统计量的合适重中心化（re-centring）并刻画其涨落。

Result: 结果显示在临界尺度 $\eta \asymp 1/(N\beta(N))$ 处存在一个转变：一侧为随机矩阵机制（Random Matrix regime），其极限方差由 $\mathsf{H}^{1/2}$-范数给出；另一侧为泊松机制（Poisson regime），其极限方差由 $L^2$-范数给出。文中亦描述了临界机制（critical regime），并给出了自由能展开（free energy expansion）。

Conclusion: 结论是 $\beta$-系综线性统计量的介观涨落由其温度参数与介观尺度共同决定，并在临界尺度处由随机矩阵型普适行为过渡到泊松型行为；所用局部律与 Stein 方法框架同时可导出自由能展开。

Abstract: This paper establishes a mesoscopic central limit theorem for linear statistics of $β$-ensembles or log-gas, as the dimension $N\to\infty$, in the temperature regime $1/N\llβ(N)\le 1$. For simplicity, we assume that the potential is one-cut regular and analytic. In this regime, the size of the fluctuations depends on $β(N)$ and the mesoscopic scale. We show that there is a transition at a critical $η\asymp 1/ Nβ(N)$ between a Random Matrix regime, where the limiting variance is given by the $\mathsf{H}^{1/2}$-norm and a Poisson regime where the limiting variance is given by the $L^2$-norm. We also describe the critical regime.
  The proof of the CLT relies on optimal local laws at intermediate temperatures and Stein's method for $β$-ensembles. In particular, in this regime, it is necessary to construct new correction terms to the classical equilibrium measure to obtain a suitable re-centring of linear statistics and describe their fluctuations. We also obtain a free energy expansion.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Beyond expressiveness in pairwise and higher-order models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26031)
*Cyril Rommens,Pietro Traversa,Guilherme Ferraz de Arruda,Yamir Moreno*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 论文主张"成对模型（pairwise models）与高阶模型（higher-order models）之争"不应被简化为表达力（expressive power）竞赛；通过区分四种常被混淆的概念并引入描述长度（description length）视角，作者提出以最小描述长度（minimum description length, MDL）为操作准则的对称立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有讨论常将图（graph）与超图（hypergraph）的比较框定为表达力之争，这一框架具有误导性：图模型虽可模拟超图模型的节点级动力学（node-level dynamics），但分组信息可能只是从结构转移到动力学中，而未被消除。因此需要更精确的概念区分与更合理的比较标准。

Method: 首先区分四个常被混同的概念：结构投影（structural projection）、功能可表示性（functional representability）、统计可辨识性（statistical identifiability）与机制充分性（mechanistic adequacy）；进而证明有限状态映射（finite-state map）的交互阶（interaction order）是映射本身的不变量（invariant），精确的表示变换无法降低潜在依赖阶。随后以描述长度（description length）重构比较，并提出结合结构代价、给定结构下的规则代价与模型拟合的操作性最小描述长度准则。

Result: 在无限制的算法层面，固定编译器（compiler）可在结构与规则之间移动信息且仅付出常数开销，故表达力本身并不偏袒图或超图；偏好仅在相对于显式模型类（model classes）、编码方案、正则性假设（regularity assumptions）与数据时才出现。对 $M$ 个规模为 $k$ 的不相交分组，团投影（clique projection）所需的边列表（edge list）渐近上比对应超边列表（hyperedge list）长 $k-1$ 倍。扩散（diffusion）、布尔动力学（Boolean dynamics）、生态交互（ecological interactions）与歧义投影（ambiguous projections）等示例分别呈现图偏好、超图偏好与未决情形。

Conclusion: 结论立场是对称的：既不应仅凭现象学（phenomenology）推断高阶结构，也不应将基于图的模拟（graph-based emulation）视为图即为最简约或科学上最充分描述的证据。

Abstract: The debate over pairwise and higher-order models is often cast as a contest of expressive power, but this framing is misleading. A graph with arbitrary multivariate node functions can emulate the node-level dynamics of many hypergraph models, yet this does not erase the grouping information encoded by the hypergraph: it may simply be shifted from structure to dynamics. We distinguish four notions that are often conflated: structural projection, functional representability, statistical identifiability, and mechanistic adequacy. We show that the interaction order of a finite-state map is an invariant of the map itself, so an exact change of representation cannot reduce the underlying order of dependence. We then recast the comparison in terms of description length. At the unrestricted algorithmic level, a fixed compiler can move information between structure and rule with only constant overhead, so expressiveness alone does not privilege graphs or hypergraphs. Preferences arise only relative to explicit model classes, coding schemes, regularity assumptions, and data. This motivates an operational minimum-description-length criterion combining structural cost, rule cost conditional on structure, and model fit. For $M$ disjoint groups of size $k$, we show that the clique projection requires an edge list asymptotically $k-1$ times longer than the corresponding hyperedge list, making projection a more expensive encoding of the same grouping. Examples spanning diffusion, Boolean dynamics, ecological interactions, and ambiguous projections illustrate graph-preferred, hypergraph-preferred, and unresolved cases. The resulting position is symmetric: higher-order structure should not be inferred from phenomenology alone, but neither should graph-based emulation be taken as evidence that a graph is the most parsimonious or scientifically adequate description.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Disorder-promoted stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25226)
*Arthur N. Montanari,Pietro Zanin,Adilson E. Motter*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该研究指出，以往关于“节点异质性（heterogeneity）抑制稳定性”的结论源自为数学可解性而做的模型简化；当节点动力学为高维并产生非厄米雅可比矩阵（non-Hermitian Jacobians）时，异质性反而可增强稳定性，且这一稳定化效应与非厄米性（non-Hermiticity）密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有网络动力学（network dynamics）研究普遍认为节点异质性会抑制稳定性，但这与自然界和工程系统中普遍存在的异质性相矛盾，作者旨在解释这一矛盾并重新审视异质性的作用。

Method: 作者通过摒弃为数学可解性引入的模型简化，考虑节点动力学为高维、进而产生非厄米雅可比矩阵的情形，并结合神经网络、电网和材料网络等系统进行分析；还讨论了通过非互易相互作用（nonreciprocal interactions）在生态网络中出现的类似效应。

Result: 在具有非厄米雅可比矩阵的系统中，节点异质性可增强稳定性，即使参数是随机无序的（randomly disordered）；非厄米性也是网络异质性稳定效应的基础，并且这种稳定性甚至可在一维节点动力学中通过非互易相互作用产生。

Conclusion: 该框架表明无序（disorder）并非负担，而是稳定复杂系统的一种通用资源。

Abstract: Previous studies of network dynamics have suggested that heterogeneity among nodes inhibits stability, at odds with the ubiquity of inherently heterogeneous natural and engineered systems. Here, we show that this conclusion arises from model reductions introduced for mathematical tractability and breaks down when nodal dynamics are higher-dimensional, yielding non-Hermitian Jacobians. In such systems, including neural, power-grid, and material networks, nodal heterogeneity can instead enhance stability, even when parameters are randomly disordered. Non-Hermiticity also underlies the stabilizing effects of network heterogeneity, which can arise even in one-dimensional nodal dynamics through nonreciprocal interactions, as shown for ecological networks. Our framework reveals disorder not as a liability but as a general resource for stabilizing complex systems.

</details>


### [221] [Measure-zero delocalization in the complex plane: exact mobility arcs in a non-Hermitian off-diagonal quasiperiodic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25877)
*Fu-Xi Zhong,Yi-Cai Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文将一维非厄米（non-Hermitian）非对角准周期无序晶格中的安德森局域化（Anderson localization）问题，从已有的厄米（Hermitian）镶嵌模型推广至非厄米情形，借助 Avila 全局理论（Avila's global theory）给出精确 Lyapunov 指数与复能量平面相图。核心发现为：存在一类新型"迁移弧"（mobility arcs），其与迁移环（mobility rings）在互补参数区间共存，且二者均统一源于广义 Joukowski 变换的代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅局限于厄米框架下的镶嵌模型（mosaic model），其非厄米推广尚属空白。作者希望刻画非厄米非对角准周期无序中迁移边（mobility edges）的一般形态，并揭示此前报道的迁移线（mobility lines）等奇异局域化现象背后的统一机制。

Method: 在一维晶格中引入非厄米非对角准周期无序，将既有厄米镶嵌模型推广至非厄米区域；采用 Avila 全局理论（Avila's global theory）推导精确 Lyapunov 指数，并据此得到复能量平面上的完整相图。

Result: 发现"迁移弧"（mobility arcs）——复平面上的开放曲线段——作为新一类迁移边及开放迁移边的一般形式，与闭迁移环（mobility rings）在互补参数区间共存。其局域化物理与迁移线一致：本征态退局域化当且仅当能量恰好落在这些集合上，任何偏离即导致局域态，构成测度为零（measure-zero）的退局域化现象，区别于迁移环所包围的有限面积退局域区域。此外，迁移环、弧与线均可追溯至广义 Joukowski 变换 P(E)=½(u−w²/u)：椭圆区域边界在多项式映射 P(E) 下的原像给出环，而椭圆内部的支割线（branch cut）在互补参数区间产生迁移弧与迁移线。

Conclusion: 非厄米非对角准周期模型中，迁移边可呈现环、弧、线三种几何形态，且共享同一代数起源；退局域化可仅发生于复平面上的零测度集合，拓展了对非厄米局域化相图的既有认识。

Abstract: We investigate Anderson localization in a one-dimensional lattice with non-Hermitian off-diagonal quasiperiodic disorder, extending a recently studied Hermitian mosaic model to the non-Hermitian regime. Using Avila's global theory, we derive the exact Lyapunov exponent and the complete phase diagram in the complex energy plane. This work contains two central findings. First, we discover mobility arcs---open curved segments in the complex plane---as a new class of mobility edges and the generic form of open mobility edges, which coexist with closed mobility rings in a complementary parameter regime. These arcs share the same localization physics as the previously reported mobility lines: eigenstates are delocalized if and only if their energies lie exactly on these sets; any deviation yields localized states. This constitutes a striking measure-zero delocalization phenomenon: delocalized states occupy only zero-measure sets (arcs or lines) in the complex plane, in sharp contrast to the mobility rings, which enclose a finite-area region of delocalized states. Second, we reveal that mobility rings, arcs, and lines all share a common mathematical origin in the generalized Joukowski transformation $P(E) = \frac{1}{2}(u - w^2/u)$, rooted in the algebraic structure of the underlying polynomial: the preimage of the boundary of an elliptical region under the polynomial map $P(E)$ gives the rings, while the branch cut inside this ellipse gives rise to the mobility arcs and lines in the complementary parameter regime.

</details>


### [222] [Topological phase transition driven by structural defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26586)
*Andrii Syrota,Andrej Mesaros,Pascal Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作通过连续引入 Stone-Wales（SW）缺陷，将装饰蜂窝晶格上的晶体态驱动至超均匀非晶态，并借助拓扑 Weaire-Thorpe 哈密顿量模型揭示 SW 缺陷可导致赝能带反转与陈数反转，从而预测非晶态的拓扑相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构缺陷（如向错与位错）的增殖会破坏长程晶体序，但晶体态向非晶态演化过程中拓扑性质如何变化尚需刻画。本文旨在建立从晶体态到超均匀非晶态的连续调控路径，并追踪其拓扑相的演化。

Method: 采用拓扑 Weaire-Thorpe 哈密顿量（Weaire-Thorpe Hamiltonian）描述电子；在装饰蜂窝晶格（decorated honeycomb lattice）上连续引入可视为位错偶极子的 Stone-Wales（SW）缺陷；结合绝热论证（adiabatic arguments）与计算高效的溢出指示量（spillage indicator）进行拓扑相图预测；并通过基于单个 SW 缺陷的有效哈密顿量中跃迁的重整化解释赝能带反转。

Result: SW 缺陷可引起赝能带反转（pseudo-band inversion），并相应地导致陈数（Chern number）反转；基于绝热论证与溢出指示量，可从晶体态出发预测非晶态的拓扑相图；有效哈密顿量中跃迁的重整化可解释赝能带反转的本质。

Conclusion: 通过 SW 缺陷的连续引入，晶体态可被驱动至超均匀非晶态，且其拓扑相变可由赝能带反转与陈数反转刻画，并可从晶体态预测非晶态的拓扑相图。

Abstract: Structural defects, such as disclinations and dislocations, destroy the long-range crystalline order as they proliferate. In this work, we continuously drive a system from a crystalline state on a decorated honeycomb lattice to a hyperuniform amorphous state, by consecutively introducing Stone-Wales (SW) defects, which can be viewed as dipoles of dislocations. Using a topological Weaire-Thorpe Hamiltonian model to describe electrons in this system, we demonstrate that SW defects can cause pseudo-band inversions and, correspondingly, a reversal of the Chern number. Using adiabatic arguments and a computationally efficient spillage indicator, we predict the topological phase diagram of the amorphous state from the crystalline one. We explain the nature of the pseudo-band inversion through the renormalization of the hopping in an effective Hamiltonian based on a single SW defect.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [The dHYM equation on crepant resolutions of Calabi-Yau cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25309)
*Eder M. Correa*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在非紧 Calabi-Yau 流形 $Z={\rm{Tot}}({\bf{K}}_X)$（$X$ 为有理齐性簇）上研究变形 Hermitian-Yang-Mills 方程，利用余齐一对称性将全非线性 PDE 约化为标量渐近自治 ODE，给出解的全局存在性条件与刚性分类，并构造首个非环面 Calabi-Yau 上的非平凡 Hermitian-Einstein 连接反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形 Hermitian-Yang-Mills（deformed Hermitian-Yang-Mills, dHYM）方程是全非线性 PDE，一般难以求解，且已知构造多局限于特定例子；本文旨在为非紧 Calabi-Yau 流形上的一类几何结构提供系统性的存在性判据并拓展已知例子的范围。

Method: 取 $X$ 为有理齐性簇（rational homogeneous variety）的 Fano 簇，$Z={\rm{Tot}}({\bf{K}}_X)$ 在反规范极化下是仿射锥顶点奇点的消解；借助 Calabi ansatz 所得 Ricci-平坦 Kähler 度量的余齐一（cohomogeneity-one）对称性，将 dHYM 方程约化为标量、渐近自治的常微分方程（ordinary differential equation, ODE）进行求解。

Result: 确定了保证解全局存在的拓扑相位（topological phase）精确条件；证明当总相位位于由 Lie 论数据给出的显式开区间内时，$Z$ 上每个全纯线丛均存在光滑整体定义、满足 dHYM 方程的 Hermitian 连接；并给出 dHYM 解退化为经典 Hermitian-Yang-Mills（HYM）连接的刚性分类。

Conclusion: 结果推广了既有构造并提供了大量新例；所提 ODE 方法可分析 dHYM 解的行为，据此构造出首个非环面（non-toric）Calabi-Yau 流形上线丛的显式非平凡 Hermitian-Einstein 连接且非 dHYM 的例子。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the deformed Hermitian-Yang-Mills (dHYM) equation on the non-compact Calabi-Yau manifold $Z = {\rm{Tot}}({\bf{K}}_{X})$, where $X$ is a rational homogeneous variety. Since $X$ is a Fano variety, $Z$ is a resolution of the singularity at the vertex of the affine cone ${\rm{Aff}}(X)$, provided the cone is built using the anticanonical polarization ${\bf{L}} = {\bf{K}}_{X}^{-1}$. Using the cohomogeneity-one symmetry of the Ricci-flat Kähler metric obtained via the Calabi ansatz on $Z$, we reduce the fully nonlinear PDE underlying the dHYM equation to a scalar, asymptotically autonomous ordinary differential equation (ODE). From this, we determine the exact condition on the topological phase that guarantees global existence of solutions. As an application, we show that every holomorphic line bundle over $Z$ admits a smooth, globally defined Hermitian connection solving the dHYM equation, provided the total phase lies in an explicit open interval determined by the Lie-theoretic data. Also, we prove a rigidity result classifying the exact geometric conditions under which the dHYM solution collapses into a classical Hermitian-Yang-Mills (HYM) connection. The results established generalize previous constructions and provide a substantial new class of examples. Furthermore, the approach presented allows one to study the behavior of the dHYM solutions through ODE methods. Using this approach, we construct the first explicit non-trivial example of a Hermitian-Einstein connection on a line bundle over a non-toric Calabi-Yau manifold which is not dHYM.

</details>


### [224] [Type changing, compact generalized Calabi--Yaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26387)
*Gil R. Cavalcanti*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用 Fei 关于 twistor 空间分支覆盖上非 Kähler Calabi–Yau 构造，首次给出类型改变（type-changing）的广义 Calabi–Yau 流形紧致例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义 Calabi–Yau 流形的类型改变现象此前缺乏紧致实例，本文旨在构造首批此类紧致例子。

Method: 基于 Fei 在 twistor 空间分支覆盖（branched covers）上构造非 Kähler Calabi–Yau 流形（non-Kähler Calabi–Yau manifolds）的方法，在三次超椭圆曲面与四维环面的乘积（$Σ_3 × \mathbb{T}^4$）上进行构造，起点为非 Kähler Calabi–Yau 结构。

Result: 所构造的结构一般为类型 1（type 1），并可改变为类型 3（type 3）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了类型改变广义 Calabi–Yau 流形的首个紧致例子，验证了 Fei 构造在广义几何框架下的适用性。

Abstract: We provide the first compact examples of type-changing generalized Calabi--Yau manifolds using Fei's construction of non-Kähler Calabi--Yau manifolds on branched covers of twistor spaces \cite{Fei16}. The structure, which is generically of type 1 and changes to type 3, is constructed on the product of a hyperelliptic surface of genus three and a four-torus, $Σ_3 \times \mathbb{T}^4$, with starting point a non-Kähler Calabi--Yau structure.

</details>

### [225] [Quarkyonic matter suppresses neutron-star $g$ modes and reverses their mass trend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25175)
*Probit J Kalita,Bharat Kumar*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该研究考察夸克物质（quarkyonic matter）对中子星 g 模（gravity modes）频率的影响：与混合星（hybrid stars）中一阶夸克相变抬高频率相反，夸克-核子化学平衡由强相互作用维持，使夸克在振荡周期内不获得热力学自由度，浮力因子骤降，g 模被限制在核心之外的核子壳层，频率由对照组的 158–522 Hz 降至 81–254 Hz，且随质量的变化趋势与对照相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: g 模是唯一反映致密物质组分（composition）而非刚度（stiffness）的中子星振荡模式，而已有关于混合星的工作表明一阶夸克相变会提高其频率。该文旨在检验夸克物质（quarkyonic matter）这一替代致密物质情景对 g 模频率与分层结构的影响，以判断 g 模能否作为区分不同致密物质状态的探针。

Method: 基于夸克-核子化学平衡由强相互作用维持的设定，推导出 $c_s^2-c_e^2$ 仅由轻子梯度构成的正定二次型，并分析核子动量壳层对声速与浮力因子的作用。选取共享同一等标量扇区（isoscalar sector）的十个状态方程（七个夸克物质模型、三个核子对照，且匹配对称能斜率），求解 $l=2$ 的相对论性 Cowling 问题（十方程），并将频率分解为动力学频率 $(GM/R^3)^{1/2}$ 与无量纲余项。

Result: 核子动量壳层使两个声速同时变硬而非分离，早期相变处浮力因子下降 9.5 至 19 倍，核心仅呈弱分层。g 模被限制在核心外的核子壳层，其赖以存在的水平流被排除在核心之外，核心近似刚性位移。频率在对照组为 158–522 Hz，在夸克物质模型中降至 81–254 Hz；在 $L=50$ MeV 的匹配对中，$1.4M_\odot$ 处下降 14%，且该频率随质量下降，而对照组随质量上升。将频率写为动力学频率乘无量纲余项后，$f$、$p_1$ 的趋势继承自质量-半径关系；核子序列中 $g_1$ 余项保持在 2–9% 内恒定，夸克物质序列中下降四分之一至三分之一，在固定致密度与 $L/K_0$ 下偏离核子 g 模关系达两倍。

Conclusion: 结果为 Cowling 近似值，属约 10% 量级的下界，但趋势符号在该量级修正下仍保持不变。g 模频率的下降及其随质量的反向变化，可作为夸克物质与核子物质状态方程的区分性特征。

Abstract: Gravity (g) modes are the only neutron-star oscillations that report on the composition of dense matter rather than on its stiffness, and work on hybrid stars has established that a first-order quark transition raises their frequency. Quarkyonic matter does the opposite. Quark-nucleon chemical equilibrium is maintained by the strong interaction, so the quarks acquire no thermodynamic freedom on an oscillation period and $c_s^2-c_e^2$ reduces to a positive-definite quadratic form in the lepton gradients alone. The nucleon momentum shell stiffens both sound speeds together instead of separating them, so the buoyancy factor collapses by a factor of 9.5 to 19 at an early transition and the core is left only weakly stratified. Solving the $l=2$ relativistic Cowling problem for ten equations of state that share one isoscalar sector, seven quarkyonic and three nucleonic controls at matched symmetry-energy slope, we found the g-mode confined to the nucleonic shell outside the core, with the horizontal flow that it lives on excluded from the core while the core is displaced almost rigidly. Its frequency falls from 158-522 Hz across the controls to $81-254$~Hz across the quarkyonic models, and by 14\% at 1.4$M_\odot$ for the matched pair at $L=50\mev$; more important than the shift, it decreases with mass where the controls rise. Writing each frequency as the dynamical frequency $(GM/R^3)^{1/2}$ times a dimensionless remainder separates structure from composition. The $f$ and $p_1$ trends prove to be inherited from the mass--radius relation; the $g_1$ remainder, constant to $2-9\%$ along a nucleonic sequence, falls by a quarter to a third along a quarkyonic one and departs by a factor of two from the nucleonic $g$-mode relation at fixed compactness and $L/K_0$. These are Cowling values, lower bounds at the ten-per-cent level, and the sign of the trend survives a correction of that size.

</details>
